|| kṛtyakalpataruḥ ||
|| śrīlakṣmīdharaviracitaḥ ||
|| śrāddhakāṇḍam ||
|| maṅgalācaraṇam ||
|| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
yaḥ puṇyātmā dhinoti pratidinam amitair hantakārair manuṣyān
kavyair avyājabhavyaiḥ pitṛgaṇam amarastomam astokahavyaiḥ |
śrīnāthadhyānasindhusravaṇasukharasopetacetomarālaḥ
saḥ śrīlakṣmīdharo ’yaṃ viracayati sudhīḥ śrāddhakāṇḍaṃ caturtham |
|| pratijñā ||
śrāddhasvarūpaṃ tadbhedāḥ kālā deśās tataḥ param |
haviṣyāṇi vivarjyāni brāhmaṇānāṃ parīkṣaṇam ||
paṅktīnāṃ pāvanā varjyā apāṅkteyā nimantraṇam |
nimantritasya niyamāḥ śrāddham āvāhanāgrataḥ ||
śrāddhasaṃpada ātithyam apāsyaś ca yathākramam |
vidhīnāṃ paribhāṣā ca japāsanamukho vidhiḥ ||
pariveṣaṇamantrasya saṅkalpo bhojane vidhiḥ |
japādividhir atrāpi karma śrāddhottaraṃ kramāt ||
[2]        pitrādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ ca śrāddhastutir anukramāt |
āmaśrāddhaṃ tathā jīvatpitṛkaśrāddham eva ca ||
atha dvipitṛkaśrāddham ekoddiṣṭam anantaram |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaśrāddhaṃ śrāddhaṃ tatkaraṇottaram ||
śrāddhādhikāriṇo ’py atra śrāddham abhyudayāśritam |
śrāddhaṃ jīvata evātra kathyate tadanantaram ||
lakṣmīdhareṇa kṛtinā nānāmunivaroktibhiḥ |
dvātriṃśatparvaṇām evaṃ śrāddhakāṇḍe nirucyate ||
[3]
||1||
|| atha śrāddhasvarūpam ||
atha śrāddhakalpaḥ |
tatra hārītaḥ [a. 12]
athātaḥ śrāddhakalpaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatiḥ | tasyodagayanaṃ śuklo ’haḥ pūrvāhaś ca | tad devānām | dakṣiṇāyanaṃ tāmisro rātriḥ aparāhnaś ca | tat pitṝṇām | nānyabhāvaḥ kṛtsnabhāvād ubhayathā ijyante | vyāmiśratvād ahar ahaḥ | athāpy atrodāharanti:
yasyeme vartmanī havyakavye vahato devā yasyārdham, pitaro’rdhaṃ tathaiva nimeṣā yasya mūlaṃ, taṃ ṛtavo vai yajantam amṛtatvam iyāt ||
śuklaś ca tāmisraś ca pakṣau | yasmād devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ caivaṃ vibhaktaḥ kālaḥ, tasmāt kṛstnasya kālasyānenaiva prakāradvayena saṃgṛhītatvāt, ‘nānyabhāvaḥ’ nānyathātvaṃ, tadvyāmiśratvāt pūrvāhnāparāhṇādibhedena | ‘ahar ahar ubhayathāpi ijyante’ prakāradvayena pūrvāhnāparāhādibhedena | ‘ime vartmanī’ devayānapitṛyānākhye | etasmād arthavādavākyāt sāmānyataḥ śrāddhakartavyatāvidhiḥ kalpyate || (1)
[4]        śrāddhasvarūpaṃ ca āpastambena vyaktam uktam | yad āha āpastambaḥ [2, 16, 1-4]
athaitan manuḥ śrāddhaśabdaṃ karma provāca | prajāniḥśreyasārthaṃ [ca] | tatra pitaro devatā brāhmaṇas tv āhavanīyārthe | māsi māsi kāryam | aparapakṣasyāparāhnaḥ śreyān || iti
‘śrāddhaśabdam’ śrāddham iti śabdo vācako yasya tat tathā | ‘brāhmaṇas tv āhavanīyārthe’ āhavanīyavad vayaktadravyapratipattisthānatvena | etena pitṝn uddiśya dravyatyāgo brāhmaṇasvīkaraṇaparyantaṃ śrāddhasvarūpaṃ pradhānam ity arthād uktaṃ bhavati | anyatra tu daivaśrāddhādau śrāddhaśabdaprayogo gauṇaḥ taddharmaprāptyarthaḥ, kauṇḍapāyinām ayanahoma ivāgnihotraśabdaḥ | atra ca pitrādīnāṃ pratyekaṃ devatātvam, na samuditānām,
namo viśvebhyo devebhya ity annam ādau prāṅmukhayor nivedayet | pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya ca nāmagotrābhyām udaṅmukheṣu”
iti viṣṇunā [ 73, 13-14] pratyekam evoddeśyatvenābhidhānāt,
tato madhughṛtāktaṃ tu soṣṇam        annaṃ tilānvitam |
gṛhītvā devatīrthena praṇavenaiva tatpunaḥ ||
etad vo ’nnam itīty uktvā viśvān devāṃś ca saṃyajet |
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam anaṃ svadhā namaḥ ||
iti brahmapurāṇavacanācca | yat tu “amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt” ityādivākyeṣu bahuvacanam, tan na pramāṇāntarasiddhapratyekadevatātvānurodhād abhidhānakriyāpekṣam' “gargā bhojyantām” itivat | nāpi pitrādeḥ sapatnīkasya devatātvam, tathātve pramāṇābhāvāt | pratyuta,
[5]        na yoṣidvayaḥ prathag dadyād avasānadinād ṛte |
svabhartṛpiṇḍamātrābhyas tṛptir āsāṃ yataḥ smṛtā ||
iti śātātapavacane bhartṛsaṃbandhipiṇḍabhāgasyaiva patnītṛptihetutvenābhidhānād viparītam eva pramāṇam asti ||
|| iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāndhivigrahikabhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau śrāddhakāṇḍe śrāddhasvarūpam ||

|| 2 ||
|| atha śrāddhabhedāḥ ||
viśvāmitraḥ |
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍanam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ goṣṭhyāṃ śuddhayartham aṣṭamam ||
karmāṅgaṃ navamaṃ proktaṃ daivikaṃ daśamaṃ smṛtam |
yātrāsv ekādaśaṃ proktaṃ puṣṭyarthaṃ dvādaśaṃ smṛtam ||
vivṛtaṃ caitad bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
ahany ahani yac chrāddhaṃ tan nityam iti kīrtitam |
vaiśvadevavihīnaṃ tad aśaktāv udakena tu ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ tan naimittikam ucyate |
tad apy adaivakaṃ kāryam ayugmān bhojayed dvijān ||
kāmāya vihitaṃ kāmī abhipretārthasiddhaye |
pārvaṇena vidhānena tad apy uktaṃ khagādhipa |
vṛddhau yat kriyate śrāddhaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tad ucyate |
sarva pradakṣiṇaṃ kāryaṃ pūrvāhṇe tūpavītinā |
gandhodakatilair yuktaṃ kuryāt pātracatuṣṭayam |
arghyārthaṃ pitṛpātreṣu pretapātraṃ prasecayet ||
ye samānā iti dvābhyām etaj jñeyaṃ sapiṇḍanam |
nityena tulyaṃ śeṣaṃ syād ekoddiṣṭaṃ striyā api ||
[7]        amāvāsyāṃ yat kriyate tat pārvaṇam udāhṛtam |
kriyate vā parvaṇi yat tat pārvaṇam udāhṛtam ||
goṣṭhayāṃ yat kriyate śrāddhaṃ goṣṭhīśrāddhaṃ tad ucyate |
bahūnāṃ viduṣāṃ saṃpat sukhārthaṃ pitṛtṛptaye ||
kriyate śuddhaye yat tad brāhmaṇānāṃ tu bhojanam |
śuddhayartham iti tat proktaṃ vainateya manīṣibhiḥ ||
niṣekakāle some ca sīmantonnayane tathā |
jñeyaṃ pusaṃvane caiva śrāddhaṃ karmāṅgam eva ca ||
devān uddiśya yac chrāddhaṃ tad daivikam ihocyate |
haviṣyeṇa viśiṣṭena saptamyādiṣu yatnataḥ ||
gacchan deśāntaraṃ yas tu śrāddhaṃ kuryāt tu sarpiṣā |
yātrārtham iti tat proktaṃ praveśe ca na saṃśayaḥ ||
śarīropacaye śrāddham arthopacaya eva ca |
puṣṭyartham etad vijñeyam aupacārikam eva vā ||
7
'vṛddhiḥ' putrajanmādiḥ | 'nityena tulyaṃ śeṣaṃ syāt' iti tu samantrāvaśyakāryaṃ pārvaṇam ekoddiṣṭaṃ cābhimatam | tenāyam arthaḥ — sapiṇḍīkaraṇasya cobhayātmakatvāt (?) pārvaṇāṃśe pārvaṇen ekoddiṣṭāṃśa ekoddiṣṭena tulyaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ aṅgajātaṃ syād iti | 'ekoddiṣṭaṃ striyā api' iti ca, atraikoddiṣṭapadenaikoddiṣṭayogāt sapiṇḍīkaraṇam evābhihitaṃ prasaṅgāt strīsaṃbandhenāpi | 'kriyate vā parvaṇi' ity atra parvaśabdaḥ saṃkrāntyādiparaḥ | 'goṣṭhī' cātra śrāddhakartṛsamudāyaḥ | 'saṃpatsukhārthaṃ' śrāddhasāmagrīsaṃpadā yat sukhaṃ tadarthaṃ bahūnāṃ viduṣāṃ kenacin nimittena yugapacchrāddhe [8] kartavya upasthite pṛthakpākādyasaṃpattau saṃbhūya śrāddhasāmagrīsaṃpādanena yac chrāddhaṃ kriyate tad goṣṭhīśrāddham iti śaṅkhadharaḥ | 'niṣekakāle some ca' ityādi śrautasmārtakarmopalakṣaṇam | deśāntaragamane gṛhapraveśe ca yac chrāddhaṃ tat tīrthayātrārthaṃ deśāntaragamane tīrthayātrāṃ kṛtvā gṛhapraveśe ca boddhavyam, 'yo yaḥ kaścit' ityādibrahmapurāṇavacanāt | 'śarīropacaye' śarīropacayahetau rasāyanādau kartavye |
|| iti śrībhahṛdayadharātmajamahāsāndhivigrahikabhaṭṭaśrīmallakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau śrāddhakāṇḍe śrāddhabhedāḥ ||

|| atha śrāddhakālāḥ ||
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 217- 218]
amāvāsyāṣṭakā vṛddhiḥ kṛṣṇapakṣo ’yanadvayam |
dravyaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpattir viṣuvatsūryasaṃkramaḥ ||
vyatīpāto gajacchāyā grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ |
śrāddhaṃ prati ruciś caiva śrāddhakālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
‘vyatīpātaḥ’ [yogaviśeṣaḥ] sūryācandramasoḥ krāntisāmyasamayaḥ | 'gajacchāyā' brahmapurāṇoktā |
yogo maghātrayodaśyoḥ kuñjaracchāyasaṃjñakaḥ |
bhaven maghāyāṃ saṃsthe ca śaśiny arke karasthite || iti |
[10] viṣṇuḥ [76, 1-2; 77, 1-6]
amāvāyās tisro ‘ṣṭakās tisro ’nvaṣṭakāḥ māghī prauṣṭhapadyūrdhvaṃ kṛṣṇatrayodaśī vrīhiyavapākau ca | iti
etāṃs tu śrāddhakālān vai nityān āha prajāpatiḥ |
śrāddham eteṣv akurvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
ādityasaṃkramaṇam | viṣuvaddvayam | viśeṣeṇāyanadvayam | vyatīpātaḥ | janmarkṣam | abhyudayaś ca |
etāṃs tu śrāddhakālān vai nityān āha' prajāpatiḥ |
śrāddham eteṣu yad dattaṃ tad ānantyāya kalpate ||
‘māghī' paurṇamāsī, tathaiva samudāyaprasiddheḥ |
manuḥ [3, 112-113]
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya vipraś candrakṣaye ’gnimān |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryān māsānumāsikam ||
pitṝṇāṃ māsikaṃ śrāddham anvāhāryaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
tac cāmiṣeṇa kartavyaṃ praśastena prayatnataḥ ||
'pitṛyajño’ atra piṇḍapitṛyajñaḥ | 'piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ ' piṇḍānām anu paścād āhriyate kriyate yad darśaśrāddhaṃ tat tathā | 'māsānumāsikaṃ ' pratimāsabhavam | 'pitṝṇāṃ masikaṃ ' iti | māsikaṃ darśaśrāddhaṃ yat piṇḍānām anu paścād āhārya kartavyaṃ viduḥ budhā ity arthaḥ | etena piṇḍānvāhāryakam iti saṃjñākaraṇam | nirvacanaṃ guṇavidhānārtham uktaṃ bhavati | 'āmiṣeṇa' praśastena khaḍgamāṃsādinā |
[11] chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ | [2,81 - 92]
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṣīṇe rājani śasyate |
vāsarasya tṛtīyāṃśe nātisandhyāsamīpataḥ ||
yadā caturdaśīyāmaṃ turīyam anupūrayet |
amāvāsyā kṣīyamāṇā tadaiva śrāddham iṣyate ||
yad uktaṃ yad ahas tv eva darśanaṃ naiti candramāḥ |
tat kṣayāpekṣayā jñeyaṃ kṣīṇe rājani cety api ||
yac coktaṃ dṛśyamāne ’pi tac caturdaśyapekṣayā |
amāvāsyāṃ pratīkṣeta tadante vāpi nirvapet ||
aṣṭame ’ṃśe caturdaśyāḥ kṣīṇo bhavati candramāḥ |
amāvāsyāṣṭamāṃśe ca punaḥ kila bhaved aṇuḥ ||
āgrahāyaṇy amāvāsyā tathā jyeṣṭhasya yā bhavet |
viśeṣam ābhyāṃ bruvate candracāravido janāḥ ||
atrendur āyaprahare ’vatiṣṭhate caturthabhāgonakalāvaśiṣṭaḥ | tadanta eva kṣayam eti kṛtsnam evaṃ jyotiścakravido vadanti ||
yasminn abde dvādaśaikaś ca yavyas
tasmiṃs tṛtīyā paridṛśyā na jāyate |
evaṃ cāraṃ candramaso viditvā
kṣīṇe tasminn aparāhṇe ca dadyāt ||
saṃmiśrā yā caturdaśyā amāvāsyā bhavet kvacit |
kharvitāṃ tāṃ viduḥ kecid upedhvam iti cāpare ||
vardhamānām amāvāsyāṃ lakṣayed apare ’hani |
yāmāṃs trīn adhikān vāpi pitṛyajñas tato bhavet ||
pakṣādāv eva kurvīta sadā pakṣādikaṃ carum |
pūrvāhna eva kurvanti viddhe ’py anye manīṣiṇaḥ ||
[12]        'kṣīṇe rājani' kṣīṇe some | 'tṛtīyāṃśe' tridhā vibhaktadivasatṛtīyabhāge, aparāhna iti yāvat | 'caturdaśīyāmaṃ’ caturdaśyā yāmam | 'turīyaṃ' caturtham | 'pūrayet' amāvāsyā kṣīyamāṇeti sambandhaḥ | ‘yad uktaṃ yadahas tv eva’ ityādinā candradarśanaśrutivirodham āśaṅkya parīhāraḥ kṛtaḥ, adarśanasya kṣayopalakṣaṇatvāt | ‘dṛśyamāne ‘py ekadā’ iti gobhilavacanam [1,7,9], tad apītthaṃbhūtacaturdaśīviṣayam | 'tadante' caturdaśīśeṣe | yadāmāvāsyā śrāddhayogyakālavyāpinī na bhavati tadāyaṃ pakṣo boddhavyaḥ | itarathā tu prathama eva pakṣa: | 'amāvāsyāṣṭamāṃśa' iti, yadāmāvāsyādyaṣṭamo ‘ṃśo ’parāhnavyāpanayogyo bhavaty aparadine tadāpy aṇurūpasya candramaso vidyamānatvāt kṣayābhāvān na tatra śrāddhaṃ kartavyam | ata eva hetor yatrāmāvāsyāyā na vṛddhikṣayau, kiṃ tu viṣṭambhenobhayadinayor aparāhṇasaṃbandhāmāvāsyāsaṃbhavas tadā pūrvadina eva śrāddhaṃ kartavyam | ‘kṣīṇe rājani śasyata’ iti manuvacanāt | āgrahāyaṇajyeṣṭhāmāvāsyayoḥ tajjyotiḥśāstrānusāreṇa candragatibailakṣaṇyāt, 'aṣṭame ‘ṃśe caturdaśyāḥ kṣīṇo bhavati' ityādiprakārasyāsaṃbhavād uttaradina evāsyāṣṭamāṃśe ’pi śrāddhaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | ‘tadanta [13] eva' iti amāvāsyānta evety arthaḥ | 'kharvitāṃ' iti nindayānuṣṭhānāṅgatvam uktam | apare tu tām eva 'upedhvaṃ' iti, arthānuṣṭhānārtham upagacchateti manyante | evaṃ hi vikalpe vyavasthām āha 'vardhamānāṃ' ityādi | etenāmāvāsyāvṛddhipakṣe caturdaśīsaṃmiśrā heyaiva | itarathā tu kṣīyamāṇatve stambhe caturdaśīmiśraiva grāhyānuṣṭanārtham ity uktam || nanv evaṃ vardhamānāmāvāsyāpakṣe tasyām evāmāyām anuṣṭheyānuṣṭhāne pratipadanuṣṭheyaṃ pakṣādicaruprabhṛti dvitīyāviddhe ’pi prasajyeta | tac cātyantaniṣiddham |
parvaṇo yaś caturtho ’ṃśa ādyāḥ pratipadas trayaḥ |
yāgakālaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ prātaryukto manīṣibhiḥ ||
iti bodhāyanenaitāvata eva kālasya yajñāṅgatvenoktatvāt | dvitīyāviddhe ‘eṣa vai sumanā nāmeṣṭiḥ’ ityāditaittirīyaśrutau nindāvacanāc ca | tatrāha |
pūrvā eva kurvanti viddhe ’py anye manīṣiṇaḥ | iti |
anenedam uktaṃ bhavati — dvitīyārahitasyeṣṭikālasya prātaryuktasya prāptau satyāṃ dvitīyāsahito ninditaḥ | aprāptau sa eva yāgāṅgam iti || amāvāsyāprasaṅgād idam uktam || brāhmapurāṇe
amāvāsyāyāṃ śravaṇaṃ rakṣoghnaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yadā |
tadā pitṛbhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ tu dattam akṣayyam ucyate ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3, 14, 7-9; 15-16] |
amāvāsyā yadā maitraviśākhāsvātiyoginī |
śrāddhe pitṛgaṇas tṛptiṃ prāpnuyād aṣṭavārṣikīm ||
amāvāsyā yadā puṣye raudre carkṣe punarvasau |
dvādaśābdaṃ tadā tṛptiṃ prayānti pitaro ’rcitāḥ ||
[14]        vāsavājaivāpād ṛkṣe pitṝṇāṃ tṛptim icchatām |
vāruṇena tv amāvāsyā devānām api durlabhā ||
māghāsite pañcadaśī kadācid
upaiti yogaṃ yadi vāruṇena |
ṛkṣeṇa kālaḥ sa paraḥ pitṝṇāṃ
na hy alpapuṇyair nṛpa labhyate ’sau ||
kāle dhaniṣṭhā yadi nāma tasmin
bhavet tu bhūpāla tadā pitṛbhyaḥ |
dattaṃ jalānnaṃ pradadāti tṛptiṃ
varṣāyutaṃ tatkulajair manuṣyaiḥ ||
tatraiva ced bhādrapadās tu pūrvāḥ
kāle yadā tat kriyate pitṛbhyaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ parāṃ tṛptim upetya tena
yugaṃ sahasraṃ pitaraḥ svapanti ||
'maitraṃ ' anūrādhā | 'raudraṃ' ārdrā | 'vāsavaṃ’ dhaniṣṭhā | 'ajaikapāt' pūrvabhādrapadā | 'vāruṇaṃ' śatabhiṣak | kātyāyanaḥ |
śraddhāyutaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvīta śākenāpi | nāparapakṣam atikramet | māsi māsi cośanam iti śrutiḥ ||
baijavāpaḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe śrāddhaṃ kurvītānaṃ saṃskṛtya |
'saṃskṛtya' paktvā |
manuḥ [3, 276, 278]
kṛṣṇapakṣe daśamyādau varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śrāddhe praśastās tithayo yathaitā na tathetarāḥ ||
yathā caivāparaḥ pakṣaḥ pūrvapakṣād viśiṣyate |
tathā śrāddhasya pūrvāhṇād aparāhno viśiṣyate ||
[15] brahmapurāṇe |
payomūlaphalaiḥ śākaiḥ kṛṣṇapakṣe ca sarvadā |
parādhīnaḥ pravāsī vā nirdhano vāpi mānavaḥ ||
manasā bhāvaśuddhena śrāddhe dadyāt tilodakam |
nāśnanti pitaraś ceti kṛtvā havyamale tu yaḥ ||
na tarpayati tān bhaktyā dehāt tadrudhiraṃ bata |
pibanti sarvanāśaṃ ca kurvanty anu pade pade ||
tasmād bhayena ca pitṝṃs tarpayet satataṃ budhaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
pakṣānte nirvapet tebhyo aparāhṇe ca vedavit |
apāṃ samīpe dūrvāsu darbheṣu sikatāsu ca ||
'tebhyaḥ' pitṛbhyaḥ, 'pitaro ’sya na gṛhṇanti ' ity atraiva prakaraṇe yamenoktatvāt | 'darbheṣu' darbhamayadeśeṣu | evaṃ dūrvādiṣv api |
tathā |
āṣāḍhyām atha kārttikyāṃ māghyāṃ trīn pañca vā dvijān |
tarpayet pitṛpūrvaṃ tu tad asyākṣayyam ucyate ||
asyārthaḥ — śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā punas trīn pañca vā viprān pṛthag eva bhojayet, tad akṣayyaphalaṃ bhavati || gautamaḥ [15, 1–4]
atha śrāddham amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt | pañcamīprabhṛti vāparapakṣasya yathā śrāddham | sarvasmin vā dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasannidhau | kālaniyamaḥ śaktitaḥ ||
[16]        yo ’yam amāvāsyādikālaniyama uktaḥ śaktasya dravyādiyogino arogiṇo
boddhavyam | asāmarthye tu tadakaraṇe ’pi na pratyavaiti ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 43] |
śrāvaṇyāgrahāyiṇyoś cānvaṣṭakyāṃ ca pitṛbhyo dadyāt |
nigamaḥ |
āhitāgneḥ pitrarcanaṃ piṇḍair eva brāhmaṇān api vā bhojayet pūrvaśruteḥ | aparapakṣe yadahaḥ saṃpadyeta | amāvāsyāyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇāṣṭakātīrthatithidravyabrāhmaṇasaṃpatsu cikīrṣeta ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
kanyāṃ gate savitari yāny ahāni tu ṣoḍaśa |
kratubhis tāni tulyāni teṣu dattam athākṣayam ||
tathā |
aśvayuk kṛṣṇapakṣe tu śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ dine dine |
tribhāgahīnaṃ pakṣaṃ vā tribhāgaṃ tv ardham eva vā ||
śyāmākais tu viśeṣeṇa prasiddhais tatpitṛpriyaiḥ |
‘dine dine’ pratidinam, sakala eva pakṣa ityarthaḥ | 'tribhāgahīnaṃ' pañcamīm āramyety arthaḥ || 'tribhāgaṃ' daśamīm ārabhya | 'ardhaṃ' pakṣasyāṣṭamīm ārabhyety arthaḥ |
tathā |
yāvac ca kanyātulayoḥ kramād āste divākaraḥ |
tāvac chrāddhasya kālaḥ syāc chūnyaṃ pretapuraṃ tadā ||
[17] ‘tāvacc hrāddhasya kālaḥ’ iti, yasya kutaścin nimittāt kanyāgatāparapakṣe śrāddhaṃ na niṣpannam, taṃ prati yāvat tulāsthaḥ savitā tāvān śrāddhakālo vihita ity arthaḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
yeyaṃ dīpānvitā rājan khyātā pañcadaśī bhuvi |
tasyāṃ dadyān na ced dattaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu mahālaye ||
'mahālaye' kanyāgatāparapakṣe | brahmapurāṇe |
śrāddhaṃ viṣuvatoḥ kuryāt tathā cāyanayor dvayoḥ |
dinalope vyatīpāte grastayoś candrasūryayoḥ ||
'dinalopaḥ' yasminn ahorātre dinatrayasparśaḥ |
tathā |
yugādyeṣu yugānteṣu śrāddham akṣayam ucyate ||
vāyupurāṇe |
pitryadānāya mūle syūr aṣṭakās tisra eva ca |
kṛṣṇapakṣe variṣṭhā hi pūrvā caindrīti bhāṣyate ||
prājāpatyā dvitīyā syāt tṛtīyā vaiśvadevikī |
ādyā yūpaiḥ sadā kāryā māṃsair anyā bhavet tathā ||
śākaiḥ kāryā tṛtīyā syād eṣa dravyagato vidhiḥ |
anvaṣṭakā pitṝṇāṃ vai nityam eva vidhīyate ||
yā cāpy anyā caturthī syāt tāṃ ca kuryād viśeṣataḥ |
āsu śrāddhaṃ ca yaḥ kuryāt sarvasvenāpi nityaśaḥ ||
kṣipraṃ prāpnoti śreyāṃsi paratreha ca modate |
pitaraḥ parvakāleṣu tithikāleṣu devatāḥ ||
sarve gṛhastham āyānti nipānam iva dhenavaḥ |
yasya te pratigaccheyur aṣṭakāsu hy apūjitāḥ ||
moghās tasya bhavanty āśāḥ paratreha ca nityaśaḥ ||
[18] 'mūle' ādau, pradhānasthāna iti yāvat | aṣṭakāyās tithiparvobhayarūpatvāt devapitṛpūjāvaśyaṃ kartavyā |
brahmapurāṇe |
aindrayāṃ tu prathamāyāṃ tu śākaiḥ saṃtarpayet pitṝn |
prājāpatyāṃ dvitīyāyāṃ māsaiḥ śuddhaiś ca tarpayet ||
vaiśvadevyāṃ tṛtīyāyām apūpaiś ca yathākramam |
varṣāsu medhyaśākais tu caturthyām eva sarvadā ||
'śuddhaiḥ' praśastaiḥ | atra ca śākādivikalpaḥ śākhābhedavyavasthitaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
ayugmās tithayaḥ sarvā yugmābhyaḥ pratipūjitāḥ |
kālataḥ pūjitau māsau māghaproṣṭhapadāv ubhau ||
pakṣayoḥ śuklapakṣād dhi bahulaḥ śrāddhapūjitaḥ |
uktā tithiparīkṣeyaṃ nakṣatreṣu pracakṣate ||
uttarāḥ śravaṇas tiṣyo mṛgaśīrṣaṃ prajāpatiḥ |
hastaḥ śatabhiṣak svātiś citrā pitryam athāśvinam ||
nakṣatrāṇi praśastāni sadaitāni tu paitṛke |
aparāṇi ca nakṣatrāṇy ucyante kāraṇaiḥ kvacit ||
yasmin guṇoditaṃ vipraṃ pātrabhūtam avāpnuyāt |
śrāddhasya pūjitaḥ kālaḥ bhavet sa eva vā punaḥ ||
'uttarāḥ ' tisraḥ | 'prajāpatiḥ' prajāpatidaivatyā rohiṇī | 'pitryaṃ’ maghā | 'āśvinaṃ' aśvinī | 'aparāṇi' dhaniṣṭhādīni | 'kāraṇaiḥ' māghapañcadaśīyogādibhiḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
tīrthe dravyopapattau tu na kālam avadhārayet |
pitṝṇām atireko ’yaṃ māsi kāryo dhruvaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
[19]        pātraṃ vā brāhmaṇaṃ prāpya sadyaḥ śrāddhaṃ vidhīyate |
aṣṭakā ye cābhyudayās tīrthayātropapattayaḥ ||
‘na kālam avadhārayet' amāvāsyāparāhṇādikaṃ na pratīkṣetety arthaḥ | 'atirekaḥ' adhikaḥ aṣṭakādir api, 'dhruvaḥ ' āvaśyaka ity arthaḥ | ‘aṣṭakā’ ityādi, māsi kāryā amāvāsyāśrāddhādayaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kaḥ śrāddhakālo deśo dravyaṃ dvijātaya iti ||
'deśo' vārāṇasī | dravyaṃ' khaḍgamāṃsādi | 'dvijātiḥ' paṅktipāvanādiḥ |
śātātapaḥ |
navodake navānne ca gṛhapracchādane tathā |
pitaras tv īhayanty annam aṣṭakāsu maghāsu ca |
tasmād dadyāt sadā yukto vidvatsu brāhmaṇeṣu ca ||
'navodake' varṣādau | 'brāhmaṇeṣu' prāpteṣv iti śeṣaḥ |
manuḥ | [3, 273 - 274]
yatkicin madhunā miśraṃ pradadyāt tu trayodaśīm |
tad apy akṣayam eva syād varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
api naḥ sa kule bhūyād yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ prākchāye kuñjarasya tu ||
'prākchāye kuñjarasya' prācyāṃ diśi gatāyāṃ hasticchāyāyām ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 39 - 40 ] |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
upāsate sutaṃ jātaṃ śakuntā iva pippalam ||
[20]        madhumāṃsaiś ca śālaiś ca payasā pāyasena ca |
eṣa no dāsyati śrāddhaṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
śaṅkhalikhita-yamāḥ |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
jātaṃ putraṃ praśaṃsanti pippalaṃ śakunā iva ||
madhumāṃsena khaḍgena payasā pāyasena vā |
eṣa dāsyati nas tṛptiṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
viṣṇuḥ [ 78, 52 - 73] |
[api] pitṛgīte gāthe bhavataḥ |
api jāyeta so’ smākaṃ kule kaścin narottamaḥ |
prāvṛṭkāle ’site pakṣe trayodaśyāṃ samāhitaḥ ||
madhūtkaṭena yaḥ śrāddhaṃ pāyasena samācaret |
kārtikaṃ sakalaṃ vāpi prākchāye kuñjarasya vā ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
chāgena sarvalohena varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca |
putro vā yadi vā pautro yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm ||
iti pitaraḥ samudīkṣante || 'sarvalohena' sarvato lohitavarṇena chāgenety arthaḥ ||
tathā haviṣyaprastāve devalaḥ |
chāgo vā sarvalohitaḥ | iti ||
yājñvalkyaḥ [1, 261] |
yad dadāti gayāsthaś ca sarvam ānantyam ucyate |
tathā varṣātrayodaśyāṃ maghāsu ca viśeṣataḥ ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
pauṣṭhapadyāṃ atītāyāṃ maghāyuktāṃ trayodaśīm |
prāpya śrāddhaṃ hi kartavyaṃ madhunā pāyasena ca ||
[21] brahmapurāṇe |
āśvayujyāṃ ca kṛṣṇāyāṃ trayodaśyāṃ maghāsu ca |
prāvṛḍṛatau yamaḥ pretān pitṝṃś cātha yamālayāt ||
visarjayati mānuṣye kṛtvā śūnyaṃ svakaṃ puram |
kṣudhārtāḥ kīrtayantaś ca duṣkṛtaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtam ||
kāṅkṣantaḥ putrapautrebhyaḥ pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyutam |
tasmāt tāṃ tatra vidhinā tarpayet pāyasena tu ||
madhvājyatilamiśreṇa tathā śītena cāmbhasā |
grāsamātraṃ paragṛhād vyaktaṃ yaḥ prāpnuyān naraḥ ||
bhikṣāmātreṇa yaḥ prāṇān saṃdhārayati vā svayam |
yo vā saṃvardhayed dehaṃ pratyahaṃ svātmavikrayāt ||
śrāddhaṃ tenāpi kartavyaṃ tais tair dravyaiḥ susaṃcitaiḥ |
trayodaśyāṃ prayatnena varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
nāsmāt parataraḥ kālaḥ śrāddheṣv anyatra vidyate |
yatra sākṣāt tu pitaro gṛhṇanty amṛtam akṣayam ||
atra ca trayodaśīviṣayān ekavākyaparyālocanayā proṣṭhapadyūrdhvaṃ kṛṣṇatrayodaśyāṃ putriṇāputriṇā vā piṇḍasahitaśrāddham evāvaśyaṃ kartavyam iti gamyate | maghāyuktāyāṃ tu tasyām eva jyeṣṭhaputravatā piṇḍarahitaṃ śrāddhaṃ kartavyam | kanyāgatāparapakṣādhikāre—
tatrāpi mahatī pūjā kartavyā pitṛdaivate |
ṛkṣe piṇḍapradānaṃ tu jyeṣṭhaputro vivarjayet ||
iti devīpurāṇavacanāt | yat tu 'yuvamāriṇaś ca bhavanti' ityādinindāśravaṇaṃ tad vakṣyamāṇatrayodaśyupaviddhakāmyaśrāddhaprayogaviṣayam ||
[22] pārvaṇaśrāddhavidhyanantaraṃ manuḥ [6, 289 - 282] |
anena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ trirabdasyeha nirvapet |
hemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu pāñcayajñikam anvaham ||
na paitṛkayajñiko homo laukike ’gnau vidhīyate |
na darśena vinā śrāddham āhitāgner dvijanmanaḥ ||
'trirabdasya' saṃvatsare vāratrayam | ayaṃ ca sāgniniragnisādhāraṇaḥ | saṃvatsaramadhye yad avaśyakartavyatvenoktaṃ pārvaṇaśrāddhaṃ kutaścid dravyādyasaṃbhavāder nimittād asaṃpattāv anukalpaḥ | pāñcayajñikaṃ tu pañcayajñāntargataśrāddhaṃ yat tat pratidinam udakādināpi kartavyam | ata eva pārvaṇaśrāddham abhidhāya devalenāpi —
etena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ kuryāt saṃvatsaraṃ kvacit |
dviś catur vā yathāśrāddhaṃ māse māse dine dine ||
iti śaktisaṃbhavāsaṃbhavatāratamyena bahavaḥ kalpāḥ pradarśitāḥ | hemantagrīṣmavarṣāsu trir anuṣṭhīyamānaṃ śrāddhaṃ kanyākumbhavṛṣasthe ’rke yasyāṃ kasyāṃ ca tithau kartavyam | āhitāgnināpi tu tatrāpi darśa eva | yasmāt śrāddhāṅgapaitṛyajñikaśabdopāttāgnau karaṇahomāṅgabhūto dakṣiṇāgnir na darśavyatirekeṇānyad asti | nimittāntarāt ta [tu] yad aṣṭakādiśrāddhaṃ tad yataḥ kutaścid vipāṇyādi niṣpādyāgnau karaṇāṅgam eva kāryam ||
devalaḥ |
mānavaḥ śrāddhakalpo’yaṃ manunā samudāhṛtaḥ |
bahupiṇḍāṣṭakāsu syād ekapiṇḍā tu nāṣṭakā ||
etena vidhinā śrāddhaṃ kuryāt saṃvatsaraṃ sakṛt |
dviś catur vā yathāśrāddhaṃ māse māse dine dine ||
yamaḥ |
rāhudarśanadattaṃ hi śrāddham ānantyam ucyate ||
[23] viṣṇuḥ [77, 8- 9] |
sandhyārātryor na kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ khalu vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
tayor api ca kartavyaṃ yadi syād rāhudarśanam ||
rāhudarśanadattaṃ hi śrāddham ācandratārakam |
guṇavatsarvakāmo ’yaṃ pitṝṇām upatiṣṭhate ||
śātātapaḥ |
rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta rāhor anyatra darśanāt |
sandhyayor ubhayoś caiva na kurvīta kadācana ||
āpastambaḥ [1,16,23-24] |
na ca naktaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvītānyatra rāhudarśanāt ||
manuḥ [3,280 ] |
rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta rākṣasī kīrtitā hi sā |
sandhyayor ubhayoś caiva sūrye caivācirodite ||
[24]
|| 4 ||
|| atha kāmyaśrāddhakālāḥ ||
tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
atha kāmyāni bhavanti | striyaḥ pratipadi | dvitīyāyāṃ strījanma | aśvās tṛtīyāyām | caturthyāṃ kṣudrapaśavaḥ | putrāḥ pañcamyām | ṣaṣṭhyāṃ dyūtaṛddhiḥ | kṛṣiḥ saptamyām | aṣṭamyāṃ vāṇijyam | ekaśaphaṃ navamyām | paricārikā ekādaśyām | dvādaśyāṃ dhanadhānyam | kupyaṃ jñātiśraiṣṭhayaṃ trayodaśyām | yuvānas tasmin mriyante śastrahatasya caturdaśyām | amāvāsyāyāṃ sarvam ||
manuḥ |
kurvan pratipadi śrāddhaṃ surūpāṃl labhate sutān |
kanyakās tu dvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ tu vandinaḥ ||
paśūn kṣudrāṃś caturthyāṃ tu pañcamyāṃ śobhanān sutān |
ṣaṣṭayāṃ dyūtaṃ kṛṣiṃ cāpi saptamyāṃ labhate naraḥ ||
aṣṭamyām api vāṇijyaṃ labhate śrāddhadaḥ sadā |
syān navamyām ekakhuraṃ daśamyāṃ dvikhuraṃ bahu ||
ekādaśyāṃ tathā ṛddhiṃ brahmavarcasvinaḥ sutān |
dvādaśyāṃ jātarūpaṃ ca rajataṃ kupyam eva ca ||
[25]        jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ tu suprajāḥ |
prīyante pitaraś cāsya ye śastreṇa hatā raṇe ||
pakṣatyādiṣu nirdiṣṭān vipulān manasaḥ priyān |
śrāddhadaḥ pañcadaśyāṃ tu sarvān kāmān samaśnute ||
'vandinaḥ' stāvakān | sadyo bhavatīti yāvat | 'dyūtaṃ' dyūtajayam | 'kupyaṃ' suvarṇarajatātiriktaṃ tāmrādidhanam | 'pakṣatyādi' iti, pakṣatiḥ pratipat, pratipadāditithiphalaṃ śobhanam amāvāsyāyām ity arthaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2,16, 6-21]
sarveśv evāparapakṣasyāhaḥsu kriyamāṇe pitṝn prīṇāti | kartus tu kālābhiniyamāt phalaviśeṣaḥ | prathame ’hani kriyamāṇe strīprāyam apatye jāyate | dvitīye stenāḥ | tṛtīye brahmavarcasvinaḥ | caturthe kṣudrapaśumān | pañcame pumāṃso bahvapatyo na cānapatyaḥ pramīyate | ṣaṣṭhe ’dhvaśīlo ’kṣaśīlaḥ | saptame karṣe rāddhiḥ | aṣṭame puṣṭiḥ | navama ekakhurāḥ | daśame vyavahāre rāddhiḥ | ekādaśe kṛṣṇāyasatrapusīsam | dvādaśe paśumān | trayodaśe bahuputro bahumitro darśanīyāpatyo yuvamāriṇaḥ [bhavanti] | caturdaśa āyudhe rāddhiḥ | pañcadaśe puṣṭiḥ ||
'satataṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvann āpnoti” ityadhikāre — [26]
viṣṇuḥ [78,36–50] |
gṛheṣv abhirūpāḥ striyaḥ pratipadi | kanyāṃ varadāṃ dvitīyāyām | sarvān kāmāṃs tṛtīyāyām | yaśaś caturthyām | [surūpān sutān ] pañcamyām | dyūtavijayaṃ ṣaṣṭhyām | kṛṣiṃ saptamyām | vāṇijyam aṣṭamyām | paśūn navamyām | vājino daśamyām | putrān brahmavarcasvina ekādaśyām | kanakarajate dvādaśyām | saubhāgyaṃ trayodaśyām | sarvān kāmān pañcadaśyām | śastrahatānāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi caturdaśī śastā ||
‘śrāddhaṃ kuryād' ityadhikāre —
hārītaḥ |
pañcamīṃ putrakāmaḥ | ṣaṣṭhīṃ dhanakāmaḥ | saptamīṃ paśukāmaḥ | aṣṭamīṃ śastrahatānām ārogyakāmaḥ | navamīṃ senām abhijetukāmaḥ | daśamīm annakāmaḥ | ekādaśīṃ vṛddhikāmaḥ | dvādaśīṃ śrīkāmaḥ | trayodaśīṃ yaśaskāmaḥ | caturdaśīṃ bhūtikāmaḥ svastikāmo vā | amāvāsyāṃ sarvakāmaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pañcamyāṃ putrakāmo yajeta | ṣaṣṭhyāṃ paśukāmaḥ | saptamyāṃ vṛddhikāmaḥ | aṣṭamyām annādyakāmaḥ | navamyāṃ brahmavarcasakāmaḥ | daśamyāṃ sarvakāmaḥ | ekādaśyāṃ dhanakāmaḥ | dvādaśyāṃ kṣetrakāmaḥ | trayodaśyāṃ strīvaśitvam | caturdaśyāṃ śastreṇa hatānām | sarvakāmo ’māvāsyāyām ||
[27] brahmapuṇe |
śrāddhaṃ tu paurṇamāsyāṃ ca kṛtvā pūrṇaphalaṃ labhet |
pratipady arthalābhāya dvitīyārthāya cāparā ||
tṛtīyāyāṃ varārthāya śatrunāśaya [-śāya?] cāpare |
pañcamyāṃ dhanalābhāya śrāddhaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tu bālarakṣārthaṃ saptamyāṃ bandhuvṛddhayoḥ |
vṛddhikāmas tathāṣṭamyāṃ strīkāmo navame ’hani ||
daśamyāṃ brahmatejo’rthī saṃtatyarthī tathāpare |
dvādaśyāṃ jayalābhāya trayodaśyāṃ vibhūtaye ||
prāyānaśanaśastrāgniviṣodbandhakināṃ tathā |
caturdaśyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ tṛptyartham iti niścayaḥ ||
amāvāsyāṃ tu svargāya bhaktyā saṃtarpayet pitṝn ||
atra ca yat tithiphalanānātvaṃ tad adhikāribhedena boddhavyam | yatra ca stainyayuvamāritvādir apatyadoṣaḥ śrūyate, tatrāpy anapatyatayā taddveṣasahitāpatyamātrārthino ’dhikāraḥ ||
manuḥ [3, 277] |
yukṣu kurvan dinakṣeṣu sarvān kāmān samaśnute |
ayukṣu tu pitṛn arcan prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām ||
yamaḥ |
yukṣu kurvan dinarkṣeṣu sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
gajacchāyāṃ pitṝn arcan prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām ||
[28] yājñavalyaḥ [9, 265-268] |
svargaṃ hy apatyam ojaś ca śaurya kṣetraṃ balaṃ tathā |
putraṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ sasaubhāgyaṃ samṛddhiṃ mukhyatāṃ śubhān ||
pravṛttacakratāṃ caiva vāṇijyaprabhṛtīṃs tathā |
arogitvaṃ yaśo vītaśokatāṃ paramāṃ gatim ||
dhanaṃ vidyāṃ bhiṣaksiddhiṃ kupyaṃ gā apy ajāvikm |
aśvān āyuś ca vidhivad yaḥ śrāddhaṃ saṃpradāsyati ||
kṛttikādibharaṇyantaṃ sa kāmān āpnuyād imān |
āstikaḥ śraddadhānaś ca vyapetamadamatsaraḥ ||
'pravṛttacakratā' sarvadāpratihatājñāśālitā |
viṣṇuḥ [ 78, 1-37] |
satatam ādityadine śrāddhaṃ kurvann ārogyam āpnoti | saubhāgyaṃ cāndre | samaravijayaṃ kauje | sarvān kāmān baudhe | vidyām abhīṣṭāṃ jaive | dhanaṃ śaukre | jīvitaṃ śanaiśvare | svargaṃ kṛttikāsu | apatyaṃ rohiṇīṣu | brahmavarcasaṃ saumye | 1 karmaṇāṃ siddhiṃ raudre | bhuvaṃ punarvasau | puṣṭiṃ puṣye | śriyaṃ sārpe | sarvān kāmān paitrye | saubhāgyaṃ bhāgye | dhanam āryamṇe | jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ haste | surūpavataḥ srutāṃs tvāṣṭre | vāṇijyasiddhiṃ svātau | kanakaṃ viśākhāsu | mitrāṇi maitre | rājyaṃ śākre | kṛṣiṃ mūle | samudrayānaśiddhim āpye | sarvān kāmān vaiśvadeve | śraiṣṭhayam abhijiti | sarvān śravaṇe | lavaṇaṃ vāsave | ārogyaṃ vāruṇe | kupyadravyam āje | gṛham āhirbudhnye | gāḥ pauṣṇe | turagān āśvine | jīvitaṃ yāmye |
[29] brahmapurāṇe |
aśvinyāṃ prāpnuyād aśvān bharaṇyām āyur uttamam |
kṛttikāyāṃ vijvaratvaṃ śrāddhakṛt samavāpnuyāt ||
prājāpatye prajāṃ puṣṭiṃ saume caujaḥ suśobhanam |
ārdrāyāṃ krūrasiddhiṃ tu putrān ceṣṭān punarvasau ||
tiṣye sutān vīradharmān sārpe ca labhate dhanam |
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ maghāsv eva bhāgye saubhāgyam uttamam ||
dānaśaktim athāryamṇe haste śraiṣṭhyaṃ maharddhimat |
tvāṣṭre duhitaraṃ puṇyaṃ svātau vāṇijyam uttamam ||
viśākhāyāṃ śubhān putrān maitre cātyāhatāṃ gatim |
ādhipatyaṃ tu jyeṣṭhāyāṃ mūle cārogyam uttamam ||
āṣāḍhāsu yaśolābham uttarāsu viśokatām |
śravaṇena gatiṃ puṇyāṃ dhaniṣṭhāsu dhanaṃ bahu ||
vāruṇena kṛṣer vṛddhiṃ viśākhe cāvikaṃ bahu |
bahvīr gāś cottarāyāṃ ca pauṣṇe kupyaṃ mahānidhim ||
iti saṃcintya yogena vidhinā sādhayec ca yat |
sa tasmin divase śrāddhe bhaktyā saṃtarpayet pitṝn ||

|| atha śrāddhadeśāḥ ||
tatra viṣṇuḥ [85, 1-67] |
atha puṣkareṣv akṣayaṃ śrāddham | japahomatapāṃsi ca | puṣkare snātamātraḥ sarvapāpebhyaḥ pūto bhavati | evam eva gayāśīrṣe | vaṭe | amarakaṇṭake | varāhaparvate | yatra kvacana narmadātīre | yamunātīre | gaṅgāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ | kuśāvarte | [binduke] bilvake | nīlaparvate | kanakhale | kubjāmre | bhṛgutuṅge | kedāre | mahālaye | naḍantikāyām | sugandhāyām | śākaṃbharyām | phalgutīrthe | mahāgaṅgāyām | taṇḍulikāśrame [ trihalikāśrame ] | kumāradhārāyām | prabhāse | yatra kvacana sarasvatyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
[31]                gaṅgātīre prayāge ca sarasvatyāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
satataṃ naimiṣāraṇye vārāṇasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
agastyāśrame | kaṇvāśrame | kauśikyām | sarayūtīre | śoṇasya jyotiṣāyāś ca saṃgame | śrīparvate | kālodake | uttaramānase | [ vaḍavāyām| ] mataṅgavāpyām | saptārṣe | viṣṇupade | svargamārgapade | godāvaryām | [ gomatyām ||] vetravatyām | [vipāśāyām | vitastāyām ||] śatadrutīre | candrabhāgāyām | īrāvatyām | sindhos tīre | dakṣiṇe pañcanade | ausaje | evamādiṣv anyeṣu tīrtheṣu | saridvarāsu | sarveṣv api saṃgameṣu | prabhaveṣu | pulineṣu | prasravaṇeṣu | parvateṣu | nikuñjeṣu | vaneṣu | upavaneṣu | gomayenopaliptagṛheṣu | manojñeṣu | atra ca pitṛgītā gāthā bhavanti |
kule ’smākaṃ sa jantuḥ syād yo no dadyāj jalāñjalīn |
nadīṣu bahutoyāsu śītalāsu viśeṣataḥ ||
api jāyeta so ’smākaṃ kule kaścin narottamaḥ |
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe śrāddhaṃ yo naḥ kuryāt samāhitaḥ ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ’pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet ||
'prabhaveṣu' nadīnām utpattideśeṣu, 'saridvarāsu, saṃgameṣu' iti prastutya 'prabhaveṣu' ityabhidhānāt | 'pulinaṃ' nadītoyotthitaḥ pradeśaḥ | 'prasravaṇaṃ' nirjharaḥ | 'nikuñjo' latādiveṣṭitapradeśaḥ | 'upavanaṃ' gṛhavāṭikā | 'manojñaṃ’ ramaṇīyam |
śaṅkhaḥ |
yad dadāti gayāsthaś ca prabhāse puṣkareṣu ca |
prayāge naimiṣāraṇye sarvam ānantyam ucyate ||
[32]        gaṅgāyamunayos tīre payoṣṇyamarakaṇṭake |
narmadābāhudātīre sarvam ānantyam ucyate ||
kurūṇāṃ ca kurukṣetre bhṛgutuṅge himālaye |
saptarṣe vṛṣṇikūpe ca dattam akṣayam ucyate ||
devalaḥ |
śrāddhasya pūjito deśo gayā gaṅgā sarasvatī |
kurukṣetraṃ prayāgaś ca naimiṣaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca ||
nadītaṭeṣu tīrtheṣu śaileṣu pulineṣu ca |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti datteneha pitāmahāḥ ||
'viviktaṃ ' vijanam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [ śrāddha, 66-67, 51] |
kāṅkṣanti pitaraḥ putrān narakāpātabhīravaḥ |
gayāṃ yāsyati yaḥ kaścit so ’smān santārayiṣyati ||
kariṣyati vṛṣotsargam iṣṭāpūrtaṃ tathaiva ca |
pālayiṣyati vārdhakye śrāddhaṃ dāsyati cānvaham ||
gayāyāṃ dharmapṛṣṭhe ca sarasi brahmaṇas tathā |
gayāśīrṣe ’kṣayavaṭe pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
brahmāraṇyaṃ dharmapṛṣṭhaṃ dhenukāraṇyam eva ca |
dṛṣṭvaitāni pitṝṃś cārcya vaṃśān viṃśatim uddharet ||
[33] vāyupurāṇe [ 77, 96 99, 101-106, 108-109] |
gayāyāṃ dharmapṛṣṭhe ca sarasi brahmaṇas tathā |
gayāgṛdhravaṭe caiva śrāddhaṃ dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
bharatasyāśrame puṇye nityaṃ puṇyatamair vṛte |
mataṅgasya padaṃ tatra dṛśyate sarvamānuṣaiḥ ||
khyāpitaṃ dharmasarvasvaṃ lokasyāsya nidarśanam |
evaṃ pañcavanaṃ puṇyaṃ puṇyakṛdbhir niṣevitam ||
yasmin pāṇḍuviśāleti tīrthaṃ sadyo nidarśanam |
tṛtīyāyāṃ tathā pāde niścīvāyāś ca maṇḍale ||
mahāhrade ca kauśikyāṃ dattaṃ śrāddhaṃ mahāphalam |
muṇḍapṛṣṭhe padaṃ nyastaṃ mahādevena dhīmatā ||
bahūn varṣagaṇāṃs taptvā tapas tīrtheṣu duścaram |
alpenāpy atra kālena naro dharmaparāyaṇaḥ ||
pāpmānam utsṛjatyāśu jīrṇāṃ tvacam ivoragaḥ |
nāmnā kanakanandeti tīrthaṃ jagati viśrutam |
udīcyāṃ muṇḍapṛṣṭhasya brahmarṣigaṇasevitam ||
tatra snātvā divaṃ yāti svaśarīreṇa mānavaḥ |
dattaṃ cāpi sadā śrāddham akṣayaṃ samudāhṛtam ||
snātvā ṛṇatrayaṃ tatra nikṣiṇoti narottamaḥ |
mānase sarasi snātvā śrāddhaṃ nirvartayet tataḥ ||
uttaraṃ mānasaṃ gatvā siddhiṃ prāpnoty anuttamām |
[34]        tasmin nirvartayec chrāddhaṃ yathāśakti yathābalam ||
kāmān sa labhate divyān mokṣopāyāṃś ca kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
matsyapurāṇe [22, 10-12] |
nandātha lalitā tadvat tīrthaṃ māyāpurīṣu ca |
tathā citrapadaṃ nāma tataḥ kedāram uttamam ||
gaṅgāsāgaram ity āhuḥ sarvatīrtham ayaṃ śubham |
tīrthaṃ brahmasaras tadvac chatadrusalilodgamaḥ ||
tathā [22, 16-18] |
kṛtaśaucaṃ mahāpuṇyaṃ sarvapāpaniṣūdanam |
yatrāste nārasiṃhas tu svayam eva janārdanaḥ ||
tīrtham ikṣumatī nāma pitṝṇāṃ durlabhaṃ sadā |
saṃgame yatra tiṣṭhanti gaṅgāyāḥ pitaraḥ sadā ||
tathā [22, 20] |
yamunā devikā kālī candrabhāgā dṛṣadvatī |
nadī dhenumatī puṇyā parā vetravatī tathā ||
tathā [22, 22-26] |
nīlakuṇḍam iti khyātaṃ pitṛtīrtha dvijottamāḥ |
tathā bhadrasaraḥ puṇyaṃ saro mānasam eva ca ||
mandākinī tathācchodā vipāśā ca sarasvatī |
tīrthaṃ mitrapadaṃ tadvad vaidyanāthaṃ mahāphalam ||
[35]        priyānandī mahākālas tathā kālañjaraṃ śubham |
vaṃśodbhedaṃ harodbhedaṃ gaṅgodbhedaṃ mahālayam ||
bhadreśvaraṃ viṣṇupadaṃ narmadādvāram eva ca ||
gayāpiṇḍapradānena samāny āhur maharṣayaḥ ||
[tathā] [22, 27–29] |
oṃkāraṃ pitṛtīrthaṃ ca kāverī kapilodakam |
saṃbhedaś caṇḍavegāyās tathaivāmarakaṇṭakam ||
kurukṣetrāc chataguṇaṃ tasmin snānādikaṃ bhavet |
śukratīrthaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ tīrthaṃ someśvaraṃ param ||
sarvavyādhiharaṃ puṇyaṃ phalaṃ koṭiśatādhikam |
tathā [22, 30- 76] |
kāyāvarohaṇaṃ nāma tathā carmaṇvatī nadī |
gomatī varaṇā tadvat tīrtham auśanasaṃ bhavam ||
bhairavaṃ bhṛgutuṅgaṃ ca gaurītīrtham anuttamam |
tīrthaṃ vaināyakaṃ nāma vatseśvaram ataḥ param ||
paraṃ tathā pāpaharaṃ puṇyā ca tapatī nadī |
mūlatāpī payoṣṇī ca payoṣṇīsaṃgamas tathā ||
mahābodhiḥ pāṭalā ca nāgatīrtham avantikā |
tathā veṇānadī puṇyā mahāśālas tathaiva ca ||
mahārudraṃ mahāliṅgaṃ daśārṇā ca nadī śubhā |
śatarudrā śatāhvā ca tathā viṣṇupadaṃ param ||
[36]        aṅgāravāhikā tadvan nadau dvau śoṇaghargharau |
kālikā ca nadī puṇyā vitastā ca nadī śubhā ||
etāni pitṛtīrthāni śasyante snānadānayoḥ |
śrāddham eteṣu yad dattaṃ tad anantaphalaṃ smṛtam ||
droṇī vāṭanadī dhārāsaraḥ kṣīranadī tathā |
dvārakā kṛṣṇatīrthaṃ ca tathārbudasarasvatī ||
nadī maṇimatī nāma tathā ca girikanyakā |
dhūtapāpaṃ tathā tīrthaṃ samudro dakṣiṇas tathā ||
gokarṇo gajakarṇaś ca tathā ca puruṣottamaḥ |
eteṣu pitṛtīrtheṣu śrāddham ānantyam ucyate ||
tīrthaṃ meghakaraṃ nāma svayam eva janārdanaḥ |
yatra śārṅgadharo viṣṇur mekhalāyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ ||
tathā mandodarītīrthe yatra campā nadī śubhā |
tathā sāmalanāthaś ca mahāśālavatī tathā ||
payoṣṇīdakṣiṇe tīre devadeveśvaraṃ tathā |
koṭīśvaraṃ tathā deva reṇukāyāḥ samīpataḥ ||
tatra koṭaṃ ca sākoṭaṃ tathā jalpeśvaraṃ mahat |
arjunaṃ tripureśaṃ ca siddheśvaram ataḥ param ||
śaileśaṃ śrīkaraṃ tīrthaṃ nārasiṃham ataḥ param |
mahendraṃ ca mahāpuṇyaṃ tathā śrīraṅgasaṃjñitam ||
[37]        eteṣv api sadā śrāddham anantaphalam aśnute |
darśanād api caitāni sarvapāpaharāṇi vai ||
tuṅgabhadrā nadī puṇyā tathā bhīmarathī sarit |
bhīmeśvaraṃ kṛṣṇaveṇā kāverī vañjulā nadī ||
nadī godāvarī nāma trisandhyā tīrtham uttamam |
tryambakaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ tu sarvatīrthanamaskṛtam ||
yatrāste bhagavān bhīmaḥ svayam eva janārdanaḥ ||
śrāddham eteṣu sarveṣu dattaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet |
smaraṇād api pāpāni vrajanti śatadhā dvijāḥ ||
śrīparṇī ca nadī puṇyā vyāsatīrtham anuttamam |
tathā matsyanadī nāma śivadhārā tathaiva ca |
bhavatīrthaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ pampātīrthaṃ tathaiva ca ||
puṇyaṃ rāmeśvaraṃ tadvad elāpuram alaṃ tathā |
aṅgārabhūtaṃ vikhyātam ānartakam alambusam |
āmrātakeśvaraṃ caiva tadvad ekāmrakaṃ param |
govardhanaṃ hariścandraṃ puraścandraṃ pṛthūdakam ||
sahasrākṣaṃ hiraṇyākṣaṃ tathā ca kadalī nadī |
rāmādhivāsas tatrāpi tathā saumitrisaṃjñakam ||
indranīlaṃ mahānādaṃ tathā ca priyamelakam |
etāny api sadā śrāddhe praśastāny adhikāny api ||
[38]        eteṣu sarvadevānāṃ sānnidhyaṃ padyate yataḥ |
dānam eteṣu sarveṣu bhavet koṭiśatādhikam ||
bāhudā ca nadī puṇyā tathā siddhavanaṃ śubham |
tīrthaṃ pāśupataṃ nāma vaṭī parpaṭikā tathā ||
śrāddham eteṣu dattaṃ ca koṭikoṭiśatottaram |
tathaiva pitṛtīrthaṃ ca yatra godāvarī nadī ||
yutā liṅgasahasreṇa savyetarajalāvahā |
jāmadagnyasya tat tīrthaṃ rāmāyatanam uttamam ||
pratīkasya bhayād bhinnā yatra godāvarī nadī |
tīrthaṃ taddhavyakavyānām apsaro’yutasaṃyutam ||
śraddhāgnikāryaṃ dānaṃ ca tatra koṭiśatādhikam |
tathā sahasraliṅgaṃ ca rāghaveśvaram uttamam ||
sendraphenā nadī puṇyā yatrendraḥ kheditaḥ purā |
nihatya namuciṃ śakras tapasā svargam āptavān ||
tatra dattaṃ naraiḥ śrāddham anantaphaladaṃ bhavet |
tīrthaṃ tu puṣkaraṃ nāma sālagrāmaṃ tathaiva ca ||
śoṇapātaś ca vikhyāto yatra vaiśvānarālayam |
tīrtha sārasvataṃ nāma svāmitīrthaṃ tathaiva ca ||
mallandarā nadī puṇyā kauśikī candrikā tathā |
vaidarbhā cātha vegā ca payoṣṇī prāṅmukhā parā ||
kāverī cottarā puṇyā tathā jālandharo giriḥ |
eteṣu śrāddhatīrtheṣu śrāddham ānantyam aśnute ||
lohadaṇḍaṃ tathā tīrthaṃ citrakūṭas tathaiva ca |
vindhyayogaś ca gaṅgāyās tathā nandātaṭaṃ śubham ||
kubjāmraṃ ca tathā tīrtham ūrvaśīpulinaṃ tathā |
saṃsāramocanaṃ tīrthaṃ tatraiva ṛṇamocanam ||
eteṣu pitṛtīrtheṣu śrāddham ānantyam aśnute |
aṭṭahāsaṃ mahātīrthaṃ gautameśvaram eva ca ||
tathā vasiṣṭhatīrthaṃ ca hārītaṃ tu tataḥ param |
brahmāvartaṃ kuśāvartaṃ hayatīrthaṃ tathaiva ca ||
piṇḍārakaṃ ca vikhyātaṃ śaṅkoddhāraṃ tathaiva ca |
ghaṇṭeśvaraṃ ca bilvaṃ ca nīlaparvatam eva ca ||
tathā ca badarītīrthaṃ rāmatīrthaṃ tathaiva ca |
jayantaṃ vijayaṃ caiva śukratīrthaṃ tathaiva ca ||
śrīpateś ca tathā tīrthaṃ tīrthaṃ raivatakaṃ tathā |
vaikuṇṭhatīrthaṃ ca paraṃ bhīmeśvaram athāpi vā |
aśvatīrthaṃ ca vikhyātam anantaṃ śrāddhadānayoḥ ||
tīrthaṃ vedaśiro nāma tathaivaughavatī nadī |
tīrthaṃ vasupradaṃ nāma chāgaliṅgaṃ tathaiva ca ||
eteṣu śrāddhadātāraḥ prayānti paramaṃ padam |
tīrthaṃ mātṛgṛhaṃ nāma karavīrapuraṃ tathā ||
saptagodāvarītīrthaṃ sarvatīrtheṣv anuttamaḥ |
tatra śrāddhaṃ pradātavyam anantaphalam īpsubhiḥ ||
[40] tathā [22, 81] |
eṣu tīrtheṣu yac chrāddhaṃ tat koṭiguṇam iṣyate |
tasmāt tatra prayatnena tīrthe śrāddhaṃ samācaret ||
tathā |
kuryāc chrāddham athaiteṣu nityam eva yathāvidhi |
prāgdakṣiṇadiśe gatvā sarvakāmacikīrṣayā ||
atra śrāddhadeśatvena kathitanānādeśīyatīrthasvarūpaviśeṣās tattaddeśanivāsibhyo ’dhigantavyāḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe [ 78, 21-23] |
triśaṅkor varjayed deśaṃ sarve dvādaśayojanam |
uttareṇa mahānadyā dakṣiṇena tu kīkaṭāt ||
deśas traiśaṅkavo nāma śrāddhakarmaṇi varjitaḥ |
kāraskarāḥ kaliṅgāś ca sindhor uttaram eva ca ||
praṇaṣṭāśramadharmāś ca deśā varjyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ |
mlecchadeśe tathā rātrau sandhyāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
na śrāddham ācaret prājño mlecchadeśaṃ na ca vrajet ||

|| 6 ||
|| atha havīṃṣi ||
atra havirviśeṣāt prītiviśeṣaḥ | tatra manuḥ [3, 266-272] |
yad dhaviś cirakālāya yac cānantyāya kalpate |
pitṛbhyo vidhivad dattaṃ tat pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ prīyante vidhivat pitaro nṛṇām ||
dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākuneneha pañca vai ||
ṣaṇmāsāṃś chāgamāṃsena pārṣatenātha sapta tu |
aṣṭāv eṇasya māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu ||
daśamāsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ |
śaśakūrmayos tu māṃsena māsān ekādaśaiva tu ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena vā |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkāḥ khaḍgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ ||
‘aurabhraṃ' meṣamāṃsam | 'śākunaṃ' pakṣimāṃsam | 'pṛṣataḥ' citramṛgaḥ | 'eṇaḥ' kṛṣṇasāraḥ | 'ruruḥ' śambaraḥ | [42] 'gavyam' iti payoviśeṣaṇam, ‘payasā gavyena saṃvatsaraḥ' iti uśanovacanāt | vārdhrīṇasasya svarūpaṃ nigame darśitam |
tripibaṃ tv indriyakṣīṇaṃ śvetaṃ vṛddham ajāpatim |
vārdhrīṇasaṃ tu taṃ prāhur yājñikāḥ pitṛkarmaṇi ||
kṛṣṇagrīvo raktaśirāḥ śvetapakṣo vihaṅgamaḥ |
sarve vārdhrīṇasaṃ proktam ity eṣā naigamī śrutiḥ ||
'mahāśalkāḥ' matsyaviśeṣāḥ, 'mahāśalkino matsyāḥ' iti yamavacanāt | 'munyannāni' nīvārādīni || yamaḥ |
śalyakaś caturo māsān ruruḥ prīṇāti paśca vai |
śaśaḥ prīṇāti ṣaṇmāsān kūrmaḥ prīṇāti sapta tu ||
aṣṭau māsān varāhas tu meṣaḥ prīṇāti vai nava |
māhiṣaṃ daśa māsāṃs tu gāvayaṃ rudrasammitān ||
tathā |
ānantyāya bhaved dattaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ pitṛkṣaye |
pitṛkṣayo gayā jñeyā tatra dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
tathā |
yat kiṃcin madhunā yuktaṃ tad ānantyāya kalpate |
upākṛtaṃ tu vidhinā mantreṇānnaṃ tathākṛtam ||
‘gāvayaṃ ' gavayamāṃsam | 'rudrasammitān' ekādaśasaṃkhyān | 'upākṛtaṃ' mantravaddarbhasaṃskṛtam | 'annaṃ tathākṛtaṃ ' upākṛtam eva ||
[43] devalaḥ |
tuṣyanti matsyair dvau māsau trīn māsān rurubhir mṛgaiḥ |
śākunaiś caturo māsān pañca tṛpyanti pārṣataiḥ ||
śaśaiḥ ṣāṇmāsikī tṛptiḥ kaurmaiḥ syāt sāptamāsikī |
aṣṭau māsān varāheṇa nava tṛpyanty aje rurau |
daśa māhiṣamāṃsena tṛpyanty ekādaśāvikaiḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
atha tṛptiḥ | grāmyābhiroṣadhībhir māsaṃ tṛptiḥ [āraṇyābhir vā] tadalābhe mūlaphalair adbhir vā sahānnenottarās tarpayanti | chāgosrameṣā ālabdhāḥ śeṣāṇi krītvā labdhvā vā svayaṃ mṛtānāṃ vāhṛtya pacet | [māsadvayaṃ mātsyaiḥ | trīn māsān hāriṇamṛgamāṃsena ||] caturaḥ śākunena | paśca rauraveṇa | ṣaṭ chāgena | sapta kaurmeṇa | aṣṭau varāheṇa | nava meṣamāṃsena | daśa māhiṣeṇa | ekādaśa pārṣatena | [saṃvatsaraṃ gavyena pāyasena payasā vā | vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena ca dvādaśa varṣāṇi ||] akṣayā tṛptiḥ khaḍgaḥ kālaśākaṃ lohitacchāgo madhu mahāśalkā varṣāsu maghāsu ca śrāddhaṃ hasticchāyāyāṃ ca ||
' uttarāḥ ' mūlaphalādayaḥ | 'usraḥ' anaḍvān | 'ālabdhāḥ' kṛtālambhāḥ ||
[44] viṣṇuḥ [70, 1-14] |
[tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalaiḥ] śākaiḥ śyāmākaiḥ priyaṅgunīvāraiḥ mudgair godhūmaiś ca māsaṃ prīyante | [dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena | trīn māsān hāriṇena tu | caturaś caurabhreṇa | pañca śākunena | ṣaṭ chāgena |] sapta rauraveṇa | aṣṭau pārṣatena | nava gavayena | daśa māhiṣeṇa | ekādaśa tūpareṇājena | saṃvatsaraṃ gavyena payasā tadvikārair vā [atrāpi pitṛgītā gāthā bhavanti |]
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkaṃ māṃsaṃ vārdhrīṇasasya ca |
viṣāṇavarjyā ye khaḍgā āsūryaṃ tān lamemahi ||
'tūparaḥ' śṛṅgotthānakāle ’pi śṛṅgarahitaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ghṛtena māsaṃ prīṇāti | kālaśākena dvimāsam | yavā- gvapūpa yasareṇa trimāsam | matsyaiścaturo māsān | ājena māṃsena pañca | pāyasena ṣaṇmāsā- | śākunena sapta māsān | aṣṭau māsānaiṇeyena | nava māsān gavyena | ekādaśamāsān māhiṣeṇa | dvādaśamāsāṃśchāgena | khaḍgena trayodaśamāsān | savalohena chāgenānantyamiti ||
‘tṛptā bhavanti' ityanuvṛttau, uśanā |
caturo māsān kṛṣṇasāraṅgeṇa | aṣṭau māsān varāheṇa | nava ruruṇā | daśa meṣeṇa ||
[45] āpastambaḥ [2, 16, 22-26, 2, 17, 1-3] |
tatra dravyāṇi tilamāṣā vrīhiyavā āpo mūlaphalāni ca | snehavati tv evānne tīvratarā [pitṝṇāṃ] prītir draghīyāṃsaṃ ca kālam | tathā dharmāhṛtena dravyeṇa tīrthe pratipannena | saṃvatsaraṃ gavyena prītiḥ | bhūyāṃsam ato māhiṣeṇa | etena grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ māṃsaṃ medhyaṃ vyākhyātam | khaḍgopastaraṇe khaḍgamāṃsenānantyaṃ kālam | tathā śatabalair matsyasya māṃsena | vārdhrāṇasasya ca ||
‘snehavati' ghṛtādyaiḥ, tadalābhe tailādy api | 'tīvratarā' atiśayavatī | 'dharmāhṛtena' nyāyopāttena | 'tīrthe pratipannena' tīrthayātrā, tadgatena dravyeṇety arthaḥ | ' khaḍgopastaraṇena' [khaḍgacarmanirmitāsanena] iti ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
dharmeṇa vittam ādāya pitṛbhyo dadyāt |
śaṅkhaḥ |
āmrān pālevatān ikṣūn mṛdvīkābhavyadāḍimān |
vidāryāṃś ca bharuṇḍāṃś ca śrāddhakāle pradāpayet ||
lājān madhuyutān dadyāt saktūn śarkarayā saha |
dadyāc chrāddhe prayatnena śṛṅgāṭabisakebukān ||
'pālevataṃ' jambīrākāraṃ phalaṃ 'voṭha' iti kāśmīreṣu prasiddham | 'mṛdvīkā' drākṣā | 'bhavyaṃ' karmaraṅgam | [46] vidāryān kandān | 'bharuṇḍāḥ' jalaprabhavāḥ kandaviśeṣāḥ iti mañjarīkāraḥ | 'kebukaṃ' ārdrakasadṛśaḥ kandaviśeṣaḥ | kebukasya niṣedhas tu āraṇyaśākaviṣayaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
kṣatriyais tu mṛgayāyāṃ vidhinā yad upāhṛtam |
śrāddhakāle praśaṃsanti siṃhavyāghrahataṃ ca yat ||
manuḥ [3, 258] |
munyannāni payaḥ somo māṃsaṃ yac cānupaskṛtam |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ caiva prakṛtyā havir ucyate ||
‘anupaskṛtaṃ' avikṛtam iti yāvat | ‘akṣāralavaṇaṃ' ūṣaramṛttikākṛtalavaṇam | yadi tu tal lavaṇaṃ ceti dvandvānantaraṃ nañ saṃbadhyate tadobhayavṛttitvāt samāsārthagauravaṃ syāt ||
mahābhārate [anu, 135,8]
vardhamānatilaṃ śrāddham akṣayyaṃ manur abravīt ||
tathā |
sarvakāmaiḥ sa yajate yas tilair yajate pitṝn |
na cākāmena dātavyaṃ tilaśrāddhaṃ kathaṃcana ||
'vardhamānatilaṃ' tilabahulam |
vāyupurāṇe |
śyāmākair ikṣubhiś caiva pitṝṇāṃ sarvakāmikam |
kuryād āgrayaṇaṃ yas tu sa śīghraṃ siddhim āpnuyāt ||
[47]        śyāmākā hastināmāno viddhi tān yajñaniḥsṛtān |
prasātikā priyaṅguś ca grāhyāḥ syuḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
etāny api samāni syuḥ śyāmākānāṃ sadā guṇaiḥ |
kṛṣṇamāṣās tilāś caiva śreṣṭhāḥ syur yavaśālayaḥ ||
mahāyavā vrīhiyavās tathaiva ca madhūlikā |
kṛṣṇā śvetā lohitā ca grāhyāḥ syuḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
bilvāmalakamṛdvīkāḥ panasāmrātadāḍimam |
bhavyapārāvatākṣoṭakharjūrāmraphalāni ca ||
kaseruḥ kovidāraś ca tālakandaṃ tathā bisam |
tamālaṃ śatakandaṃ ca gandhālū śītakandakam ||
kāleyaṃ kālaśākaṃ ca suniṣaṇṇaṃ suvarcalā |
māṃsaṃ śākaṃ dadhi kṣīraṃ cañcur vetrāṅkaras tathā ||
kaṭphalaṃ kaṅkaṇī drākṣā lakucaṃ mocam eva ca |
karkandhur bhīrakaṃ vāraṃ tiṇḍukaṃ madhusāhvayam ||
vaikaṅkataṃ nālikeraṃ śṛṅgāṭakaparūṣakam |
pippalī maricaṃ caiva paṭolaṃ bṛhatīphalam ||
sugandhi matsyamāṃsaṃ ca kalāyāḥ sarva eva ca |
evamādīni cānyāni svādūni madhurāṇi ca ||
nāgaraṃ cātra vai deyaṃ dīrghamūlakam eva ca ||
'prasātikā' prasāṭīti madhyadeśaprasiddhaṃ dhānyam | ‘mahāyavāḥ’ vaṇḍayavāḥ || 'madhūlikā' yāvanālaviśeṣaḥ | asyā eva viśeṣaṇaṃ 'kṛṣṇā śvetā lohitā ca' iti | 'akṣoṭaṃ' girisaṃbhavapīluphalam | 'kovidāraḥ' śvetakāñcanāraviśeṣaḥ | 'tālakandaṃ' tālamūlīti prasiddham | 'śatakandaṃ ' śatāvarī | 'śītakandakaṃ' śālūkam | 'kāleyaṃ' karālākhyaṃ śākam iti govindarājaḥ | 'suniṣaṇṇaṃ' cāṅgerīsadṛśaṃ śākaṃ [48] jalaprabhavam | 'suvarcalā ' sūryabhaktā | ' cañcuḥ ' ceñcu iti prasiddhaṃ śākam | ' kaṭphalaṃ' kaṭphalavṛkṣam eva | ' kaṅkaṇī' amlarasā drākṣā | 'mocaṃ' kadalīphalam | 'karkandhuḥ' badarīviśeṣaḥ |
ādityapurāṇe |
madhūkaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ caiva karpūraṃ maricaṃ guḍam |
śrāddhakarmaṇi śastāni saindhavaṃ trapusaṃ tathā ||
' rāmaṭhaṃ ' hiṅgu |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [32, 10] |
yavavrīhisagodhūmatilā mudrāḥ sasarṣapāḥ |
priyaṅgavaḥ kovidārā niṣpāvāś cātiśobhanāḥ ||
'niṣpāvā' vallāḥ śimbisadṛśā dakṣiṇāpathe prasiddhāḥ |
vāyupurāṇe [80, 42-48 ] |
bhakṣāṇy emyaḥ karambhaṃ ca iṣṭakā ghṛtapūrakāḥ |
kṛsaraṃ madhu sarpiś ca payaḥ pāyasam eva ca ||
snigdham uṣṇaṃ ca yo dadyād agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet |
dadhi gavyaṃ ca saṃsṛṣṭaṃ bhakṣān nānāvidhān api ||
dattvā na śocati śrāddhe varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca |
ghṛtena bhojayed viprān ghṛtaṃ bhūmau samutsṛjet ||
śarkarāḥ kṣīrasaṃyuktāḥ pṛthukā nityam akṣayāḥ |
[49]        syuś ca saṃvatsaraṃ prītā 'jhaṣakair meṣakaiṇakaiḥ ||
saktūn lājāṃs tathāpūpān kulmāṣān vyañjanaiḥ saha |
sarpissiddhāni sarvāṇi dadhnā saṃskṛtya bhojayet ||
śrāddheṣu tāni yo dadyāt pitaraḥ prīṇayanti tam ||
'karambho' dadhimiśrāḥ saktavaḥ | 'ghṛtaṃ bhūmau samutsṛjet' tathā ghṛtena pātraṃ pūraṇīyaṃ yathā bhūmau ghṛtaṃ kṣaratīty arthaḥ | 'pṛthukāḥ' cipiṭāḥ ||
matsyapurāṇe |
yatkicin madhunā miśraṃ gokṣīraghṛtapāyasaiḥ |
dattam akṣayam ity āhuḥ pitaraḥ pūrvadevatāḥ |
hārītaḥ |
tilaiḥ śrāddhaṃ puṣṭikāmaḥ kuryāt | apūpai ṛddhikāmaḥ | ghṛtaguḍatilais tejaskāmaḥ | saubhāgyakāma ārogyakāmo vā paramānnena | kṛsaramāṃsadadhiyavāgūbhiḥ sarvakāmaḥ ||
atra śrāddhadravyeṣu yatra phalaviśeṣasaṃyogas tatra guṇaphalārthinaḥ pravṛttiḥ | yatra guṇasaṃyogo nāsti teṣāṃ nityavad aṅgatvāt tair vinā śrāddhāṅgāniṣpattir eva | sāmānyato vihitasyābhyavaharaṇīyadravyasya yathālābham upakalpitasya śrāddhasvarūpasaṃpādakatvam | tadalābhe tv avihitāpratiṣiddhaṃ vihitasadṛśam upādātavyam | yat tu śrāddhaprakaraṇaniṣiddhaṃ tatpratinidhitvenāpi nopādeyam | yatra tu phalaviśeṣasaṃyogas tasya śrāddhasvarūpasaṃpādakatve sati tattadviśeṣaniṣpādakatvam api | yat tu niṣiddhaṃ gomahiṣamāṃsādi phalaviśeṣārthaṃ ca vihitaṃ tatphalāthinaivopādeyam | nātadarthinā śrāddhasvarūpamātrasaṃpādakatvena | [50] yat tv atraiva vihitaṃ niṣiddhaṃ ca māhiṣamāṃsādi kāryakārivihitadravyābhāve tad upādeyam | yat tu tasminn eva dravye pitṛtṛptikālālpīyastvaṃ bhūyastvaṃ tat tasyaiva dravyāvasthāviśeṣāpekṣayā vyavasthitaṃ boddhavyam |
[51]
|| 6 ā ||
|| atha vivarjyāni ||
tatra hārītaḥ |
atha varjyāni | pālaṅkyānālikāpātīkaśigrusṛmukavārtākabhūstṛṇakapelumāṣa-masūrakṛtalavaṇāni ca śrāddhe na dadyāt ||
'sṛmukaṃ' jalaprabhavakhādiraśākam | 'bhūstṛṇakapelū grāmyāraṇyau śākaviśeṣau kāśmīreṣu prasiddhau' iti hārītabhāṣyakāraḥ | māṣaniṣedho ’tra gauramāṣaviṣayaḥ, 'māṣās tilāś ca' iti vārupurāṇe māṣāṇāṃ vidhānāt |
viṣṇuḥ [79, 17-18] |
pippalīsṛmukabhūstṛṇaśigrusarṣapasurasā[sarjakasuvacala] kūṣmāṇḍālābuvārtāka-pālaṅkayo[podakī]taṇḍulīyakakusambhapiṇḍālukamahiṣīkṣīrāṇi varjayet | rājabhāṣāsurīparyuṣitakṛtalavaṇāni ||
'āsurī' rājasarṣapaḥ ||
[52] śaṅkhaḥ |
bhūstṛṇaṃ surasaṃ śigruṃ pālaṅkyāṃ sṛmukaṃ tathā |
kūṣmāṇḍālābuvārtākakovidārāṃś ca varjayet ||
pippalīṃ maricaṃ caiva tathā vai piṇḍamūlakam |
kṛtaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ vaṃśāgraṃ ca vivarjayet ||
rājamāṣān masūrāṃś ca kodravān koradūṣakān |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān śrāddhakarmaṇi varjayet ||
'surasaṃ' tulasī | 'vaṃśāgraṃ' vaṃśakīram' | 'koradūṣakaḥ' vanakodravaḥ |
mahābhārate |
aśrāddheyāni dhānyāni kodravāś caṇakās tathā |
hiṅgu dravyeṣu sarveṣu alābū laśunas tathā ||
palāṇḍusaubhāñjanakau tathā gṛñjanakādayaḥ |
kukkuṇḍakāny alābūni kṛṣṇaṃ lavaṇam eva ca ||
grāmyavārāhamāṃsaṃ ca yac caivāprokṣitaṃ bhavet |
kṛṣṇājaṃ jīrakaṃ caiva śītapākī tathaiva ca ||
aṅkurādyās tathā varjyā iha śṛṅgāṭakāni ca ||
varjayel lavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ tathā jambūphalāni ca |
avakruṣṭāvaruditaṃ tathā śrāddheṣu varjayet ||
[53] 'kukkuṇḍakāni' kavakāni chatrākasamānaprakṛtidravyāṇi piṇḍopamāni | 'kṛṣṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ' sauvarcalam | 'kṛṣṇājaṃ' kṛṣṇajīrakam | 'śītapākī' kākajaṅghā | 'hiṅga dravyeṣu' dravyeṣu madhye hiṅgu aśrāddheyam ity arthaḥ | ayaṃ tu niṣedhaḥ svarūpeṇa dīyamānasya | yat tu 'madhūkaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ ca’ ity ādityapurāṇe vidhānaṃ tad dṛvyāntarasaṃskārakatvena | evaṃ marīcādāv api ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
akṛtāgrayaṇaṃ caiva dhānyajātaṃ dvijottama |
rājamāṣān aṇūṃś caiva masūrāṃś caiva varjayet ||
hārītaḥ |
viṣachadmahataṃ māṃsaṃ vyādhatiryagghataṃ ca yat |
na praśaṃsanti vai śrāddhe yac ca mantravivarjitam ||
vāyupurāṇe |
varjanīyāni vakṣyāmi śrāddhakarmaṇi nityaśaḥ |
karambhaṃ yāni cānyāni rasagandhahatāni ca ||
avedoktāś ca niryāsā lavaṇāny auṣarāṇi ca |
durgandhi phenilaṃ caiva tathā vai palvalodakam ||
labhate yatra gaustṛptiṃ naktaṃ yac caiva gṛhyate |
āvikaṃ mārgam auṣṭraṃ ca sarvam ekaśaphaṃ ca yat |
māhiṣaṃ cāmaraṃ caiva payo varjyaṃ vijānatā ||
[54] 'palvalaṃ' alpasaraḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [32, 18, 16, 17] |
pitryarthaṃ me prayacchasvety uktvā yac cāpy upāhṛtam |
varjanīyaṃ sadā sadbhis tat payaḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
durgandhi phenilaṃ caiva tathālpapradarodakam |
yac ca sarvārtham utsṛṣṭaṃ yac cābhojyanipānajam ||
tadvarjyaṃ salilaṃ tāta sadaiva pitṛkarmaṇi ||
'abhojyanipānajaṃ' patitādikāritapuṣkariṇyāḍijalam ||
matsyapurāṇe |
masūraśaṇaniṣpāvarājamāṣakulatthakāḥ ||
padmabilvakadhattūrapāribhadrāḍharūṣakāḥ |
adeyāḥ pitṛkāryeṣu payaś cājāvikaṃ tathā |
kodravo hāravaraṭakapitthamadhukākasī |
etāny api na deyāni pitṛbhyaḥ priyam icchatā ||
‘pāribhadraṃ' pharahadda iti prasiddham |
brahmapurāṇe |
dviḥ sinnaṃ paridagdhaṃ ca tathaivāgrāvalehitam |
śarkarākīṭapāṣāṇaiḥ keśair yac cāpy upadrutam ||
piṇyākaṃ mathitaṃ caiva tathātilavaṇaṃ ca yat |
siddhāḥ kṛtāś ca bhakṣyāś ca pratyakṣalavaṇīkṛtāḥ ||
vāgbhāvaduṣṭāś ca tathā duṣṭaiś copahatās tathā |
vāsasā cāvadhūtāni varjyāni śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
dadhi śākaṃ tathā bhakṣyaṃ śuktaṃ cauṣadhivarjitam |
varjayec ca tathā cānyān sarvān abhiṣavān api ||
'agrāvalehitaṃ' upabhuktāgrabhāgam | 'siddhāḥ kṛtāś ca ' ityāder ayam arthaḥ — yeṣu siddheṣūttarakālaṃ pratyakṣaṃ lavaṇaprakṣepaḥ kṛtaḥ |
[55]
|| 7 ||
|| atha brāhmaṇaparīkṣā ||
tatra manuḥ, śātātapaś ca dvitīye |
tatra ye bhojanīyāḥ syur ye ca varjyā dvijottamāḥ |
yāvantaś caiva yaiś cānnais tān pravakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
dūrād eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam |
tīrthaṃ tad dhavyakavyānāṃ pradāne so ’tithiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sahasraṃ hi sahasrāṇām anṛcāṃ yatra bhuñjate |
ekas tān mantravit prītaḥ sarvān arhati dharmataḥ ||
jñānotkṛṣṭāya deyāni kavyāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
na hi hastāv asṛgdigdhau rudhireṇaiva śudhyataḥ ||
'dūrātparīkṣā' pitṛpitāmahādyabhijanaparīkṣā | tīrtham iva 'tīrthaṃ' phalaprāptihetur ity arthaḥ | 'pradāne so ’tithiḥ' asmin pradāne so ’tithivat pūjyaḥ |
[56] yamaḥ |
pūrvam eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇān vedapāragān |
śarīraprabhavair doṣair viśuddhāṃś caritavratān ||
dūrād eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇān vedapāragān |
iṣṭān vā yadi vāniṣṭāṃs tāṃs tu tat kālam ānayet ||
'pūrvaṃ' nimantraṇād iti śeṣaḥ | iṣṭāniṣṭāv atrāpātataḥ prītyaprītiviṣayau | arimitrayoḥ 'nāriṃ na mitram' iti niṣedhāt |
manuḥ [3, 149] |
na brāhmaṇaṃ parīkṣeta daive karmaṇi dharmavit |
pitrye karmaṇi saṃprāpte parīkṣeta prayatnataḥ ||
‘śrotriyādīn bhojayet | durvālādīṃś ca na bhojayet ' iti prakramya', gautamaḥ [15, 29-30]
haviḥṣu caivam | durvālādīn śrāddha ekaike ||
'haviḥṣu' havyeṣu, daiveṣv ity arthaḥ |
[57] manuḥ [3, 118] |
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni dātṛbhiḥ |
arhattamāya viprāya tasmai dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [ 3, 8] |
aśrotriyāya yad dattaṃ pitṝnnaiti na devatāḥ ||
manuḥ, śātātapaś ca dvitīye [manu, 3, 133, 135, 136] |
dātṝna pratigṛhītṝṃś ca kurute phalabhāginaḥ |
vidvadbhayo dakṣiṇā dattā vidhivat pretya ceha ca ||
yatnena bhojayec chrāddhe bahvṛcaṃ vedapāragam |
śākhāntagaṃ vāpy adhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ vā samāptigam ||
eṣām anyatamo yasya bhuñjīta śrāddham arcitaḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ tasya tṛptiḥ syāc chāśvatī sāptapauruṣī ||
‘samāptigaṃ' samastaśākhādhyāyinam |
yamaḥ |
bahvṛco vā trisauparṇaś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
pañcāgnir vā caturvedī tv athavā brahmaśīrṣakaḥ ||
[58]        ātmavic cāṅgavic caiva brahmadeyāsutas tathā |
sarve ca te yathāvat syur ekadeśo ’pi vā bhavet ||
trayaḥ pare ‘varāś caiva ātmā tatraiva saptamaḥ |
te saptapuruṣāḥ sarve tadannam upabhuñjate ||
'trisauparṇaḥ' 'caturvargā yuvatiḥ supeṣā' ityādi ṛktraye suparṇapadopalakṣitaḥ | ' jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ' jyeṣṭhasāmādhyetā | pañcāgniḥ pañcāgniprakāśakamantrādhyetā | 'brahmaśīrṣakaḥ' atharvaśiraso ’dhyetā | 'brahmadeyā' brāhmavivāhenoḍhā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe chandogaṃ tatra bhojayet |
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni tritayaṃ tatra vidyate ||
aṭeta pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām |
yadi labhyeta pitrarthe sāmnām akṣaracintakaḥ ||
ṛcā tu tṛpyati pitā yajuṣāpi pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ pitāmahaḥ sāmnā chandogo ’bhyadhikas tataḥ ||
[59] yama-śātātapau |
chandogaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe vaiśvadeve ca bahvṛcam |
puṣṭikarmaṇi cādhvaryuṃ śāntikarmaṇy atharvaṇam ||
śātātapaḥ |
bhojayed yas tv atharvāṇaṃ daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
anantam akṣayaṃ caiva phalaṃ tasyeti vai śrutiḥ ||
yas tv anyaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe vidyamāneṣv atharvasu |
nirāśās tasya gacchanti devatāḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena śrāddhakāle tv atharvaṇam |
bhojayed dhavyakavyeṣu pitṝṇāṃ ca tad akṣayam ||
manuḥ [3, 124-125] |
jñānaniṣṭhā dvijāḥ kecit taponiṣṭhās tathā pare |
svādhyāyajñānaniṣṭhāś ca karmaniṣṭhās tatha pare ||
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratiṣṭhāpyāni yatnataḥ |
havyāni tu yathāśrāddham eteṣv eva caturṣv api ||
aśrotriyaḥ pitā tasya putraḥ syād vedapāragaḥ |
[aśrotriyo vā putraḥ syāt pitā syād vedapāragaḥ || ]
jyāyāṃsam anayor vidyād yasya syāc chrotriyaḥ pitā |
mantrasaṃpūjanārthaṃ tu satkāram itaro ’rhati ||
[60] yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 219,221] |
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu śrotriyo brahmavid yuvā |
vedārthavij jyeṣṭhasāmā trimadhus trisuparṇakaḥ ||
karmaniṣṭhās taponiṣṭhāḥ pañcāgnir brahmacāriṇaḥ |
pitṛmātṛparāś caiva brāhmaṇāḥ śrāddhasaṃpadaḥ ||
'agryāḥ’ prakṛṣṭāḥ | 'trimadhuḥ' ṛgvedaikadeśas tadvrataṃ ca | tadyogāt  puruṣo ’py abhidhīyate |
bhojayed ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ [15, 9-11] |
śrotriyān vāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannān | yuvabhyo dānaṃ prathamam |
eke pitṛvat ||
'śrotriyān' chando’dhyāyinaḥ | 'vāksaṃpannān' saṃskṛtabhāṣiṇaḥ | 'rūpasaṃpannān' manojñavapuṣaḥ | 'vayaḥsaṃpannān' abālān | 'śīlasaṃpannān' samyakpraṇihitamanovākkāyān | vayaḥ panneṣv api 'yuvabhyo dānaṃ' 'prathamaṃ' pradhānam | ayaṃ hi prathamaḥ kalpo yad yuvabhyo dānam ity arthaḥ | 'eke pitṛvat' yadvayaskāḥ pitrādayaḥ pramītās tadvayaskān bhojayed ity eke manyante ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 16-17] |
[aparapakṣa ūrdhvaṃ caturthyāḥ] pitṛbhyo dadyāt | pūrvedyur brāhmaṇān saṃnipātya yatīn gṛhasthān sādhūn vāpariṇatavayaso ‘vikarmasthān śrotriyān aśiṣyān antevāsinaḥ ||
'saṃnipātya' saṃnidhāya | 'sādhūn' kṣīṇadoṣān | āśramasthān anyān api 'avikarmasthān' akutsitakarmaṇaḥ | [61] 'aśiṣyān antevāsinaḥ' adhyayanaṃ parimāpya gurulanivṛttān ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
tadahar brāhmaṇān āmantrayet | pūrvedyur vā | snātakān eke | yatīn gṛhasthān sādhūn vā | śrotriyān anavadyān svakarmasthān | abhāve ’pi śiṣyān sācārān ||
āpastambaḥ [2,17,10] |
tulyaguṇeṣu vayovṛddhaḥ śreyān dravyakṛśaś cepsan |
tulyaguṇeṣv eva daridraḥ śrāddhīyadravyārthī śreyān ity arthaḥ ||
manuḥ [4,31] |
vedavidyāvratasnātān śrotriyān gṛham āgatān |
pūjayed dhavyakavyābhyāṃ viparītāṃs tu varjayet ||
yamaḥ |
vedavidyāvratasnātāḥ śrotriyā vedapāragāḥ |
svadharmaniratāḥ kṣāntāḥ kriyāvantas tapasvinaḥ ||
tebhyo havyaṃ ca kavyaṃ ca praśāntebhyaḥ pradīyate |
na vedavikrayakṛtaḥ svadharmacchidradarśinaḥ |
[62] āpastambaḥ [2, 15, 11] |
śucīn mantravataḥ sarvā sarvakṛtyeṣu bhojayet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
prayatāya śucaye sarvatra [ deyaṃ ] havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca | tasmāc chucir iti vijāyate ||
matsyapurāṇe |
sarvajño vadavit satrī jñānavidyākulānvitaḥ |
purāṇavettā dharmajñaḥ prājñaḥ svādhyāyakṛtparaḥ ||
śivabhaktaḥ pitṛparaḥ sūryabhakto ’tha vaiṣṇavaḥ |
brāhmaṇo yogavic chānto vijitātmā ’tha śīlavān ||
bhojayec cāpi dauhitraṃ yatnataḥ śvaśuraṃ gurum |
viṭpatiṃ mātulaṃ bandhum ṛtvigācāryasomapān ||
'viṭpatiḥ' jāmātā ||
skandapurāṇe |
kulaśrutābhyāṃ saṃyuktād alubdhas tu viśiṣyate |
[tābhyām api ca viprābhyāṃ ciravāsī viśiṣyate ||]
yamaḥ |
nakṣatratithipuṇyāhamuhūrtān maṅgalāni ca |
na nirdiśanti ye viprās tair bhuktaṃ hy akṣayaṃ bhavet ||
[63]
|| 8 ||
|| paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
brāhmaṇeṣv apy apāṅkteyān havyakavyeṣu varjayet |
parīkṣya bahudhā viprān āharet paṅktipāvanān ||
tatra manuḥ [3, 173-176] |
apaṅktyopahatā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yair dvijottamaiḥ |
tān nibodhata kārtsnyena dvijāgryān paṅktipāvanān ||
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca |
śrotriyānvayajāś caiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisauparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
brahmadeyānusantāno 'dharmavij jeṣṭhasāmagaḥ ||
vedārthavit pravaktā ca brahmacārī sahasradaḥ |
śatāyuś caiva vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
'triṇāciketaḥ' adhvaryuvedabhāgas tadvrataṃ ca | tadyogāt puruṣo’pi tathā ||
[64] yamaḥ |
ye somapā virajaso dharmajñāḥ śāntabuddhayaḥ |
vratino niyamasthāś ca ṛtukālābhigāminaḥ ||
pañcāgnir apy adhīyāno yajurvedavid eva tu |
bahvṛcaś ca trisauparṇas trimadhur vātha yo bhavet ||
triṇāciketo virajāś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
atharvaśiraso ’dhyetā sarve te paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
śiśur apy agnihotrī ca nyāyavic ca ṣaḍaṅgavit |
mantrabrāhmaṇavic caiva yaś cānyo dharmapāṭhakaḥ ||
ṛṣivratī ṛṣīkaś ca tathā dvādaśavārṣikaḥ |
brahmadeyāsutaś caiva garbhaśuddhaḥ sahasradaḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇavratacaraḥ satyavādī purāṇavit |
niṣṇātaḥ sarvavidyāsu śānto vigatakalmaṣaḥ ||
gurudevāgnipūjāsu prasakto jñānatatparaḥ |
vimuktaḥ sarvato dhīro brahmabhūto dvijottamaḥ ||
anamitro na cāmitro maitraś cātmavid eva ca |
snātako japyanirataḥ sadā puṣpaphalapradaḥ ||
ṛjur ghṛṇī kṣamī dāntaḥ śāntaḥ satyavrataḥ śuciḥ |
vedajñaḥ sarvaśāstrajña upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ ||
[65]        gṛhastho brahmacārī ca caturvedavid eva ca |
vedavidyāvratasnātā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
brahmacaryāsu niratā ye kṛśāḥ kṛśavṛttayaḥ |
atyutkrāntās tv adharmebhyas te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
satriṇo niyamasthāś ca ye viprāḥ śrutisaṃgatāḥ ||
prāṇihiṃsānivṛttāś ca te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
agnihotraratāḥ śāntāḥ kṛpāvanto ’nasūyakāḥ ||
ye pratigrahaniḥsnehās te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
satyavantaś ca dhīrāś ca nityaṃ dānaparāś ca ye ||
maṅgalācārayuktāś ca te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
ete vai bhūtale devāḥ pūrvaṃ devair vinirmitāḥ ||
yat kiṃcit pitṛdaivatyam ebhyo dattaṃ tad akṣayam |
dūrād eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇāṃś caritavratān ||
sthavirāṃs tapasvino dāntān niṣṇātān yadi vetarān |
tān sarvān bhojayec chrāddhe sādhubhiḥ saṃnimantrayet ||
tathā |
vidvāṃsaḥ suvratāḥ śāntā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
tair bhuktaṃ vrajate devāṃs tair bhuktaṃ vrajate pitṝn ||
'ṛṣīkaḥ' ṛṣeḥ kiṃcid ūnaḥ | 'dvādaśavārṣikaḥ' dvādaśa varṣāṇi vedavratacārī | 'sahasradaḥ' gosahasrada iti medhātithiḥ | 'vimuktaḥ sarvataḥ' ṛṇād iti śeṣaḥ | 'anamitraḥ’ śatrurahitaḥ | 'na cāmitraḥ' na kasyāpi śatruḥ | 'maitraḥ' maitrīvataḥ | 'ghṛṇī' kṛpāluḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ |
brahmadeyānusantāno brahmadeyāpradāyakaḥ |
brahmadeyāpatir yaś ca brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
yajuṣāṃ pārago yaś ca sāmnāṃ yaś cāpi pāragaḥ |
atharvaśiraso ’dhyetā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
nityaṃ yāgaparo vidvān samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
dhyānaśīlo yatir vidvān brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
sthitir avicchinnavedaveditāyonisaṃkaratvam ārṣeyatvaṃ ceti kulaguṇāḥ | [vedo] vedāṅgāni dharmo ’dhyātmaṃ vijñānaṃ [67] sthitiś ceti ṣaḍvidhaṃ śrutam | brahmaṇyatā devapitṛbhaktatā samatā saumyatāparopatāpitānasūyatā mṛdutāpāruṣyaṃ maitratā priyavāditvaṃ kṛtajñatā śaraṇyatā praśāntiśc eti trayodaśavidhaṃ śīlam | kṣamā damo dayā dānam ahiṃsā gurupūjanaṃ śaucaṃ snānaṃ japo homas tapaḥ svādhyāyaḥ satyavacanaṃ saṃtoṣo dṛḍhavratatvaṃ ceti ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ vṛttam | kulīnāḥ śrutavantaḥ śīlavanto vratasthāḥ satyavādino ’vyaṅgāḥ pāṅkteyāḥ | dvādaśobhayataḥ śrotriyāḥ triṇāciketas trimadhus trisauparṇas triśīrṣā jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ paścāgniḥ ṣaḍaṅgavid rudrajāpī tūrdhvaretā ṛtukālābhigāmī tattvavic eti paṅktipāvanā bhavanti | athātrodaharanti —
pacanaḥ pācanas tretā yasya pañcāgnayo gṛhe |
sāyaṃprātaḥ pradīpyante sa vipraḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ ||
[68]        sahasrasaṃmitaṃ prāhuḥ snātakaṃ pūrvavadguṇaiḥ |
pañcāgnyādiguṇair yuktaḥ śatasāhasra ucyate ||
anṛcāṃ yadi vā kṛtsnāṃ paṅktiṃ yojanam āyatām |
punāti vedavid vipro niyuktaḥ paṅktimūrdhani ||
dvādaśa vakṣyamāṇā ubhayataḥ śrotriyādayaḥ | 'pacanaḥ' āvasathyāgniḥ | 'pācanaḥ' sabhyo ’gniḥ | 'pūrvavadguṇaiḥ’ pūrvavadguṇair yuktaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
atha pāṅkteyā vedavedāṅgavit pañcāgnir anūcāno vyavahārī śrotriyas triṇāciketas trimadhus trisauparṇo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ sandhyāsnāyī vedaparaḥ sadāgniko mātṛpitṛśuśrūṣur dharmaśāstravid iti ||
'anūcānaḥ' prāsaṅgapravacanasyādhyetā | 'vyavahārī' śāstrānuṣṭhānaśīlaḥ | 'sadāgnikaḥ’ avicchinnanityakṛtyaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [3,19] |
[triṇāciketaḥ] pañcāgnis trisuparṇaś caturmedhī vājasaneyī ṣaḍaṅgavid brahmadeyānusantānaś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago mantrabrāhmaṇavid yaś ca svadharmān adhīyate | yasya [ca] [69] daśapuruṣaṃ mātṛpitṛvaṃśaḥ śrotriyo [vi]jñāyate vidvāṃsaḥ snātakāś ca te paṅktipāvanā bhavanti ||
'caturmedhī' cāturmāsyayājī | tatra hi catvāro medhā vaiśvadevādayaḥ kriyante ||
uśanā |
tatra brāhmaṇo vedavidyāvratasnātakaḥ pañcapuruṣapāraṃparyād avikhaṇḍitacāritramaryādas triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇaś chandovij jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ śatavarṣo ’luptadharmādhyayanaḥ mantraprabhṛtijāpyapravacanādhyāyaparaḥ ṛtukālābhigāmī chandasā śuddhadaśapuruṣaḥ snātaka iti paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [73, 1-21] |
atha paṅktipāvanāḥ | triṇāciketaḥ | pañcāgniḥ | jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ | vedapāragaḥ | vedāṅgasyāpy ekasya pāragaḥ | purāṇetihāsavyākaraṇapāragaḥ | [dharmaśāstrasyāpy ekasya pāragaḥ] | tīrthapūtaḥ | yajñapūtaḥ | [tapaḥpūtaḥ] | satyapūtaḥ | dānapūtaḥ | mantrapūtaḥ | gāyatrījapanirataḥ | brahmadeyānusaṃtānaḥ | trisauparṇī | jāmātā | dauhitraś ceti pātram | viśeṣeṇa ca yoginaḥ | atra pitṛgītā gāthā bhavati —
api syāt sa kule ’smākaṃ bhojayed yas tu yoginam |
vipraṃ śrāddhe prayatnena yena tṛpyāmahe vayam ||
[70] matsyapurāṇe |
yas tu vyākurute vācaṃ yaś ca mīmāṃsate ’dhvaram |
sāmasvaravidhijñaś ca paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
aṣṭavarṣā tu yā dattā śrutaśīlasamanvite |
sā gaurī tatsuto gauraḥ paṅktipāvanapāvanaḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
yatis tu sarvaviprāṇāṃ sarveṣām agrabhug bhavet |
itihāsapañcamān vedān yaḥ paṭhed dvijasattamaḥ ||
anantaraṃ yate so ’yaṃ niyoktavyo vijānatā |
trivedo ’nantaraṃ tasya dvivedas tadanantaram |
ekavedas tataḥ paścān nityādhyāyī tataḥ param ||
brahmapurāṇe |
ṣaḍaṅgavij jñānayogī yajñatattvajña eva ca |
ayācitārthī vipro vā śrāddhakalpavid eva ca ||
aṣṭādaśānāṃ vidyānām ekasyāpi tu pāragaḥ |
triviśuddhas trisauparṇī pañcāgnir jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ ||
[71]        cāndrāyaṇaiḥ parākair vā niyamair yas tu saṃskṛtaḥ |
bārhaspatyaṃ nītiśāstraṃ śabdavidyāṃ ca vetti yaḥ ||
itihāsapurāṇaiś ca yaḥ pavitrīkṛtaḥ sadā ||
akṛtyam api kurvāṇaḥ sa pavitraśatād varaḥ |
bahunātra kim uktena itihāsapurāṇavit ||
atharvaśiraso ’dhyetā tāv ubhau pitṛbhiḥ purā |
tapaḥ kṛtvā tu yogāt tu prathitau pitṛkarmaṇi ||
vāyupurāṇe |
gṛhasthānāṃ sahasreṇa vānaprasthaśatena ca |
brahmacārisahasreṇa yogī tv eko viśiṣyate ||
nāstiko vā vikarmā vā saṃkīrṇas taskaro ’pi vā |
nānyat tu kāraṇaṃ dāne yogiṣv āha prajāpatiḥ ||
pitaras tasya tuṣyanti suvṛṣṭeneva karṣakāḥ |
putro vāpy atha vā pautro dhyāyinaṃ yas tu bhojayet ||
atha manuḥ [3, 147 – 148] |
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pradāne havyakavyayoḥ |
anukalpas tv ayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ ||
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svakīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhum ṛtvigyājyau ca bhojayet ||
[72] paṅktipāvanānantaraṃ — baudhāyanaḥ [2,14,3-4] |
tadabhāve rahasyavit | ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmānīti śrāddhasya mahimā | tasmād evaṃvidhaṃ sapiṇḍam apy āśayet ||
āpastambaḥ [2,17,4-6] |
bhojayed brāhmaṇān brahmavido yonigotramantrāntevāsyasaṃbandhān | guṇahānyāṃ tu pareṣāṃ samuditaḥ sodaryo ’pi bhojayitavyaḥ | etenāntevāsino ’pi vyākhyātāḥ ||
‘na bhojayed' ityanuvṛttau — gautamaḥ [15, 19-21] |
śiṣyāṃś caike sagotrāṃś ca | bhojayed ūrdhvaṃ tribhyaḥ | guṇavantam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11,18] |
śiṣyānapi guṇavato bhojayet ||
[73] mahābhārate [anu, 61, 24-25, 30-31] |
cīrṇavratā guṇair yuktā bhaveyur ye ’pi karṣakāḥ |
sāvitrījñāḥ kriyāvantas te rājan ketanakṣamāḥ ||
kṣātradharmiṇam apy ājau ketayet kulajaṃ dvijam |
na tv evaṃ vaṇijaṃ tāta śrāddheṣu parikalpayet ||
avratī kitavaḥ stenaḥ prāṇivikrayiko ’pi vā |
paścāc cet pītavān somaṃ so ’pi ketanam arhati ||
arjayitvā dhanaṃ pūrṇaṃ dāruṇaiḥ kṛṣikarmabhiḥ |
bhavet sarvātithiḥ paścāt sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
[74]
|| 9|| ||
|| atha varjyāḥ ||
tatra manuḥ [3, 138 - 141] |
na śrādve bhojayen mitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo ’sya saṃgrahaḥ |
nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt taṃ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam ||
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca ||
tasya pretya phalaṃ nāsti śrāddheṣu ca haviḥṣu ca ||
yaḥ saṃgatāni kurute mohāc chrāddhena mānavaḥ |
sa svargāc cyavate lokāc chrāddhamitro dvijādhamaḥ ||
saṃbhojanī sā ’bhihitā paiśācī dakṣiṇā dvijaiḥ |
ihaivāste tu sā loke gaur andhevaikaveśmani ||
'saṃbhojanī' sahabhujyate yayā |
tathā [3, 144] |
kāmaṃ śrāddhe ’rcayen mitraṃ nābhirūpam api tv arim |
dviṣatā hi havir bhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalam ||
manur yamaś ca prathame [manu, 3,168, 142] |
brāhmaṇo hy anadhīyānas tṛṇāgnir iva śāmyati |
tasmai havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate ||
yatheriṇe bījam uptaṃ na vaptā labhate phalam |
tathā ‘nṛce havir datvā na dātā labhate phalam ||
[75] vasiṣṭhaḥ [3,12] |
vidvadbhojyāny avidvāṃso yeṣu śrāddhāni bhuñjate |
tāny anāvṛṣṭim ṛcchanti mahad vā jāyate bhayam ||
manu-yama-śātātapāḥ |
yāvato grasate piṇḍān havyakavyeṣv amantravit |
tāvato grasate pretya dīptān śūlarṣṭyayoguḍān ||
hārītaḥ |
api vidyākulair yuktā hīnavṛttā narādhamāḥ |
duṣprāpaṇāt tu dehasya durātmāno hi te smṛtāḥ ||
kṣaudreṣu niratā nityaṃ yātudhānāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
yad evaṃ bahubhiḥ pāpaiḥ sadā yanti narādhamāḥ ||
pakṣimīnamṛgaghnā ye sarpakacchapaghātinaḥ |
nānājantuvadhe saktāḥ proktā durbrāhmaṇā hi te ||
tathā |
śūdrapreṣyo bhṛto rājñā vṛṣalo grāmayājakaḥ |
vadhabandhopajīvī ca ṣaḍ ete brahmabāndhavāḥ ||
'kṣaudraṃ', kṣudrakarma, prāṇivadhādi | 'dehasya' manuṣyaśarīrasya | 'brahmabāndhavāḥ’ dvijādhamāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
yasya vedaś ca vedī ca vicchidyete tripauruṣam |
sa vai durbrāhmaṇo nāma yaś caiva vṛṣalīpatiḥ ||
[76] uśanā |
yathārthahetoḥ patitān ādiṣṭān manur abravīt |
vedavikrayiṇo hy ete naiva śrāddheṣu bhojayet ||
yathārtham ādiṣṭān' adhyāpanam ity arthaḥ ||
tathā |
asamānayājakāś ca śrutivikrayakāś ca ye |
anyapūrvaprajātāś ca patitān manur abravīt ||
asaṃskṛtādhyāpakāś ca bhṛtakādhyāpakāś ca ye |
tamo viśanti te ghoram iti vaivasvato ’bravīt ||
'asaṃskṛto’ ‘trānupanītaḥ | 'āmantrayed' ity anuvṛttau — kātyāyanaḥ |
dvirnagnaśuklaviklidhaśyāvadantaviddhaprajananavyādhitavyaṅgaśvitrikuṣṭhi-kunakhivarjam ||
'dvirnagno' vidharmā | 'śuklaḥ' atigauraḥ | 'viklidhaḥ ' vicarcikādiyutaḥ | 'viddhaprajananaḥ' kṛtaśiśnavikārāviśeṣo dākṣiṇātye prasiddhaḥ ||
gautamaḥ [15, 15 - 19] |
na bhojayet stenaklībapatitanāstikatadvṛttivīrahāgredidhiṣūdidhiṣūpatis trīgrāmayājakājapālotsṛṣṭāgnimadyapakucara[77]kūṭasākṣiprātihāritān | upapatir yasya ca saḥ | kuṇḍāśisomavikayyagāradāhigaradāvakīrṇigaṇapreṣyāgamyāgāmi- hiṃsraparivittiparivettṛparyāhitaparyādhātṛtyaktātmadurbalakunakhiśyāvadanta-paunarbhavakitavājaparājapreṣyaprātirūpikaśūdrāpatinirākṛtikilāsikusīdavaṇik-śilpopajīvijyāvāditratālanṛtyagītaśīlān | pitrā cākāmena vibhaktān | śiṣyāṃś caike sagotrāṃś ca ||
'tadvṛttiḥ' patitārthopajīvī | 'strīgrāmayājakaḥ' strīṇāṃ bahūnāṃ cāviśeṣeṇa yājakaḥ | 'madyapaḥ' surāvyatiriktamadyapātā, surāpasya patitapadenoktatvāt | 'kucaraḥ’ kutsitācāraḥ | 'prātihārikaḥ' pratihāra indrajālam, tena jīvati prātihārikaḥ | 'yasya ca sa' iti, 'upapatir yasya vidyate' yasya vyabhicāriṇī bhāryety arthaḥ | 'ātmatyāgī' sāhasikaḥ | 'durbalo’ ’tra napuṃsakaḥ | ' ajapaḥ' japarahitaḥ | " prātirūpikaḥ' kūṭapaṇyasya vikretā | 'kilāsī" sidhmalaḥ | 'jyāśīlaḥ ' āyudhābhyāsarataḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 222-224] |
rogī hīnātiriktāṅgāḥ kāṇaḥ paunarbhavas tathā |
avakīrṇī kuṇḍagolau kunakhaḥ śyāvadantakaḥ ||
[78]        bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ klībaḥ kanyādūṣy abhiśastakaḥ |
mitradhruk piśunaḥ somavikrayī paranindakaḥ ||
mātāpitror guros tyāgī kuṇḍāśī vṛṣalātmajaḥ |
parapūrvāpatistenakarmaduṣṭāś ca ninditāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
nāśnanti piśune devā nāśnanty anṛtavādini |
bhāryājitasya nāśnanti yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
na kharair upajātasya na rakto ’ntaravāsasaḥ |
dvyaṅgulātītakarṇasya bhuñjate pitaro haviḥ ||
ya etair lakṣaṇair yuktās tāṃs tv apaṅktyān vinirdiśet |
ye tv etair aśubhair doṣair nivṛttās tān niyojayet ||
devalaḥ |
gobhartṛviśvastānnadapravrajitamitraghātakāḥ | mātṛpitṛputradārāgnihotratyāgino yajñopanihantā vṛṣalīpatiḥ somavikrayī vrātyo niṣkriyaś ceti patitāḥ | jāropapati-kuṇḍāśikuṇḍagolakabhartṛdidhiṣūpatimūḍhabhṛtakādhyāpakāyājyayājakabrahma- dharmaduṣṭadravyavikrayikadaryavarṇasaṃbhedakānāryabhraṣṭaśaucādhanya-devalakavārdhuṣikagotrabhitparivittiparivettṛkṛṣṇapṛṣṭhakāṇḍapṛṣṭhādhikṛta-nirākṛtyavakīrṇimlecchāvareṭakaraṇāraṇyāgāradāhinaḥ ṣaḍvidhāḥ klībāś cety upapātakinaḥ 1 | anapatyakūṭasākṣipuṃstvopaghātistrījitasetubhedaka- [79] tālāvacaraṇaraṅgopajīvividharmapāṭhakaprāyaścittanimittadhūrtasāhasika-mṛgayukitavanāstikapiśunaśaṭhavaṇigvandipaunarbhavātmaṃbhariśukti-samudrayāyikṛtyābhicāraśīlamelakaśvayugvaidyabhṛtakakanyādūṣakakṛtaghna-krūrakuhakamitradhrugdattāpavyayisamayabhedakavāgdaṇḍaparuṣakāruka-śilpakahastyārohāśvabandhakāś ceti varjyāḥ pātanīyakāḥ | aṣṭābhiḥ pāparogair abhibhūtā vikalendriyā hīnāṅgādhikāṅgāśc eti paṅktidūṣakāḥ | unmādas tvagdoṣo rājayakṣmā śvāso madhu prameho bhagandaro mahodaramaramarīty aṣṭau pāparogāḥ | jaḍāndhakāṇabadhirakuṇi iti vikalendriyāḥ | ubhayabhedasaṃkledā duṣṭavraṇāḥ | pāpiṣṭhatamāś ceti |
ete pañcavidhāḥ proktā varjanīyā narādhamāḥ |
svasaṃjñālakṣaṇās te syuḥ viśeṣaś cātra vakṣyate ||
ete durbrāhmaṇāḥ sarve kramaśaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ |
karmaṇā yonitaś caiva dehadoṣaiś ca kutsitāḥ ||
eteṣāṃ karmadoṣeṇa patitā ye narādhamāḥ |
yānti te nirayān ghorān tyaktāḥ sadbhir ihaiva ca ||
yonidoṣeṇa ye duṣṭā ye ca doṣaiḥ śarīrajaiḥ |
ihaiva varjanaṃ teṣāṃ bhaved anaparādhinām ||
kṛtaghnaḥ piśunaḥ krūro nāstikaḥ kuhakaḥ śaṭhaḥ |
mitradhruk ceti sarveṣāṃ viśeṣān nirayālayāḥ ||
[80]                sarve punar abhojyāḥ syur adānārhāś ca karmasu |
brahmabhāvān nirastāś ca pāpadoṣavaśānugāḥ ||
'tālāvacaraṇaḥ' tālavādanajīvī | 'vidharmapāṭhakaḥ ' [śrutismṛtyavihitānāṃ dharmāṇām adhyetā ] puṣkasaḥ | 'melakaḥ " mṛgavadhajīvī | 'śvayuk' śvakrīḍī | 'dattāpavyayī' dattasyāsadvyayakartā | 'vāgdaṇḍaparuṣaḥ' vāgdaṇḍābhyām ugraḥ | 'aśvabandhaḥ' vikrayārthaṃ yo ’śvān badhnāti | 'kuṇiḥ' pāṇivikalaḥ | anye ca ' rājñaḥ purohitaprabhṛtayaḥ' smṛtikārair eva niruktā agre pramṛśyanta iti vakṣyāmaḥ ||
iti varjyāḥ ||
[81]
|| 10 ||
|| athāpāṅkteyāḥ ||
tatra yamaḥ |
apāṅkteyā dvijāś cānye tān me nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
yebhyo dattaṃ na devānāṃ na pitṝṇāṃ ca karmakṛt ||
kāṇāḥ kuṇḍāś ca paṇḍāś ca kṛtaghnā gurutalpagāḥ |
brahmaghnāś ca surāpāś ca stenā goghnāś cikitsakāḥ ||
rāṣṭrakāmās tathonmattāḥ paśuvikrayiṇaś ca ye |
mānakūṭās tulākūṭāḥ śilpino grāmayājakāḥ ||
vṛṣalībhiḥ prapītāś ca śreṇīrājanyayājakāḥ |
rājabhṛtyāndhabadhiramūkakhalvāṭapaṅgavaḥ ||
kalopajīvinaś caiva brahmavikrayiṇas tathā |
[82]        daṇḍadhvajāś ca ye viprā grāmakṛtyakarāś ca ye ||
agāradāhinaś caiva garadā vanadāhakāḥ |
kuṇḍāśino devalakāḥ paradārābhimarṣakāḥ ||
śyāvadantāḥ kunakhinaḥ kuṣṭhinaḥ śilpinaś ca ye |
vaṇijo madhuhantāro hastyaśvadamakā dvijāḥ ||
kanyānāṃ dūṣakāś caiva brāhmaṇānāṃ ca dūṣakāḥ |
sūcakāḥ poṣakāś caiva kitavāś ca kuśīlavāḥ ||
samayānāṃ ca bhettāraḥ pradāne ye ca bādhakāḥ |
ajāvikā māhiṣikāḥ sarvavikrayiṇaś ca ye |
vaiṣṇavīṣu ca ye saktā alājādāhinas tathā |
dhanuṣkartā dyūtavṛttir mitradhruk śaṭha eva ca ||
iṣukartā tathā varjyo yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ |
pāṇḍurogī gaṇḍamālī yakṣmī ca bhrāmarī tathā ||
piśunaḥ kūṭasākṣī ca dīrgharogī vṛthāśramī |
pravrajyopanivṛttaś ca vṛthāpravrajitaś ca yaḥ ||
[83]        yaś ca pratrajitāj jātaḥ 'pravrajyāvasitaś ca yaḥ |
tāv ubhau brahmacāṇḍālāv āha vaivasvato yamaḥ ||
rājñaḥ preṣyakaro yaś ca grāmasya nagarasya ca |
samudrayāyī vāntāśī keśavikrayiṇaś ca ye ||
avakīrṇī śarīraghno gurughnaḥ pitṛdūṣakaḥ |
govikrayī ca durvālaḥ pūgānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ ||
madyapaś ca kadaryaśc a saha pitrā vivādakaḥ |
dāṇḍiko bandhakībhartā tyaktātmā dāradūṣakaḥ ||
sadbhiś ca ninditācāraḥ svakarmaparivarjitaḥ |
parivittiḥ parivettā bhṛtyācāryo nirākṛtiḥ ||
śūdrācāryaḥ sutācāryaḥ śūdraśiṣyaś ca nāstikaḥ |
duṣṭaś ca dārakācāryo mānakṛt tailikas tathā ||
caurā vārdhuṣikā duṣṭāḥ parasvānāṃ ca nāśakāḥ |
caturāśramabāhyāś ca sarve te paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
ity etair lakṣaṇair yuktāṃs tān dvijān na niyojayet |
'paṇḍo' ‘tra ṣaṇḍaḥ | 'cikitsakaḥ' cikitsājīvī | 'rāṣṭrakāmāḥ’ rāṣṭrādhipatyakāmāḥ | 'mānakūṭāḥ' kūṭamānavyavahāriṇaḥ [84] | 'śreṇī' sajātīyasamūhaḥ | 'khalvāṭaḥ' khalatiḥ | 'daṇḍadhvajaḥ' sāhasadaṇḍādiṣu rājñā kṛtadaṇḍacihnaḥ | 'poṣakāḥ' pakṣiṇām iti śeṣaḥ | 'vaiṣṇavī' śūdrā | 'alājādāhinaḥ' lājānāṃ dāho vivāhas tadrahitāḥ, te ca kṛtasamāvartanā boddhavyāḥ | 'śaṭho' māyāvī | 'bhrāmarī' apasmārī | 'durvālo' ‘tra lohitakeśaḥ, khalvāṭagaṇḍayoḥ pṛthagupādānāt | 'pūgaḥ ' saṅghaḥ | 'dārakāḥ' anupanītāḥ | 'tailikaḥ' tailavikrayopajīvī |
manuḥ [3, 150 167] |
ye stenāḥ patitāḥ klībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
tān havyakavyayor viprān anarhān manur abravīt ||
jaṭilaṃ cānadhīyānaṃ durvālaṃ kitavaṃ tathā |
yājayanti ca ye pūgāṃs tāṃśca śrāddhe na bhojayet ||
cikitsakān devalakān māṃsavikrayiṇas tathā |
vipaṇena ca jīvanto varjyāḥ syur havyakavyayoḥ ||
preṣyo grāmasya rājñaś ca kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ |
pratiroddhā guroś caiva tyaktāgnir vārdhuṣis tathā ||
yakṣmī ca paśupālaś ca parivettā nirākṛtiḥ |
brahmadviṭ parivittiś ca gaṇābhyantara eva ca ||
[85]        kuśīlavo ‘vakīrṇī ca vṛṣalīpatir eva ca |
paunarbhavaś ca kāṇaś ca yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitaś ca yaḥ |
śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva vāgduṣṭaḥ kuṇḍagolakau ||
akāraṇāt parityaktā mātāpitror guros tathā |
brāhmair yaunaiś ca saṃbandhaiḥ saṃyogaṃ patitair gataḥ ||
agāradāhī garadaḥ kuṇḍāśī somavikrayī |
samudrayāyī vandī ca tailikaḥ kūṭakārakaḥ ||
pitrā vivadamānaś ca kekaro madyapas tathā |
pāparogy abhiśastaś ca dāmbhiko rasavikrayī ||
dhanuḥśarāṇāṃ kartā ca yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ ||
[86]        mitradhrug dyūtavṛttiś ca putrācāryas tathaiva ca |
bhrāmarī gaṇḍamālī ca śvitry atho piśunas tathā ||
unmatto ’ndhaś ca varjyāḥ syur vedanindaka eva ca |
hastigo’śvoṣṭradamako nakṣatrair yaś ca jīvati ||
pakṣiṇāṃ poṣako yaś ca yuddhācāryas tathaiva ca |
srotasāṃ bhedakaś caiva teṣāṃ cāvaraṇe rataḥ ||
gṛhasaṃveśako dūto vṛkṣāropaka eva ca |
śvakrīḍī śyenajīvī ca kanyādūṣaka eva ca ||
hiṃsro vṛṣalaputraś ca gaṇānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ |
ācārahīnaḥ klībaś ca nityaṃ yācanakas tathā ||
kṛṣijīvī ślīpadī ca sadbhir nindita eva ca |
aurabhriko māhiṣikaḥ parapūrvāpatis tathā ||
pretaniryātakaś caiva varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
ete vigarhitācārā apāṅkteyā narādhamāḥ ||
dvijātipravaro vidvān ubhayatra vivarjayet ||
'jaṭilo' brahmacārī | atra 'anadhīyānaḥ' upanītamātro ’nādiṣṭavedavrataḥ | yatra 'brahmacārī' 'sahasrada' ityatra [87] paṅktipāvanatvam uktaṃ, tad adhyayanayuktasya boddhavyam | 'durvālaḥ' khalatiḥ, na tu duścarmā, tasya padenaiva pṛthagupādānāt | 'pratiroddhā' guror āsedhakartā | 'gaṇābhyantaraḥ' gaṇasya sāmānyapuruṣaḥ | 'vṛkṣāropakagṛhasaṃveśakadūtā' atra bhṛtijīvino boddhavyāḥ ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
brāhmaṇā ye vikarmasthā baiḍālavṛtikās tathā |
hīnāṅgā cādhikāṅgāś ca vijñeyā paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
kunakhī śyāvadantaś ca duścarmā vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
kuṣṭhī ca rājayakṣmī ca brāhmaṇāḥ paktidūṣakāḥ ||
gurūṇāṃ pratikūlāś ca vedāgnyutsādinaś ca ye |
gurūṇāṃ tyāginaś caiva brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
anadhyāyeṣv adhīyānāḥ śaucācāravivarjitāḥ |
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
amṛtavādī taskaro rājabhṛtyo vṛkṣāropakavṛttir garado ‘gnidaḥ śūdropādhyāyo vandī ghāṇṭiko devalako purohito [88] nakṣatrādeśavṛttir brahmapuruṣo madyapo brahmavikrayī kitavaḥ kūṭakārako mānuṣapaśuvikrayiṇaś cety apāṅkteyāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
cikitsakṣavṛṣalapreṣyakārukakitavaśvakrīḍakaśakunijīvanamīnaghātakaceṣṭita-puṃścalavārdhuṣikāhituṇḍikapratyavasitabhṛtakādhyāpakatailikasūcaka-niyāmakakuśīlavādīn daive pitrye ca varjayet | nāstikaklībakadarya-paurandhrikākrīḍino ’nṛtavādino japahomasandhyāśaucavarjitāḥ yaunasrauva-maukhasaṃkarasaṃkīrṇāś cāpuṇyā brāhmaṇavṛttiplavavadhabandhopajīvi-vṛṣalīpatiśūdragrāmayājakataskarāgāradāhigaradasomavikrayigāyananartaka-dhanuḥśarayojakāḥ kuṇḍagolakau narāśaṃsakāpilakadevalaghāṇṭika-nakṣatrajīvimahodadhigāmidīrgharogimahāpathikāḥ patitāḥ patitapurohitāś cānivartamānāḥ paṅktidūṣakā bhavanti ||
'vṛṣalapreṣyaḥ' śūdrājñākārī | 'śakunijīvanaḥ’ śyenajīvī | 'mīnaghātakaceṣṭitaḥ' — 'mīnaghātako' bakaḥ, tasyeva ceṣṭitaṃ vṛttir yasya sa tathā, bakavṛttir ity arthaḥ | 'āhituṇḍikaḥ ' sarpakrīḍājīvī | 'niyāmakaḥ' potavāhakaḥ, bhṛtim ādāya yaḥ parān niyacchati vā | 'kuśīlavaḥ' cāraṇaḥ | 'ākrīḍī' satataṃ [89] krīḍanaśīlaḥ | 'anṛtavādī' anṛtabhāṣaṇaśīlaḥ | 'narāśaṃsaḥ' manuṣyastutiśīlaḥ | 'dhanuḥśarayojakaḥ' dhanuḥkartā śarakartā ca | 'kapilaḥ' kutsitakapilaḥ, atikapilamūrtir iti yāvat | 'dīrghamahāpathikaḥ' dīrghādhvanā maraṇe kṛtodyamaḥ | 'anivartamānāḥ' tatraivābhiratāḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [ 82, 3 - 30]
hīnādhikāṅgān vivarjayet | vikarmasthāṃś ca | baiḍālavratikān | vṛthāliṅginaḥ | nakṣatrajīvinaḥ | devalakān | cikitsakān | anūḍhāputrān | tatputrāṃś ca | bahuyājinaḥ | grāmayājinaḥ | śūdrayājinaḥ | [ayājyayājinaḥ ||] vrātyān | anṛtayājanaḥ | parvakārān | sūcakān | [bhṛtakādhyāpakān |] bhṛtakādhyāpitān | śūdrānnapuṣṭān | patitasaṃsargān | anadhīyānān | saṃdhyopāsanabhraṣṭān | rājasevakān | nagnān | pitrā vivadamānān | pitṛmātṛgurvagnisvādhyāyatyāginaś ceti ||
brāhmaṇāpasadā hy ete kathitāḥ paktidūṣakāḥ |
etān vivarjayed yatnāc chrāddhakarmaṇi paṇḍitaḥ ||
[90] ‘parvakāraḥ' iṣukāraḥ | 'ugro' ’tra dṛṣṭāntaḥkaraṇakopaḥ |
āpastambaḥ [2,17,21] |
śvitriḥ śipiviṣṭaḥ paratalpagāmy āyudhīyaputraś śūdrotpano brāhmaṇyām ity ete bhuñjānāḥ paṅktidūṣaṇāḥ [ bhavanti ||]
'śvitrī' duścarmā | 'āyudhīyaputraḥ ' āyudhajīvinaḥ putraḥ | 'śudrotpanno brāhmaṇyām’ asamavarṇadāraparigrahe brāhmaṇyāṃ putram anutpādya śūdrāyām utpāditaputra iti kapardī ||
sumantuḥ |
taskarakitavājapālagaṇikāśudrapreṣyā[gamyāgāmi]parivettṛparivittiparyāhita-[paryādhātṛ]paunarbhavāndhabadhiracāraṇaklībāvakīrṇivārdhuṣikagaradāyika-kūṭasākṣināstikavṛṣalīpatyahutādotsṛṣṭāgnisomavikrayi[vikretṛpaustikakathaka]- kuṇḍāśi[kuṇḍagolaka]yantrakārakāṇḍapṛṣṭhaduścarmaṣaṇḍa[caṇḍa]-[91]-viddhaśiśnadevalakaṇṭhārūḍha[ṣaṇḍārūḍa]patitaprāyotthitakunakhikilāśi- śyāvadantavaṇikśilpivāditranṛtyatālo[nṛtyagītavādyo]pajīvimūlyasāṃvatsarika- mahāpathikāśmakuṭṭakahīnāṅgātiriktāṅgavirāgavāsasaś cāpāṅkteyāḥ ||
preṣyapadasya gaṇādipadaiḥ pratyekam abhisaṃbandhaḥ | 'yantrakārakaḥ' ikṣutilayantrakārī | 'prāyotthitaḥ ' anaśanavratabhraṣṭaḥ | 'mūlyasāṃvatsarikaḥ’ mūlyena yo nakṣatrādi kathayati, jyautiṣaka ity arthaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
