[kunakhikilāśiśyāvadantāḥ pitrā vivadamānaś ca yaś ca strījitaḥ] kuṣṭhī klībaḥ kitavo durvālo duvarmā surāgarbho viddhaprajananaḥ patitaḥ piśunaḥ somavikrayī yajñavedavikrayī vāṇijako grāmayājī mithyābhiśasto vṛṣalyām abhijātaḥ parivittiḥ parivettā didhiṣūpatiḥ punarbhūputraḥ golakuṇḍājāvikāś cauraḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ sevakaś cety apāṅkteyāḥ | śrāddhe naitān bhojayet | na yajñakarmaṇi ||
'surāgarbhaḥ' surā garbhe yasmin, yasmin garbhasthe mātrā surā pītā |
[92] uśanā |
apāṅkteyā bhavanti | tad yathā jaḍabadhirajātāndhaśvitrikuṣṭhikuṇiśyāvadanta-vārdhuṣikadevalakavaṇikśaṭhagaṇābhyantaracikitsakapaunarbhavakāṇa-śilpopajīvipatitasaṃprayoktṛkitavānṛtadāmbhikaparaparibhāvitaprabhṛtayaḥ ||
'kuṇiḥ' vikalapāṇiḥ | 'paraparibhāvitaḥ’ paratvena śatrutvena paribhāvitaḥ parīkṣita ity arthaḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
yas tiṣṭhed vāyubhakṣas tu cāturāśramyabāhyataḥ |
ayatir mokṣavādī ca ubhau tau paṅktidūṣakau ||
ugreṇa tapasā yuktaś citravādī bahuśrutaḥ |
anāśramī tapaḥsteye taṃ vipraṃ na nimantrayet ||
aupapattis tathā śākyo nāstiko vedanindakaḥ |
dhyāninaṃ ye ca nindati sarve te paktidūṣakāḥ ||
vṛthāmuṇḍāś ca jaṭilāḥ sarve kārpaṭikās tathā |
nirghṛṇān bhinnavṛttāṃś ca sarvabhakṣyāṃś ca varjayet ||
[93]        prāha vedān vedabhūto vedān yaś copajīvati |
ubhau tau nārhataḥ śrāddhaṃ putrikāpatir eva ca ||
vṛthā dārāṃś ca yo gacched yājayec ca vṛthādhvaraiḥ |
nārhatas tāv api śrāddhaṃ dvijo yaś caiva nāstikaḥ ||
ātmārthaṃ yaḥ paced annaṃ na devātithikāraṇāt |
nārhaty asāv api śrāddhaṃ patito brahmarākṣasaḥ ||
striyo raktāmbarā yeṣāṃ parivādaratāś ca ye |
arthakāmaratā ye ca na tāñ śrāddheṣu bhojayet ||
santi vedavirodhena kecid vijñānamāninaḥ |
ayajñapatayo nāma te bhuñjanti yathā rajaḥ ||
muṇḍān jaṭilakāṣāyāñ śrāddhe yatnena varjayet |
śikhibhyo dhāturaktebhyas tridaṇḍibhyaḥ pradāpayet ||
'ayatiḥ' asaṃyataḥ | 'aupapattiḥ' upapattimātravyavahārī, haituka ity arthaḥ | 'śākyaḥ' atra śākyadharmābhirataḥ | 'vṛthāmuṇḍāḥ' niṣiddhamuṇḍanakartāraḥ | 'vṛthājavilāḥ' śāstrānanumata-jaṭādhāriṇaḥ | 'kāpaṭikāḥ' karpaṭavyavahāriṇaḥ | 'nirghṛṇāḥ' nirdayāḥ | 'bhinnavṛttāḥ' tyaktācārāḥ | 'prāha vedān vedabhṛta' iti, yo vedādhyayanārthaṃ bhṛtaḥ san vedān prāha ity arthaḥ | 'putrikāpatiḥ' putrikātvena kalpitāyāḥ patiḥ | 'vṛthā dārāṃś ca yo gacchet' strīratiḥ putrārthitā- vyatirekeṇa | punaḥ nāstikagrahaṇaṃ nindātiśayārtham | 'striyo raktāmbarāḥ' rajasvalāḥ | 'arthakāmaratāḥ' dharmamokṣavyatirekeṇārthakāmaratāḥ | 'ayajñapatayaḥ' ity atra yajñaśabdo varṇāśramaprayuktakarmopalakṣaṇārthaḥ | 'muṇḍāḥ' atra lūnaśikhāḥ | 'jaṭilasya' punarvacanaṃ nindātiśayārtham |
mahābhārate [ anu. pa., 61,18,20,21] |
agraṇīr yaḥ kṛtaḥ pūrvaṃ varṇāvaraparigrahaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ sarvavidyo ’pi rājan nārhati ketanam ||
aparijñātapūrvāś ca gaṇapūrvāś ca bhārata |
putrikāputrapūrvāś ca śrāddhe nārhanti ketanam ||
ṛṇakartā ca yo rājan yaś ca vārdhuṣiko dvijaḥ |
prāṇavikrayavṛttiś ca rājan nārhati ketanam ||
'agraṇīḥ' iti, yaḥ pūrvaṃ pradhānīkṛtaḥ śiṣṭaiḥ pañcān nikṛṣṭavarṇaparigrahasaṃsargaṃ karoti, saḥ śrāddhe varjanīya ity arthaḥ | 'ketanam’ āmantraṇam | 'aparijñātapūrvāḥ' ajñātāḥ pūrve pitrādayo yeṣāṃ te tathā | 'gaṇapūrvāḥ' saṅghe pradhānībhūtāḥ | 'putrikāputrapūrvāḥ' putrikāputrajāḥ | ' ṛṇakartā’ atra ṛṇaṃ gṛhītvā yo na dhanikāya dātum icchati | 'prāṇavikrayavṛttiḥ' prāṇasaṃśayāpādakakarmajīvī ||
matsyapurāṇe |
kṛtaghnān nāstikāṃs tadvan mlecchadeśanivāsinaḥ |
triśakūn barbarān āndhrān ṭaṅkadraviḍakoṅkaṇān ||
karṇāṭakāṃs tathā kīrān kaliṅgāṃś ca vivarjayet ||
[95] brahmapurāṇe |
bhoktaṃ śrāddheṣu nārhanti dainyopahatacetasaḥ |
ṣaṇḍo mūkaś ca kunakhī khalvāṭo dantarogavān ||
śyāvadantaḥ pūtināsaś chinnāṅgaś cādhikāṅguliḥ |
galarogī gaṇḍamālī sphuṭitāṅgaś ca sajvaraḥ ||
khañjatūvaramuṇḍāś ca tathānye dīrgharogiṇaḥ |
vṛthājātā viraktāś ca vyādhibhāraprapīḍitāḥ ||
'tūvaraḥ' śmaśrukāle ’pi śmaśrurahitaḥ | 'vṛthājātāḥ ' dharmaparāṅmukhāḥ | 'viraktāḥ' atra yativyatiriktāḥ |
atha manuḥ [3,170,180–182] |
avatair yad dvijairb huktaṃ parivettrādibhis tathā |
apāṅkteyair yad anyaiś ca tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhojayet ||
somavikrayiṇe viṣṭhā bhiṣaje pūyaśoṇitam |
apratiṣṭhaṃ vārdhuṣike naṣṭaṃ devalake bhavet ||
yat tu vāṇijake dattaṃ neha nāmutra tad bhavet |
bhasmanīva hutaṃ havyaṃ tathā paunarbhave dvije ||
itareṣu tv apāṅktyeṣu yathoddiṣṭeṣv asādhuṣu |
medo’sṛṅmāṃsamajjāsthi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
ye vyapetā svakarmabhyaḥ stenās te parikīrtitāḥ |
kavyaṃ dadāti yas tebhyas tasya tat pretya naśyati ||
[96]        jñānapūrvaṃ tu ye tebhyaḥ prayacchanty alpamedhasaḥ |
purīṣaṃ bhuñjate sarvaṃ śataṃ varṣāṇi suvrata ||
tathā |
madyapāya na dātavyaṃ daive pitrye tathā haviḥ |
tasmai naśyati tad dattaṃ yathā bhasmani vai hutam ||
dātuś ca chidyate hasto bhoktur jihnātha bhidyate |
jvalitān grasate grāsān śrāddhaṃ tad anṛtaṃ bhavet ||
manuḥ [3,176] |
apāṅktyo yāvataḥ pāṅktyān bhuñjānān anupaśyati |
tāvatāṃ na phalaṃ tatra dātā prāpnoti bāliśaḥ ||
tathā [3,178, 177] |
yāvataḥ saṃspṛśed aṅgair brāhmaṇān śūdrayājakaḥ |
tāvatāṃ na bhaved dātuḥ phalaṃ dānasya paurtikam ||
vīkṣyāndho navateḥ kāṇaḥ ṣaṣṭeḥ śvitrī śatasya tu |
pāparogī sahasrasya dātur nāśayate phalam ||
yamaḥ |
ṣaṣṭiṃ kāṇaḥ śataṃ ṣaṇḍaḥ śvitrī yad anupaśyati |
śataṃ vai pāṇḍurogī ca sahasraṃ patitas tathā ||
[97] vasiṣṭhaḥ [11,20 ] |
atra cen mantrasaṃyuktaḥ śarīraiḥ paṅktidūṣakaiḥ |
adūṣyaṃ taṃ yamaḥ prāha paṅktipāvana eva saḥ ||
'śarīraiḥ' śarīraprabhavaiḥ kāṇatvādibhir na tu svayam utpādyair api stenatvādibhiḥ ||
atha devalaḥ |
paradārābhigo mohāt puruṣo jāra ucyate |
sa evopapatirj ñeyo yaḥ sadā saṃviśed gṛhe ||
jīvato jārajaḥ kuṇḍo mṛte bhartari golakaḥ |
yas tayor annam aśnāti sa kuṇḍāśīti kathyate ||
brahmapurāṇe |
catuḥṣaṣṭipalaiḥ śuddhaiḥ kuṇḍaṃ prasthacatuṣṭayam |
bhavet tad yas tu nigiret sa kuṇḍāśī pataty adhaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
vratī yaḥ striyam abhyeti so ’vakīrṇī nirucyate |
punarbhyās tu patir yo vai sa bhaved didhiṣūpatiḥ ||
devalaḥ |
jyeṣṭhasya bhāryāṃ saṃprāptaḥ sakāmād didhiṣūpatiḥ ||
manuḥ [3,173 ] |
bhrātur mṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo ’nurajyeta kāmataḥ |
[98]        dharmeṇopaniyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ ||
laugakṣiḥ |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyāyām uhyate ’nujā |
sā cāgredidhiṣūr jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣūḥ smṛtā ||
yamaḥ |
mahiṣīty ucyate nārī sā caiva vyabhicāriṇī |
taṃ doṣaṃ kṣamate yas tu sa vai māhiṣikaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
yaś ca duṣṭatapaścaryaḥ sa gūḍho nāma saṃjñayā |
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitaś ca yaḥ ||
tāv ubhau patitau viprau svādhyāyakrayavikrayāt |
yaḥ śūdrān patitāṃś cāpi yājayed arthakāraṇāt ||
yājito vā punas tābhyāṃ brāhmaṇo ’yājyayājakaḥ |
dattānuyogān dravyārthaṃ patitān manur abravīt ||
brahmavikrayiṇo hy ete varjitāḥ savanādiṣu |
iṣṭaṃ pūrtam adhītaṃ vā taptaṃ vāpy ātmanā tapaḥ ||
prayacchanty aparebhyaś ca dhanārthaṃ dharmavikrayī |
gorasānāṃ ca vikretā govikretā ca yo dvijaḥ ||
[99]        guḍalavaṇatilānāṃ vikretā duṣṭavikrayī |
ātmānaṃ dharmakṛtyaṃ ca putradārāṃś ca pīḍayan ||
lobhādyaḥ pracinoty arthān sa kadarya iti sthitiḥ |
nikṛṣṭotkṛṣṭamadhyeṣu yo varṇeṣv anavagrahaḥ ||
ācaraty aparācāraṃ varṇasaṃbhedakas tu saḥ |
anāhitāgniḥ śatagur anījānaḥ sahasraguḥ ||
śūdrājīvopajīvī ca nārhanty arghān dvijātayaḥ |
kūpamātrodake grāme vipraḥ saṃvatsaraṃ vasan ||
śaucācāraparibhraṃśād brāhmaṇyād vipramucyate |
anupāsitasaṃdhyā ye nityam asnātabhojanāḥ ||
bhraṣṭaśaucāḥ patanty ete śūdratulyāś ca dharmataḥ |
ekākī vyasanākrānto dhanya ity ucyate budhaiḥ ||
devakośopajīvī ca nāmnā devalako bhavet ||
‘anavagrahaḥ' tyaktaśāstrapratiniyamaḥ | 'aparācāram' antyavarṇocitācāram ||
yamaḥ |
samarghaṃ paṇyam āhṛtya mahārghe yaḥ prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma yaś ca vṛddhyā prayojayet ||
tathā |
yaś ca nindyāt paraṃ jīvan praśaṃsaty ātmano guṇān |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ ||
'jīvan' jīvanahetor itya rthaḥ |
tathā |
agnīn utsādayed yas tu vīrahā sa nirucyate ||
[100] devalaḥ |
yaḥ sagotrāṃ samārūḍho brāhmaṇo gotrabhid bhavet |
vaiśyāpatiḥ kṛṣṇapṛṣṭhaḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭho naro bhavet ||
kṣatriyavaiśyavṛttyanantaraṃ nāradaḥ [4, 60] |
tasyām eva ca yo vṛttau brāhmaṇo ramate rasāt |
kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaś cyuto mārgāt so ’pāṅkteyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
śūdrāputrāḥ svayaṃdattā ye caite krītakāḥ sutāḥ |
sarve te maitriṇā proktāḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhā na saṃśayaḥ ||
kāṇḍameṣāṃ kulasyākhyā parvāṇy āhur yugāni tu |
yas tu taṃ nyāyato rakṣet sa kulīna iti smṛtaḥ ||
svakulaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā yo vai parakulaṃ vrajet |
tena duścaritenāsau kāṇḍapṛṣṭho na saṃśayaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
āpadgato hy upanato yaś ca syād vaiṣṇavīsutah |
sarve te manunā proktāḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhās trayaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
kulaṃ kāṇḍam iti khyātaṃ tasmāt parvāṇi te jaguḥ |
tatra jyeṣṭhataro yasmāt taṃ vai kāṇḍeti nirdiśet ||
svakulaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā yo vai parakulaṃ vrajet |
tena duścaritenāsau kāṇḍapṛṣṭha iti smṛtaḥ ||
[101] devalaḥ |
rājapreṣyakaro nityaṃ brāhmaṇo vikṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ |
adhītavismṛte vede bhaved vipro nirākṛtiḥ ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
yaḥ svādhyāyāgnimāṃs tasmād devādīnnaibhir iṣṭavān |
nirākartāmarādīnāṃ sa vijñeyo nirākṛtiḥ ||
‘devādīnnaibhiḥ' iti, avaśyakṛtyadarśapaurṇamāsādiyāgadvāreṇa |
devalaḥ |
gūḍhaliṅgy avakīrṇī syād yaś ca bhagnavratas tathā |
dharmādharmavibhāgajño niviṣṭo vedaśāsane ||
kṛpālajjākṣamādhīmān āryo mleccho viparyaye |
dvitīyasya pitur yo ’nnaṃ bhuktvā pariṇato dvijaḥ ||
avareḍha iti jñeyaḥ śūdradharmeṇa jīvanaḥ |
bhavet kāraṇasaṃjñaś ca sarveṣu vyavahāravān ||
agāradāhī sa jñeyaḥ pretadagdhā hy anekaśaḥ |
sa cāpy agāradāhī syād yo dveṣād veśmadāhakaḥ ||
kṣetrasasyavanānāṃ ca dagdhāro ’raṇyadāhinaḥ |
ṣaṇḍako vātajaḥ ṣaṇḍaḥ ṣaṇḍaḥ klībo napuṃsakaḥ ||
kīlakaś ceti ṣaṭko ’yaṃ ṣaṇḍabhedo vibhāvitaḥ |
teṣāṃ strītulyavākceṣṭaḥ strīdharmā ṣaṇḍako bhavet ||
[102]        pumān bhūtvā svaliṅgāni paścāc chindyāt tathaiva ca |
strī va puṃbhāvam āsthāya puruṣācāravadguṇā ||
vātajo nāma ṣaṇḍaḥ syāt strīṣaṇḍo vāpi nāmataḥ |
asalliṅgo ’pi ṣaṇḍaḥ syāt ṣaṇḍas tu mlānamehanaḥ ||
sa kīlaka iti prokto yaḥ klaibyād ātmanaḥ striyam |
anyena saha saṃyojya paścāt tām eva sevate ||
skandapurāṇe |
īśvara uvāca-
parīkṣya śrāddhaṃ dātavyam ity evaṃ vaidikī śrutiḥ |
parīkṣaṇāc ca rambhoru ārjavaṃ guṇavattaram ||
aparīkṣya tu yo dadyāc chrāddham ārjavam āsthitaḥ |
tasya tuṣyanti pitaro devatāś ca na saṃśayaḥ ||
[103]
|| 11 ||
|| atha nimantraṇaṃ nimantritaniyamāśca ||
tatra devalaḥ |
akrodho nibhṛtaḥ svasthaḥ śraddhāvān atvaraḥ śuciḥ |
samāhitamanās tatra kriyāyām asakṛt sadā ||
[ tathā ]
śvaḥ kartāsmīti niścitya dātā viprān nimantrayet |
nirāmiṣaṃ sakṛd uktvā sarvabhuktajane gṛhe ||
asaṃbhave paredyar vā brāhmaṇāṃs tān nimantrayet |
ajñātīn asamānārṣān ayugmān ātmaśaktitaḥ ||
'nibhṛto’ ‘tra viviktadeśasthitaḥ | 'samāhitamanāḥ' dravyādau | ayam arthaḥ — 'asakṛt' punaḥ punaḥ, śrāddhe tathā bhavitavyaṃ yathā mano nāsaṃyataṃ bhavatīti | 'nirāmiṣaṃ sakṛd bhuktvā' iti pūrvedyuḥ paredyur nimantraṇayoḥ sādhāraṇam | 'sarvabhuktajane' sarve bhuktavanto janā yasmin sa tathā | ‘asaṃbhave' kutaścin nibhittāt pūrvedyur nimantraṇāsaṃpattau | 'ārṣāḥ ' pravarāḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitau ||
janmaśarīralakṣaṇaguṇopetān brāhmaṇān āmantrayet | śvo ‘dyeti vā śrāddham ācariṣya iti ||
[104] mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe [31,30,33,34] |
nimantrayeta pūrvedyuḥ pūrvoktān dvijasattamān |
aprāptau taddine vāpi hitvā yoṣitprasaṅginam ||
bhikṣārtham āgatān vāpi kāle saṃyamino yatīn |
bhojayet praṇipātādyaiḥ prasādya yatamānasaḥ ||
‘aprāptiḥ' atrāsaṃbhavaḥ ||
manuḥ [3, 187 ] |
pūrvedyr aparedyar vā śrāddhakarmaṇy upasthite |
nimantrayeta tryavarān samyag viprān yathoditān ||
uśanā |
tatra gomayodakair bhūmimārjanaṃ bhāṇḍaśaucaṃ kṛtvā śvaḥ kartāsmīti brāhmaṇān nimantrayec caturavarān ||
'caturavarān' dvau daive, ekaikaṃ pitṛmātāmahavargayoḥ ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
vastraśaucādi kartavyaṃ śvaḥ kartāsmīti jānatā |
sthānopalepanaṃ bhūmiṃ kṛtvā vipraṃ nimantrayet ||
dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca visṛjed brahmacārī śucir bhavet |
'bhūmiṃ kṛtvā' dakṣiṇāpravaṇādiguṇopetām | 'visṛjet ' brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt ||
[105] āpastambaḥ [2,17,11-17] |
pūrvedyur nivedanam | apareyur dvitīyam | tṛtīyam āmantraṇam | triḥprāyam eke śrāddham upadiśanti | yathā prathamam evaṃ dvitīyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca ||
sarveṣv eva vipreṣu nivedanaṃ śvo mayā śrāddhaṃ kartavyam, tatra bhavanto nimantraṇīyā iti | brāhmaṇacodanam apapareyuḥ prātaḥkāle | bhojanakāle cāmantraṇam ||
matsyapurāṇe |
dakṣiṇaṃ jānum ālabhya tvaṃ mayātra nimantritaḥ |
evaṃ nimantrya niyamāñ śrāvayet paitṛkān budhaḥ ||
tān evāha |
akrodhanaiḥ śaucaparaiḥ satataṃ brahmacāribhiḥ |
bhavitavyaṃ bhavadbhiś ca mayātra śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
yamaḥ |
sādhubhiḥ sannimantrayet ||
tasya te pitaraḥ śrutvā śrāddhakālam upasthitam |
anyonyaṃ manasā dhyātvā saṃpatanti manojavāḥ ||
tathā |
brāhmaṇaiś ca sahāśnanti pitaro hy antarikṣagāḥ |
vāyubhūtā na dṛśyante bhuktvā yānti parāṃ gatim ||
‘sādhubhiḥ saṃnimantrayed’ iti svayaṃ nimantraṇāsaṃbhave | 'bhavitavyaṃ bhavadbhiś ca mayātra śrāddhakarmaṇi' [106] ityabhidhānena liṅgena svayaṃ nimantraṇasya mukhyatvāt | yama-śātātapau |
abhojyaṃ brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ vṛṣalena nimantritam |
tathaiva vṛṣalasyānnaṃ brāhmaṇena nimantritam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 225] |
nimantrayeta pūrvedyur brāhmaṇān ātmavān śuciḥ |
taiś cāpi saṃyatair bhāvyaṃ manovākkāyakarmabhiḥ ||
śrāddhādhikāre āpastambaḥ [2, 17, 24] |
ārabdhe cābhojanam ā samāpanāt ||
|| nimantritaniyamāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
āmantritās tu ye viprāḥ śrāddhakāla upasthite |
vaseyur niyatāhārā brahmacaryaparāyaṇāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
nimantrito dvijaḥ pitrye niyatātmā bhavet sadā |
na ca chandāṃsy adhīyīta yasya śrāddhaṃ ca tad bhavet ||
hārītaḥ |
pūrvedyur mantritān viprān pitaraḥ saṃviśanti vai |
yajamānaṃ ca tāṃ rātriṃ vaseyur niyatās tataḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ [śrāddhakalpa, 1, 23-25 ] |
tad ahaḥ śucir akrodhano tvarito ’pramattaḥ satyavādī syād adhvamaithunaśramasvādhyāyān varjayet | āvāhanādi vāgyata upasparśanāt | āmantritāś caivam ||
[107] ‘upasparśanāt’ bhoktṝṇām antyācamanaparyantam |
yamaḥ |
ahiṃsā satyam akrodho dūre cāgamanakriyā |
abhārodvahanaṃ kṣāntiḥ śrāddhasyopādanāvidhiḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ [śrāddhakalpa, 1, 12-13] |
anindyenāmantrito nāpakrāmet | nimantrito nānyad annaṃ pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
yamaḥ, manuś ca prathame [manu, 3, 190]
ketitas tu yathānyāyaṃ brāhmaṇo havyakavyayoḥ |
kathaṃcid apy atikrāman pāpaḥ sūkaratāṃ vrajet ||
brāhmaṇaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtvā devatāpitṛbhiḥ saha |
yad annaṃ samupāśnanti tasmāt tan na vyatikramet ||
ketanaṃ kārayitvā tu yo ’tipātayati dvijaḥ |
brahmahatyām avāpnoti śūdrayonau ca jāyate ||
tathā |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe tv anyasya kurute kṣaṇam |
saṃvatsarakṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ tasya naśyati durmateḥ ||
[108] tathā |
āmantrya brāhmaṇaṃ yas tu yathānyāyaṃ na pūjayet |
atikṛcchrāsu ghorāsu tiryagyoniṣu jāyate ||
‘anyasya kurute kṣaṇam' anyasyāmantraṇaṃ svīkarotīty arthaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
āmantritaś ciraṃ naiva kuryād vipraḥ kadācana |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca dātur anyasya caiva hi ||
cirakārī bhaved drogdhā pacyate narakāgninā |
'cirakārī' vilambakārī |
mahābhārate [anu., 61, 54] |
devaṃ vā yadi vā pitryaṃ yo ’śnīyād brāhmaṇādiṣu ||
hārītaḥ |
deve vā yadi vā pitrye nimantraya brāhmaṇaṃ yadi |
tarpayen na yathānyāyaṃ sa tu tasya phalaṃ haret ||
pramādād vismṛtaṃ jñātvā prasādyainaṃ prayatnataḥ |
tarpayitvā viśeṣeṇa sarvaṃ tatphalam āpnuyāt ||
devalaḥ |
śrāddhe nimantrito vipro vṛṣalīṃ yadi gacchati |
dātuḥ pāpaṃ samagraṃ tat kṣaṇena pratipadyate ||
[109]        pūrvaṃ nimantrito ’nyena kuryād anyaparigraham |
bhuktāhāreṇa vā bhuṅkte sukṛtaṃ tasya naśyati ||
tasmād doṣān parityajya tān etān aparān api |
brahmacārī śucir bhūtvā śrāddhaṃ bhuñjīta śaktimān ||
trīn eva yantrito dātā bhuktāhāreṇa bhojayet |
trīn eva nimantritabrāhmaṇān | 'yantritaḥ' niyataḥ | 'bhuktāhāreṇa' bhojanaṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ ||
atra yamaḥ ādyaślokatraye uśanā ca |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe adhvānaṃ vrajati dvijaḥ |
bhavanti pitaras tasya taṃ māsaṃ pāṃsubhojanāḥ ||
āmantritas tu yaḥ śraddhe bhāram udvahati dvijaḥ |
pitaras tasya taṃ māsaṃ bhavanti svedabhojanāḥ ||
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe hiṃsāṃ vai kurute dvijaḥ |
taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya bhavanti rudhirāśanāḥ ||
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe āyāsaṃ kurute dvijaḥ |
bhavanti pitaras tasya taṃ tu māsam anirvṛtāḥ ||
āmantritastu yaḥ śrāddhe vṛṣalyā saha modate |
bhavanti pitaras tasya taṃ māsaṃ śuklabhojanāḥ ||
[110]        āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe kalahaṃ kurute dvijaḥ |
bhavanti pitaras tasya taṃ māsaṃ malabhojanāḥ ||
tasmān nimantritaḥ śrāddhe niyatātmā bhaved dvijaḥ
akrodhanaḥ śaucaparaḥ satyavādī jitendriyaḥ ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
nimantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe maithunaṃ sevate dvijaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ dattvā ca bhuktvā ca yuktaḥ syān mahatainasā ||
manuḥ [3, 192-201] |
akrodhanāḥ śaucaparāḥ satataṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ |
nyastaśastrā mahābhāgāḥ pitaraḥ pūrvadevatāḥ ||
yasmād utpattir eteṣāṃ sarveṣām apy aśeṣataḥ |
ye ca yair upacaryāḥ syur niyamais tān nibodhata ||
manor hiraṇyagarbhasya ye marīcyādayaḥ sutāḥ |
teṣām ṛṣīṇāṃ sarveṣāṃ putrāḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
virāṭsutāḥ somasadaḥ sādhyānāṃ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ |
agniṣvāttāś ca devānāṃ mārīcā lokaviśrutāḥ ||
daityadānavayakṣāṇāṃ gandharvoragarakṣasām |
suparṇakinnarāṇāṃ ca smṛtā barhiṣado ’trijāḥ ||
somapā nāma viprāṇāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ havirbhujaḥ |
veśyānām ājyapā nāma śūdrāṇāṃ tu sukālinaḥ ||
[111]        somapāś ca kaveḥ putrā haviṣmanto ’ṅgiraḥsutāḥ |
pulastyasyājyapā nāma vasiṣṭhasya sukālinaḥ ||
agnidagdhānagnidagdhān kāvyān barhiṣadas tathā |
agniṣvāttāṃś ca saumyāṃś ca viprāṇām eva nirdiśet ||
ya ete tu gaṇā mukhyāḥ pitṝṇāṃ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣām apīha vijñeyaṃ putrapautram anantakam ||
ṛṣibhyaḥ pitaro jātāḥ pitṛbhyo devamānavāḥ |
devebhyaś ca jagat sarvaṃ carasthāṇvanupūrvaśaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
somo yamo ’ṅgirāś caiva somapāḥ pitaras tathā |
agniṣvāttā barhiṣado hutādaḥ ṣaḍvidho gaṇaḥ ||
candramā ṛtavaś caiva mṛtaṃ yo ’gnir dahaty api |
somopahūtāḥ prāksomā anījānāś ca brāhmaṇāḥ ||
ṣaḍvidho ’py ayaṃ gaṇo 'hutādaḥ’ |
atra nigamaḥ |
ye hatā tatra saṅgrāme pitaras te tridhābhavan |
yad dhutaṃ pitṛyajñeṣu teṣāṃ gacchati tad dhaviḥ ||
[112] asya ca sarvasyāyaṃ tātparyārthaḥ | yathā āvāhanakāle pramītāḥ pitrādayo brāhmaṇādivarṇair agniṣvāttādirūpiṇo dhyeyā iti ||
|| iti nimantraṇaṃ nimantritaniyamāś ca ||
[113]
|| 12 ||
|| prāgāvāhanācchrāddha vidhiḥ ||
agrāvāhanāt pūrvaṃ śrāddhavidhiḥ | tatra 'pūrvedyur brāhmaṇān āmantrayed' ityadhikāre—hārītaḥ |
atha śvobhūte dakṣiṇāṃ diśaṃ gatvā dakṣiṇāpraṇatān samūlān darbhān āharet | aparigṛhītāś cāpaḥ |
[ yamaḥ] |
samūlas tu bhaved darbhaḥ pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
mūlena lokān jayati śakrasya tu mahātmanaḥ ||
gobhilaḥ [1,5,17-19] |
[viśākhāni pratilūnāḥ kuśā] barhiḥ | upamūlalūnāḥ pitṛbhyaḥ | tadalābhe śūkatṛṇaśaraśīryabalbajamutavanalaśuṇṭhavarjaṃ sarvatṛṇāni ||
'śūkatṛṇaṃ' śūkavattaṇam | śaraśīryabalvajāś caṇḍāṃśutṛṇajātiviśeṣās tattaddeśaprasiddhāḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
haritāś ca sapiñjūlāḥ snigdhāḥ puṣṭāḥ samāhitāḥ |
gokarṇamātrās tu kuśāḥ sakṛcchinnāḥ samūlakāḥ |
pitṛtīrthena deyāś ca dūrvāḥ śyāmākam eva ca |
kāśāḥ kuśā balbajāś ca tathānye tīkṣṇaromaśāḥ ||
mauñjaṃ ca śādvalaṃ caiva ṣaḍdarbhāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
śrāddhe varjyāḥ prayatnena hy ulapakṣupabalvajāḥ ||
dūrvāyāḥ śyāmākasya ca kāśādivat kuśābhāve vidhiḥ | 'piñjūlaṃ' pavitram | 'samāhitāḥ' nirdoṣāḥ | 'gokarṇamātrāḥ' prādeśamātrāḥ sāgrā iti yāvat | 'ulapa:' kuśasadṛśas tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
ratnipramāṇāḥ śastā vai pitṛtīrthena saṃskṛtāḥ |
upamūle tathā lūnāḥ prastarārthe kuśottamāḥ ||
tathā |
gundrā śrāddhe kṣīrakuśā balvajā gandhakī tathā |
vīraṇāś colapaś caiva lambā varjyaś ca nityaśaḥ ||
'saṃskṛtāḥ' saṃsṛṣṭāḥ | 'prastaraḥ' atra piṇḍasaṃstaraḥ | 'gundraḥ' bhadramustakaḥ | 'kṣīrakuśaḥ' pratyagrajātaḥ | 'gandhakī' [115] gandhatṛṇāni || 'lambāḥ' uktapramāṇādhikāḥ kuśā eva dīrghāḥ ||
manur-bṛhaspatiś ca prathame |
śuciṃ deśaṃ viviktaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet |
dakṣiṇāpravaṇaṃ caiva prayatnenopapādayet ||
avakāśeṣu cokṣeṣu jalatīrtheṣu caiva hi |
vivikteṣu ca dattena tṛpyanti pitaraḥ sadā ||
'avakāśeṣu cokṣeṣu' manohareṣu deśeṣu | 'vivikteṣu' vijaneṣu vanādiṣv api ||
yamaḥ |
rūkṣaṃ kṛmivṛtaṃ klinnaṃ saṃkīrṇāniṣṭagandhikam |
deśaṃ tv aniṣṭaśabdaṃ ca varjayec chrāddhakarmaṇi ||
dakṣiṇāpravaṇaṃ snigdhaṃ viviktaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam |
śuciṃ deśaṃ parīkṣyātha gomayenopalepayet ||
agāreṣu vivikteṣu tīrtheṣu ca nadīṣu ca |
vivikteṣu ca deśeṣu tuṣyanti pitaraḥ sadā ||
pārakye bhūmibhāge tu pitṝṇāṃ nirvapet tu yaḥ |
tadbhūmisvāmipitṛbhiḥ śrāddhakarma vihanyate ||
tasmāc chrāddhāni deyāni puṇyeṣv āyataneṣu ca |
nadītīreṣu tīrtheṣu svabhūmau ca prayatnataḥ ||
[116]        upahvare nitambeṣu tathā parvatasānuṣu |
gomayenopalipteṣu vivikteṣu gṛheṣu ca ||
'klinnam' paṅkilam | ‘upahvaram' atra parvatāntakaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
parakīyagṛhe yas tu svān pitṝn tarpayej jaḍaḥ |
tadbhūmisvāminas tasya haranti pitaro balāt ||
agrabhāgaṃ tatas tebhyo dadyān mūlyaṃ ca jīvatām |
'agrabhāgaḥ' atra śrāddhīyadravyasya | 'tebhyaḥ' tadbhūmisvāmipitṛbhyaḥ |
yamaḥ |
aṭavyaḥ parvatāḥ puṇyā nadyas tīrthāni yāni ca |
sarvāṇy asvāmikāny āhur na hi teṣu parigrahaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
jaghāna dānavau viṣṇuḥ pūrvaṃ tu madhukaiṭabhau |
vṛtraṃ mahendraś ca tataḥ pṛthvī tanmedasāvṛtā ||
ato ’rthe medinī sā tu loke vijñāyate janaiḥ |
tasmāc chrāddhe pañcagavyair lepyā śodhyā tatholmukaiḥ ||
gauramṛttikā channā prakīrṇatilasarṣapā
devalaḥ |
tathaiva yantrito dātā snātaḥ prātaḥ sahāmbaraḥ |
ārabheta navaiḥ pātrair annārambhaṃ ca bāndhavaiḥ ||
[117]        tilāṃś ca vikaret tatra sarvato bandhayed ajān |
asuropahataṃ sarvaṃ tilaiḥ śuddhyaty ajena ca ||
tato ’nnaṃ bahusaṃskāraṃ naikavyañjanabhakṣyavat |
coṣyapeyasamṛddhaṃ ca yathāśakty upakalpayet ||
tato ’nivṛtte madhyāhne luptalomanakhān dvijān |
abhigamya yathāmārgaṃ prayacched dantadhāvanam ||
tailam abhyañjanaṃ snānaṃ snānīyaṃ ca pṛthagvidham |
pātrair audumbarair dadyād vaiśvadaivatyapūrvakam ||
tataḥ snātvā nivṛttebhyaḥ pratyutthāya kṛtāñjaliḥ |
pādyam ācamanīyaṃ ca saṃprayacched yathākramam ||
'annārambhaḥ ' pākopakramaḥ | 'udumbaraṃ' tāmram |
śrāddhakāle samāhūtān alaṃkurvīta tān dvijān |
śmaśrukarmaśiraḥsnānadhūpanābhyañjanāñjanaiḥ ||
matsyapurāṇe |
udapātraṃ ca kāṃsyaṃ ca mekṣaṇaṃ ca samitkuśān |
āhared apasavyena sarvaṃ dakṣiṇataḥ śanaiḥ ||
evam āsādya tat sarvaṃ bhavanasyāgrato bhuvi |
gomayenopaliptāyāṃ gomūtreṇa tu maṇḍale ||
[118]        akṣatābhiḥ sapuṣpābhis tad abhyarcyāpasavyavat |
viprāṇāṃ kṣālayet pādān abhivandya punaḥ punaḥ ||
'udapātram' avanejanādyartham | ‘kāṃsyaṃ' caruprakṣepārtham | atha śrāddhakalpe piṇḍapitṛyajñāṅgānāṃ mekṣaṇādīnām upādānaṃ śrāddhapiṇḍapitṛyajñayor ekatantratvābhiprāyam | matsyapurāṇa eva śrāddhapiṇḍapitṛyajñayor ekatantratvaṃ prastutya carūdvāsanānantaraṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñasāmagrīpratipādanāt ||
āsanaṃ kutapaṃ dadyād itarad vā pavitravat |
pādaprakṣālanaṃ kuryāt svayam eva vinītavat ||
āsadhvam iti tān brūyād āsanaṃ saṃspṛśann api |
dakṣiṇāsaṃsthā āsīran na spṛśeyuḥ parasparam ||
brahmoyāś ca kathāḥ kuryuḥ pitṝṇām etad īpsitam |
'brahmodyāḥ' 'kaḥ svid ekākī carati' ityādikāni vedavyākhyānāni ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
prayato ’parāhṇe śuciḥ śuklavāsā darbheṣu tiṣṭhan svāgatam iti brūyāt | pādyārghyācamanīyodakāni datvā brāhmaṇān upasaṃgṛhyopaveśayed āsanam anvālabhya ||
brahmapurāṇe |
tatrāsanāni deyāni celājinakuśaiḥ saha |
pṛthak pṛthak cāsaneṣu tilatailena dīpakāḥ ||
[119]        avicchinnās tathā deyās te tu rakṣyās tu vai dvijaiḥ ||
'āsaneṣu' āsanasaṃnidhau | 'avicchinnāḥ’ śrāddhasamāptiparyantasthāyinaḥ ||
[devala-kūrmapurāṇau] |
ye cātra pitrye devānāṃ viprāḥ pūrvaṃ nimantritāḥ |
prāṅmukhāny āsanāny eṣāṃ dvidarbhopahitāni ca ||
dakṣiṇāmukhayuktāni pitṝṇām āsanāni tu |
dakṣiṇāyaikadarbhāṇi prokṣitāni tilodakaiḥ ||
'dakṣiṇāmukhayuktāni' dakṣiṇāgrāṇi | agratvaṃ ca kāṣṭhamūlabhāgāpekṣayā,
amāvāsyāṃ mahāprājño viprān ānāyya pūjitān |
dakṣiṇāvṛttikāḥ sarvā bṛsīḥ svayam athākarot ||
dakṣiṇāgrās tato darbhā viṣṭareṣu niveśitāḥ |
pādayoś caiva viprāṇāṃ ye tv annam upabhuñjate ||
iti bhāratavacanāt (anu, 138, 12, 14) | 'dakṣiṇāvṛttikāḥ' dakṣiṇādiśiraskāḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 226-228] |
aparāhne samabhyarcya svāgatenāgatāṃs tu tān |
pavitrapāṇir ācāntān āsaneṣūpaveśayet ||
[120]        daive yugmān yathāśakti pitrye ’yugmāṃs tathaiva ca |
pariśrite śucau deśe dakṣiṇāpravaṇe tathā ||
dvau daive prāk trayaḥ pitrya udag ekaikam eva vā |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ tantraṃ vā vaiśvadevikam ||
'pariśrite' āvṛte | 'prāk' prāṅmukhāḥ |
viṣṇuḥ [73,2] |
dvitīye ’hni śuklapakṣasya pūrvāhne kṛṣṇapakṣasyāparāhṇe viprān susnātān ācāntān yathābhūyo vidyākrameṇa kuśottareṣvāsaneṣūpaveśayet ||
'dvitīye ’hni' nimantraṇadināpekṣayā | 'yathābhūyo vidyā' bahuvidyam anatikramya krameṇa daivapūrvam |
śātātapaḥ |
dvau deve ’tharvaṇau viprau prāṅmukhāv upaveśayet |
pitrye tūdaṅmukhān trīṃś ca bahvṛco ’dhvaryusāmagān ||
manu-baudhāyanau śātātapaś ca prathame |
dvau deve pitṛkṛtye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ’pi na prasajyeta vistare ||
satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmān neheta vistaram ||
[121] brahmapurāṇe |
deśakāladhanābhāvād ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
śeṣān vittānusāreṇa bhojayed anyaveśmani ||
yasmād brāhmaṇabāhulyād doṣo bahutaro bhavet |
śrāddhanāśo maunanāśaḥ śrāddhatantrasya vismṛtiḥ ||
ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃsparśo nindā dātrannabhoktṛṣu |
vitaṇḍayā parīvādo jalpās te te pṛthagvidhāḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe [3, 15, 17-18] |
prāṅmukhān bhojayed viprān devānām ubhayātmakān |
pitṛmātāmahānāṃ ca bhojayec cāpy udaṅmukhān ||
pṛthak tayoḥ kecid āhuḥ śrāddhasya karaṇaṃ budhāḥ |
ekatraikena pākena vadanty anye maharṣayaḥ ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2,17,12] |
uraḥsthāḥ pitaras tasya vāmapārśve pitāmahāḥ |
prapitāmahā dakṣiṇataḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ piṇḍatarkakāḥ ||
‘uraḥsthāḥ' ityādyekabrāhmaṇapakṣe | 'piṇḍatarkakāḥ' piṇḍalepābhilāṣiṇaḥ ||
gautamaḥ [15, 7-8] |
navāvarān bhojayet | ayujo vā yathotsāhaṃ vā ||
[122] āśvalāyanagṛhyam [4,7,1-4] |
athātaḥ pārvaṇe śrāddhe kāmya ābhyudayika ekoddiṣṭe vā | brāhmaṇān śrutaśīlavṛttasaṃpannān ekena vā kāle jñāpitān snātān kṛtapacchaucān ācāntān udaṅmukhān pitṛvad upaveśyaikaikasya dvau dvau trīṃs trīn vā vṛddhau phalabhūyastvaṃ na tv evaikaṃ sarveṣām | kāmam anādye | piṇḍair vyākhyātam ||
'ekena' śrutādīnāṃ samuditānām asambhava eṣām anyatamenāpi saṃpannān | 'kṛtapacchaucān' kṛtapādaprakṣālanān pūrvaṃ viśvebhyo devebhyo brāhmaṇān upaveśya | 'pitṛvat' pitrādikrameṇa | 'dvau dvau' ityābhyudayikaśrādaviṣayam, smṛtyantareṣu pārvaṇādāv ayugmabrāhmaṇaniyamāt | atraivopakrame 'athātaḥ pārvaṇe śrāddhe kāmye ābhyudayike' ityabhidhānāt | 'piṇḍair vyākhyātam' iti, yathā bahūnāṃ pitṝṇāṃ pratipuruṣaṃ piṇḍas tathā brāhmaṇo ’pi pratipuruṣam iti | 'anādye' ādyam atra pārvaṇadharmakāṇām ādibhūtaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaśrāddham, tadvyatirikte śrāddhe [123] kāmye ekaḥ pitṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇa iti | 'anādye' adanīyadravyābhāve eko ’pi bhojanīya iti kaścit ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
śvaḥ śrāddhaṃ kariṣyāmīti saṃkalpya brāhmaṇān sapta pañca vā śrotriyān nimantrya śuciḥ śucau deśe gavāṃ goṣṭhe ’gnyagāre vā gomayena vilipya puṣpopahāraṃ kṛtvā prāṅmukhān viśvedevān upaveśayet | barhiṣmatsv āsaneṣu pitṝn dakṣiṇapūrveṇetyuktaṃ śrāddhakalpaś ca ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2,14,6] |
caraṇavato ’nūcānān yonigotramantrāsaṃbandhān śucīn mantravatas tryavarān ayujaḥ pūrvedyuḥ prātar eva cāmantrya sadarbhavat klṛpteṣv āsaneṣu prāṅmukhān upaveśayed udaṅmukhān vā ||
‘caraṇavataḥ’ svaśākhāyāṃ vyavasthitān | 'mantrāsaṃbandhāḥ' śiṣyopādhyāyavyatiriktāḥ | 'mantravataḥ' mantrādhyayanayuktāniti ||
hārītaḥ [a. 12] |
kṛtaveśmakarmāṇaḥ sastrībālavṛddhāḥ surabhisnātāḥ śucayaḥ śucivāsasaḥ | śucīn upaspṛṣṭācamanīyān śucau dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu prāṅmukhān brāhmaṇān bhojayet | udaṅmukhān ity eke | na vā prāṅnirdeśāt krūrā hy ete’bhimukhā hiṃsakā [124] bhavantīti maitrāyaṇiḥ | tasmāt prāṅmukhān eva [brāhmaṇān] bhojayet | udaṅmukhān vaiśvadeve | te hi dakṣiṇāṃ rakṣanti ||
'śucivāsasaḥ' syur iti śeṣaḥ | 'na vā' na udaṅmukhān bhojayet | 'prāṅnirdeśāt' iti, pūrvaṃ yasmād ete pitṝn uddiśya nirdiṣṭās tadarthatvena kalpitanimantraṇādinā nimantraṇottarakālaṃ ca,
nimantritān hi pitara upatiṣṭhanti tān dvijān |
vāyuvac cānugacchanti tathāsīnān upasate ||
iti dṛṣṭyā pitradhiṣṭhānatvāt 'ete krūrāḥ’ | ato dakṣiṇābhimukhasya yajamānasya śrāddhaṃ kurvato ’bhimukhā hiṃsakā bhavanti | tasmāt prāṅmukhān svayaṃ ca dakṣiṇābhimukho bhojayet | 'dakṣiṇāḥ' dakṣiṇādigavasthitāḥ | idaṃ ca pitryabrāhmaṇanāṃ prāṅmukhopaveśanaṃ maitrāyaṇīyānāṃ vyavasthitam ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 29-31] |
api vā bhojayed ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ vedapāragam |
śrutaśīlopasaṃpannaṃ sarvālakṣaṇavarjitam ||
yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe daivaṃ tatra kathaṃ bhavet |
annaṃ pātre samuddhṛtya sarvasya prakṛtasya tu ||
[125]        devtāyatane kṛtvā tathā śrāddhaṃ pravartayet |
prāsyed annaṃ tadagnau tu dadyād vā brahmacāriṇe ||
'alakṣaṇāni' kuṣṭhitvādādīni |
śaṅkhaḥ |
bhojayed atha vāpy ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ paktipāvanam |
divye kṛtvā tu naivedyaṃ paścāt tasya tu nirvapet ||
'divye' viśvedevasthāne | 'naivedyaṃ' devārtham upakalpitam |
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 86,88] |
yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe svalpatvāt prakṛtasya tu |
stokaṃ stokaṃ samuddhṛtya tebhyo ’nnaṃ vinivedayet |
'prakṛtaṃ ' śrāddhocitaṃ dravyam | 'stokaṃ stokaṃ samuddhṛtya ' ekasminn eva pātre pitryādibhyo vibhajyānaṃ saṃkalpayed ity arthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
saṃtiṣṭhamāneṣv arhatsu yo ‘narho ’grāsanaṃ śrayet |
gṛhṇāti sakalaṃ paṅkter āyuṣā ca viyujyate ||
caturṇāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ harati brāhmaṇo vighnakārakaḥ ||
annasyānnapateḥ paṅkter bhojanākāṅkṣiṇāṃ tathā ||
[sumantuḥ] |
vidyātapo’dhikānāṃ vai prathamāsanam ucyate |
[126] yamaḥ |
prāyeṇa mūrkhaḥ śrutimantrahīno
yo ’grāsanaṃ gacchati mūrkhabhāvāt |
nānyad bhayaṃ paśyati mūḍhacetā
dehapraṇāśe narakaṃ sughoram ||
yo vai vidvān na ca brahmacārī
na cāśramastho na ca mantrapūtaḥ |
havīṃṣi cāśnāti dhuri sthitaś ca
so ’śnati paṅktyā malakilbiṣāṇi ||
paṅktipāvanānantaraṃ paiṭhīnasiḥ |
teṣām ekaikaḥ punāti paṅktiniyukto mūrdhani sahasrair apy upahatām | tasmān nāniyuṅkto ’grāsanaṃ gacchet | yadi gacchet paṅktyā harati duṣkṛtam ||
[127]
|| 13 ||
|| atha śrāddhasampadaḥ ||
tatra brāhmaṇāḥ śāstrasaṃpaddhetavo darśitāḥ 'agrayāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu' ityādiyājñavalkyokteḥ | anyad apy āha manuḥ [3, 255 - 256] |
aparāhṇas tilā darbhā vāstusaṃpādanaṃ tathā |
sṛṣṭir mṛṣṭir dvijāś cāgryāḥ śrāddhakarmasu saṃpadaḥ ||
darbhāḥ pavitraṃ pūrvāhṇo haviṣyāṇi ca sarvaśaḥ |
pavitraṃ yac ca pūrvoktaṃ vijñeyā havyasaṃpadaḥ ||
‘vāstur’ atra śrāddhadeśaḥ, tasya saṃpādanaṃ 'vāstusaṃpādanam' dakṣiṇāpravaṇatva-samīkaraṇopalepanādikaraṇam | 'sṛṣṭiḥ' visargaḥ, akārpaṇyenānnavyañjanādidānam | [128] 'mṛṣṭiḥ,' annādeḥ svādutvasaṃpādanam | 'saṃpadaḥ' samṛddhayaḥ | 'pavitraṃ' mantrāḥ | 'pavitraṃ' pāvanaṃ, śucyācārādi | ‘pūrvoktaṃ' vāstusaṃpādanādi | 'havyaṃ' atra daivikaṃ karma | devalaḥ |
darbhās tilā gajacchāyā dauhitro madhusarpiṣī |
kutapo nīlakaṇṭhaś ca pavitrāṇīha paitṛke ||
'nīlakaṇṭho' nīlavṛṣaḥ | so ’tra pitṛkarmaprasaṅgād uktaḥ |
yamaḥ |
yatnena bhojayec chrāddhe dauhitraṃ vratinaṃ śiśum |
dattvāsanaṃ tu kutapaṃ tilair abhyavakīrya ca ||
'śiśuṃ' bālam api |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
tilā dauhitraḥ kutapā iti pavitrāṇi śrāddhe | satyaṃ krodhaṃ [cākrodhaṃ?] ca śaucaṃ cācāraṃ ca praśaṃsantīti ||
viṣṇuḥ [ 79, 16] |
[khaga]kutapakṛṣṇājinatilasiddhārthakākṣatāni [ca] pavitrāṇi rakṣoghnāni ca dadyāt |
[129] vāyupurāṇe |
kṛṣṇājinasya sāṃnidhyaṃ darśanaṃ dānam eva ca |
rakṣoghnaṃ brahmavarcasyaṃ paśūn putrāṃś ca dāpayet ||
hārītaḥ |
darbhair adbhis tilair dattaṃ tūṣṇīm apy āpnute divam |
vidhinā tv ānupūrvyeṇa tv akṣayaṃ parikalpate ||
kāñcanādiṣu darbhādyair mantravat pratipāditāḥ |
pitṝṇām akṣayā yānty amṛtā bhūtvā mahormibhiḥ ||
'tūṣṇīṃ’ śrāddhamantravivarjitam | iyaṃ ca darbhādīnāṃ vidhyānupūrvīsahitānāṃ stutiḥ | 'kāñcanādiṣu' pātreṣv iti śeṣaḥ | 'pratipāditā' āpatsv iti śeṣaḥ |
yamaḥ |
rakṣanti darbhā asurān tilā rakṣanti rākṣasān |
vedavid rakṣati tv annaṃ yataye dattam akṣayam ||
hārītaḥ |
tilā rakṣanti daiteyān darbhā rakṣanti rākṣasān |
rakṣanti śrotriyāḥ paṅktiṃ snātake dattam akṣayam ||
viṣṇuḥ [18, 4] |
tilaiḥ sarṣapair vā yātudhānān visarjayet ||
[130] uśanā |
kuśā darbhāḥ samākhyātāḥ kutapo bṛsayas tathā |
duhituś caiva ye putrā dauhitrās te prakīrtitāḥ ||
'bṛsī' āsanaviśeṣaḥ, tṛṇamayaṃ vartulam āsanam ||
śātātapaḥ |
dauhitraṃ khaḍgaśṛṅgaṃ tu lalāṭe yad dhi jāyate |
tasya śṛṅgasya yat pātraṃ dauhitram iti tad viduḥ ||
divasasyāṣṭame bhāge mandī bhavati bhāskaraḥ |
sa kālaḥ kutapo nāma pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kuto ’pi śrāddhavelāyāṃ śrotriyo yadi dṛśyate |
śrāddhaṃ punāti vai yasmāt kutapas tena saṃjñitaḥ ||
atra ca kutapadauhitrayor anekārthatvād ekatamopādāne ’pi śrāddhasamṛddhir bhavatīti mantavyam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
yatis tridaṇḍī karuṇā rājataṃ pātram eva ca |
dauhitraḥ kutapaḥ kālaś chāgaḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ tathā ||
[131]        gaurāḥ kṛṣṇās tathāraṇyās tathaiva trividhās tilāḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ tṛptaye sṛṣṭā daśaite brahmaṇā svayam ||
darbhair mantrais tilair hemnā rājatena vinā jalam |
dattaṃ haranti rakṣāṃsi tasmād dadyān na kevalam ||
matsyapurāṇe |
tathā khāḍgaṃ ca tatpātraṃ yaś ca nepālakambalaḥ |
rukmaṃ darbhās tilā gāvo dauhitraś cāṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pāpaṃ kutsitam evāhus tasya saṃtāpakāriṇaḥ |
aṣṭāv ete yatas tasmāt kutapā iti viśrutāḥ ||
[132]
|| 14 ||
|| athātithyam ||
tatra yamaḥ |
bhikṣuko brahmacārī ca bhojanārtham upasthitaḥ |
upaviṣṭeṣv anuprāptaḥ kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet ||
yasya vai yajamānasya nāgre bhuṅkte yatis tathā |
aniṣṭam ahutaṃ tasya harate rakṣasāṃ gaṇaḥ ||
prakṛter guṇatattvajño yasyāśnāti yatir haviḥ |
sa tu vedavidāṃ tasya sahasrād atiricyate ||
tasmād agrāsane bhojyo yajatā saṃśito yatiḥ |
śrotriyo brahmacārī vā yajñas tena na lupyate ||
sahasraṃ tu sahasrāṇāṃ snātakā yatra bhuñjate |
ekas tān yogavit prītaḥ sarvān arhati dharmataḥ ||
'anuprāptaḥ' atithitveneti śeṣaḥ | 'yajamānasya' śrāddhaṃ kurvāṇasya | 'aniṣṭam' iti, iṣṭaṃ hutaṃ ca yasmād rakṣasāṃ gaṇo harati tasmād aniṣṭam ahutaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | 'prakṛter guṇatattvajñaḥ' prakṛtir atra paramātmā, 'prakṛtiś ca pratijñādṛṣṭāntānuparodhāt' iti bādarāyaṇasūtradarśanāt | tasya guṇāś cidānandādayaḥ | teṣāṃ tattvaṃ svarūpāvyatirekaḥ, tajjña ity arthaḥ | 'agrāsane' tasyām eva paṅktau mūrdhani | 'arhati' ātmanā sa svīkaroti ||
[133] chāgaleyaḥ |
pūjayec chrāddhakāle ’pi yatiṃ sabrahmacāriṇam |
viprān uddharate pāpāt pitṛmātṛgaṇān api ||
bhuñjate yatra tatrāpi yatayo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
gṛhṇanti pitaro devāḥ sa ca yāti parāṃ gatim ||
tārayanti ca dātāraṃ putrān dārān pitṝṃs tathā ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena arcayed āśramāgatam ||
alābhe dhyānabhikṣūṇāṃ bhojayed brahmacāriṇam |
tadalābhe ’py udāsīnaṃ gṛhastham api bhojayet ||
brahmacārisahasrais tu vānaprasthaśatair api |
gṛhasthānāṃ sahasrais tu yatir eko viśiṣyate ||
gandhamālyaphalaiś caiva bhojanaiḥ kṣīrasaṃskṛtaiḥ |
saṃpūjayed yatiṃ śrāddhe pitṝṇāṃ tuṣṭikāriṇam ||
brahmacārī tapasvī ca pūjanīyau hi nityaśaḥ |
tatkṛtaṃ sukṛtaṃ yat tu tasmāt ṣaḍbhāgam āpnuyāt ||
jāpyaṃ yogaś ca yajñaś ca yatas tasmai trayaṃ smṛtam |
tasya praṇāmaḥ pūjā ca dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam ||
gṛhasthasyāśramaṃ gacched brahmacārī yatis tathā |
khādyaṃ pānaṃ phalaṃ puṣpam ātmānam api vedayet ||
satataṃ yogayuktānāṃ vītarāgatapasvinām |
sarvārambhanivṛttānāṃ yatīnāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
[134]        yataye vītarāgāya dattam annaṃ supūjitam |
na kṣīyate śraddhayāpi kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
brahmacārī yatiś caiva pakvānnasvāmināv ubau |
pacamānāḥ patanty ete tāpasaḥ patate punaḥ ||
yoginaṃ samatikramya gṛhasthaṃ yadi bhojayet ||
na tat phalam avāpnoti sarvaṃ gotraṃ prapātayet ||
yoginaṃ samatikramya pūjayanti parasparam |
dātā bhoktā ca narakaṃ gacchate saha bāndhavaiḥ ||
aparasparadānāni lokayātrā na dharmataḥ |
tasmād yatnena dātavyam anyathā patito bhavet ||
sabrahmacāriṇaṃ brahmacārisahitam || 'alābhe' iti dhyānabhikṣvādīnāṃ sahāgamane ‘pi pūrvapūrvotkṛṣṭajñāpanārtham | 'tapasvī' yatiḥ | 'vedayet' nivedayet | 'gotraṃ' kulam | 'prapātayet' atikrānto yatir iti śeṣaḥ | 'aparasparadānāni' parasparadānāni na kartavyāni | yataḥ 'sā lokayātrā na dharmataḥ', nañsamāsaś cātrāśrāddhabhojītivat prasajya pratiṣedhe | 'dharmataḥ' iti prathamāntāt tasiḥ | 'anyathā' parasparadānena ||
manuḥ [3,283] |
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ cāpi bhojanārtham upasthitam |
brāhmaṇair abhyanujñātaḥ śaktitaḥ pratipūjayet ||
[135] śātātapaḥ |
atithir yasya nāśnāti tac chrāddhaṃ na praśasyate |
śrutavratavihīnaiś ca bhuktam aśrotriyaiś ca yat ||
śātātapagranthāntare |
ātithyarahite śrāddhe bhuñjate ye ’budhā dvijāḥ |
vṛthā tenānnapākena kākayoniṃ vrajanti te ||
[136]
|| 15 ||
|| athāpāsanīyāḥ ||
tatra manuḥ [3,239-241] |
cāṇḍālaś ca varāhaś ca kukkuṭaś ca tathaiva ca |
rajasvalā ca ṣaṇḍaś ca nekṣerann aśnato dvijān ||
home pradāne bhojye ca yad ebhir abhivīkṣitam |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca tad bhavaty ayathātatham ||
ghrāṇena sūkaro hanti pakṣavātena kukkuṭaḥ ||
śvā tu dṛṣṭinipātena sparśenāvaravarṇajaḥ ||
'ayathātathaṃ ' yadarthaṃ kriyate tadviparītam | 'kukkuṭaḥ pakṣavātena' ityādi saṃnidhiviśeṣaṇaṃ vāraṇaparam, yāvati deśe kukkuṭādīnāṃ pakṣavātādi saṃbhāvyate tāvato deśād apaneyā ity arthaḥ | 'avaravarṇajaḥ' śūdraḥ ' ||
yamaḥ |
kukkuṭo viḍvarāhaś ca kākaḥ śvā ca biḍālakaḥ |
vṛṣalīpatiś ca vṛṣalaḥ ṣaṇḍo nārī rajasvalā |
etāni śrāddhakāleṣu parivarjyāni nityaśaḥ |
kukkuṭaḥ pakṣavātena hanti śrāddham asaṃvṛtam ||
[137]        ghrāṇena viḍvarāhaś ca vāyasas tu rutena tu |
śvā tu dṛṣṭinipātena mārjāraḥ śravaṇena tu ||
vṛṣalīpatiś ca dānena cakṣurbhyāṃ vṛṣalas tathā |
chāyayā hanti vai ṣaṇḍaḥ sparśena ca rajasvalā ||
khañjaḥ kāṇaḥ kuṇiḥ śvitrī dātuḥ preṣyakaro bhavet |
nyūnāṅgaś cātiriktāṅgas tam āśu ninayet tataḥ ||
devalaḥ |
hīnāṅgaḥ patitaḥ kuṣṭhī vraṇī pulkasanāstikau |
kukkuṭaḥ śūkaraśvānau varjyā śrāddheṣu dūrataḥ ||
bībhatsam aśuciṃ nagnaṃ mattaṃ dhūrtaṃ rajasvalām |
nīlakāṣāyavasanaṃ chinnakarṇaṃ ca varjayet ||
śastraṃ kālāyasaṃ sīsaṃ malināmbaravāsasam |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ vāpi śradveṣu parivarjayet ||
'bībhatsaḥ' vikṛto udvegakaraḥ padārthaḥ | malinam ambaraṃ vasta [vastra?] iti 'malināmbaravāsāḥ' ||
mahābhārate |
rajasvalā ca yā nārī vyaṅgitā karṇayos tathā |
nirvāpe nopatiṣṭheta saṃgrāhyā nānyavaṃśajā ||
'nirvāpe' pākārambhaprabhṛtiśrāddhakarmaṇi | 'anyavaṃśajā' mātāpitṛvaṃśāsaṃbaddhā | 'na saṃgrāhyā' na vyāpārayitavyety arthaḥ ||
[138] viṣṇuḥ [81,5 - 10] |
saṃvṛte na śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | na rajasvalāṃ [paśyet] | na śvānam | na viḍvarāham | na grāmakukkuṭam | prayatnāc chrāddham ajasya darśayet ||
punar viṣṇuḥ [81,15-17] |
na hīnādhikāṅgāś ca śrāddhaṃ paśyeyuḥ | na śūdrāḥ | na patitāḥ |
na mahārogiṇaḥ ||
uśanā |
viḍvarāhamārjārakukkuṭanakulaśūdrarajasvalāśūdrībhartāś ca dūram
apanetavyāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddhakāṇḍa, 48,50 ] |
śvapākaṣaṇḍapiśunāḥ śvānakukkuṭasūkarāḥ |
rajasvalā ca cāṇḍālāḥ śrāddhe kāryās tv adarśanāḥ ||
pariśriteṣu dadyāc ca tilair vā vikiren mahīm |
ninayec copaviṣṭas tu taṃ doṣaṃ paṅktipāvanaḥ ||
'śvapākaḥ' antyajātiviśeṣaḥ |
[139] hārītaḥ |
daive vā yadi vā pitrye surāpī yat tu saṃspṛśet |
rajasvalā puṃścalī vā rakṣaḥ saṃgacchate hi tat ||
vāyupurāṇe |
nagnādayo na paśyeyuḥ śrāddham evaṃ vyavasthitam |
gacchanti tais tu dṛṣṭāni na pitṝn na pitāmahān ||
sarveṣām eva bhūtānāṃ trayī saṃvaraṇaṃ smṛtam |
tāṃ tyajanti tu ye mohāt te vai nagnādayo janāḥ ||
bauddhaśrāvaṇanigranthāḥ śākyajīvakakāpilāḥ |
ye ‘dharmān anuvartante te vai nagnādayo janāḥ ||
vṛthājaṭī vṛthāmuṇḍī vṛthānagnaś ca yo naraḥ |
mahāpātakino ye ca te vai nagnādayo janāḥ ||
kulatvamāninaḥ śāvā vyādhā muṣṭikalaṅghanāḥ |
kukarmasaṃśritās tv ete kupathāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
ebhir nirdhūlitaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ śraddhaṃ gacchati dānavān |
devatānām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca pāpavādaratāś ca ye |
asurān yātudhānāṃś ca dṛṣṭam etair vrajaty uta |
apumān apaviddhaś kukkuṭī grāmasūkaraḥ ||
[140]        śvā caiva hanti śrāddhāni darśanād eva sarvaśaḥ |
śvāviṭsūkarasaṃmṛṣṭaṃ dīrgharogibhir eva ca ||
patitair malinaiś caiva na draṣṭavyaṃ kathaṃcana |
annaṃ paśyeyur ete yat tan na syād dhavyakavyayoḥ ||
utsraṣṭavyaṃ pradhānārthaṃ saṃskāras tv āpadi smṛtaḥ |
haviṣā saṃskṛtānāṃ tu pūrvam eva hi mārjanam ||
mṛtsaṃyuktābhir adbhis tu prokṣaṇaṃ tu vidhīyate |
siddhārthakaiḥ kṛṣṇatilaiḥ kāryaṃ caivāvakīraṇam ||
gurusūryāgnibastānāṃ darśanaṃ vā prayatnataḥ |
'bauddhaśrāvaṇakādayaḥ' pākhaṇḍaviśeṣāḥ | 'kulatvamāninaḥ' ye kulābhimānena tyaktācārāḥ | 'śāvāḥ' mṛtahāriṇaḥ | 'muṣṭikaḥ' mallaḥ | 'pradhānārthaṃ' śrāddhārtham | 'utsraṣṭavyaṃ’ śrāddhe tan na niyojyam | asaṃbhave tu mṛtsaṃyuktābhir adbhir mārjanādichāgadarśana-paryantasaṃskāraṃ kṛtvā viniyojyam iti samudāyārthaḥ ||
[141]
|| 16 ||
|| atha vidhiparibhāṣā ||
tatra śātātapaḥ |
nityaśrāddham adaivaṃ syād ekoddiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca |
mātṛśrāddhaṃ tu yugmaiḥ syād adaivaṃ prāṅmukhaiḥ pṛthak ||
yojayed devapūrvāṇi śrāddhāny anyāni yatnataḥ |
tathā |
sadaivaṃ bhojayec chrāddhaṃ tatpūrvaṃ ca pravartayet |
anyathā tv avalumpanti sadaivāsurarākṣasāḥ ||
'tatpūrvaṃ' devapūvam |
vāyupurāṇe |
nāprokṣitaṃ spṛśet kiṃcic chrāddhe daive tathā punaḥ |
uttareṇa hared vedyā dakṣiṇena visarjayet ||
'vediḥ' atra dakṣiṇāpravaṇādiśrāddhadeśaḥ | 'daive' devaśrāddhe |
manuḥ [3, 203 - 205] |
devakāryād dvijātīnāṃ pitṛkāryaṃ viśiṣyate |
daivaṃ hi pitṛkāryasya pūrvam āpyāyanaṃ smṛtam ||
teṣām ārakṣabhūtaṃ tu pūrvaṃ daivaṃ niyojayet |
[142]        rakṣāṃsi hi vilumpanti śrāddham ārakṣavarjitam ||
daivādyantaṃ tad īheta pitrādyantaṃ na tad bhavet |
pitryādyantaṃ tv īhamānaḥ kṣipraṃ naśyati sānvayaḥ ||
'āpyāyanaṃ' pitṛśrāddhasya pradhānībhūtasya samṛddhikaram aṅgabhūtaṃ na tu svataḥ pradhānam | 'ārakṣabhūtaṃ' sarvato rakṣākaram | 'daivādyantaṃ' devatādyārambhāvasāne yasya tat tathā | etenaitad uktaṃ bhavati — nimantraṇāni daivapūrvaṃ, visarjanaṃ tu viparītaṃ kartavyam ||
devalaḥ |
yatra tu kriyate karma paitṛke brāhmaṇān prati |
tat sarvaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ vaiśvadaivatyapūrvakam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [ śrāddhakāṇḍa, 91-94] |
ṛtur dakṣo vasuḥ satyaḥ kālaḥ kāmas tathaiva ca |
dhuriś ca rocanaś caiva tathā caiva purūravāḥ ||
mārdravaś ca daśaite tu viśvedevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
iṣṭiśrāddhe krtur dakṣaḥ satyo nāndīmukhe vasuḥ ||
naimittike kālakāmau kāmye ca dhurirocanau |
purūravā mārdravaś ca pārvaṇe samudāhṛtau ||
utpattiṃ nāma caiteṣāṃ na vidur ye dvijātayaḥ |
ayam uccāraṇīyas taiḥ ślokaḥ śraddhāsamanvitaiḥ ||
āgacchantu mahābhāgā viśvedevā varapradāḥ |
ye yatra yojitāḥ śrāddhe sāvadhānā bhavantu te ||
[143] 'iṣṭiśrāddham' icchāśrāddham | 'naimittikaṃ' sāṃvatsarikam | ekoddiṣṭaṃ tad yasya sāgnyādeḥ pārvaṇavat kriyate | 'yatra' viśvedevānāṃ saṃbhavāt |
śātātapaḥ |
udaṅmukhas tu devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
pradadyāt pārvaṇe śrāddhe devapūrvaṃ vidhānataḥ ||
śrāddhakalpe kātyāyanaḥ [1, 1,24-25 1, 2, 1-5] |
āvāhanādi vāgyata upasparśanāt | āmantritāś caivam | daivapūrvaṃ hi śrāddham | piṇḍapitṛyajñavad upa|cāraḥ pitrye | dviguṇās tu darbhāḥ | pavitrapāṇir dadyād āsīnaḥ sarvatra | praśneṣu paṅktimūrdhanyaṃ pṛcchati ||
'sarvān vā pitrya' iti vacanāt tarpaṇādy api pitrye dviguṇaiḥ kuśaiḥ samācaranti, na ṛjubhir eva ||
manuḥ [3,289] |
prācīnāvītinā samyag apasavyam atandriṇā |
pitryam ānidhanāt kāryaṃ vidhivad darbhapāṇinā ||
'apasavyaṃ' vāmapārśve | 'atandriṇā' analasena | 'ānidhanāt' āsamāpteḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1,23] |
dakṣiṇaṃ pātayej jānu devān paricaran sadā |
pātayed itaraṃ jānu pitṝn paricaran sadā ||
[iti] śrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau vidhibhāṣā[parva  ||
[144]
|| 17 ||
|| atha japāsanādividhiḥ ||
atha japāsanadānāvāhānārghyapādyakaraṇāni |
tatra brahmapurāṇe |
upaviśya japed dhīmān gāyatrīṃ tadanujñayā |
tathā |
pāpāpahaṃ pāvanīyam aśvamedhaphalaṃ tathā |
mantraṃ vakṣyāmy ahaṃ tasmād amṛtaṃ brahmanirmitam ||
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca mahāyogibhya eva ca |
namaḥ svadhāyai svāhāyai nityam eva bhavantv iti ||
ādyāvasāne śrāddhasya trirāvṛttaṃ bhavet sadā |
aśvamedhaphalaṃ hy etad dvijaiḥ satkṛtya pūjitaḥ ||
piṇḍanirvāpaṇe vāpi japed etat samāhitaḥ |
paṭhyamānam idaṃ śrutvā śrāddhakāle hy upasthite ||
pitaraḥ kṣipram āyānti rākṣasāḥ pradravanti ca |
matsyapurāṇe |
viśvān devān yavaiḥ puṣpair abhyarcyāsanapūrvakam |
pūrayet pātrayugmaṃ tu sthāpya darbhapavitrake ||
yājñvalkyaḥ [1,220 - 235] |
pāṇiprakṣālanaṃ datvā viṣṭarārthaṃ kuśān api |
āvāhayed anujñāto viśvedevāsa ity ṛcā ||
[145]        yavair anvavakīryātha bhājane sapavitrake |
śanno devyā apaḥ kṣiptvā yavo ’sīti yavāṃs tathā ||
yā divyā iti mantreṇa hasteṣv arghyaṃ vinikṣipet |
datvodakaṃ gandhadhūpamālyadānaṃ sadīpakam ||
[tathācchādanadānaṃ ca karaśaucārtham ambu ca] |
apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pitṝṇām apradakṣiṇam ||
dviguṇāṃs tu kuśān kṛtvā uśantas tvety ṛcā pitṝn |
āvāhya tadanujñāto japed āyantu nas tataḥ ||
[apahatā iti tilān vikīrya ca samantataḥ | ]
yavārthās tu tilaiḥ kāryāḥ kuryād arghyādi pūrvavat ||
datvā saṃsravāṃs teṣāṃ pātre kṛtvā vidhānataḥ |
pitṛbhyaḥ sthānam asīti nyubjaṃ pātraṃ karotya dhaḥ ||
'pāṇiprakṣālanaṃ' prakṣālyate ’neneti prakṣālanam udakam | 'viṣṭarārtham ' āsanārtham | 'anujñātaḥ' anumataḥ | anujñātavacanād eva 'viśvān devān āvāhayiṣye' ityādipraśno ’rthāt pūrvaṃ veditavyaḥ | 'yavair anvavakīrya' āvāhanānantaram āsanārthaṃ yavavakiraṇaṃ kṛtvā | 'yavo ’sīti' 'yavo ’si dhānyarājo vā' ityādinā | 'āyantu na' iti, 'āyantu naḥ pitara' ityādimantraḥ | ' yavārthāḥ' yavaprayojanāni vikiraṇādīni | [146] 'saṃsravāḥ' arghye dīyamāne saṃsravantaḥ udakabindavaḥ | 'teṣāṃ' arghyāṇām | 'nyubjaṃ' adhomukham | 'adhaḥ ' bhūmau ||
matsyapurāṇe |
yā divyety arghyam utsṛjya dadyād gandhādikaṃ tataḥ |
vastrottaraṃ cānupūrvaṃ dattvā saṃsravaṇādi ca ||
pitṛpātraṃ nidhāyātha nyubjam uttarato nyaset ||
'vastrottaraṃ ' vastradānam uttaram antyaṃ yatra gandhādidāne tat tathā | 'anupūrvaṃ' anukrameṇa | 'saṃsravaṇaṃ' saṃsravodakam | ādiśabdena tilapuṣpapavitrāṇāṃ grahaṇam | 'uttarataḥ ' upaviṣṭabrāhmaṇād uttarasyāṃ diśi ||
śrāddhakalpe kātyāyanaḥ [2,5-14] |
āsaneṣu darbhān āstīrya viśvān devān āvāhayiṣya iti pṛcchati | āvāhayety anujñāto viśvedevāsa āgatety anayāvāhyādakīrya viśvedevāḥ śṛṇutemam iti japitvā pitṝn āvāhaviṣya iti pṛcchati | āvāhayety anujñāta uśantas tvety anayarcāvāhyāvakīryāyantu na iti japitvā yajñiyavṛkṣacamaseṣu pavitrāntarhiteṣv ekaikasminn apa āsiñcati śanno devīr iti | ekaikasminn eva tilān āvapati 'tilo ‘si pitṛdaivatyo gosavo devanirmitaḥ | pratnam adbhiḥ pṛktaḥ svadhayā pitṛṃl lokān prīṇāhi naḥ svāheti | [147] sauvarṇarājataudumbarakhaḍgamaṇimayānāṃ pātrāṇām anyatameṣu pātreṣu yāni vā vidyante | patrapuṭeṣu vā | ekaikasyaikaikena dadāti sapavitreṣu hasteṣu ‘yā divyā āpaḥ payasā saṃbabhūvur yā antarikṣa uta pārthivīryāḥ | hiraṇyavarṇā yajñiyās tā na āpaḥ śivāḥ śaṃsyonāḥ suhavā bhavantv ity asāv eṣate ’rgha’ iti | prathamapātre saṃstravān samavanīya pitṛbhyaḥ sthānam asīti nyubjaṃ pātraṃ nidadhāti | atra gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpānāṃ pradānam ||
'asau' iti sabodhanāntaṃ pitrādināmopalakṣaṇam | 'atra' etasmin kāle |
āśvalāyanagṛhyam [4,7,5 - 14] |
apaḥ pradāya | darbhān dviguṇabhugnān āsanaṃ pradāya | apaḥ pradāya | taijasāśmamayamṛnmayeṣu triṣu pātreṣv ekadravyeṣu vā darbhāntarhiteṣv apa āsicya 'śanno devīr abhiṣṭaya' ity anumantritāsu tilān āvapati ‘tilo ’si somadevatyo gosave devanirmitaḥ | pratnavadbhiḥ prattaḥ svadhayā pitṝn imāṃl lokān prīṇayāhi naḥ svadhā nama” iti | prasavyena | itarapāṇyaṅguṣṭhāntareṇopavītitvād dakṣiṇena vā savyopagṛhītena pitar idaṃ te arghyaṃ pitāmahedaṃ te arghyam iti | apūrvam | [148] tāḥ pratigrāhayiṣyan sakṛt sakṛt svadhā arghyā iti | prasṛṣṭāsv anumantrayeta ‘yā divyā āpaḥ pṛthivī saṃbabhūvur yā antarikṣyā uta pārthivīryāḥ | hiraṇyavarṇā yajñiyās tā na āpaḥ śaṃsyonā bhavantu’ iti saṃsravān samavanīya tābhir adbhiḥ putrakāmo mukham anakti |
noddharet prathamaṃ pātraṃ pitṝṇām arghyapātitam |
āvṛtās tatra tiṣṭhanti pitaraḥ śaunako ’bravīt ||
uddhared yadi tat pātraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
abhojyaṃ tad bhavec chāddhaṃ kruddhaiḥ pitṛgaṇair gataiḥ ||
etasmin kāle gandhamālyadhūpadīpācchādanānāṃ pradānam ||
'darbhān āsanaṃ pradāya' darbhān āsanatvena pradāya ity arthaḥ | 'taijasāśmamayamṛnmayeṣu’ yathākramaṃ pitrādīnāṃ dravyatrayanirmiteṣu | 'ekadravyeṣu' dravyatrayāsaṃbhave mṛdāyekajātīyadravyamayeṣu | 'prasavyena' vāmena hastena | tatrāpi na kevalena vāmenaiva, api tu 'itarapāṇyaṅguṣṭhāntareṇa', itarasya dakṣiṇasya pāṇeḥ aṅguṣṭhāntareṇa pitṛtīrthena | tenaitad uktaṃ bhavati — vāmahastena arghyapātraṃ dhṛtvā, dakṣiṇahastena pitṛtīrthaṃ saṃspṛśya, yathā brāhmaṇahasteṣu jalaṃ patati tathā deyam iti,
[149]        tato vāmena hastena gṛhītvā camasān kramāt |
pitṛtīrthena tat toyaṃ dakṣiṇena ca pāṇinā |
iti brahmapurāṇavacanāt | atraiva hetuḥ 'upavītatvād' iti | yathā dakṣiṇahastena karma kurvan vāmaskandhasthitena yajñopavītenopavītī bhavati tathā vāmahastena karma kurvan dakṣiṇaskandhasthitena yajñopavītenopavītī bhavati | 'dakṣiṇena vā savyopagṛhītena' dakṣiṇenaivāvāmahastena vāmahastānvārabdhenārghyān dadyāt | 'prasṛṣṭāsu' dattāsv apsu | 'noddharet prathamaṃ pātraṃ' iti – prathamaṃ yathoktoddharaṇakālāt ||
baijavāpagṛhyam |
annaṃ saṃskṛtya brāhmaṇāna yugmān anavadyān anūcānān yathārham upaveśya prācīnāvītī pātrāṇy appūrṇāni sadarbhāṇi satilāni paścād agner darbheṣu nidhāya tiṣṭhan pitṝn āvāhayiṣyāmīty āmantryośantas tvety anayā yajamānasya pitaraṃ pitāmahaṃ nāmabhir āvāhyāyantu naḥ pitara iti japtvā pātrāṇy anudiśati | pitar etat te arghyaṃ pitāmahedaṃ te arghyaṃ prapitāmahedaṃ te arghyam iti brāhmaṇāñjaliṣu pātrāṇi ninīya pitṛbhyo ’kṣayam astv iti śeṣaṃ darbheṣv avanejayati ||
[150] 'śeṣam' arghyaṃ dattvāvaśiṣṭam arghyaṃ pātrasthame va | 'darbheṣu' arghyapātradhāraṇārtham upakḷpteṣu ||
śāṅkhāyanagṛhayam [4,1,2-4] |
brāhmaṇān vedavido ’yugmān tryavarārghyān pitṛvad upaveśyāyugmāny udapātrāṇi tilair anvavakīryāsāv etat te ity anudiśya brāhmaṇānāṃ pāṇau ninayet ||
'tryavarārghyāḥ’ trayo ‘varārghyā nikṛṣṭasaṃkhyā yeṣu teṣu brāhmaṇeṣu |
tathā nigamaḥ |
śvaḥśvo nāpitābhyaṅgonmardanasnānadantadhāvanādibhir abhyarcya snātān āgatān pādyārghyācamanīyadarbhāsanānulepanadhūpasumanobhir abhyarcya pitrādīn āvāhahiṣya iti pṛcchati | pratipitṝn ekaikaṃ mātur mātāmahāṃś ca vṛddhāvabhyāse darbhān dviguṇān āsaneṣu pradāya pādebhyo ’nyāṃś collikhya dakṣiṇālekhāyāṃ kṛtvā lauhāṃś camasāṃś caturaḥ sraktilapayodadhimadhughṛta-miśrān mahāvyāhṛtyāpo hi ṣṭhā śanno devīr ity adbhiḥ prapūrya tābhir vyatiṣaṅgam avadānavad dhutvā hasteṣv apo ’bhiṣiñcaty amuṣyeti nāmagrāhaṃ caturthena mātāmahādīnām ||
'śvaḥśvaḥ' nimantraṇadivasād dvitīyadine | 'nāpitābhyarcanaṃ' nakhakalpanādinā | 'vṛddhāvabhyāse' vṛddhinimittake 'abhyāse' āvṛttau śrāddhasya, mātṛmātāmahādīnāṃ pratidaivatam āvāhanādi kartavyam | 'anyān lauhān' tāmrādimayān | 'catura' iti pitrāditrayasya pātratrayam, mātāmahādīnāṃ caikam eva, 'caturthena mātāmahādīnām' ityabhidhānāt | 'srak' grathitakusumāni | 'tābhiḥ' adbhiḥ 'vyatiṣaṅgaṃ' [151] kṛtvā iti śeṣaḥ | 'avadānavat' avadānadharmeṇa ||1
śaṅkhalikhitau |
apasavyaṃ vāsoyajñopavīte kṛtvā tilair avakīrya savasurabhitilonmiśraṃ pātreṣūdakaṃ datvā śan no devīr iti brāhmaṇān anujñāpya pitṝn āvāhayiṣyāmīty uśantas tvety āvāhya pavitrāntariteṣu brāhmaṇahasteṣūdakaṃ ninayet ||
brahmapurāṇe |
devān āvāhaviṣye ’haṃ prāhur āvāyatsu ca |
viprāṅguṣṭhaṃ gṛhītvā tu viśvedevān samāhvayet ||
tato mantraṃ japen maunī viśvedevāsa āgata |
dvitīyaṃ ca japen mantraṃ viśvedevāḥ śṛṇuteti ca ||
japet tṛtīyaṃ mantraṃ tu paurāṇaṃ puṇyavardhanam |
oṃ āgacchantu mahābhāgā viśvedevā varapradāḥ ||
ye yatra yojitāḥ śrāddhe sāvadhānā bhavantu te |
idaṃ vaḥ pādyam arghyaṃ ca puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ vilepanam ||
ayaṃ dīpaprakāśaś ca viśvedevāḥ samarpyate |
pitṝn āvāhayiṣye ’haṃ śeṣān viprān vadet tataḥ ||
[152]        āvāhayeti coktas taiḥ sāvadhānā bhavantv iti |
idaṃ vaḥ pādyam arghyaṃ ca caturthyantaṃ nivedayet ||
pitṝn pitāmahān yakṣye bhojanena yathākramam |
prapitāmahān sarvāṃś ca tatpitṝṃś cānupūrvaśaḥ ||
uśantas tveti ca japan pitṝn āvāhayet tataḥ |
somavanto barhiṣada agniṣvāttāś ca ye ’pare ||
pitaraḥ puṇyayaśasaḥ sarve ’py āyantu nas tv iti |
tatas tilān gṛhe tasmin vikirec cāpradakṣiṇam ||
śraddhayā parayā yukto japed apahatās tv iti |
tato yajñiyavṛkṣoktapātreṣu sakuśeṣu ca ||
gṛhītvā yaḥ pavitrāṃś ca śan no devīṃ japan kṣipet |
vikiret teṣu ca tilān tilo ’sīti japakramāt ||
arghyaiḥ puṣpaiś ca gandhaiś ca tāḥ prapūjyāś ca śāstravat |
tato vāmena hastena gṛhītvā camasān kramāt ||
pitṛtīrthena tat toyaṃ dakṣiṇena ca pāṇinā |
dattadarbhodake haste viprebhyaś ca pṛthak pṛthak ||
dadyān mantraṃ japaṃś cātha yā divyā āpa ity api |
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam astu tilodakam ||
eṣa te ’rghyam iti procya tebhyo dadyād athāṣṭadhā |
jalaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ tilataṇḍulasarṣapāḥ ||
kuśāgrāṇi ca puṇyāni dattvācāmet tataḥ svayam |
[ayaṃ vo gandha ity uktvā gandhaṃ dadyāt suśobhanam] ||
idaṃ ca puṣpam ity uktvā puṣpāṇi ca nivedayet |
ayaṃ vo dhūpa ity uktvā tadagre tu dahet tataḥ ||
ayaṃ vo dīpa ity uktvā dīpaṃ hṛdyaṃ nivedayet |
anaṅgalagnaṃ sadvastraṃ vibhave tadyugaṃ śubham ||
[153]        [idaṃ vo vastram ity uktvā tritayaṃ vā nivedayet] |
idaṃ vo mālyam ity uktvā dadyān mālyaṃ suśobhanam ||
śvetacandanakarpūrakusumāni śubhāni ca |
vilepanārthaṃ dadyāt tu yac cānyat pitṛvallabham ||
'maunī' vihitetaravāgvyāharaṇanivṛttaḥ | ‘āgacchantu' ityādikas tu tṛtīyo mantraḥ viśvedevotpattināmaviśeṣāsane boddhavyaḥ | 'utpattiṃ nāma caiteṣām' ityādi bṛhaspativacanānusārāt | 'caturthyantaṃ' amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam iti prakāreṇa | 'sakuśeṣu' pavitrasvarūpakuśasahiteṣu | 'śāstravat' vihitair gandhapuṣpādibhir ity arthaḥ | 'aṣṭadhā' aṣṭaprakārakatvam atra dravyāṣṭakanibandhanam | 'yac cānyat pitṛvallabham' rajatādi, jīvatāṃ vā yad abhilaṣitaṃ pitrādīnām ||
tathā |
teṣu saṃsravapātreṣu jalapūrṇeṣu tatra saḥ |
putrakāmo mukhaṃ paśyen mantraṃ pūrvamukho japet ||
śundhantāṃ lokāḥ pitṛsadanāḥ pitṛsadanam asīti ca ||
tatas teṣv arghyapātreṣu sapidhāneṣu tatpitṝn |
pūjayet pitṛpūrvaṃ tu pādyārghyakusumādibhiḥ ||
tathā |
srastabhāṇḍāni varjyāni pitṛdaivatakarmaṇi |
pṛthivī pitṛbhir dugdhā pātre raupyamaye purā ||
[154]        svadhāmantraṃ ca tat tasmāt tebhyaḥ priyataraṃ sadā |
raupyapātreṇa pādyādi tasmāt sūkṣme ’pi kārayet ||
dattvā hemamaye pātre rūpavān syāt sa mānavaḥ |
dattvā ratnamaye pātre sarvaratnādhipo bhavet |
pālāśe brahmavarcasvī cāśvatthe rājyam āpnuyāt ||
pātra audumbare dattvā sarvabhūtādhipo bhavet |
dattvā nyagrodhapātre tu prajāṃ tuṣṭiṃ śriyaṃ labhet ||
rakṣoghne kāśmarīpātre datvā puṇyaṃ labhed yaśaḥ |
saubhāgyaṃ cātha mādhūke phalgupātre ca sampadam ||
śvetārkamandāramaye dattvā ca matimān bhavet |
bilvapātre dhanaṃ buddhiṃ dīrgham āyur avāpnuyāt ||
atha padmapuṭe datvā munīnāṃ vallabho bhavet |
sikte madhughṛtābhyāṃ ca yathāsaṃbhavam eva vā ||
'srastabhāṇḍaṃ' viśīrṇabhāṇḍam | 'phalgupātraṃ' kāṣṭhodumbarapātram |
vāyupurāṇe |
tathārghyapiṇḍabhojyeṣu pitṝṇāṃ rajataṃ varam |
amaṅgalyaṃ prayatnena devakāryeṣu varjayet ||
varjayed ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ [ 79,5-11] | [155]
ugragandhīny agandhīni kaṇṭakijātāni raktāni puṣpāṇi ca | sitāni sugandhīni kaṇṭakijātāny api jalajāni raktāny api dadyāt | vasāmajjādyaṃ dīpārthaṃ na dadyāt | jīvajaṃ ca sarvaṃ na dhūpārthe | tailaghṛte dadyāt | madhughṛtasahitaṃ gugguluṃ dadyāt | candanakuṅkumakarpūrāgurupadmakāny anulepāya ||
'vasāmajjādyaṃ' ityādyaśabdād anyad api jīvajam evaṃvidhaṃ varjyam iti gamyate | 'tailaghṛte dadyāt', dīpārtham iti śeṣaḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ |
jalodbhavāni deyāni raktāny āpi viśeṣataḥ |
ūrṇāsūtraṃ pradātavyaṃ kārpāsam athavā navam ||
daśāṃ vivarjayet prājño yady apy ahatavastrajām |
ugragandhīny agandhīni caityavṛkṣodbhavāni ca ||
puṣpāṇi varjanīyāni raktavarṇāni yāni ca |
ghṛtena dīpo dātavyas tilatailena vā punaḥ ||
dhūpārthe gugguluṃ dadyāt ghṛtayuktaṃ madhūtkaṭam |
[156] brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
śuklāḥ sumanasaḥ śreṣṭhās tathā padmotpalāni ca |
gandharūpopapannāni yāni cānyāni kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
japādikusumaṃ bhāṇḍī rūṣikā ca kuraṇṭakaḥ |
puṣpāṇi varjanīyāni śrāddhakarmaṇi nityaśaḥ ||
japādiśabdād evaṃvidhaṃ raktakusumam | 'bhāṇḍī' mañjiṣṭhā | 'rūṣikā' arkapuṣpam | 'kuruṇṭakaḥ' pītāmlātakapuṣpam |
bahmapurāṇe |
ugragandhīny agandhīni duṣṭāni ca vivarjayet |
candanāguruṇī cobhe tathaivośīrapadmakam ||
turuṣkaṃ gugguluṃ caiva ghṛtāktaṃ yugapad dahet |
ghṛtaṃ na kevalaṃ dadyād duṣṭaṃ vā tṛṇaguggulum ||
'turuṣkaṃ ' siṃhakarasaḥ | 'tṛṇagugguluḥ' sarjarasaḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
loke śreṣṭhatamaṃ sarvam ātmanaś cāpi yat priyam |
sarvaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dātavyaṃ tad evākṣayam icchatā ||
jāmbūnadamayaṃ divyaṃ vimānaṃ sūryasaṃnibham |
divyāpsarobhiḥ saṃpūrṇam annado labhate ’kṣayam ||
ācchādanaṃ tu yo dadyād ahataṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
āyuḥ prākāmyam aiśvaryaṃ rūpaṃ ca labhate śubham ||
[157]        yajñopavītaṃ yo dadyāc chrāddhakāle tu dharmavit |
pāvanaṃ sarvaviprāṇāṃ brahmadānasya tat phalam ||
kṛtaṃ vipreṇa yo dadyāc chrāddhakāle kamaṇḍalum |
madhukṣīrasravā dhenur dātāram anugacchati ||
cakraviddhaṃ tu yo dadyāc chrāddhakāle kamaṇḍalum |
dhenuṃ sa labhate divyāṃ ghaṭopakṛtadohanām ||
tūlapūrṇe tu yo dadyāt pāduke śrāddhakarmaṇi |
śobhanaṃ labhate yānaṃ pādayoḥ sukham eva ca ||
vyajanaṃ tālavṛntaṃ ca dadyād viprāya satkṛtam |
prāpnuyāt sparśayuktāni sugandhīni mṛdūni ca ||
śrāddheṣūpānahau dadyād brāhmaṇebhyaḥ sadā budhaḥ |
divyaṃ ca labhate cakṣur vājiyuktāṃs tathā rathān ||
śreṣṭhaṃ chatraṃ ca yo dadyāt puṣpamālāvibhūṣitam |
prāsādo hy uttamo bhūtvā gacchantam anugacchati ||
śaraṇaṃ ratnasaṃpūrṇaṃ saśayyāsanabhojanam |
śrāddhe datvā pitṛbhyas tu nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate ||
muktāvaidūryavāsāṃsi ratnāni vividhāni ca |
vāhanāni ca mukhyāni ayutāny arbudāni ca ||
sumahajjvalanaprakhyaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam |
candrasūryaprabhaṃ divyaṃ vimānaṃ labhate dhruvam ||
apsarobhiḥ parivṛtaṃ kāmagaṃ tu manojavam |
sa vasan vai vimānāgre stūyamānaḥ surottamaiḥ ||
divyaiḥ puṣpaiḥ prasiñcanti pūrṇavṛṣṭibhir eva vā |
gandharvāpsarasas tatra gāyantyo vādayanti ca ||
kanyāyuvatimadhyasthāhasitābharaṇasvanaiḥ |
susvanais te vibodhyante satataṃ hi manoramaiḥ ||
aśvadānasahasreṇa rathadānaśatena ca |
[158]        dantināṃ hi sahasreṇa yogiṣv āvasatho varaḥ ||
dadyāt pavitraṃ yogibhyo yas tūjjvalanam ambhasi |
atha niṣkasahasrasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
jīvitasya pradānād dhi nānyadānaṃ viśiṣyate |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena deyaṃ prāṇābhirakṣaṇam ||
ahiṃsā sarvadaivatyā pavitrā sarvadāyinī |
dānaṃ hi jīvitasyāhur dānānāṃ paramaṃ budhāḥ ||
lavaṇena suvarṇāni śrāddhe pātrāṇi dāpayet |
rasās tam upatiṣṭhanti bhakṣyaṃ saubhāgyam eva ca ||
tilān ikṣūṃs tathā bhojyaṃ śrāddhe satkṛtya dāpayet |
mitrāṇi labhate loke strīṣu saubhāgyam eva ca ||
pātraṃ vai taijasaṃ dadyān manojñaṃ śrāddhabhojane |
pātraṃ bhavati kāmānāṃ vidyānāṃ ca dhanasya ca ||
rājataṃ kāñcanaṃ caiva dadyāc chrāddheṣu yaḥ pumān |
datvā ca labhate dānāt prākāmyaṃ dhanam eva ca ||
dhenuṃ śrāddheṣu yo dadyād gṛṣṭiṃ kumbhopadohanīm |
gāvas tam upatiṣṭhanti gavāṃ puṣṭis tathaiva ca ||
dadyād yaḥ śiśire hy agniṃ bahukāṣṭhaṃ prayatnataḥ |
kāyāgnidīptiprākāmyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ rūpam eva ca ||
indhanāni ca yo dadyād dvijebhyaḥ śiśirāgame |
nityaṃ jayati saṃgrāme śriyā yuktaś ca dīpyate ||
surabhīṇi tu snānāni gandhavanti tathaiva ca |
pūrayitvā tu pātrāṇi śrāddhe satkṛtya dāpayet ||
gandhavāhā mahānadyaḥ sukhāni vividhāni ca |
dātāram upatiṣṭhanti yuvatyaś ca pativratāḥ ||
śayanāsanayānāni bhūmayo vāhanāni ca |
śrāddhe hy etāni yo dadyād aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
[159]        vanaṃ puṣpaphalopetaṃ datvā gosavam aśnute |
kūpārāmataḍāgāni kṣetraghoṣagṛhāṇi ca ||
tān datvā modate svarge nityam ācandratārakam |
svāstīrṇaśayanaṃ datvā śrāddhe ratnavibhūṣitam ||
pitaras tasya tuṣyanti sukhaṃ cānantyam aśnute |
asmiṃl loke vasan modet syandanais tu savāhanaiḥ ||
rājabhiḥ pūjyate vāpi dhanair dhānyaiś ca vardhate |
varṇakauśeyapatrorṇe tathā prāvārakambalaḥ ||
ajinaṃ kṣaumajaṃ paṭṭuṃ praveṇīmṛgalomikāḥ |
datvā caitāni viprebhyo bhojayitvā yathāvidhi ||
prāpnoti śraddadhānas tu vājapeyasya yat phalam |
vidyā nāryaḥ surūpāś ca putrā bhṛtyāś ca kiṃkarāḥ ||
vaśe tiṣṭhanti bhūtāni asmiṃl loke tv anāmayam |
kṣaumakauśeyakārpāsaṃ dukūlam ahataṃ tathā ||
śrāddhe hy etāni yo dadyāt kāmān āpnoty anuttamān |
alakṣmīṃ nāśayaty āśu tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā ||
bhrājate ca vimānāgre nakṣatreṣv iva candramāḥ |
vāso hi sarvadaivatyaṃ sarvadeveṣv abhiṣṭutam ||
vastrābhāve kriyā nāsti yajñā vedās tapāṃsi ca |
tasmād vastrāṇi deyāni śrāddhakāle viśeṣataḥ ||
'prākāmyaṃ' aiśvaryaviśeṣaḥ | 'gṛṣṭiḥ' prathamaprasūtā gauḥ | 'varṇakauśeyaṃ' pītādivarṇakauśeyam | 'prāvārakambalaḥ ' ācchādanayogyaḥ sūkṣmakambalaḥ | ' ajinaṃ' kaṭisūtram | 'kṣaumajaṃ' atisūkṣmapaṭṭanirmitaṃ carma iti prasiddham | 'praveṇī'
gajāstaraṇakambalaḥ ||
tathā |
rājataṃ rajatāktaṃ vā pitṝṇāṃ pātram ucyate |
[160]        rajatasya kathā vāpi darśanaṃ dānam eva ca ||
anantam akṣayaṃ svargaṃ rājataṃ dānam ucyate ||
brahmapurāṇe |
maṇḍalāni ca kāryāṇi nīvāraiś cūrṇakaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
gauramṛttikayā vāpi praṇītenātha bhasmanā ||
pāṣāṇacūrṇasaṃkīrṇam āhataṃ tatra varjayet ||
[161]
|| 17 – a ||
|| athānnaukaraṇam ||
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ [1,236-238] |
agnau kariṣya ādāya pṛcchaty annaṃ ghṛtaplutam |
kuruṣvety abhyanujñāto hutvāgnau pitṛyajñavat ||
hutaśeṣaṃ pradadyāt tu bhājaneṣu samāhitaḥ |
yathālābhopapanneṣu raupyeṣu ca viśeṣataḥ ||
'agnau kariṣye' iti pṛcchati | 'ghṛtaplutam annam ādāya' ity anvayaḥ | 'pitṛyajñavat' piṇḍapitṛyajñāṅgabhūte hone yāvatī itikartavyatā tayā ity arthaḥ | 'yathālābhopapaneṣu' vihitānām anyatameṣu ||
āśvalāyanaḥ [gṛ. sū. 4,9, 2-9] |
uddhṛtya ghṛtāktam annam anujñāpayaty agnau kariṣye karavai karavāṇīti [vā ] | pratyabhyanujñā kriyatāṃ kuruṣva kurv iti | athāgnau juhoti yathoktaṃ purastāt | abhyanujñātāyāṃ pāṇiṣv eva vā | agnimukhā vai devāḥ pāṇimukhāḥ pitara iti ha brāhmaṇam | yadi prāṇiṣv ācānteṣv anyad annam anudiśati | annam anne | sṛṣṭaṃ dattam ādhnukam iti ||
[162] 'kariṣye' ityādipraśnatraye 'kriyatā' ityādyuttaratraye ca yathāsaṅkhyaṃ prayoganiyamaḥ | anyathā paryāyapadānāṃ sahopādānavaiyarthyāt | 'yathoktaṃ purastād' iti, prācīnāvītīdhmam upasamādhāya ity āśvalāyanaśrautasūtre | 'pāṇiṣv eva vā' ity agnyabhāve boddhavyam,         agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇāv evopapādayet |
iti manuvacanāt [3,212]  | 'yadi pāṇiṣu” iti, yadi pāṇiṣu hūyate tadā 'ācānteṣv anyad annam ' 'anudiśati' dadāti | idam ācamanīyaṃ piṇḍapradānāntaraṃ ca naimittikam | na tu dattasya bhakṣaṇāśaṅkā kartavyā,
annaṃ pāṇitale dattaṃ pūrvam aśnanty abuddhayaḥ |
ity atraiva nindāśruteḥ ||
baijavāpagṛhyam |
yajñopavīty agnau karavāṇīty āmantryānnaukaraṇaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñavat | āvṛto vā sarvam anukrāmet ||
nigamaḥ |
avanejyānnam udhṛyājyenābhighāryāgnau kariṣyāmīti pṛcchatiti | kuruṣvety anujñātaḥ samitpūrvakam agnāv āhutīr juhoti ||
'avanejya' arghyapradānānantaraṃ brāhmaṇahaste jalaṃ datvā ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
dhūpagandhamālyair alaṃkṛtya brāhmaṇān saghṛtam annaṃ samanujñāpya darbhair dakṣiṇāmukhair agniṃ paristīrya juhuyāt | yamāyāṅgirase pitṛmate svāhā | agnaye kavyavāhanāya svāhā | somāya pitṛmate svāheti ||
[163] hārītaḥ |
āsādyodapātreṣu samanyāyantīty apa āsicya sumanasaś cotpūya brāhmaṇair anujñātaḥ samītsvetīdhmam ādhāyāvāhyāgniṣvāttādīn brāhmaṇasthān udapātrair yathārtham arcayitvā mekṣaṇapāṇir anujñāyate | yat somāya pitṛmate juhoti somajyeṣṭhān tena somapāṃś ca pitṝn prīṇāti | yad yamāyāṅgirase tena yamajyeṣṭhān barhaṣadas tenāgniṣvāttāṃś ca | yad agniṃ kavyavāhanaṃ sviṣṭakṛtaṃ tena yan mekṣaṇam ādadhaty ātmānaṃ prīṇāti | hutocchiṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradāya piṇḍebhyo ’vaśeṣayet ||
'āsādya' udapātrāṇi | 'utpūya' prokṣaṇīvan mantravarjam | 'anujñātaḥ samītsveti', agniṃ samītsva iti brāhmaṇair anujñātaḥ ||
[164] viṣṇuḥ [73, 5- 14] |
āmaśrāddheṣu kāmyeṣu ca prathamapañcakenāgniṃ hutvā | paśuśrāddheṣu madhyamapañcakena | amāvāsyāṃ [amāvāsyāsu] uttamapañcakena | āgrahāyaṇyā ūrdhvaṃ kṛṣṇāṣṭakāsu ca krameṇaiva prathamamadhyamottamapañcakaiḥ | [anva]ṣṭakāsu ca | tato brāhmaṇānujñātaḥ pitṝn āvāhayet | āyantu surā [apayantv asurā] iti dvābhyāṃ tilair yātudhānavivarjanaṃ [yātudhānānāṃ visarjanaṃ] kṛtvā | etad vaḥ [eta] pitaraḥ sarvāṃs tān agna ā me yantu eta [etadvaḥ] pitara ity āvāhanaṃ kṛtvā kuśatilamiśreṇa gandhodakena yās tiṣṭhanty amṛtā vāg iti yan me māteti ca pādyaṃ [nirvartya nivedyārghyaṃ kṛtvā] nivedya [ca] anulepanavastra[kuśastilavastra]puṣpālaṃkāradhūpaiḥ [dhūpadīpair yathā]śaktyā viprān samabhyarcya [ghṛtaplutam annam ādaya] ādityā [ rudrā ] vasava iti vīkṣyāgnau kariṣya ity uktvā [karavāṇīty uktvā] anujñātaś ca vipraiḥ [tatra vipraiḥ] prāgvad agnāv āhutitrayaṃ hutvā [kurv ity ukta āhutivaraṃ dadyāt] | ye māmakāḥ pitaraḥ etad vaḥ pitaro ’yaṃ yajña iti ca sarvābhimantraṇaṃ [165] [haviranumantraṇaṃ] kṛtvā [vṛtvā] yathopapanneṣu pātreṣu viśeṣād rajatamayeu [annaṃ] namo viśvebhyo devebhya ity annam ādau prāṅmukhayor nivedayet | pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya [ca] nāmagotrābhyām udaṅmukheṣu ||
'āmaśrāddhaṃ' laugākṣigṛhyānusāreṇa jyotiṣṭomādāv ārambhe nāndīmukhaśrāddhaṃ māṃsarahitaṃ yat kriyate | 'kāmyaśrāddhaṃ' pañcamyādau putrārthinā kriyamāṇam | 'prathamapañcakena' iti, iyam eva seti pañcadaśarco ’nuvākaḥ taittarīyake kāṭhake ca paṭhyate | tasya yathākramaṃ prathamadvitīyatṛtīyapañcakāḥ sthālīpākenāgnaukaraṇahome viniyujyante | 'paśuśrāddhaṃ' samāpte ’bde paśuśrāddhaṃ vidhivat pratipādayed iti hārītoktaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaśrāddham | 'ādityā vasava iti vīkṣya' 'ādityā vasava' ity anena mantreṇa brāhmaṇān īkṣitvā ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2, 14, 7, 14] |
athainās tilamiśrā apaḥ pratigrāhya gandhair mālyena cālaṃkṛtyāgnau kariṣyāmīty anujñāto ’gnim upasamādhāya saṃparistīrya [+ agnimukhāt kṛtvāyasyaiva] tisra āhutīr juhoti – "somāya pitṛmate [pitṛpītāya] svadhā namaḥ svāhā | yamāyāṅgirasvate pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ svāhā | agnaye kavyavāhanāya sviṣṭakṛte svadhā namaḥ [166] svāhā” iti | taccheṣeṇānnam abhighāryānnasyaitā eva tisro [+ āhutīḥ] juhuyāt | vayasāṃ piṇḍān [ṇḍaṃ] dadyāt | "vayasāṃ hi pitaraḥ pratimayā caranti" iti vijñāyate | athetarānnaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya brāhmaṇasyāṅguṣṭhenānudiśati | pṛthivīsamaṃ tasya[+te] agnir upadraṣṭar caste mahimā dattasyāpramādāya pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brāhmaṇasya [brahmaṇas tvā]] mukhe ’mṛte ’mṛtaṃ juhomi brāhmaṇānāṃ tvā vidyāvatāṃ  prāṇāpānayor juhomy akṣitam asi mā pitṝṇāṃ kṣeṣṭhā amutrāmuṣmin loka iti | pitṛbhyaḥ svadhā namaḥ | dvitīyam anudiśati | antarikṣasamaṃ tasya te [+vāyur upaśrotā] yajūṃṣi te mahimā dattasyāpramādāya | pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brāhmaṇasya mukhe amṛtaṃ [brahmaṇas tvā mukhe] juhomi brāhmaṇānāṃ tvā vidyāvatāṃ prāṇāpānayor juhomy akṣitam asi mā pitāmahānāṃ kṣeṣṭhā amutrāmuṣmin loke iti | tṛtīyam anudiśati | dyausamaṃ tasya ta ādityo ’nukhyātā sāmāni te mahimā dattasyāpramādāya pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brahmaṇas tvā mukhe amṛtaṃ juhomi brāhmaṇānāṃ tvā vidyāvatāṃ prāṇāpānayor juhomy akṣitam asi mā prapitāmahānāṃ kṣeṣṭhā amutrāmuṣmin loke iti ||
[167]        atrāpy udāharanti —
agnaukaraṇaśeṣeṇa tad annam abhighārayet |
tasmiṃs tu prāśite dadyād annaṃ yat prakṛtaṃ bhavet |
niraṅguṣṭhaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ tan na prīṇayate pitṝn ||
'atha' upaveśanānantaram | 'taccheṣeṇa' hutājyaśeṣeṇa | 'vayasām' ityādinā anaukaraṇahomānantaram eva piṇḍapradānaṃ kṛtvā tān piṇḍān pakṣibhyo dadyād ity uktam | eṣa ca samācāro baudhāyanīnāṃ kvacic chrāddhe | 'vayasāṃ pratimayā' pakṣiṇāṃ rūpeṇa || paiṭhīnasiḥ |
'devāṃś ca pitṝṃś cāvāhayiṣya' ity anujñāpya saumyāḥ santv ity uktvāgnau kariṣyāmīty anujñāpya kuruṣvety ukte, 'havir ity evaṃ sāṅguṣṭham annavidhiṃ havir’ ity uktvā, brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt ||
'havir ity evaṃ sāṅguṣṭham annavidhiṃ havir ity uktvā’ yaḥ sāṅguṣṭho annavidhir annaprakāraḥ taṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyād ity arthaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
tato ghṛtāktam annaṃ ca soṣṇam ādāya sādhitam |
tān agnau kariṣya iti prayataḥ prārthayan dvijān ||
oṃ kuruṣveti tair ukto dakṣiṇāgniṃ samāhvayet |
dakṣiṇāgneś caturdikṣu darbhān āstīrya vāgyataḥ ||
dadyāc ca samidhas tisras tasmin prādeśamātrikāḥ ||
palāśaḥ samidarthaṃ ca niyojyaḥ pitṛkarmaṇi ||
ghṛtāktāḥ samidho hutvā dakṣiṇāgrāḥ samantrakāḥ |
mekṣaṇena ghṛtāktena juhuyād āhutitrayam ||
tathā kṣuttṛṭkrodhayuto hīnamantro juhoti yaḥ |
aprabuddhe sadhūme vā so ’ndhaḥ syād anyajanmani ||
svalpe rūkṣe sphuliṅge vā vāmāvarte bhayānake |
ārdrakāṣṭhaiś ca sampūrṇe phūtkāravati pāvake ||
kṛṣṇārciṣi sadurgandhe tathā lihati medinīm |
āhutiṃ juhuyād yas tu tasya nāśo bhaved dhruvam ||
vāyupurāṇe |
anujñātaḥ kuruṣveti tathaiva dvijasattamaiḥ |
patnīmādāya putrāṃś ca juhuyād dhavyavāhanam ||
tathā |
āhṛtya dakṣiṇāgniṃ tu homārthaṃ vai prayatnataḥ |
agnyarthaṃ laukikaṃ vāpi juhuyāt karmasiddhaye ||
[169] 'agnyarthaṃ' agnaukaraṇahomārtham | 'laukikaḥ ' āvasathyaḥ |
tathā |
puṣpāṇāṃ ca phalānāṃ ca bhakṣyāṇām annatas tathā |
agram uddhṛtya sarveṣāṃ juhuyād dhavyavāhanam ||
dakṣiṇato ’gnaye nityaṃ somāyottaratas tathā |
etayor antare nityaṃ juhuyād vai vivasvate ||
tathā |
palāśaphalgunyagrodhaplakṣāśvatthavikaṅkatāḥ |
udumbarās tathā bilvaś candanaṃ yajñiyāś ca ye ||
saralo devadāruś ca sālaś ca khadiras tathā |
samidarthe praśastāḥ syur ete vṛkṣā viśeṣataḥ ||
grāhyāḥ kaṇṭakinaś caiva yajñiyā ye ca kecana |
pūjitāḥ samidarthe tu pitṝṇāṃ vacanaṃ yathā ||
samidbhiḥ ṣaṭphalotthābhir juhuyād yo hutāśanam |
phalaṃ yat karmaṇas tasya tan me nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
akṣayaṃ sarvakāmīyam aśvamedhaphalaṃ hi tat |
[170]        śleṣmātako naktamālaḥ kapitthaḥ śālmalis tathā ||
nīpo vibhītakaś caiva śrāddhakarmaṇi garhitāḥ |
ciribilvas tathāṅkolas tindukāmrātakau tathā ||
bakulaḥ kovidāraś ca ete śrāddheṣu garhitāḥ |
nivāsā yeṣu kīṭānām ayajīyāś ca garhitāḥ ||
gulmaiś ca veṣṭitā ye vai vallībhiś ca samāvṛtāḥ |
śakunīnāṃ nivāsāś ca varjayet tān mahīruhān ||
anyāṃś caivaṃvidhān sarvān ayajñīyāṃś ca varjayet |
matsyapurāṇe [18, 32-33] |
agnau kuryād anujñāto vipro viprair yathāvidhi |
svagṛhyeoktena vidhinā kāṃsye kṛtvā caruṃ tataḥ ||
agnīṣomayamābhyāṃ tu kuryād āpyāyanaṃ tataḥ |
dakṣiṇāgnau praṇīte vā ya ekāgnir dvijottamaḥ ||
'praṇīte' āvasathyāt pṛthakkṛte | 'ekāgniḥ’ āvasathyamātravān | ayaṃ cāgnaukaraṇahoma āhitāgner dakṣiṇāgnāv eva | tadasambhave āvasathyena | tasyāpy asambhave brāhmaṇapāṇyādau ||
[171] viṣṇupurāṇe [3,15,26] |
juhuyād vayañjanakṣāravarjam annaṃ tato ’nale |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
agnaukaraṇahomaś ca kartavya upavītinā |
prāṅmukhenaiva devebhyo juhotīti śrutiśruteḥ ||
apasavyena vā kāryo dakṣiṇābhimukhena tu |
nirupya havir anyasmād anyasmai na hi hūyate ||
svāhā kuryān na cātrānte na caiva juhuyād dhaviḥ |
svāhākāreṇa hutvāgnau paścān mantraṃ samāpayet ||
'apasavyena vā kāryaḥ' iti yo ’yaṃ savyāpasavyavikalpaḥ svagṛhyānusāreṇa śākhābhedavyavasthito boddhavyaḥ | 'svāhā kuryād' ityāder ayam arthaḥ — ādāv eva svāhety uktvā 'somāya pitṛmate' iti mantraḥ samāpanīyaḥ | evaṃ 'svāhāgnaye kavyavāhanāya' ity api ||
manuḥ, śātātapaś ca prathame |
agnyabhāve tu viprasya prāṇāv evopapādayet |
yo hy agniḥ sa dvijo viprair mantravidbhir udāhṛtaḥ ||
akrodhanān suprasādān vadanty etān purātanān |
lokasyāpyāyane yuktān śrāddhadevān dvijottamān ||
'agnyabhāve' agnaukaraṇahomaviniyuktaśrautasmārtāgnidvayābhāve ||
[172] matsyapurāṇe
agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇau vātha jale ’pi vā |
ajākarṇe ’śvakarṇe vā goṣṭhe vātha śivāntike ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
pitrye yaḥ paṅktimūrdhanyastasya pāṇāv anagnikaḥ |
datvā mantravad anyeṣāṃ tūṣṇīṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet ||
yamaḥ |
agnaukaraṇaśeṣaṃ tu pitrye tu pratipādayet |
pratipādya pitṝṇāṃ tu na dadyād vaiśvadevike ||
