|| 18 ||
|| atha pariveṣaṇavidhiḥ ||
tatra paiṭhīnasiḥ |
brāhmaṇān alaṃkṛtya māṃsāpūpadadhighṛtamadhupāyasakṛsaraphalavikārān iti matsyāṃś copasādayet |
itiśabda ādyartho ‘tra śākādiparaḥ | 'upasādayet' upanayet, piṇḍapradānānantaram |
manuḥ, bṛhaspatiś ca ṣaṣṭhe
pāṇibhyāṃ tūpasaṃgṛhya svayam annasya vardhitam |
viprāntike pitṝn dhyāyan śanakair upanikṣipet ||
muktaṃ hy ubhābhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ yad annam upanīyate |
tad dhi pralumpanty asurāḥ sahasā duṣṭacetasaḥ ||
guṇāṃś ca sūpaśākādyān payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
vinyaset prayataḥ pūrvaṃ bhūmāv eva samāhitaḥ ||
[174]        bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ mūlāni ca phalāni ca |
hṛdyāni caiva māṃsāni pānāni surabhīṇi ca ||
upānīya tu tat sarvaṃ śanakaiḥ susamāhitaḥ |
pariveṣayec ca prayato guṇān sarvān pracodayan ||
yad yad roceta viprebhyas tat tad dadyād amatsaraḥ |
brahmodyāś ca kathāḥ kuryāt pitṝṇām etad īpsitam ||
'annasya' iti tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | 'vardhitaṃ' pūritam | 'ubhābhyāṃ muktaṃ' hastadvayenāsaṃbaddham | 'guṇān' apradhānān, bhojane annasya prādhānyāt |
devalaḥ |
atha piṇḍāvaśiṣṭānnaṃ vidhivad bhojayed dvijān |
bhojanaiḥ satilaiḥ snehair bhakṣyāpūpavimiśritaiḥ ||
tato ’nnaṃ ca yathākāmaṃ bhojayet tān dvijottamān |
sthitvaivaṃ nibhṛtaḥ kartā muditaḥ sādaraḥ śuciḥ ||
yamaḥ |
tato viśadam ānīya bhojayet prayato dvijān |
annaṃ sūpaṃ ghṛtaṃ śākaṃ māṃsaṃ dadhi payo madhu ||
tathā |
dhānāś ca madhusaṃyuktā ikṣūṃś caiva sagorasān |
śarkarāphalamūlaṃ ca sarvaṃ dadyād amatsaraḥ ||
'viśadaṃ' vimalam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tatas tilaimīsaiḥ śākaiḥ sūpaiḥ kṛsarapāyasāpūpair lajair bhavyaiḥ pānair madhunā ghṛtena dadhnā pāyasena ca prabhūtam iṣṭato ’nnaṃ dadyād anasūyaḥ ||
'iṣṭataḥ' icchātaḥ ||
gautamaḥ [15, 5-6] |
[kālaniyamaḥ] śaktitaḥ | prakarṣed guṇasaṃskāravidhīnannasya ||
'guṇasaṃskāravidhīn', 'guṇāḥ' vyañjanāni, 'saṃskārāḥ ' surabhitvādayaḥ, tān 'prakarṣet' sātiśayān kuryād ity arthaḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
uṣṇaṃ sviṣṭam annaṃ śaktyā vā dadyāt ||
suṣṭhu iṣṭaṃ 'sviṣṭam' |
śaṅkhaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā prayatnena tvarākrodhavivarjitaḥ |
uṣṇam annaṃ dvijātibhyaḥ śraddhayā vinivedayet ||
anyatra phalamūlebhyaḥ pānakebhyaś ca paṇḍitaḥ |
bhojayed vidhivat paścād gandhamālyojjvalān dvijān ||
'śraddhaṃ kṛtvā’ iti pūrvakālatā na vivakṣitā | 'anyatra phalamūlebhyaḥ' ity uṣṇāpekṣayā boddhavyam | tena phalamūlādikam anuṣṇam api deyam ||
[176] yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 240] |
annam iṣṭaṃ haviṣyaṃ ca dadyād akrodhano ’tvaraḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 36] |
saṃskṛtaṃ vyañjanādyaṃ ca payomadhughṛtānvitam |
śraddhayā dīyate yasmāc chrāddhaṃ tena nigadyate ||
āpastambaḥ [2, 19, 13-16, 20, 1-2] |
naiyamikaṃ [tu] śrāddhaṃ snehavad eva dadyāt | sarpir māsam iti prathamaḥ kalpaḥ | abhāve tailaṃ śākam iti | maghāsu cādhikaṃ śrāddhakalpena sarpir brāhmaṇān bhojayet | māsikaśrāddhe tilānāṃ droṇaṃ [droṇaṃ] yenopāyena śaknuyāt tenopayojayet | samudetāṃś ca bhojayet ||
'naiyamikam' amāvāsyāśrāddhaṃ, tat snehena vinā na dadyāt | 'adhikaṃ brāhmaṇān sarpir bhojayet śrāddhakalpena' ity anvayaḥ | maghāśrāddhe vaikalpikam api tailaṃ deyam iti tātparyam | 'tilānāṃ droṇam upayojayed' iti brāhmaṇabhūyastvapakṣe | 'yenopāyena śaknuyād' iti modakādiprakāreṇa | 'samudetān' guṇavataḥ ||
manuḥ [3, 229 - 230] |
nāsram āpātayej jātu na kupyen nānṛtaṃ vadet |
na pādena spṛśed annaṃ na caitam avadhūnayet ||
[177]        asraṃ gamayati pretān kopo ’rīn anṛtaṃ śunaḥ |
pādasparśas tu rakṣāṃsi duṣkṛtīn avadhūnanam ||
‘duṣkṛtīn’ pāpakāriṇaḥ |
devalaḥ |
asraṃ na pātayec chrāddhe na jalpen na hasen mithaḥ |
na vibhramen na ca krudhyen nodvijec cātra karhicit ||
prāpte ’pi kāraṇe śrāddhe naiva krodhaṃ samutsṛjet |
āśritaḥ svinnagātro vā na tiṣṭhet pitṛsaṃnidhau ||
na cātra śyenakākādīn pakṣiṇaḥ pratiṣedhayet |
tadrūpāḥ pitaras tatra samāyāntīti vaidikam ||
'kāraṇe' krodhasyeti śeṣaḥ | 'samutsṛjet ' abhivyañjyāt | 'āśritaḥ' apāśritaḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [81, 1-3] |
nānnam āsanam āropayet | na padā spṛśet | nāvakṣutaṃ kuryāt ||
yamaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ niveditaṃ dattaṃ bhuktaṃ japtaṃ tapaḥ śrutam |
yātudhānāḥ pralumpanti śaucabhraṣṭaṃ dvijanmanaḥ ||
tathā krodhena yad dattaṃ bhuktaṃ yat tvarayā punaḥ |
ubhayaṃ tad vilumpanti yātudhānāḥ sarākṣasāḥ ||
pitṝn āvāhayitvā tu nāyuktaprabhavo bhavet |
tasmān niyamya vācaṃ ca krodhaṃ ca śrāddham ācaret ||
na krodhaṃ kasyacit kuryāt kasmiṃścid api kāraṇe |
akruddhapariviṣṭaṃ hi śrāddhe prīṇayate pitṝn ||
'ayuktaprabhavaḥ' ayuktasyāsaṃbandhapralāpādeḥ kāraṇaṃ na bhaved ity arthaḥ ||
[178] baudhāyanaḥ |
aśraddhā paramaḥ pāpmā pāpmā hy ajñānam ucyate |
ajñānāl luptadharmaḥ syāl luptadharmo ’dhamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
śraddhayā śodhyate buddhiḥ śraddhayā śodhyate matiḥ |
śraddhayā prāpyate dharmaḥ śraddhā pāpmapramocanī ||
tasmād aśraddadhānasya havir nāśnanti devatāḥ |
'matiḥ' atrecchā, buddheḥ pṛthagupādānāt ||
kātyāyana |
śraddhānvitaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurvīta śākenāpi |
manuḥ [3, 275] |
yad yad dadāti vidhivat samyak chraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
tat tat pitṝṇāṃ bhavati paratrānantam akṣayam ||
yamaḥ |
yathā dhenusahasreṣu vatso vindati mātaram |
tathā śrāddheṣu miṣṭānnaṃ mantraḥ prāpayate pitṝn ||
manuḥ [3, 233] |
harṣayed brāhmaṇāṃs tuṣṭo bhojayec ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
annādyenāsakṛc caitān guṇaiś ca paricodayet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 100] |
bhakṣyabhojyaguṇān uktvā bhojayed brāhmaṇān śanaiḥ |
ākhyānaiḥ setihāsaiś ca pūrvavṛttaiś ca harṣayet ||
[179] āpastambaḥ [2, 17, 4] |
prayataḥ prasannamanāḥ sṛṣṭo bhojayed brāhmaṇān ||
'sṛṣṭaḥ' utsāhī |
yamaḥ, śātātapaś ca dvitīye |
yāvad dhaviṣyaṃ bhavati yāvat sviṣṭaṃ pradīyate |
tāvad aśnanti pitaro yāvan nāha dadāmy aham ||
brāhmaṇānnaṃ dadac chūdraḥ śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo dadat |
tayor annam abhojyaṃ tu bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
caturbhiḥ srauta āpanne hastenonmārjayed ghṛtam |
ubhāv eva tu bāsrautāṃ dātā bhoktā na saṃśayaḥ ||
srutaṃ nakhaiś caturbhiś ca yo dadyāt pāṇinā ghṛtam |
dātā puṇyaṃ na cāpnoti bhoktā pāpataraṃ vrajet ||
hastena ghṛtaṃ śaudraṃ yad api kim ānīyatāṃ tadapi śaudram |
śītalam annaṃ śaudraṃ śūdraiḥ pariveṣṭitaṃ bhavati śaudram ||
mākṣikaṃ phāṇitaṃ śākaṃ gorasaṃ lavaṇaṃ ghṛtam |
hastadattāni bhuktvā ca datvā sāntapanaṃ caret ||
hastadattā ca yā bhikṣā salilaṃ vyañjanāni ca |
bhuktvā tv aśucitāṃ yāti dātā svargaṃ na gacchati ||
'śaudraṃ' śūdrasyaiva śrāddhayogyam ity arthaḥ | 'kim ānīyatām' iti pṛṣṭvā yad ānīyate annādikaṃ tad api 'śaudram ' ||
viṣṇuḥ [ 79, 12-15] |
pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ ca na dadyāt | hastena ghṛtavyañjanādi | taijasāni pātrāṇi dadyāt | viśeṣato rājatāni ||
[180] śaṅkhaḥ |
saindhavaṃ lavaṇaṃ yac ca tathā mānasasaṃbhavam |
pavitre parame hy ete pratyakṣam api nityaśaḥ ||
śātātapo, vasiṣṭha-vṛddhaśātātapau prathame ca |
hastadattās tu ye snehā lavaṇaṃ vyañjanāni ca |
dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhanti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam ||
tasmād antaritaṃ deyaṃ parṇena ca tṛṇena vā |
na pradadyāt tu hastena nāyasena kadācana ||
vṛddhaśātātapa-laghuhārītau |
āyasena tu pātreṇa yad annaṃ saṃpradīyate |
bhoktā viṣṭhāsamaṃ bhuṅkte dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
manuḥ [3, 202] |
rājatair bhājanair eṣām atho vā rajatānvitaiḥ |
vāry api śraddhayā dattam akṣayāyopapadyate ||
hārītaḥ |
kāñcanena tu pātreṇa rājatodumbareṇa ca |
dattam akṣayatāṃ yāti khaḍgenāryakṛtena ca ||
'āryakṛtam' traivarṇikanirmitam anyad api pātram abhimatam |
viṣṇuḥ [ 79, 22] |
ghṛtādidāne taijasāni pātrāṇi [khaḍgapātrāṇi] phalgupātrāṇi ca praśastāni ||
[181] vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
pātre tu mṛnmaye yas tu śrāddhe bhojayate pitṝn |
tatra dātā purodhāś ca bhoktā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
masūramāṣakodravāś cety ayajñiyāḥ | lohānāṃ sīsakāyasapāṣāṇahīnapātrāṇi bhagnapātrāṇi ca ||
'lohānām' iti, yogyatayā sīsakāyasāpekṣayā nirdhāraṇam | 'hīnam' atikṣudram ||
hārītaḥ |
paṅktyāṃ caivopaviṣṭebhyaḥ samaṃ gandhādibhojanam |
na paktyāṃ viṣamaṃ dadyān na yācen na ca dāpayet ||
yācako dāpakaś caitāv ubhau narakagāminau |
bhujyata iti 'bhojanam' odanādi |
yamaḥ |
na paṅktyāṃ viṣamaṃ dadyān na yācen na ca dāpayet |
yācitā dāpitā dātā sarve nirayagāminaḥ ||
tasmād vidvān na vai dadyān nābhiyācen na dāpayet |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ viṣamaṃ yaḥ prayacchati ||
duṣkṛtaṃ harate paṅktyā dattaṃ gṛhṇāti yaś ca tat |
kunadīsetukārasya kanyāvighnakarasya ca ||
[182]        paṅktyāṃ viṣamadātuś ca niṣkṛtir nopapadyate |
'kunadī' svalpajalā, tatra setunā jalapravāhasyātyantavicchedāt tadupajīvināṃ bahūnāṃ pīḍhā jāyate |
hārītaḥ |
yas tv ekapaṅktyāṃ kurute viśeṣaṃ
snehād bhayād vā yadi vārthahetoḥ |
ṛṣipraṇītāṃ smṛtivedadṛṣṭāṃ
tāṃ brahmahatyāṃ munayo vadanti ||
]183]
|| 18 – a ||
|| athānnasaṃkalpaḥ |
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 238] |
datvānnaṃ pṛthivīpātram iti pātrābhimantraṇam |
kṛtvedaṃ viṣṇur ity anne dvijāṅguṣṭhaṃ nivedayet ||
yamaḥ |
aṅguṣṭhamātro bhagavān viṣṇuḥ paryaṭate mahīm |
rākṣasānāṃ vadhārthāya ko me ’dya prahariṣyati ||
tasmāc chrāddheṣu sarveṣu tv aṅguṣṭhagrahaṇaṃ smṛtam |
niraṅguṣṭhaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ bahirjānu ca yat kṛtam ||
bahirjānu ca yad bhuktaṃ sarvam evāsuraṃ bhavet |
viṣṇuḥ [73, 13-14] |
namo viśvebhyo devebhya ity annam ādau prāṅmukhayor nivedayet | pitre pitāmahāya prapitāmahāya ca nāmagotrābhyām udaṅmukheṣu ||
śāṅkhāyanagṛhyam [4, 1,8] |
annaṃ cāsāv etat ta ity anūddiśya bhojayet ||
[184] brahmapurāṇe |
tato madhughṛtāktaṃ tu soṣṇam annaṃ tilānvitam |
gṛhītvā devatīrthena praṇavenaiva tat punaḥ ||
etad vo ’nnam itīty uktvā viśvedevāṃś ca saṃyajet |
pitṛbhyaś ca tato dadyād annam āmantraṇena tu ||
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam annaṃ svadhā namaḥ |
atha datvā samagraṃ tu jalānnaṃ bhojanaṃ kramāt ||
viśvebhyaḥ śrāddhadevebhyaḥ kuryāc ca pratipādanam |
dvijāṅguṣṭhaṃ gṛhītvā tu paṭhen mantraṃ ca vāgyataḥ ||
pṛthivī te pātram ity annaṃ gṛhītaṃ cintayet paṭhan |
kṛṣṇa havyam idaṃ rakṣa madīyam iti kīrtayet ||
punas tenaiva mantreṇa pitṛbhyaś ca nivedayet |
ekaikasyātha viprasya gṛhītvāṅguṣṭham ādarāt ||
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam annaṃ svadhā namaḥ |
tilam annaṃ ca pānīyaṃ sakuśaṃ tena cāgrataḥ ||
vikiret pitṛbhṛtyebhyo japann apahatā iti |
tebhyo dadyād apośānaṃ bhavantaḥ prāśayantv iti ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2, 14, 11-12; 15, 1-2] |
athetarad brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya brāhmaṇasyāṅguṣṭhenānakhenānudiśati | pṛthivīsamaṃ tasya te ’gnir upadṛṣṭarcas te mahimā dattasyāpramādāya pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brāhmaṇasya mukhe ’mṛtaṃ juhomi | brāhmaṇānāṃ tvā vidyāvatāṃ prāṇāpānayor juhomy akṣitam asi mā pitṝṇāṃ kṣeṣṭhā [185] amutrāmuṣmin loka iti | dvitīyam anudiśati | antarikṣasama tasya te vāyur upaśrotā yajūṃṣi te mahimā dattasyāpramādāya pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brāhmaṇasya mukhe ’mṛtaṃ juhomi brāhmaṇānāṃ tvā vidyāvatāṃ prāṇāpānayor juhomy akṣitam asi mā pitāmahānāṃ kṣeṣṭhā atrāmuṣmin loka iti | tṛtīyam anudiśati | dyausamaṃ tasya ta ādityo ’nukhyātā sāmāni te mahimā dattasyāpramādāya | pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brāhmaṇasya mukhe ’mṛtaṃ juhomi brāhmaṇānāṃ tvā vidyāvatāṃ prāṇāpānayor juhomy akṣitam asi mā prapitāmahānāṃ kṣeṣṭhā amutrāmuṣmin loka iti ||
athāpyudāharanti—
agnaukaraṇaśeṣeṇa yad annam abhidhāryate |
tasmiṃś ca prāśite dadyād yad anyat prakṛtaṃ bhavet ||
niraṅguṣṭhaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ na tat prīṇayate pitṝn |
'itarat' odanavyañjanādīn | 'agnaukaraṇaśeṣeṇa' iti, agnaukaraṇājyaśeṣeṇa | 'annaṃ' agnaukaraṇāya yad uddhṛtam | 'prāśite' prakṣipte huta iti yāvat | 'anyat prakṛtaṃ' bhojanārthaṃ yad odanavyañjanādi prasādhitam | 'niraṅguṣṭhaṃ' uktaprakārāṅguṣṭhasparśaśūnyam ||
[186] dadyāditi vakṣyamāṇe kātyāyanaḥ [ śrāddhakalpasūtra, 2, 2-3] |
hutaśeṣaṃ datvā pātram ālabhya japati pṛthivī te pātraṃ dyaur apidhānaṃ brāhmaṇasya mukhe ’mṛtaṃ juhomi svāheti | vaiṣṇavyarcā yajuṣā vāṅguṣṭham avagāhya ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
havir ity evaṃ sāṅguṣṭham annavidhiṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadyāt |
asyāyamarthaḥ — 'annavidhiḥ' annaprakāraḥ pāyasādiḥ '’sāṅguṣṭham’ brāhmaṇāṅguṣṭhaniveśanapūrvakaṃ 'idaṃ havir” ity abhidhāya saṅkalpanīya iti |
hārītaḥ |
niraṅguṣṭhaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ tan na prīṇayate pitṝn |
tasmād aṅguṣṭham ākramya japen mantraṃ samāhitaḥ ||
'mantraḥ' 'idaṃ viṣṇur vicakrame' iti |
tathā |
athānaṃ nihitaṃ pātre pihitaṃ tad varṣīyasā |
saṃskṛtaṃ sthānam āyāti tadvad aṅguṣṭhaskṛtam ||
'varṣīyasā' adhaḥpātrād bṛhattareṇa ||
[187]
|| 19 ||
|| atha bhojanavidhiḥ ||
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 239] |
savyāhṛtikāṃ gāyatrīṃ madhuvātā iti tryṛcam |
japtvā yathāsukhaṃ vācyaṃ bhuñjīraṃs te ’pi vāgyatāḥ ||
manuḥ, hārīta-viṣṇu-yama-śātātapā uśanā ca dvitīye |
atyuṣṇaṃ sarvam annaṃ syād bhuñjīraṃs te ’pi vāgyatāḥ |
na ca dvijātayo brūyur dātrā pṛṣṭā havirguṇān ||
yāvad ūṣma bhavaty anne yāvad aśnanti vāgyatāḥ |
tāvad aśnanti pitaro yāvan noktā havirguṇāḥ ||
havirguṇā na vaktavyā yāvad vai pitṛtarpaṇam |
taiś cāpi tarpitaiḥ samyag vaktavyaṃ śobhanaṃ haviḥ ||
vāgyatatvābhidhānenaiva śrāddhabhoktur havirguṇānabhidhāne siddhe 'na ca dvijātayo brūyur iti punarvacanaṃ hastasaṃjñādināpi guṇapratipādananiṣedhaparam ||
devalaḥ |
brāhmaṇaś ca yathā śuddhaḥ prasannendriyamānasaḥ |
paitṛkānnam upāśnīyād asaṃbhrāntaḥ prasannavān |
'prasannavān' prasannaḥ |
[188] tathā |
annapānakaśītodaṃ dadadbhayo hy avalokitaḥ |
vaktavye kāraṇe saṃjñāṃ kurvan bhuñjīta pāṇinā ||
asyārthaḥ — annādy upanīya taddānārthaṃ dātrā 'avalokitaḥ' saṃjñāṃ kuryād eva | 'vaktavye' kāraṇe svīkārāsvīkārahetau ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
brāhmaṇā annaguṇadoṣau nābhivadeyuḥ | nānṛtaṃ brūyuḥ | anyonyaṃ na praśaṃseyuḥ | annapānaṃ na prabhūtam iti brūyur anyatra hastasaṃjñāyāḥ | yāvad bhūmau yāvad apraśastaṃ yāvat soṣma tāvad aśnanti pitaraḥ | anyatra phalamūlapānakebhyaḥ ||
'yāvad bhūmau' iti, bhojanapātraṃ tiṣṭhatīti śeṣaḥ | tena tasyoddharaṇaṃ na kartavyam | 'apraśastaṃ' akṛtapraśaṃsam |
tathā ca varāhapurāṇe |
uddhared yadi pātraṃ tu brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
haranti rākṣasās tasya bhuñjato ’nnaṃ vasundhare || iti
yamaḥ |
yas tu pāṇitale bhuṅkte yaḥ savāyus tathāśnute |
na cāsya pitaro ’śnanti yaś caivāgre praśaṃsati ||
śrāddhe niyukto bhuñjāno na pṛcchel lavaṇādiṣu |
ucchiṣṭāḥ pitaro yānti pṛcchato nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
dātuś ca patato bāhur jihvā bhoktuś ca bhidyate |
'savāyuḥ ' phūtkārasahitaḥ |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
yaś ca phūtkāravad bhuṅkte yaś ca pāṇitale dvijaḥ |
[189]        na tad aśnanti pitaro yat savāyuḥ samaśnute ||
vārahapurāṇe |
yācate yadi dātāraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānavarjitaḥ |
pitaras tasya nāśnanti dātur bhoktur na saṃśayaḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [81, 11-14 ] |
aśnīyuś ca brāhmaṇā vāgyatāḥ | na veṣṭitaśirasaḥ | na sopānatkāḥ | na pīṭhopahitapādāḥ |
manu-śātātapau |
yad veṣṭitaśirā bhuṅkte yad bhuṅkte dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
sopānatkaś ca yad bhuṅkte tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate ||
yo ‘prasannamanā bhuṅkte sopānatko ’pi vā punaḥ |
pralāpaśīlaḥ kruddho vā sa vipraḥ pitṛdūṣakaḥ ||
prahasan vāpi yo bhuṅkte sa nāpyāyayate pitṝn |
yo veṣṭitaśirā bhuṅkte yo vā bhuṅkte vigarhayan ||
yamaḥ |
yo veṣṭitaśirā bhuṅkte yad bhuṅkte vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
sopānatkaś ca yad bhuṅkte yac cāśnāti tiraskṛtam ||
tat sarvaṃ dānavendrāya brahmā bhāgam akalpayat |
hārītaḥ |
uddhṛtya [uddhṛta?] pāṇir vihasan sakrodhaṃ vismayānvitaḥ |
śrāddhakāleṣu yad bhuṅkte na tat prīṇāti vai pitṛn ||
āsuraṃ vismayānnaṃ syāt krodhānnaṃ rākṣasaṃ viduḥ |
asatkṛtam avajñātaṃ paiśācaṃ paricakṣate ||
[190] vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 25-26] |
ubhayor hastayor muktaṃ pitṛbhyo ’nnaṃ niveditam |
tadantaraṃ pratīkṣante hy asurā duṣṭacetasaḥ ||
tasmād aśūnyahastena kuryād annam upāgatam |
bhājanaṃ vā samālabhya tiṣṭhetoccheṣaṇād budhaḥ
'tasmād aśūnyahastena' dātur bhoktur vā saṃbandhasahitam |
baudhāyanaḥ [2, 15, 3-9] |
ubhayoḥ śākhayor muktaṃ pitṛbhyo ’nnaṃ niveditam |
tadantaram upāsante asurā duṣṭacetasaḥ ||
yātudhānāḥ piśācāś ca pratilumpanti tad dhaviḥ |
tilādāneṣv adāyādās tathā krodhavaśe ’surāḥ ||
kāṣāyavāsā yān kurute japahomapratigrahān |
na tad devagamaṃ bhavati havyakavyeṣu yad dhaviḥ ||
yac ca dattam anaguṣṭhaṃ yac caiva pratigṛhyate |
ācāmati ca yas tiṣṭhan na sa tena samṛdhyate ||
ādyantayor apāṃ pradānaṃ sarvatra | japaprabhṛti yathāvidhānam | śeṣam uktam aṣṭakāhome ||
'adāyādāḥ' asaṃbandhinaḥ | 'krodhavaśe' dātari satīti śeṣaḥ | 'ādyantayor apāṃ pradāne' ādāv apāṃ pradānam apo’śanam, 'ante' bhojanasamāptau | prabhṛtiśabdāt pitṛdhyānādyupādānam ||
śātātapaḥ |
upavītaṃ kaṭau kṛtvā kuryād gātrānulepanam |
ekavāsā śca yo ’śnīyān nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ ||
[191]        upavītī tataḥ kuryād vṛtaḥ śrāddhe ’nulepanam |
na niyuktaḥ śikhāvarjaṃ mālyaṃ śirasi dhārayet ||
savyād aṃsāt paribhraṣṭaṃ nābhideśe vyavasthitam |
ekavastraṃ tu taṃ vidyād daive pitrye ca varjayet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
nāgrāsanasthaḥ pūrvam aśnīyāt | nābhyadhikaṃ dadyāt | na pratigṛhṇīyāt ||
'agrāsanasthaḥ ' paṅktimūrdhany upaviṣṭaḥ | 'pūrvam’ itarabrāhmaṇabhojanopakramāt prāk | 'adhikaṃ' snehādinā viṣamam ||
yamaḥ |
agrāsanopaviṣṭas tu yo bhuṅkte prathamaṃ dvijaḥ |
bahūnāṃ paśyatāṃ so ’jñaḥ paṅktayā harati kilbiṣam ||
śātātapaḥ |
hastaṃ prakṣālya yaś cāpaḥ pibed bhuktvā dvijaḥ sadā |
tad annam asurair bhuktaṃ nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ ||
'bhuktvā' bhojanaṃ parisamāpya | 'āpo’ atra bhojanāntyaculukagatāḥ |
yamaḥ |
yat tu bhuktvā punar bhuṅkte yac ca tailābhighāritam |
rajasvalābhir yad dṛṣṭaṃ tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate ||
niroṅkāreṇa yad bhuktaṃ pariviṣṭaṃ samanyunā |
durātmanā ca yad bhuktaṃ tad vai rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
kīṭākeśāvapanaṃ ca kṣutaṃ śvabhir avekṣitam |
ruditaṃ cāvadhūtaṃ ca tad vai rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
[192]        avedavratacāritrās tribhir varṇair dvijātayaḥ |
mantravat pariviṣyante tad vai rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
vidhihīnam amṛṣṭānnaṃ mantrahīnam adakṣiṇam |
aśraddhayā hutaṃ dattaṃ tad vai rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
'tailābhighāritaṃ' ghṛtāsaṃbhave | 'niroṅkāraḥ’ sarvadhā vaidikamantrarahitaḥ | 'avadhūtaṃ' vāsaseti śeṣaḥ | 'mantrahīnaṃ' 'pṛthivī te' ity abhimantraṇarahitam ||
uśanā |
niyuktaś caiva yaḥ śrāddhe yat kiñcit parivarjayet |
pitaras tasya taṃ māsaṃ nairāśyaṃ pratipedire ||
paṅktipariviṣṭam arocamānam api yathāśakti kiścid api bhakṣaṇīyam iti ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 34] |
niyuktas tu yatiḥ śrāddhe daive vā māṃsam utsṛjet |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan nirayam ṛcchati ||
āpastambaḥ [2, 18, 12] |
na cātadguṇāyocchiṣṭaṃ dadyāt |
'atadguṇāya' śrāddhabhoktṛguṇarahitāya ||
manuḥ [3, 249] |
śrāddhaṃ bhuktvā yad ucchiṣṭaṃ vṛṣalāya prayacchati |
sa mūḍho narakaṃ yāti kālasūtram avākśirāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
śrāddhabhojī samucchiṣṭaṃ vṛṣalebhyo dadāti cet |
sa mūḍho ’niṣkṛatiḥ prāhuḥ prāyaścittena śudhyati ||
[193] 'aniṣkṛtiḥ' prāyaścittaṃ vinā tasya niṣkṛtir nāstīti 'prāhuḥ' ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
strīśūdrāyānupetāya śrāddhocchiṣṭaṃ na dāpayet |
yo dadyād rāgasaṃmohān na tad gacchati vai pitṝn |
tasmān na deyam ucchiṣṭam annādyaṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
anyatra dadhisarpirbhyāṃ śiṣyāya ca sutāya ca ||
'anyatra dadhisarpirbhyāṃ' iti, dadhisarpirvyatiriktam ucchiṣṭaṃ na kasmaicid api dātavyam | dadhisarpiṣos tu śiṣyaputrayor api śrāddhabhoktṛguṇopetayor abhyanujñā |

atha japādividhiḥ ||
tatra kāyātyanaḥ |
aśnatsu japed vyāhṛtipūrvāṃ gāyatrīṃ sapraṇavāṃ sakṛt trir vā rakṣoghnīḥ pitryamantrān puruṣasūktam anyāni ca pavitrāṇi |
'rakṣoghnīḥ' 'kṛṇuṣva pājaḥ' ityādyā ṛcaḥ | 'pitryamantrāḥ ' 'udīratāma vara' ityādayaḥ | 'pavitrāṇi' śatarudrīyaprabhṛtīni |
nigamaḥ |
bhuñjatsu japet savitṛmantrān ṛgyajuḥsāmetihāsapurāṇarakṣonīḥ pāvamānīr udīratām avareti madhumatīś ca ||
[195] viṣṇuḥ [ 73, 15 - 16] |
tatas tv adatsu brāhmaṇeṣu yan me prakāmād ahorātrair yad vaḥ kravyād iti japet | itihāsapurāṇadharmśāstrāṇi ca ||
baudhāyanaḥ [2, 14, 5] |
rakṣoghnāni ca sāmāni svadhāvanti yajūṃṣi ca |
madhvṛco ’tha pavitrāṇi śrāvayed āśayan śanaiḥ ||
'rakṣoghnāni ca sāmāni' 'yad vā u viśvapati' ityādi ṛcy utpannāni | 'svadhāvanti yajūṃṣi ca ' 'pitṛbhyaḥ svadhāvibhyaḥ' ityādi | 'madhvṛcaḥ' 'madhuvātā ṛtāyata' ityādyāḥ' |
hārītaḥ |
yad yat pūtaṃ manyeta punantu mā pitara iti ṣoḍaśapāvamānīr japed āditas trīn | atra pitara iti yajuḥ | namo vaḥ pitara iti yajuḥ syāmāntam | ‘madhuvātā ṛtāyata' iti tisraḥ | ‘punantu mā pitara' ity anuvākaḥ | tvaṃ soma pracakita iti caiṣā [196] pitryā saṃhitā | etān japan pitṝn prīṇāti pitṝṇāṃ cāsyākṣayaṃ bhavati ||
'āditas trīn' 'punantu mā pitaraḥ' ity upakramya trīn mantrān sadaiva śrāddhe japed ity arthaḥ | 'yajuḥ syāmāntam' 'madhutvaṃ syāma' ityantam āśvalāyanasūtrapaṭhitam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
pavitrāñjalipāṇir darbheṣv āsīno madhu vātā iti japet | pavitraṃ dharmaśāstram apratirathaṃ ca madhye gāyatrīm anuśrāvya |
'apratirathaṃ' 'āśuḥ śiśāna' ityādi | 'anuśrāvya' śrāvayitvā ||
manuḥ [3, 132] |
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayet pitrye dharmaśāstrāṇi caiva hi |
ākhyānānītihāsāṃś ca purāṇāni khilāni ca |
"khalāni' śrīsūktādīni |
matsyapurāṇe |
brahmaviṣṇvarkarudrāṇāṃ stotrāṇi vividhāni ca |
indreśasomasūktāni pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ ||
bṛhadrathantare tadvaj jyeṣṭhaṃ sāma tathā bṛhat |
tathaiva śāntikādhyāyaṃ madhubrāhmaṇam eva ca ||
[197]        maṇḍalabrāhmaṇaṃ tadvat prītikāri ca yat punaḥ |
viprāṇām ātmanaś caiva tat sarvaṃ samudīrayet ||
brahmapurāṇe |
vīṇāvaṃśadhvanī vātha viprebhyaḥ saṃnivedayet ||
viṣṇupurāṇe 3, 15, 31-37] |
rakṣoghnamantrapaṭhanaṃ bhūmer āstaraṇaṃ tilaiḥ |
kṛtvā dhyeyāś ca pitaras ta eva dvijasattamāḥ ||
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
mama tṛptiṃ prayāntv adya vidvaddeheṣu saṃsthitāḥ ||
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
tṛptiṃ prayāntu piṇḍena mayā dattena bhūtale ||
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
tṛptiṃ prayāntu madbhaktyā mayaitat samudāhṛtam ||
mātāmahas tṛptim upaitu tasya
tathā pitā tasya pitā tu yo ’nyaḥ |
viśve ca devāḥ paramāṃ prayāntu
tṛptiṃ praṇaśyantu ca yātudhānāḥ ||
yajñeśvaro havyasamastakavya-
bhoktāvyayātmā harirīśvaro ’tra |
tatsaṃnidhānād apayāntu sadyo
rakṣāṃsy aśeṣāṇy asurāś ca sarve ||
'mayaitat samudāhṛtam' iti mantraliṅgāt pitṝn dhyāyatā stotram etat paṭhanīyaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanakāle |
[198] vāyupurāṇe |
saptārciṣaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvakāmapradaṃ śubham |
amūrtīnāṃ samūrtīnāṃ pitṝṇāṃ dīptatejasām ||
namasyāmi sadā teṣāṃ dhyānināṃ yogacakṣuṣām |
indrādīnāṃ janetāro dakṣamārīcayos tathā ||
saptarṣīṇāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca tān namasyāmi kāmadān |
manvādīnāṃ janetṝṃś ca sūryācandramasau tathā ||
tān namaskṛtya sarvān vai pitṝn apsu dadhiṣv api |
prajāpataye kaśyapāya somāya varuṇāya ca ||
yogeśvarebhyaś ca sadā namasyāmi kṛtāñjaliḥ |
nakṣatrāṇāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca vāyvagnyoś ca pitṝṃs tathā ||
dyāvāpṛthivyoś ca tathā namasyāmi kṛtāñjaliḥ |
devarṣīṇāṃ janetṝṃś ca sarvadevanamaskṛtān ||
abhayasya sadā dātṝn namasye ’haṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ |
pitṛgaṇebhyaḥ saptabhyo namo lokeṣu saptaṣu ||
svayambhuve namaś caiva brahmaṇe yogacakṣuṣe |
etad uktaṃ tu saptārcir devarṣigaṇapūjitam ||
pavitraṃ paramaṃ hy etac chrīmad rakṣonirāsanam ||

|| atha śrāddhottaraṃ karma ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 240-248] |
ā tṛptes tu pavitrāṇi japtvā pūrvajapaṃ tathā ||
annam ādāya tṛptāḥ stha śeṣaṃ caivānumānya ca |
tad anaṃ prakired bhūmau dadyāc cāpaḥ sakṛt sakṛt ||
sarvam annam upādāya satilaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau piṇḍān pradadyāt pitṛyajñavat ||
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ dadyād ācamanaṃ tataḥ |
svastivācyaṃ tataḥ kuryād akṣayyodakam eva ca ||
dattvā tu dakṣiṇāṃ śaktyā svadhākāram udāharet |
vācyatām ity anujñātaḥ prakṛtebhyaḥ svadhocyatām ||
brūyur astu svadhety ukte bhūmau siñcet tato jalam |
viśvedevāś ca prīyantāṃ vipraiś cokte idaṃ japet ||
dātāro no ’bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti ||
ity uktvoktvā priyā vācaḥ praṇipatya visarjayet |
vāje vāja iti prītaḥ pitṛpūrvaṃ visarjanam ||
[200]        yasmiṃs te saṃsravāḥ pūrvaṃ pitṛpātre niveśitāḥ |
pitṛpātraṃ tad uttānaṃ kṛtvā viprān visarjayet ||
'pūrvajapaḥ' savyāhṛtigāyatrī madhuvātā ityādi | 'dadyāc cāpaḥ sakṛt sakṛt' bhojanasamāptau culakārthaṃ datvāmṛtāpidhānaṃ tu viprebhyaśc a sakṛt sakṛd iti ||
kātyāyanaḥ [śrāddhakalpasūtra, 3, 7-18] |
tṛptān jñātvānnaṃ prakīrya sakṛt sakṛd apo datvā pūrvavad gāyatrīṃ japitvā [japtvā] madhumatīr madhu madhv iti [ madhumatī] ca | tṛptāḥ stheti pṛcchati | tṛptāḥ sma ity anujñāśeṣam annam anujñāpya sarvam annam ekatoddhṛtyocchiṣṭasamīpe darbheṣu trīṃs trīn piṇḍāna avanejya dadyāt | ācānteṣv ity eke | ācānteṣūdakaṃ puṣpākṣatān akṣayyodakaṃ ca dadyāt [dattvā] aghorāḥ pitaraḥ santu santv ity ukte gotraṃ no vardhatāṃ vardhatām ity ukte
dātāro no ’bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no ‘stu |
ity āśiṣaḥ pratigṛhya svadhāvācanīyān sapavitrān kuśān āstīrya svadhāṃ vācayiṣya iti pṛcchati | vācyatām ity anujñātaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ pitāmahebhyaḥ prapitāmahebhyo mātāmahebhyaḥ pramātāmahebhyaḥ vṛddhamātāmahebhyaś ca svadhocyatām iti | astu svavety ucyamāne svadhāvacanīyeṣv apo [201] niṣiñcati [ūrjam iti] | | uttānaṃ pātraṃ kṛtvā yathāśakti dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt [brāhmaṇebhyaḥ ] | viśvedevāḥ prīyantām iti daive vācayitvā vāje vāje ’vateti visṛjya | ā mā vājasyety anuvrajya pradakṣiṇīkṛtyopaviśeta [praviśet ] ||
āśvalāyanagṛhyam [4, 9,10- 14] |
tṛptān jñātvā madhumatīḥ śrāvayed akṣannamīmadanteti ca | saṃpannaṃ pṛṣṭvā yad yad annam upabhuktaṃ tatas tataḥ sthālīpākena saha piṇḍārtham uddhṛtya śeṣaṃ nivedayet | abhimate’numate vā [202] bhuktavatsv anācānteṣu piṇḍān nidadhyāt | prakīryānnam upavīryo svadhocyatām iti visṛjed astu svadheti vā ||
'sthālīpākena saha' ityabhidhānāt, kevalāvasathyāgnimantaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñārthaṃ sthālīpākaṃ śrapayitvānnadānāntaṃ śrāddhaṃ nirvartya sthālīpākasyāgreṇagnaukaraṇahomaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā śrāddhaśeṣānnena sthālīpākānnam ekīkṛtya piṇḍapradānaṃ kartavyam | 'abhimate' śrāddhabhoktrā svīkṛte | 'anumate' tenaiva iṣṭaiḥ saha bhujyatām ity anujñāte ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
gāyatrīm anuśrāvya tṛptān jñātvā svaditam iti pṛṣṭvā śeṣam annam anujñāpya prakṛtād annavikiraṃ kuryāt | svadhāṃ vācayitvā viṣṭarāṃs trīn nidadhyāt | trīṇy evodapātrāṇi satilāni sapavitrāṇi mṛnmayāśmamayaudumbarāṇi vā dhūpagandhamālyādarśadīpāñjanādīni copaharet | sarvānnaprakāram ādāya piṇḍān nidadhyāt | apasavyena pāṇinā dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim upadhāryaikaikaṃ tribhir abhimantryāsāv etat ta iti darbheṣu teṣv eva pāṇī pramṛjyodapātraiḥ pariśrāvya brūyād dātāraḥ santv iti bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti ||
yamaḥ |
tato bhuktavatāṃ śeṣād annaśeṣaṃ nivedayet |
yathā brūyus tathā kuryād anujñātas tu tair dvijaiḥ ||
tṛptān ācāmayet pṛṣṭvā vai dadāti prayatnataḥ |
ācāntān anujānīyād abhivādya kṛtāñjaliḥ ||
[203]        bhavanto ramatām atra jñātvānujñātalakṣaṇam |
svadheti ceti vaktavyaṃ prīyantāṃ pitaras tv iti ||
akṣayyam annapānaṃ ca vācyaṃ prītair dvijātibhiḥ |
tato nirvapaṇaṃ kuryāt piṇḍānāṃ tadanantaram ||
śucau deśe vivikte ca prayatnenopapādayet |
viṣṭarāṃs trīn vapet tatra nāmagotrasamanvitān ||
adbhir abhyukṣya vidhivat tilair abhyavakīrya ca |
tato darbheṣu taṃ hastaṃ nirmṛjyāl lepabhāginām ||
tataḥ pānīyakumbhena tarpayet prayataḥ pitṝn |
pūrvapiṇḍaṃ prayacchet tu pitryaṃ ca pṛthivīti ca ||
pitāmahāya tv aparam antarikṣe ca dāpayet |
prapitāmahāya ca tatas tṛtīyaṃ tu nivedayet ||
dyaur davir iti mantreṇa śrutir eṣā sanātanī |
tato ’bhivādya praṇato varān yācet pitāmahān ||
dātāro no ’bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti ||
'pṛṣṭvā vai dadāti prayatnataḥ' ity anenedam ucyate śeṣānnānujñāpanaṃ na vāṅmātreṇa, kiṃ tu taddāne prayatnam api svayaṃ kuryād iti | 'dadātir' atrārthaparaḥ, tiṣṭhan homān yajata itivat | 'jñātvā anujñātalakṣaṇaṃ' abhiratāḥ smety anujñāsūcakaṃ cihnam || 'viṣṭarān' piṇḍāsanakuśān | 'vapet tatra' piṇḍān iti śeṣaḥ | 'lepabhāgināṃ' prapitāmahāt pareṣām ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 106 - 108, 104, 105, 129, 109] |
pṛcchet tṛptās tatas te tu yad brūyus tat samācaret |
tatsamīpe prakuryāc ca piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ tataḥ ||
[204]        sarvasmāt prakṛtād annāt piṇḍān madhutilānvitān |
pitṛmātāmahādīnāṃ dadyād gṛhyavidhānataḥ ||
dīpagandhair mālyadhūpais tathā vastrāñjanādinā |
samabhyarcyodapātraṃ ca teṣām upari nikṣipet ||
upaspṛṣṭodakānāṃ tu brāhmaṇānāṃ tato ’grataḥ |
sodakaṃ vikared annaṃ mantraṃ cemaṃ samuccaret ||
agnidagdhāś ca ye jīvā ye ’py adagdhāḥ kule mama |
bhūmau dattena tṛpyantu tṛptā yāntu parāṃ gatim ||
vāje vāja iti proktvā brāhmaṇāṃs tān visarjayet |
viprān pradakṣiṇīkṛtya viprāṇāṃ prārthayed varān ||
dātāro no ’bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti ||
hārītaḥ |
tṛptān svaditam iti pṛcchet svaditam iti pratyāhuḥ | śṛtam iti daiveṣv āyuṣyam iti svaireṣvācānteṣu bhūmau vikiraṃ ninayet | pratyācānteṣu samūlatilair brāhmaṇapāṇiṣu ninayet | oṃ svadhety āhur astu svadheti pratyāhuḥ | tato ’nujñāvacanam abhiramyatām ity eke | abhiratāḥ smeti pratyāhuḥ | vāje vāja ity abhivrajya śeṣasya piṇḍān pitṛyajñavan nidadhyāt | pariṣecanam abhiṣecanam udakāñjalim arghyabhājanam arghyapratyavanejanaṃ mārjanapravāhanaṃ vikirodakaṃ samūlatilair yac cānyat tūṣṇīṃ tad daśamam ekādaśakaṃ śrāddhasam ṛddhikāmaḥ kuryāt ||
'śṛtam’ iti daiveṣu' —
devān uddiśya yac chrāddhaṃ tat tu daivikam ucyate |
haviṣyena viśiṣṭena saptamyādiṣu yatnataḥ ||
[205] iti bhaviṣyapurāṇoktaṃ devaśrāddham, tatra śṛtam iti pṛcchet | 'śṛtaṃ' siddham | 'śṛtaṃ pārka' iti nipātanāt | 'svaireṣu' "śrāddhaṃ prati ruciś caiva " iti kathitaśrāddheṣu | 'samūlatilaiḥ samūlā atra kuśāḥ, tena samūlakuśatilaiḥ saha | 'ninayet' jalam iti śeṣaḥ | ‘tato ’nujñāvacanaṃ' kāryam iti śeṣaḥ | tac ca yathāruci sthīyatām ityādi | ‘abhiramyatām ity eke ' eke ’bhiramyatām ity anujñāvacanaśabdena niyamaṃ manyante ||
punar hārītaḥ |
śvaśvādīnāṃ tathā patnī piṇḍān dadyāt suyantritā |
udakāñjalidīpaṃ ca añjanābhyañjanaṃ tathā ||
'śvaśvādīnāṃ' iti, yatra śākhāyāṃ mātṛprabhṛtīnāṃ pṛthaktvena piṇḍapradānaṃ vihitaṃ tatra yajamānatvād amantravat | idaṃ strībhyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ keṣāṃcid eva śākhinām ||
śāṅkhāyanagṛhyam [4, 1,5-9] |
bhuktavatsu piṇḍān dadyāt | purastād eke | piṇḍān paścimena tatpatnīnāṃ kiṃcid antardhāya | brāhmaṇebhyaḥ śeṣaṃ nivedayet | agnaukaraṇādi piṇḍapitṛyajñena kalpe vyākhyātam ||
'purastāt' brāhmaṇabhojanāt prāk | 'eke' ayaṃ ca bheda: śākhābhedavyavasthitaḥ | 'piṇḍān paścimena' pitrādipiṇḍānāṃ paścimapradeśe anatidūre kiṃcid antardhāyikaṃ kṛtvā | 'agnaukaraṇādi piṇḍapitṛyajñena' agnaukaraṇādītikartavyatāvatā piṇḍapitṛyajñena |
[206]  nigamaḥ |
tṛpteṣv āmantrya tṛptāḥ stheti pūrvavan niṣicya pūrveṣāṃ svastivācya oṃ svadhocyatām ity uktvābhiramyatāṃ yathā sukham iti ca saṃpūjyānuvrajet ||
'pūrvavan niṣicya' pratyavanijya | 'svastivācya' svastīti vācayitvā ||
uśanā |
tṛptāṃś cemān prabhūtaṃ prabhūtam ity uktavataḥ sadakṣiṇena tilonmiśritodakena ca sicya trīn pūrvān anukīrtayet | darbhāstīrṇāyāṃ bhūmau piṇḍān nivedayet | tataḥ svadhāṃ vācayet | piṇḍān avadhārya toyena pratiṣicyodakabhājanāny utsṛjet |
'sadakṣiṇena' dakṣiṇādravyasahitena | 'pratiṣicya' pratyavanijya ||
'adatsu brāhmaṇeṣu' ityanuvṛttau, viṣṇuḥ [73, 17–28, 32] |
ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu pṛthivī darvir akṣatety ekaṃ piṇḍaṃ pitre nidadhyāt | antarīkṣaṃ darvir akṣateti dvitīyaṃ pitāmahāya | dyaurd arvir akṣateti tṛtīyaṃ prapitāmahāya | ye ‘tra pitaraḥ pretā iti vāso deyam | vīrānnaḥ pitaro dhatta ity annam | atra pitaro mādayadhvaṃ yathābhāgaṃ darbhamūle svakarābhivarṣaṇam | ūrjaṃ vahantīr ity anena sodakena pradakṣiṇaṃ piṇḍānāṃ vikiraṇaṃ kṛtvārghapuṣpadhūpānulepanānnādibhakṣyāṇi nivedayet | udakapātraṃ madhughṛtatilaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ ca | bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu tṛptim āgateṣu mā me kṣeṣṭety annaṃ satṛṇam [207] abhyukṣyānnavikiraṇam ucchiṣṭāgrataḥ kṛtvā tṛptā bhavantaḥ saṃpannam iti ca pṛṣṭvodaṅmukheṣv ācamanādau dat vā tataḥ prāṅmukheṣu [datvā tataś ca saṃprokṣitam iti śrāddhadeśaṃ saṃprokṣya darbhapāṇiḥ sarvaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ prāṅmukhāgrato] yan me rāma iti pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā pratyetya ca yathāśakti dakṣiṇāṃ ca datvābhiramantu bhavanta ity uktvā tair ukto ’bhiratāḥ sma iti devāś ca pitaraś ceti [abhi]japet | akṣayyodakaṃ [ ca] nāmagotrābhyāṃ datvā viśvedevāḥ prīyantām iti ca prāṅmukhebhyas tataḥ prāñjalir idaṃ tanmanā yāceta |
dātāro no ’bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti' ||
vāje vāja iti tato brāhmaṇāṃś ca visarjayet |
pūjayitvā yathānyāyam anuvrajyābhivādya ca ||
'yan me rāma iti' yan me rāmaḥ śakunir ityādiko mantraḥ |
manuḥ [3, 214-220; 223] |
apasavyam agnau kṛtvā sarvam āvṛtparikramam |
apasavyena hastena nirvaped udakaṃ śuci ||
[208]        trīṃs tu tasmād dhaviḥśeṣaṃ piṇḍān kṛtvā samāhitaḥ |
audakenaiva vidhinā nirvapad dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
nyupya piṇḍāṃs tatas tāṃs tu prayato vidhipūrvakam |
teṣu darbheṣu taṃ hastaṃ nirmṛjyāl lepabhāginām ||
ācamyodakparāvṛtya trir ācamya śanair asūn |
ṣaḍṛtūṃś ca namas kuryāt pitṝn eva ca mantravit ||
udakaṃ ninayec cheṣaṃ śanaiḥ piṇḍāntike punaḥ |
avajighrec ca tān piṇḍān yathā nyuptān samāhitaḥ ||
piṇḍebhyaḥ svalpikāṃ mātrāṃ samādāyānupūrvaśaḥ |
tān eva viprān āsīnān vidhivat pūrvam āśayet ||
teṣāṃ datvā tu hasteṣu sapavitraṃ tilodakam |
tatpiṇḍāgraṃ prayacchet tu svadhaiṣām astv iti bruvan ||
'apasavyaṃ' apradakṣiṇaṃ yathā bhavati | 'āvṛtparikramaḥ' 'apasavyena' dakṣiṇenaiva | 'nirvapet' nikṣipet, piṇḍārthāstṛtakuśeṣv iti śeṣaḥ | 'śuci’ anupahatam | 'tasmāt' haviḥśeṣād agnaukaraṇahomāvaśiṣṭāt | 'audakenaiva vidhinā' avanejanodakadānaprakāreṇa | 'nyupya' datvā | 'vidhipūrvakaṃ' svagṛhyoktavidhiprakārayuktam | 'udakparāvṛtya' udaṅmukhībhavituṃ parāvartanaṃ kṛtvā | 'mantravat' 'namo vaḥ pitaro rasāya' ityādimantrayuktam | 'ninayet' dadyāt | 'śeṣaṃ' prathamāvanejanāvaśiṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇārcanānantaram ||
devalaḥ |
atha saṃgṛhya kalaśaṃ sadarbhaṃ pūrṇam ambhasā |
purastād upaviśyaiṣāṃ piṇḍāvāpaṃ nivedayet ||
[209]        tatas tair abhyanujñāto dakṣiṇāṃ diśam etya saḥ |
upalipte śucau deśe sthānaṃ kurvīta saikatam ||
maṇḍalaṃ caturaśraṃ vā dakṣiṇāvanataṃ mahat |
ekadarbheṇa tanmadhyam ullikhet triś ca taṃ tyajet ||
tasmin sthāne tato darbhān ekamūlān śivān bahūn |
dakṣiṇāgrān udakpādān sarvāṃs tāṃs tṛṇuyāt samam ||
atha sāñjalir utthāya sthitvā cāvāhayet piṝṛn |
pitaro me prasīdantu prayāntu ca pitāmahāḥ ||
iti saṃkīrtayaṃs tūṣṇīṃ tiṣṭhet kṣaṇam anucchvasan |
āvāhayitvā darbhāgrais teṣāṃ sthānāni kalpayet ||
teṣv āsīneṣu pātreṣu prayacchen mārjanodakam |
puṣpāṇi vikiret tatra nānāvarṇāṃs tilān api ||
tataś carum upādāya sapavitreṇa pāṇinā |
caturdhā vibhajet piṇḍān ghṛtāktān bhājanena vai ||
abhyajya madhusarpirbhyāṃ tān vapet kuśasaṃcaye |
chāyāyāṃ hastino chāye bastadauhitrasaṃnidhau ||
pitṛpūrvaṃ ca nāmāni trayāṇāṃ kīrtayec chanaiḥ |
apasavyam apāṅguṣṭhaṃ mantrapūrvaṃ svadheti ca ||
etat te tata ye ca tvām anvityādyāvapet pituḥ |
tathā pitāmahasyāpi tatpituś ca tathācaret ||
[210]        sāmānyam iti śeṣāṇāṃ caturthaṃ piṇḍam āvapet |
evaṃ niveśya piṇḍāṃs tān alaṃkuryāc ca pūrvavat' ||
alaṃkṛtya yathākalpaṃ juhuyāt pāvakaṃ budhaḥ |
havirbhiḥ kavyamantreṇa svadhākāraṃ ca ghoṣitam ||
hutvaivam agnipiṇḍānāṃ saṃnidhau tadanantaram |
pakvānnena baliṃ tebhyaḥ piṇḍebhyo vāpayed dvijaḥ ||
tato dattabalibhyaś ca piṇḍebhyo mantrapūrvakam |
piṇḍapātreṇa tenaiva dadyād ācamanodakam ||
haviḥśeṣaṃ tato muṣṭim ādāyaikaikam āditaḥ |
kramaśaḥ pitṛpatnīnāṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ caret ||
tataḥ piṇḍam upādāya haviṣaḥ saṃskṛtaṃ mahat |
jñātivargasya sarvasya sāmānyam iti nirvapet ||
dakṣiṇāṃ sarvabhogāṃś ca pratipiṇḍaṃ pradāpayet |
bhakṣyān apūpān ikṣūṃś ca vyañjanāny aśanāni ca ||
tataś ca piṇḍapātreṇa samuddhṛtya tilodakam |
piṇḍāṃs tāṃs triḥ parikrāmed ajasram apasavyataḥ ||
tato ’bhivādya prayato varaṃ yācet tataḥ pitṝn |
vaṃśo no vardhatāṃ bhūyo bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti ||
ekoddiṣṭe ’nyamāse vā tathā saṃvatsare ’pi ca |
vidhānaṃ caitad eva syāt piṇḍaś caiko ’tra pūjyate ||
evaṃ piṇḍān samabhyarcya hutvā cāgniṃ yathāvidhi |
abhyetya brāhmaṇasthānaṃ brāhmaṇāṃs tān samarcayet ||
atha piṇḍāvaśiṣṭānaṃ vidhinā bhojayed dvijān |
'eṣāṃ’ śrāddhopaveśitabrāhmaṇānām | 'dakṣiṇādik' śrāddhakartrapekṣayā | 'etya' prāpya | 'saikataṃ vālukāmayam | [211] 'triḥ' vāratrayam | 'taṃ' darbham | 'ekamūlān' ekamūla-saṃlagnabahuśikhān | 'udakpādān' udaṅmūlān | 'samaṃ' ekakālam | 'teṣāṃ' pitṝṇām | 'sthānāni kalpayet' āstṛtadarbhoparīti śeṣaḥ | 'mārjanodakaṃ' avanejanodakam | 'bastaḥ' chāgaḥ | 'apāṅguṣṭhaṃ' apagatāṅguṣṭhasaṃbandham | etat te tata' iti pitrādipadaṃ sambodhanāntaṃ prayoktavyam | 'śeṣāṇāṃ' vṛddhaprapitāmahādīnām | 'pūrvavat' brāhmaṇārcanavat | 'kavyamantreṇa ' agnaye kavyavāhanāyetyādimantreṇa | 'ghoṣitaṃ' ucair uccāritam, kuryād iti śeṣaḥ | 'ācamanodakaṃ' pratyavanejanodakam | 'tataś ca' iti, tataḥ satilodakadhāraṇaṃ vāratrayam apasavyaṃ piṇḍān veṣṭyety arthaḥ | idaṃ ca manu-devalābhyāṃ piṇḍavidhānaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanāt pūrvam uktam api piṇḍaprakaraṇānurodhenātra likhitam ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
ucchiṣṭasannidhau kāryaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ budhaiḥ |
ādau vāpi tathā kuryād agnikāryaṃ yathāvidhi ||
yat kiṃcit pacyate gehe bhakṣyaṃ vā bhojyam eva vā |
anivedya na bhoktavyaṃ piṇḍamūle kathaṃcana ||
bhojayitvā yathābhaktyā ācāntān dattadakṣiṇān |
svasti vācya prasannātmā anuvrajya visarjayet ||
‘anivedya na bhoktavyam' iti sarvam annam upakḷptaṃ piṇḍabalitvena vinivedayed ity arthaḥ | 'piṇḍamūle' piṇḍasamīpe |
manur viṣṇuś ca prathame |
sārvavarṇikam annādyaṃ sannīyāplāvya vāriṇā |
samutsṛjed bhuktavatām agrato vikired bhuvi ||
[212]        pṛṣṭvā svaditam ity evaṃ tṛptān ācāmayet tataḥ |
ācāntāṃś cānujānīyād abhi bho ramyatām iti ||
svadhāstv ity eva te brūyur brāhmaṇās tadanantaram |
svadhākāraḥ parā hy āśīḥ sarveṣu pitṛkarmasu |
tato bhuktavatāṃ teṣām annaśeṣaṃ nivedayet |
yathā brūyus tathā kuryād anujñāto dvijottamaiḥ ||
pitrye svaditam ity eva vākyaṃ goṣṭhe tu suśrutam |
saṃpannam ity abhyudaye daive rocata ity api ||
visṛjya brāhmaṇāṃs tāṃs tu niyato vāgyataḥ śuciḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśam ākāṅkṣan yācetemān varān pitṝn ||
dātāro no ’bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahudeyaṃ ca no ’stv iti ||
'sārvavarṇikaṃ' sarvam upakḷptam | 'sannīya' miśrayitvā | 'ākāṅkṣan' nirīkṣyamāṇaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [3, 69-71 |
rocata iti sāyaṃprātar aśanāny abhipūjayet | svaditam iti pitryeṣu | saṃpannam ity ābhyudayikeṣu ||
devalaḥ |
evaṃ tṛpteṣu pānīyaṃ dadyād ācamanaṃ tathā |
ucchiṣṭaṃ cāpy apanayet pitryāṇāṃ nāma varjayet ||
tataḥ sarvāśanaṃ pātre gṛhītvā vidhivat svayam |
teṣām uccheṣaṇasthāne tena pātreṇa nikṣipet ||
ācāntebhyo dvijebhyaś ca prayacched atha dakṣiṇām |
hiraṇyaratnabhogānām anyeṣāṃ ca svaśaktitaḥ ||
[213]        tato muṣṭim upādāya haviṣo ’ntas tataḥ svayam |
kalaśaṃ jalapūrṇaṃ ca gṛhītvobhayam eva ca ||
pitṝṇāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca hastāgrāt prabhṛti kṣaran |
apasavyaṃ kramet sarvān tāṃś ca piṇḍān bahirmukhaḥ ||
trir evaṃ patanaṃ toyaṃ parītya śrāvayec chanaiḥ |
parikramya tu tatpātraṃ tatraivāpy avakubjayet ||
atha vai vaiśvadevatyān brāhmaṇān svasti vācayet |
akṣayaṃ vācayet pitryāṃś caramaṃ satilodakam ||
atha viprān gṛhītārthān saṃtṛptān abhivādayet |
yathāvad vaiśvadevatyān pitṛpūrvaṃ punaḥ pitṝn ||
pūrvam utthāpayet pitryān ṛdhyatām iti ca bruvan |
utthitān anugacchec ca tebhyaḥ śeṣaṃ ca saṃharet ||
paścāt tu vaiśvadevatyān viprān utthāpayet tathā |
ete hi pūrvam āsīnāḥ samuttiṣṭhanti paścimāḥ ||
'pānīyaṃ' bhojanānte amṛtābhidhānamasīyam | ' evaṃpatanaṃ' evam uktaprakāraṃ patanaṃ yasya tat tathā | 'caramaṃ' paścāt |
brahmapurāṇe |
ucchiṣṭe satilān darbhān dakṣiṇāgrān nidhāpayet |
yad yan niveditaṃ kiṃcit pitṛbhyaś cātha tatra ca ||
tasmāt tasmāc ca tadbhāgaṃ gṛhītvā ca same śubhe |
dattvāmṛtāpidhānaṃ tu viprebhyaś ca sakṛt sakṛt ||
kaccit saṃpannam etan me bho viprā iti tān vadet |
iti prāhuḥ susampannaṃ tāṃs tān punar udāharet ||
aho bhavantas tṛpyantu tṛptāḥ smeti vadanti te |
sa tān āha punaḥ śeṣaṃ ka deyaṃ cānnam ity api ||
śiṣṭebhyo dīyatāṃ caitad iti taṃ pravadanti te |
atha tṛptāṃs tu tān jñātvā bhūmāv annaṃ jalaṃ kṣipet ||
[214]        ye ‘smatkulejā pitaro luptapiṇḍodakakriyāḥ |
ye cāpy akṛtacūḍās tu ye ca garbhād viniḥsṛtāḥ ||
yeṣāṃ dāho na kriyate ye ’gnidagdhās tathāpare |
bhūmau dattena tṛpyantu tṛptā yāntu parāṃ gatim ||
tataḥ prakṣālya hastau tu tathācamya hariṃ smaret |
pretabhāgaṃ visṛjyātha prāyaścittopaśāntaye ||
savyāhṛtiṃ sapraṇavāṃ gāyatrīṃ ca tato japet |
paṭhen madhumatīḥ puṇyās tathā ca madhu madhv iti ||
'ucchiṣṭe' ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau | 'prāyaścittopaśāntaye' prāyaścittasādhyā yā upaśāntiḥ, tasyai |
tathā |
tatra dakṣiṇapūrvasyāṃ kāryā vedī tathā diśi |
hastamātrāvāhyabhūmeś caturaṅgulam ucchritā ||
piṇḍanirvāpaṇārthāya ramaṇīyā viśeṣataḥ |
oṃ nihanmi sarvaṃ yad amedhyavad bhaved
dhatāś ca sarve ’suradānavā mayā |
yakṣāś ca rakṣāṃsi piśācasaṅghā
hatā mayā yātudhānāś ca sarve ||
anena mantreṇa susaṃyatātmā
vedīṃ ca sarvāṃ sakṛd ullikhec ca |
śivāṃ ca buddhiṃ dhruvam icchamānaḥ
kṣipet kuśāṃs tatra ca dakṣiṇāgrān ||
tathā |
gṛhītvā darbhapiñjalīṃ tato vāmena pāṇinā |
tayā ca dakṣiṇāgrāṃ tu rekhām ullaṅghayet paṭhet ||
pavitrapāṇiś ca japan mantraṃ rakṣonnam uttamam |
samūlān dakṣiṇāgrāṃs tu rekhāyāṃ prastaret kuśān ||
[215]        atha vai camasādibhyas tv arghapātrebhya eva ca |
sapuṣpāñjalim ādāya teṣāṃ pṛṣṭhe pṛthak pṛthak ||
apradakṣiṇaṃ nenijyād gotranāmānumantritam |
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam annaṃ svadhā namaḥ ||
avanenikṣa ityante japec ca susamāhitaḥ |
piṇḍāṃs tathārghapātreṣu triṣu datvā ca pūrvavat ||
madhvājyajalasaṃpṛktaṃ sarvavyañjanasaṃyutam |
uṣṇam ādāya piṇḍaṃ tu kṛtvā bilvaphalopamam ||
dadyāt pitāmahādibhyo darbhamūle yathākramam |
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyaṃ piṇḍeti saṃjapet ||
ye ca tvām iti mantras tu sarvebhyaś ca japet pṛthak |
tato darbheṣu vidhivat saṃmārjya ca karau tataḥ ||
prakṣālya ca jalenātha trir ācamya hariṃ smaret |
tebhyaḥ saṃsravapātrebhyo jalenaivāvanejanam ||
datvātra pitaraś ceti paṭhec codaṅmukhaḥ sthitaḥ |
cintayaṃś ca pitṝṃs tuṣṭān sarvān bhāsvaramūrtikān ||
amī madanta pitaras tv iti paśyan dhiyā paṭhet |
nīvīṃ vistrasya ca japen namo vaḥ pitaras tv iti ||
yatheha puruṣaṃ taṃ ca mantraṃ japtvā kṛtāñjaliḥ |
etad vaḥ pitaro vāsas tv iti jalpan pṛthak pṛthakūṃ ||
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyaṃ vāsaḥ paṭhet tataḥ |
dadyāt krameṇa vāsāṃsi śvetavastrabhavā daśāḥ ||
gate vayasi vṛddhāni svāni lomāny athāpi vā |
kṣaumaṃ sūtraṃ navaṃ dadyāc chāṇaṃ kārpāsam eva vā ||
kṛṣṇorṇānīlaraktāktakauśeyāni vivarjayet |
madhu sābhyañjanaṃ cārghaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ vilepanam ||
baliṃ dadyāc ca vidhivat piṇḍo ’ṣṭāṅgo bhaved yathā |
[216]        pūjayitvā tu piṇḍasthān pitṝṃś ca praṇamed ṛtūn ||
oṃ vasantāya namas tubhyaṃ grīṣmāya ca namo namaḥ |
varṣābhyaś ca śaratsaṃjñaṛtave ca namaḥ sadā ||
hemantāya namas tubhyaṃ namas te śiśirāya ca |
māsasaṃvatsarebhyaś ca divasebhyo namaḥ sadā ||
'gṛhītvā darbhapiñjūlīm' iti, vāmahastena darbhapiñjūlīṃ gṛhītvā ubhābhyāṃ hastābhyām ullikhed ity arthaḥ | “savyottarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kuryād ullekhanaṃ dvijaḥ" iti vāyupurāṇa- vacanāt' | 'paṭhet rakṣoghnaṃ mantram' avahatā ityādikam | 'teṣāṃ pṛṣṭhe' piṇḍārthāstṛtakuśānām upari | 'datvā' nikṣipya | 'pūrvavat' pitrādyarghapātreṣu | 'yathākramam' vidhivat, apasavyena mantravac ca | 'saṃmāya' prokṣya | 'tebhyaḥ' piṇḍasthapitṛbhyaḥ | ‘tebhyaḥ saṃsravapātreyaḥ’ saṃsravapātrato jalam ādāyāvanejanaṃ datvety arthaḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
savyottarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kuryād ullekhanaṃ dvijaḥ ||
tathā |
nihanmi sarvaṃ yad amedhyavad bhaved
dhatāś ca sarve ’suradānavā mayā |
rakṣāṃsi yakṣāḥ sapiśācasaṅghā
hatā mayā yātudhānāś ca sarve ||
etena mantreṇa susaṃyatātmā
vedīṃ ca sarvāṃ sakṛd ullikhya dhīraḥ |
[217]        śivāṃ hi buddhiṃ dhruvam icchamānaḥ
kṣiped dvijātir diśam uttarāntaḥ ||
evaṃ pitrye dṛṣṭam etad dvijasya
tasyāsurā varjayantīha śrāddham |
yasmin deśe paṭhyate hy eṣa mantras
taṃ vai deśaṃ rākṣasā varjayanti ||
'kṣipet' ullekhanasādhanam iti śeṣaḥ ||
tathā |
sakṛd eva hared darbhān piṇḍārthaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhān |
prāgdakṣiṇāgrān niyato vidhiś cāpy atra vakṣyate ||
madhusarpistilair yuktāṃs trīn piṇḍān nirvaped budhaḥ |
jānu kṛtvā tathā savyaṃ bhūmau pitṛparāyaṇaḥ ||
piṇḍān parikṣipet samyag apasavyam atandritaḥ |
annenādbhiś ca puṣpaiś ca phalair bhakṣyaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
pṛthaṅmātāmahānāṃ ca kecid icchanti mānavāḥ |
trīn piṇḍān ānupūrvyeṇa sāṅguṣṭhān muṣṭibandhanān ||
sānucarāṇāṃ yatnena pṛthag dadyād yathākramam |
savyetarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ vaden mantram imaṃ sadā ||
namo vaḥ pitaras tuṣṭā ity evaṃ śraddhayā punaḥ |
savyetarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ prathamaṃ piṇḍam āvahet ||
namo vaḥ pitaraḥ saumyā nityam evam atandritaḥ |
udakasya ca rekhāyām udapātrāvasecanam ||
[218]        kṣaumasūtraṃ navaṃ dadyāc chāṇaṃ kārpāsikaṃ tathā |
patrorṇaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃ ca kauśeyaṃ ca vivarjayet ||
varjayec ca daśāṃ prājño yady apy ahatakā bhavet |
śreṣṭham āhus traikakudam añjanaṃ nityam eva hi ||
gṛhītvā darbhapiñjūlais tribhiḥ kuryād yathāvidhi |
apasavyaṃ pitṛbhyas tu dadyād añjanam uttamam ||
tailaṃ kṛṣṇatilebhyaś ca yatnāt suparirakṣitam ||
'dakṣiṇāmukhatāprāgdakṣiṇāgratayor' vikalpaḥ | 'parikṣipet' pariveṣṭayet, vakṣyamāṇatajjalapuṣpādinā | 'apasavyaṃ' apradakṣiṇam | 'daśāṃ ca varjayet' iti śvetavastravyatiriktadaśāviṣayam, "dadyāt krameṇa vāsāṃsi śvetavastrabhavāṃ daśām” iti brahmapurāṇavacanāt | 'traikakudaṃ' sauvīrāñjanam ||
tathā |
peṣayitvāñjanaṃ samyag vedyā uttarato dvijaḥ ||
tathā |
tailaṃ piṇḍena dātavyaṃ piṇḍasyābhyañjanaṃ hi tat |
añjanābhyañjane gandhaḥ sūtrapraṇayanaṃ tathā ||
kauśaiḥ punarbhavaiḥ kāryam aśvamedhaphalārthinā |
ete vai pitaro devā devāś ca pitaraḥ punaḥ ||
puṣpagandhādidhūpānām eṣāṃ mantra udāhṛtaḥ |
brūyāc chrāddhe ca sāvitrīṃ piṇḍe piṇḍe samāhitaḥ ||
somāyeti ca vaktavyaṃ tathā pitṛmateti ca ||
tathā |
stotavyā ceha pṛthivī nivāpasya tu dhāriṇī |
vaiṣṇavī kāśyapī caiva tathaivaikṣvākavīti ca ||
udakānayane caiva stotavyo varuṇaḥ prabhuḥ |
[219] śātātapaḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā tu śaktyā dadyāt tu dakṣiṇām |
abhipretārthasiddhyartham iṣṭān kāmāṃs tu vācayet ||
dīrghā nadāś ca nadyaś ca viṣṇos trīṇi padāni ca |
evaṃ cāpaḥ prasiñcāno dīrgham āyur avāpnuyāt ||
apāṃ madhye sthitā devāḥ sarvam apsu pratiṣṭhitam |
brāhmaṇasya kare nyastāḥ śivā āpo bhavantu naḥ ||
lakṣmīr vasati puṣpeṣu lakṣmīr vasati puṣkare |
lakṣmīr vaste sadā goṣṭhe saumanasyaṃ sadās tu me ||
akṣataṃ cāstu me puṇyaṃ śāntiḥ puṣṭir dhṛtis tu me |
dadyāc chreyaskaraṃ loke tat tad astu sadā mama ||
dakṣiṇāyāṃ tu sarvatra bahu deyaṃ tathāstu naḥ |
pratigṛhyāśiṣas teṣāṃ kṛtvā viprān pradakṣiṇam ||
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya punas teṣāṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ |
uktvā caiva priyāṃ vācaṃ praṇipatya visarjayet ||
bhojane ’vatiṣṭhamāne svasti kurvanti ye dvijāḥ |
tad annam asurair bhuktaṃ nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
ācānteṣūdakaṃ dadyāt puṣpāṇi śayanāni ca |
yavo ’sīti japen mantraṃ śraddhābhaktisamanvitaḥ ||
dadyāt tad akṣayajalaṃ tilājyamadhusaṃyutam |
sapavitrān kuśān bhūmāv āstīrya salilaṃ tathā ||
tataḥ saṃsravapātrebhyo jalam ādāya cārcitam |
ūrjaṃ vahantiś ca japan piṇḍāṃś cāpy avasiñcati ||
ācamya prayato bhūtvā madhyamaṃ cātra jighrati |
patnyai prayacchanti ca taṃ śeṣān pātre nidhāpayet ||
samūlāṃs tān kuśān vahnāv ādhatte tat tatholmukam |
[220]        suvarṇaraupyapātrāṇi manojñāni śubhāni ca ||
hastyaśvarathayānāni śayanāni gṛhāṇi ca |
upānatpādukācchatracāmaravyajanāni ca ||
yajñeṣu dakṣiṇāḥ puṇyā iti saṃcintayan hṛdi |
daridro ’pi yathāśaktyā dadyād viprāya dakṣiṇām ||
matsyapurāṇe |
tataś ca svadhāvācakaṃ viśvedeveṣu codakam |
datvāśiṣaḥ pragṛhṇīyād dvijebhyaḥ prāṅmukho dvijaḥ ||
aghorāḥ pitaraḥ santu santv ity uktaḥ punar dvijaḥ |
gotraṃ tathā vardhatāṃ nas tathety uktaś ca taiḥ punaḥ ||
dātāro no’ bhivardhantām annaṃ caivety udīrayet |
etāḥ satyāśiṣaḥ santu santv ity uktaś ca taiḥ punaḥ ||
svastivācanakaṃ kuryāt piṇḍān uddhṛtya śaktitaḥ |
tathā |
tatas tān agrataḥ sthitvā pratigṛhyodapātrikām |
vāje vāje iti paṭhan kuśāgreṇa visarjayet ||
bahiḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt padāny aṣṭāv anuvrajet |
bandhuvargeṇa sahitaḥ putrabhāryāsamanvitaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
śrāddhāpavarge viprasya vaktavyaṃ svaditaṃ bhavet |
kṣatriyasyāpy atha brūyāt prīyantāṃ pitaras tv iti ||
apavarge tu viprasya śrāddhakarmaṇi bhārata |
akṣayyam abhidhātavyaṃ svasti śūdrasya bhārata ||
puṇyāhavācanaṃ daive brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
etad eva niroṃkāraṃ kṣatriyasya vidhīyate ||
vaiśyasya caiva vaktavyaṃ prīyantāṃ devatā iti |
[221] brahmapurāṇe |
icchanti kecid ācāryāḥ paścāt piṇḍanivedanam |
pūjanaṃ caiva viprāṇāṃ pūrvam eveha nityaśaḥ ||
tad dhi dharmārthakuśalo nety uvāca bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrvaṃ nivedayet piṇḍān paścād viprāṃś ca bhojayet ||
yamaḥ |
yad annaṃ viśadaṃ nāma kriyate tarpitair dvijaiḥ |
na ca nāmnā tad ākhyātaṃ tac ca bhūmau pradīyate ||
ye ‘kṛtacūḍā ye bālā ye ca garbhād viniḥsṛtāḥ |
anūḍhā yā mṛtāḥ kanyās teṣāṃ bhūmau pradīyate ||
apāṃ samīpe darbheṣu sikatāsu ca sarvaśaḥ |
anena vidhinā dattaṃ pitṝṇām akṣayaṃ bhavet ||
'viśadaṃ' vimalam, vikiraṇayogyaṃ kṛtvā bhūmau yad vikired ity arthaḥ | 'tarpitair dvijaiḥ' iti, 'vatsarair amāvāsyāyām' iti yat kālalakṣaṇā | tena tarpiteṣu dvijeṣv ity arthaḥ | 'na ca nāmnā tad ākhyātam' iti, uddeśyanāmaviśeṣeṇānirdiṣṭam | 'apāṃ samīpe' ityādi guṇaphalādhikāraḥ ||
manu-viṣṇū [manu, 3,247 - 246, viṣṇu, 81,22-23] |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syād darbheṣu vikiraś ca yaḥ ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigatam ajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca |
dāsavargasya tat pitrye bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate ||
[222] 'tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitāṃ’ akāraṇena kulastrītyāgavatām | 'ucchiṣṭaṃ' pātrastham asaṃskṛtapramītādīnām | 'bhūmigataṃ' 'uccheṣaṇaṃ' ucchiṣṭaṃ, 'dāsavargasya' | 'pitrye' pitṛkarmaṇi ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11,23-24] |
prāk saṃskārāt pramītānāṃ saṃpreṣyāṇām iti śrutiḥ |
bhāgadheyaṃ manuḥ prāha ucchiṣṭoccheṣaṇe ubhe ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigataṃ vikirel lepam audakam |
anupreteṣu visṛjed aprajānām anāyuṣām ||
'saṃpreṣyāḥ' dāsāḥ | 'lepam audakaṃ' yadannodakadigdhasya hastasya darbheṣu mārjanam | 'anupreteṣu' pitryādyanuyāyiṣu mṛteṣu madhye 'aprajānām anāyuṣāṃ' 'visṛjet', 'uccheṣaṇaṃ' vikiraṇaṃ lepam odakaṃ ca bhāgadheyaṃ kalpayed ity arthaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [ śrāddha, 110] |
agnau hutena devasthāḥ pitṛsthā dvijatarpaṇaiḥ |
narakasthāś ca tṛpyanti piṇḍair dattais tribhir bhuvi ||
śātātapaḥ |
agnau hutena devasthāḥ pitṛsthās tarpitair dvijaiḥ |
bhūmau dattena tiryaksthās tṛpyanti pitaraḥ sadā ||
hārītaḥ |
āyurdaḥ prathamaḥ piṇḍo dvitīyaḥ putradaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ṛddhidas tu tṛtīyo vai tasmān madhyamam āśayet ||
yā patnī putrakāmā syān madhyamaṃ piṇḍam aśnute |
prājāpatyena vidhinā sa tasyāḥ putradaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
[223] 'prājāpatyo vidhiḥ' 'apāṃ vauṣadhīnāṃ rasaṃ prāśayāmi bhūtakṛtaṃ garbhaṃ dhatsva' iti madhyamaṃ piṇḍaṃ patnyai prayacchati | “ādhatta pitaro garbhaṃ kumāraṃ puṣkarasrajam | yatheha puruṣo ’sad iti taṃ patnī prāśnīyāt" ity āpastambokteḥ ||
yamaḥ |
apsv ekaṃ plāvayet piṇḍam ekaṃ patnyai nivedayet |
ekaṃ ca juhuyād agnau trayaḥ piṇḍāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
yaṃ piṇḍaṃ plāvayed apsu candraḥ prīṇāti tena saḥ |
śaśī prīṇayate devān pitṝṃś caiva mahāyaśāḥ ||
tathā ca madhyamaṃ piṇḍaṃ patnī prāśnāti vāgyatā |
putrakāmāṃ saputrāṃ tāṃ kurvanti prapitāmahāḥ ||
yad evam aparaṃ piṇḍaṃ juhoty agnau dvijottamaḥ |
śāśvatān prāpyate lokāṃs tuṣya saḥ prapitāmahaiḥ ||
'śāśvatān' iti, 'prapitāmahaiḥ' 'tuṣya' parituṣya, 'śāśvatāṃl lokān' 'saḥ' śrāddhakartā, 'prāpyate' labhata ity arthaḥ |
manuḥ [3, 267 - 263] |
evaṃ nirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍāṃs tāṃs tadanantaram |
gāṃ vipram ajam agniṃ vā prāśayed apsu vā kṣipet ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kecit purastād eva kurvate |
vayobhiḥ khādayanty etān prakṣipanty anale ’psu vā ||
[224]        pativratā dharmapatnī pitṛpūjanatatparā |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍam adyāt samyak sutārthinī ||
āyuṣmantaṃ sutaṃ vidyād yaśomedhāsamanvitam |
dhanavantaṃ prajāvantaṃ dhārmikaṃ sātvikaṃ tathā ||
'evaṃ nirvapaṇam' iti, brāhmaṇabhojanāt prāk piṇḍadānapakṣe tisraḥ pratipattayo niyatāḥ pakṣidānādikāḥ | 'prakṣipanty anale ’psu vā’ iti pūrvam uktayor api jalānalayor anuvādaḥ pakṣikhādanavidhānārthaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [ śrāddha, 123, 125] |
piṇḍāṃs tu go’javiprebhyo dadyād agnau jaleṣu vā |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍam adyāt samyak sutārthinī ||
anyadeśagatā patnī garbhiṇī rogiṇī tathā |
tadā taṃ jīrṇavṛṣabhaś chāgo vā bhoktum arhati ||
asyārthaḥ — sarvapiṇḍānāṃ tāvad gojalādigocaratayā pratipattiḥ | madhyamapiṇḍasya tu yadā vṛṣe ’je vā pratipattis tajjīrṇe |
[225] matsyapurāṇe |
piṇḍāṃs tu go’javiprebhyo dadyād agnau jale ‘pi vā |
viprānte vāpi vikired vayobhir athavāśayet ||
palīntu madhyamaṃ piṇḍam āśayed vinayānvitām |
ādhatta pitaro garbham antaḥ santānavardhanam ||
vāyupurāṇe |
piṇḍam agnau sadā dadyād bhogārthī satataṃ naraḥ |
patnyai prajārthaṃ dadyād dhi madhyamaṃ mantrapūrvakam ||
uttamāṃ gatim anvicchan gobhyo nityaṃ prayacchati |
ājñāṃ prajñāṃ yaśaḥ kīrtim apsu nityaṃ nidhāpayet ||
prārthayan dīrgham āyuś ca vāyasebhyaḥ prayacchati |
ākāśaṃ gamayed apsu sthito vā dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
pitṝṇāṃ sthānam ākāśaṃ dakṣiṇādik tathaiva ca |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
evaṃ varān yācayitvā namaskṛtya piṇḍān agnau vāpsu vā kṣipet | patnīṃ vā madhyamaṃ piṇḍam aśnīyād ārtavasnātām |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 257] |
piṇḍāṃs tu go’javiprebhyo dadyād agnau jale ’pi vā |
prakṣipet satsu vipreṣu dvijocchiṣṭaṃ na mārjayet ||
tatra devalaḥ |
nivṛtte pitṛmedhe tu dīpaṃ pracchādya pāṇinā |
ācamya pāṇiṃ prakṣālya jñātīn śeṣeṇa toṣayet ||
tato jñātiṣu tuṣṭeṣu svān bhṛtyān pratipūjayet |
paścāt svayaṃ ca patnībhiḥ pitṛśeṣam udāharet ||
ekoddiṣṭeṣu śeṣaṃ ca brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samutsṛjet |
tataḥ svayaṃ ca bhuñjīta punar maṅgalabhojanam ||
[226] 'pracchāya' nirvāpya 'udāharet' | 'ekoddiṣṭeṣu' ekāhādiśrāddheṣu ||
manuḥ [3, 264 - 265] |
prakṣālya hastāv ācamya jñātiprāyaṃ prakalpayet |
jñātibhyaḥ satkṛtaṃ datvā bāndhavān api pūjayet ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ tu tat tiṣṭhed yāvad viprā visarjitāḥ |
tato gṛhabaliṃ kuryād iti dharmoṃ vyavasthitaḥ ||
'jñātiprāyaṃ’ jñātibahulam | 'tato gṛhabalim' iti, gṛhabaliśabdo bhūtayajñābhidhāyako vaiśvadevādinityamahāyajñopalakṣaṇaparaḥ |
tathā ca matsyapurāṇe |
nirvṛtya pratipatyarthaṃ paryukṣyāgniṃ sa mantravit |
vaiśvadevaṃ prakurvīta nityakaṃ balim eva ca ||
ādityapurāṇe |
pitṝn saṃtarpya vidhivad baliṃ dadyād vidhānataḥ |
vaiśvadevaṃ tataḥ kuryāt paścād brāhmaṇabhojanam ||
ata eva balivaiśvadevakramo matsyapurāṇānusāreṇa boddhavyaḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
nityakriyāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca kecid icchanti sattamāḥ |
na pitṝṇāṃ tathaivānye śeṣaṃ pūrvavad ācaret ||
pṛthakpākena nety anye kecit sarvam apūrvavat |
vasiṣṭhaḥ [11, 21-22] |
śraddhe nodvāsanīyāni ucchiṣṭānyādinakṣayāt |
cyotante vai sudhādhārās tāḥ pibanty akṛtodakāḥ ||
[227]         ucchiṣṭaṃ na pramṛjyāt tu yāvan nāstam ito raviḥ |
kṣīradhārās tato yānty akṣayāḥ saṃcarabhāginaḥ ||
'cyotante' kṣaranti | 'nodvāsanīyāni ucchiṣṭāni' iti bhūmiṣṭhocchiṣṭaviṣayam | 'ucchiṣṭaṃ na pramṛjyāt' iti bhūmigatocchiṣṭapātraviṣayam | 'saṃcarabhāginaḥ', anyasmai dattaṃ anyaṃ saṃcarati yad annaṃ tat 'saṃcaram, tad ye bhajante dāsādayaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
astaṃ gate tataḥ sūrye viprapātrāṇi cāmbhasi |
niḥkṣipet prayato bhūtvā sarvāṇy adhomukhāny api ||
dvitīye ’hani sarveṣāṃ bhāṇḍānāṃ kṣālanaṃ tathā |
anantā jāyate tṛptiḥ pitṝṇāṃ yena sarvadā ||
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ tathā peyaṃ yat kiṃcit pacyate gṛhe |
na bhoktavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tad anivedya kathaṃcana ||
jñātibhyaḥ satataṃ datvā bāndhavān api pūjayet |
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 155] |
evaṃ devān pitṝṃs trīṃś ca tarpayitvā vidhānataḥ |
putrabhṛtyādisahito gṛhastho bhoktum arhati ||
śātātapaḥ |
śeṣam annam anujñātaṃ bhuñjīta tadanantaram |
iṣṭaiḥ sārdhaṃ tu vidhivad dbuddhimāṃs tu samāhitaḥ ||
uśanā |
tair anujñātaḥ śeṣam iṣṭebhyo dadyāt svayaṃ vā bhuñjīta |
'svayaṃ vā' iti vāśabdaḥ samuccaye |
[228] āpastambaḥ [2, 17, 16] |
sarvataḥ samavadāya śeṣasya grāsāvarārdhyaṃ prāśnīyād yathoktam |
‘grāsāvarārdhya' grāsāsūnam | 'yathoktam' gṛhyoktaṃ, 'prāṇe niviṣṭo ’mṛtaṃ juhomi brahmaṇi ātmā ’mṛtatvāya' iti mantreṇa |
brahmapurāṇe [tai. āra., 10, 34, 1] |
bhaginyo bāndhavāḥ pūjyāḥ śrāddheṣu ca sadaiva hi ||
tathā |
vandimāgadhasūtāś ca tauryatrikavidas tathā |
alabdhalābhāḥ śrāddheṣu nāśayanti mahad yaśaḥ ||
tasmāt te ’pi vibhaktavyāḥ sakalaṃ ca vibhajya ca ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1,249] |
pradakṣiṇam anuvrajya bhuñjīta pitṛsevitam |
brahmacārī bhavet tāṃ tu rajanīṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha ||
nigamaḥ |
anujñāto gṛhyān bālavṛddhāṃś ca paritoṣya bhuñjīta |
tasyāha ubhayo rātrim ubhāv api brahmacāriṇau syātām ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ, vṛddhaśātātapaś ca prathame |
śrāddhaṃ datvā ca bhuktvā ca maithunaṃ yo ’dhigacchati |
bhavanti pitaras tasya tanmāse retaso bhujaḥ ||
[229]        yas tato jāyate garbho datvā bhuktvā ca paitṛkam |
na sa vidyām avāpnoti kṣīṇāyuś caiva jāyate ||
bṛhaspatir manu-devalau ca dvitīye |
tāṃ niśāṃ brahmacārī syāc chrāddhabhoktā tathaiva ca |
anyathā vartamānau tau syātāṃ nirayagāminau ||
śrāddhabhug vṛṣalītalpaṃ tadahar yo ’dhigacchati |
tasyāḥ purīṣe taṃ māsaṃ pitaras tasya śerate ||
tasmāt pradātā bhoktā ca śrāddhe niyamito bhavet |
arcakaś cārcitas tūbhau syātāṃ narakagāminau ||
'vṛṣalītalpam' iti maithunopalakṣaṇam |
śātātapaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā paraśrāddhe bhuñjate ye tu vihvalāḥ |
patanti pitaras teṣāṃ luptapiṇḍodakakriyāḥ ||
matsyapurāṇe |
punarbhojanam adhvānaṃ dyūtam āyāsamaithunam |
śrāddhakṛc chrāddhabhuk caiva sarvam etad vivarjayet ||
svādhyāyaṃ kalahaṃ caiva divāsvapnaṃ ca sarvadā |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
śrāddhe niyukto bhoktā vā bhojayitvā niyojya ca |
vyavāyī retaso garte majjayaty ātmanaḥ pitṝn ||
'vyavāyī' maithunakartā ||
