|| athapitrādisvarūpam ||
tatra manuḥ [3,284] |
vasūn vadanti tu pitṝn rudrāṃś caiva pitāmahān |
prapitāmahāṃs tathādityān śrutir eṣā sanātanī ||
devalaḥ |
sarvataḥ pitaraḥ pūjyā devatānāṃ ca devatāḥ |
śucayo nirmalāḥ puṇyā dakṣiṇāṃ diśam āśritāḥ ||
basavaḥ pitaro jñeyā rudrā jñeyāḥ pitāmahāḥ |
prapitāmahās tathādityāḥ śrutir eṣā sanātanī ||
pretān uddiśya yat karma kriyate mānuṣair iha |
tuṣyanti pitaras tena na pretāḥ pitaraḥ smṛtāḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1,269] |
vasurudrāditisutāḥ pitaraḥ śrāddhadevatāḥ |
[231]        prīṇayanti manuṣyāṇāṃ pitṝn śrāddhena tarpitāḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ya evaṃ vidvān pitṝn yajate vasavo rudrā ādityāś cāsya prītā bhavanti |
ka ete pitaro nāma yebhyo dattam ihākṣayam ||
vasavaḥ pitaro rudrāḥ pitāmahā ādityāḥ prapitāmahāḥ tebhyo dattam ihākṣayaṃ bhavati ||
atra yady api - "vasurudrāditisutāḥ pitaraḥ śrāddhadevatāḥ" ityādivākyebhyaḥ śrāddhe vasvādīnāṃ devatātvaṃ pratīyate, tathāpi 'amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam' ityādivākyebhyaḥ asāv etat ta ity evaṃ yajamānasya pitre, asāv etat ta iti pitāmahāya asāv etat ta iti prapitāmahāyeti śruteś ca, gotranāmasaṃbandhaviśeṣakīrtanena, svajanakādīnāṃ caturthyantatvena śravaṇād devatātvam | vasvādirūpeṇa ta eva dhyeyāḥ, 'ya evaṃ vidvān pitṛn yajate' iti paiṭhīnasivacanāt |
[232]
|| 23 ||
|| atha śrāddhastutiḥ ||
tatra yamaḥ |
ye yajanti pitṛn devān brāhmaṇān sahutāśanān |
sarvabhūtāntarātmānaṃ viṣṇum eva yajanti te ||
datvānena vidhānena labhate caturo varān |
dhanam annaṃ sutān āyur dadate pitaro bhuvi ||
āyuḥ putrān yaśaḥ svargaṃ kīrtiṃ puṣṭiṃ balaṃ śriyam |
paśūn sukhaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ prāpnuyāt pitṛpūjanāt ||
devalaḥ |
arogaḥ prakṛtaḥ svasthaś cirāyuḥ putrapautravān |
arthavān arthabhogī ca śrāddhakāmo bhaved iha ||
paratra paramāṃ tuṣṭiṃ lokāṃś ca vipulān śubhān |
śrāddhakṛt sa samāpnoti yaśaś ca vipulaṃ naraḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 270] |
āyuḥ prajāṃ dhanaṃ vidyāṃ svargaṃ mokṣaṃ sukhāni ca |
prayacchanti tathā rājyaṃ nṛṇāṃ prītāḥ pitāmahāḥ ||
hārītaḥ [a. 12] |
imaṃ śrāddhavidhiṃ puṇyaṃ kuryād vāpi paṭhet tu yaḥ |
sa sarvakāmasaṃyukto hy amṛtatvaṃ ca vindate ||
[233] bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 41] |
ya evaṃ vetti matimāṃs tasya śrāddhaphalaṃ bhavet |
upadeṣṭānumantā ca loke tulyaphalau smṛtau ||
hārītaḥ [a. 12] |
na tatra vīrā jāyante nārogā na śatāyuṣaḥ |
na ca śreyo ’dhigacchanti yatra śrāddhaṃ vivarjitam ||
api mūlaiḥ phalair vāpi tathā hy udakatarpaṇaiḥ |
avidyamāne kurvīta naiva śrāddhaṃ vivarjayet ||
[234]
|| 24 ||
|| athāmaśrāddham ||
tatra matsyapurāṇe |
āmaśrāddhaṃ yadā kuryād vidhijñaḥ śrāddhadas tadā |
tenāgnaukaraṇaṃ kuryāt piṇḍaṃs tenaiva nirvapet ||
jābāliḥ |
āmaśrāddhaprado ’nagniḥ sarvatraiva manīṣiṇām |
tenāgnaukaraṇaṃ kuryāt piṇḍaṃs tenaiva nirvapet ||
atredaṃ tātparyam—'sarvatra' amāvāsyādau śrāddhanimitte upasthite ’nagnir āmaśrāddhaṃ kuryāt | anagniś cātra pākasamarthāgnirahitaḥ | etac ca sakalapākasāmagryabhāvopalakṣaṇam | na punar anagnir anāhitāmiḥ, anupanītabhāryādirahitapravāsasthānāṃ pākena śrāddhābhidhānāt |
tathā ca matsyapurāṇe |
ubhābhyām api hastābhyām āhṛtya pariveṣayet |
praśāntacittaḥ salilaṃ darbhapāṇir aśeṣataḥ ||
śubhāṃs tu sūpaśākādyān nānābhakṣyāṃs tathaiva ca |
annaṃ tu sadadhikṣīraṃ goghṛtaṃ śarkarānvitam ||
tathā |
bhuktavatsu tatas teṣu bhojanopāntike nṛpa |
sarvavarṇikam annādyam aśnīyāt plāvya vāriṇā ||
samutsṛjed bhuktavatām agrato vikired bhuvi |
[235] tathā |
tṛptān jñātvodakaṃ dadyāt sakṛd viprakare tathā |
ācānteṣv api cācamya vāri dadyāt sakṛt sakṛt ||
tathā |
uccheṣaṇaṃ tu tat tiṣṭhed yāvad viprā visarjitāḥ ||
evamādivākyaiḥ pākena śrāddham abhidhāya tatraivoktam adhyāye |
etac cānupanīto ’pi kuryāt sarveṣu parvasu |
śrāddhaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ nāma sarvakāmaphalapradam ||
bhāryāvirahito ’py etat pravāsastho ’pi bhaktimān |
śūdro ’py amantravat kuryād anena vidhinā budhaḥ || iti |
etenaitad uktaṃ bhavati sāgner niragneś cāpady evāmaśrāddham iti,
sāgner niraneś cāpady evāmaśrāddhaṃ sadaiva hi |
evaṃ śūdro ’pi sāmānyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ ca sarvadā ||
namaskāreṇa mantreṇa kuryād āmānnavad budhaḥ |
iti matsyapurāṇa evābhidhānāt ||
[236]
|| 25 ||
|| atha jīvatpitṛjadiśrāddham ||
tatra manuḥ [3, 220 - 222] |
dhriyamāṇe tu pitari pūrveṣām eva nirvapet |
vipravad vāpi taṃ śrāddhe svakaṃ pitaram āśayet ||
pitā yasya tu vṛttaḥ syāj jīved vāpi pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ sa nāma saṃkīrtya kīrtayet prapitāmaham ||
pitāmaho vā tac chrāddhaṃ bhuñjītety abravīn manuḥ |
kāmaṃ vā tadanujñātaḥ svayam eva samācaret ||
‘dhriyamāṇe' avatiṣṭhamāne jīvatīti yāvat | nāmasaṃkīrtanam atra śrāddhopalakṣaṇārtham | 'śrāddham' atra śrāddhārtham | 'kāmaṃ vā tadanujñātaḥ' iti, pitrādibhojanādidaṃ pakṣāntaram | tena pitrādinānujñātaḥ 'kāmam' abhilaṣitaṃ ' samācared' ity arthaḥ |
tathā [9, 186] |
trayāṇām udakaṃ kāryaṃ triṣu piṇḍaḥ pravartate |
caturthaḥ saṃpradātaiṣāṃ pañcamo nopapadyate ||
viṣṇuḥ [75, 1-7] |
pitari jīvati yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt sa yeṣāṃ pitā kuryāt teṣāṃ kuryāt | pitari pitāmahe ca jīvati yeṣāṃ pitāmahaḥ | pitari pitāmahe prapitāmahe ca jīvati naiva kuryāt | yasya [237] pitā pretaḥ syāt sa pitre piṇḍaṃ nidhāya pitāmahāt paraṃ dvābhyāṃ dadyāt | yasya pitā pitāmahaś ca pretau syātāṃ sa tābhyāṃ piṇḍau datvā pitāmahapitāmahāya dadyāt | yasya pitāmahaḥ pretaḥ syāt sa tasmai piṇḍaṃ nidhāya prapitāmahāt paraṃ dvābhyāṃ dadyāt |
mānāmahānām apy evaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
mantroheṇa yathānyāyaṃ śeṣāṇāṃ mantravarjitam ||
'pitā pretaḥ syād' iti, pitaiva preto bhavati pitāmahaprapitāmahau ca jīvata ityarthaḥ | 'mantroheṇa' 'śundhantāṃ pitara' ityādimantreṣu pitara ity atra mātāmahā ityādipadaprakṣepalakṣaṇena | 'śeṣāṇāṃ pitṛvyamātulādīnām | 'mantravarjitam' āvāhanādikaṃ mantravarjaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
jīve pitari vai putraḥ śrāddhakālaṃ vivarjayet |
yeṣāṃ vāpi pitā dadyāt teṣām eva pracakṣate ||
pitāmahe ca jīve vai pitary eva samāpayet |
udakāñjaliṃ ca dīpaṃ ca añjanaṃ ca prayatnataḥ ||
[238] “na jīvantam atikramya dadyāt” iti śrutibalād amāvāsyādāv apy ekasyaiva śrāddhaṃ dadyāt | 'udakāñjalim' ityādivacanāc ca gṛhamedhīyavad apūrvam iti jayasvāmī ||
yajñapārśvapariśiṣṭam |
homāntaḥ pitṛyajñaḥ syāj jīve pitāri jānataḥ |
pitaraṃ bhojayitvā ca piṇḍau nipṛṇuyāt parau ||
yebhyaḥ piṇḍān pitā dadyāt tebhyaḥ putro ’pi dāpayet |
na jīvantam atidadyād ity eṣā naigamī śrutiḥ ||
jīvet pitāmaho yasya pitā cāntarito bhavet |
pitur ekasya dātavyam evam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
trīn vā piṇḍān samāropya madhyamaṃ tena bhojayet |
dvau piṇḍau brāhmaṇe dadyād agnau cāmbhasi vā kṣipet ||
ubhau yasya vyatītau ca jīvec cet prapitāmahaḥ |
piṇḍau nipṛṇuyāt pūrvau bhojayet prapitāmaham ||
nigamaḥ |
yo vā jīvati pitṝṇāṃ taṃ bhojayet | sapiṇḍān ity eke | jīvatām ajīvatāṃ vā deyam eveti hiraṇyaketuḥ | kasmād yajñavidhitvād darśasyāṅgatvāt | ebhya eva etad bhāgaṃ karotīti ca śruter ekasminn api jīvaty adānam iti kātyāyanaḥ ||
'yajñavidhitvāt' iti jīvanam ajīvanaṃ vā vivakṣitam | kiṃ tu 'yajñavidhitvāt ' pitrādyuddeśena [239] tyāgarūpasya yajñasyāvaśyakatayā vihitatvāt | 'darśasyāṅgatvāt' darśasya darśākhyasya karmaṇaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajño ’ṅgaṃ yata ity arthaḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
sapituḥ piṇḍakṛtyeṣu adhikāro na vidyate |
na jīvantam atikramya kiṃcid dadyād iti śrutiḥ ||
pitāmahe priyamāṇe pituḥ pretasya nirvapet |
pitus tasya ca vṛddhasya jīvec ca prapitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ sa nāma saṃkīrtya kīrtayet prapitāmaham |
pituḥ pituḥ pituś caiva tasyāpi pitur eva ca |
kuryāt piṇḍatrayaṃ yasya saṃsthitaḥ prapitāmahaḥ |
jīvantam atidadyād vā pretāyānnodake dvijaḥ ||
pituḥ pitṛbhyo vā dadyāt sa pitety aparā śrutiḥ |
'tasya ca vṛddhasya' iti, anena pitāmahaḥ parāmṛśyate | 'tasyāpi pituḥ pitāmahasyāpi pituḥ prapitāmahasya | 'saṃsthitaḥ ' mṛtaḥ |
[240] piṇḍapitṛyajñe kātyāyana[śrauta]sūtram |
pretebhyo dadāti jīvatpitṛko ’pi jīvāntarhite ’pi | jīvatpitṛkasya homāntam | anārambho vā | na vyavete jātūkarṇyaḥ | na jīvantamatīti śruteḥ ||
jīvatpitṛkasya trayaḥ pakṣāḥ — jīvantaṃ pitaram atikramya yebhyaḥ pitā dadyāt tebhyo dadyād ity ekaḥ | homāntaṃ 'somāya pitṛmate' [āpa. śrauta. 2,6,12.] ityādi dakṣiṇāgnau homamātram ity aparaḥ | 'anārambho vā' akaraṇam eva vā iti tṛtīyaḥ | 'na vyavete' iti na jīvatā kenacid vyavahite deyam ity arthaḥ |
pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahādhikāre piṇḍapitṛyajñe, āśvalāyanaśrautasūtram [2, 6, 22-24 ] |
juhuyāj jīvebhyaḥ | sarvahutaṃ sarvajīvinaḥ | nāmāny avidvāṃs tatapitāmahaprapitāmaheti ||
'juhuyāj jīvebhyaḥ' iti, pitrādīnāṃ madhye yo jīvati tasmai piṇḍaṃ juhuyāt | pretāya tu piṇḍapitṛyajñavidhānena dadyāt | sarve yadi jīvanti tadā trīn piṇḍān juhuyād ity arthaḥ |
[241]
|| 26 ||
|| atha dvipitṛkaśrāddham ||
tatra śāṅkhāyanasūtram |
piṇḍān yathāvanejitaṃ nidhāyobhāv ekasmin piṇḍe pitṛbhede | na jīvatpitur asti | na jīvāntarhitāya | yebhyo vā pitā tebhyaḥ putro homāntaṃ vā |
'ubhāv ekasmin' ubhau bījikṣetriṇau | 'ekasmin piṇḍe' ekasminn api piṇḍe saṃkīrtayed ity arthaḥ |
pravarādhyāyaḥ |
atha yadi pitṛvyena jñātinā vaikāryeṇa jātās te parigṛhītur eva bhavanti | atha yady eṣāṃ svabhāryāsv apatyaṃ na syāt tadrikthaṃ hareyuḥ | piṇḍaṃ caibhyas tripuruṣaṃ dadyuḥ | yady api syād ubhābhyām eva dadyur ity ācāryavacanam | dve śrāddhe kuryād ekaṃ vā | dve vā pṛthag uddiśya ekapiṇḍe dvāv anukīrtayet pratigṛhītāraṃ cotpādayitāraṃ ca | tṛtīyāt puruṣāt parigṛhītā ||
'eṣāṃ' kṣetrapitṛvyaprabhṛtīnāṃ bījinām ||
hārītaḥ |
nābījaṃ kṣetraṃ phalati nākṣetraṃ bījaṃ rohatīty ubhayadarśanād ubhayor apatyam ity apare | teṣām utpādayituḥ prathamaḥ pravaro bhavati | dvau dvau piṇḍau nirvāpe dadyur ekapiṇḍe vā [242] dvāv anukīrtayet | dvitīye putraḥ | tṛtīye pautro lepinaś ca | trīn anvācakṣāṇa ā saptamād ity eke |
'teṣām’ iti, teṣāṃ bījikṣetriṇāṃ pitṝṇāṃ madhye bījinaḥ pravaram ārṣeyaṃ prathamaṃ nirdiśet | tataḥ kṣetriṇa iti dvipravaratā | asya kratvaṅgārṣeyapravareṇa 'nirvāpe' pitṛyajñe, ekasminn eva dvāv anukīrtayed iti | atra vīpsā draṣṭavyā | "yadi dvipitā syād ekaikasmin dvau dvāv upalakṣayet” ity āpastambavacanānusārāt | tasmād ekasminn eva piṇḍe dvau dvau pitarau pitāmahau prapitāmahau cānukīrtanīyāv ity arthaḥ | 'dvitīye' pitāmahapiṇḍe | dvitīya iti prapitāmahapiṇḍasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | 'putraḥ' dvāmuṣyāyaṇasya | atrāpi dvau dvāv anukīrtayed ity anuṣaṅgaḥ | 'tṛtīye' prapitāmahapiṇḍe | 'pautro' dvāmuṣyāyaṇasya | atrāpi dvau dvāv anukīrtayed iti sambandhaḥ | 'lepinaś ca' anukīrtayed iti śeṣaḥ | ' trīn anvācakṣāṇaḥ' pitrādipiṇḍān anuvadan | 'ā saptamāt' saptamapuruṣaparyantam |
yājñavalkyaḥ [2,128] |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpāditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ ||
marīciḥ |
saṃgotrād vānyagotrād vā yo bhaved vidhavāsutaḥ |
piṇḍaśrāddhavidhānaṃ ca kṣetriṇe prāk pradāpayet ||
bījine tu tataḥ paścāt kṣetre jīvati cet kvacit |
bījine dadyur ādau tu mṛte paścāt pradīyate ||
ubhau yadi mṛtau syātāṃ bījiny ādau tadā dadet |
kṣetriṇy ādau na dattaṃ syād bījine nopatiṣṭhati ||
'sagotrād anyagotrād vā' iti savarṇamātrād ity arthaḥ ||
[243] nāradaḥ |
jātā ye tv aniyuktāyām ekena bahubhis tathā |
arikthabhājas te sarve bījinām eva te sutāḥ ||
dadyus te bījinaḥ piṇḍaṃ mātā cec chulkato hṛtā |
aśulkopanatāyāṃ tu piṇḍadā vodur eva te ||
'arikthabhājaḥ' kṣetriṇo rikthaṃ na bhajante |
|| 26-a ||
|| atha putrikāputraśrāddham ||
tatra śaṅkhalikhitau |
putrikā putravad iti pracetasas tasyā apatyaṃ putrikāsuto mātāmahapitāmahānāṃ piṇḍadaḥ | putradauhitrayor na viśeṣo ‘sty anugrahe |
'mātāmahapitāmahānām' iti vaṃśadvayopalakṣaṇam | 'putradauhitrayoḥ' aurasaputraputrikāsutayoḥ | 'anugrahe' narakottāraṇādisvarūpe |
manuḥ [9,132] |
dauhitro hy akhilaṃ riktham aputrasya pitur haret |
sa eva dadyād dvau piṇḍau pitre mātāmahāya ca ||
dvitīyaṃ tu pitus tasyās tṛtīyaṃ tu pituḥ pituḥ ||
'dauhitro' atra putrikāsutaḥ |
iti śrīmadgovindacandradevamahāsandhivigrahika-bhaṭṭaśrīhṛdayadharātmajaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite
kṛtyakalpatarau ḍyāmuṣyāyaṇaparva ||
[244]
|| 27 ||
|| athaikoddiṣṭam ||
tatra śātātapaḥ |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ naimittikam ihocyate |
tad apy adyaiva kartavyam ayugmān āśayed dvijān ||
matsyapurāṇe |
tata ekādaśāhe tu dvijān ekādaśaiva tu |
kṣatrādisūtakānte tu bhojayed ayujo dvijān ||
dvitīye ’hni punas tadvad ekoddiṣṭaṃ samācaret |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1,251-252] |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ daivahīnam ekārghaikapavitrakam |
āvāhanāgnaukaraṇarahitaṃ hy apasavyavat ||
upatiṣṭhatām ity kṣayya sthāne vipravisarjane |
abhiramyatām iti vaded brūyus te’ bhiratāḥ sma ha ||
'akṣayyasthāne' akṣayapadaprayogaprasaṅge 'upatiṣṭhatām' iti prayoktavyam ||
śāṅkhāyanaḥ |
athāta ekoddiṣṭam ekapavitrakam eko ’rgha ekaḥ piṇḍo nāvāhanaṃ nāgnaukaraṇaṃ nātra viśvedevāḥ | svaditam iti tṛptipraśnaḥ | upatiṣṭhatām ity akṣayyasthāne | ābhiramyatām iti visargaḥ | saṃvatsaram evaṃ prete |
[245] matsyapurāṇe |
svaditaṃ vikire brūyād visarge cābhiramyatām |
āśvalāyanagṛhyapariśiṣṭam |
atas tv ekādaśe ’hani śrāddhaṃ tasya vidhīyate |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yac chrāddham asapatnam iti sthitiḥ ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇam kṛtvā tripakṣaṃ pariśeṣayet |
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigataṃ vikiraṃ lepam audakam ||
anupreteṣu visṛjed aprajānām anāyuṣām |
ekoddiṣṭeṣu sarveṣu na svadhā nābhiramyatām ||
nāgnaukaraṇamantraś ca ekaś cātha tilodakam |
anūddiśya tu pretasya sarvatraiva pradīyate ||
ekaṃ pavitram eko ‘rghya ekaḥ piṇḍo vidhīyate |
anapatyeṣu sarveṣu na svadhā nābhiramyatām ||
svasty astu visṛjed evaṃ sakṛt praṇavavarjitaḥ |
ekoddiṣṭasya piṇḍe tu anuśabdo na yujyate ||
pitṛśabdaṃ na kurvīta pitṛhā copajāyate |
'asapatnaṃ' dvitīyarahitam | 'sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā' ityāder ayam arthaḥ — yaḥ tripakṣād arvāk akṛtasapiṇḍīkaraṇaḥ saḥ pakṣatraye pūrṇe ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhaṃ kuryād iti | 'anūddiśya tu pretasya' iti, yatra kvacid ekoddiṣṭe pārvaṇādau pretasya uddeśata utsṛjyate tatra sarvatraivam evānupreteṣūccheṣaṇavikirādikaṃ deyam | 'na svadhā' iti, svadhāvācanaṃ na kuryād ity arthaḥ | “svadhā vācyatām iti visṛjet” ity āśvalāyanoktāntimasvadhākāraniṣedhaḥ | 'nābhirabhyatām’ iti pretaikoddiṣṭaviṣayam | yat tu śāṅkhāyanavacanam 'abhiramyatām iti visargaḥ' iti tad itaraikoddiṣṭaviṣayam | 'anuśabdaḥ' iti, anuśabdavān 'ye [246] ca tvām anu' ityādimantraḥ 'pitṛśabdaṃ na kurvīta' 'namo vaḥ pitara' ityādimantraṃ na paṭhed ity arthaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha, 128, 132, 135, 136, 138]
ekoddiṣṭavidhānena yad ekasya pradīyate |
āvāhanāgnaukaraṇarahitaṃ daivavarjitam ||
vastrālaṃkāraśayyādyaṃ pitur yad vāhanāyudham |
gandhamālyaiḥ samabhyarcya śrāddhabhoktre tad arpayet ||
bhojanaṃ cānekavidhaṃ kārayed vyañjanāni ca |
yathāśaktatyā pradadyāc ca gobhūhemādikaṃ tathā ||
śrotriyā bhojanīyās tu nava saptaṃ trayodaśa |
jñātayo bāndhavā niḥsvās tathā cātithayo ’pare ||
pradadyād dakṣiṇāṃ teṣāṃ sarveṣām anurūpataḥ |
hatam aśrotriyaṃ śrāddhaṃ hatā yajñās tv adakṣiṇāḥ ||
tasmāt paṇaṃ kākiṇī vā phalaṃ puṣpam athāpi vā |
pradadyād dakṣiṇāṃ yajñe tayā samaphalo bhavet ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
ekādaśāhe kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ pretāya yatnataḥ |
śvaḥ kariṣya iti jñātvā brāhmaṇāmantraṇakriyā ||
snātvā caiva śucir bhūtvā pretaṃ vipreṣu yojayet |
gato ’si divyalokaṃ tvaṃ kṛtāntavihitāt pathaḥ ||
manasā vāyubhūtena vipre tvāhaṃ niyojaye |
pūjayiṣyāmi bhogena evaṃ vipraṃ nimantrayet ||
astaṃ gate tathāditye gatvā vipraniveśanam |
datvā tu pādyaṃ vidhivan namaskṛtya dvijottamam ||
pādau prakṣālya tailena pretasya hitakāmyayā |
prabhātāyāṃ tu śarvaryām udite ca divākare ||
śmaśrukarma kārayitvā nakhacchedaṃ tataḥ param |
[247]        snapanābhyañjanaṃ dadyād viprāya vidhipūrvakam ||
gṛhṇīyād bhūmibhāgaṃ ca sthaṇḍilaṃ tadanantaram |
nivāpadeśaṃ saṃgṛhya kartavyaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ mahat ||
nadīkūle nivāse vā pretabhūmiṃ vinirdiśet |
akṛtvā pṛthivībhāgaṃ nivāpaṃ ye tu kurvate ||
tadadhīnaṃ jagadbhadre taducchiṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet |
na devāḥ pitaras tasya gṛhṇantīha kadācana ||
patanti niraye ghore tenocchiṣṭena sundari |
sacailasnapanaṃ kṛtvā gatvā caiva mahānadīm ||
tīrthāni manasā dhyātvā taiḥ svam abhyukṣayed budhaḥ |
evaṃ śuddhiṃ tataḥ kṛtvā brāhmaṇaṃ śīghram ānayet ||
āgataṃ ca dvijaṃ dṛṣṭvā kartavyā svāgatakriyā |
arghyaṃ pādyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dṛṣṭatuṣṭena mādhavi ||
āsanaṃ copakalpeta mantreṇa vidhipūrvakam |
atrāsane devatānām anujñāto viśramyatām ||
prasādayet tv āsanaṃ gṛhyapūtaṃ
jñānāgniśuddhena kareṇa vipraḥ |
prakṣālya pāṇī śirasābhivandya
jānūni viśrāvya ca mantravac ca ||
upaveśyāsane bhadre chatraṃ tatra prakalpayet |
āvaraṇārthaṃ chatraṃ tad brāhmaṇāya pradīyate ||
paścād upānahau dadyāt pādasparśakare ubhe |
santaptavālukāṃ bhūmim asikaṇṭakitāṃ tathā ||
saṃtārayati durgāṇi pretaṃ dadad upānahau |
tilopacāraṃ kṛtvā tu viprasya niyatātmanā ||
nāmagotram udāhṛtya pretāya tadanantaram |
śīghram āhvānayed bhūmiṃ darbhahasto ’tha bhūtale ||
[248] 'mantra:'-
iha lokaṃ parityajya gato ’si paramāṃ gatim |
evam āhvānite gandhapuṣpadhūpān samarpayet ||
'gandhamantraḥ ' - 'sarvaḥ sugandhaḥ | 'puṣpamantra: ' – 'śriyā devyā' | 'dhūpamantro' 'bṛhasātirasān' |
tato vastrāṇi viprāya sarvāṇy ābharaṇāni ca |
punaḥ pakvānnakaṃ caiva kalpayec ca vasundhare ||
triṣu varṇeṣu kartavyaṃ pākaṃ bhojanam eva ca |
śuśrūṣām abhipannānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca varānane ||
dine trayodaśe prāpte pākena bhojayed dvijān |
evaṃ vidhiḥ prayoktavyaḥ śūdrāṇāṃ mantravarjitaḥ ||
amantrasya ca śūdrasya vipro mantreṇa gṛhyate |
bhujyamānasya vipreṇa pretabhāgasya sarvaśaḥ ||
jñātivargeṣu mitreṣu saṃbandhisvajaneṣu ca |
bhāgas tasya pradātavyo yenārtho yasya vidyate ||
śucir bhūtvā tu vidhivat kṛtvā śāntyudakāni ca |
praṇamya śirasā devi nivāpasthānam āgatam ||
stotavyāni ca mantrāṇi samabhaktyā vyavasthitam |
mantraḥ —
namo namo medini lokadhātri
urvi mahi śailagiridhāriṇi dharaṇe namaḥ |
dharaṇi kāśyapi jagatpratiṣṭhe
vasudhe namo ‘stu vaiṣṇavi bhūtadhātri ||
namo ’stu te sarvarasapratiṣṭhe
nivāpanāvīti namo namo ’stu te |
dadyāt tilodakaṃ tasya nāmagotram udāharan ||
[249]        saṃvāhya caraṇau tasya namaskṛtya dvijottamam |
saṃgṛhya pāṇinā pāṇiṃ mantreṇotthāpayed dvijam ||
dadyāc chayyāsanaṃ devi tathaivāñjanakāṅkatam |
añjanaṃ kaṅkataṃ gṛhya śayyām ākramya sa dvijaḥ ||
muhūrtaṃ tatra viśramya pāvanasthānam āgatam ||
gavāṃ lāṅgūlam uddhṛtya brāhmaṇo hastam ādadhat |
pātra audumbare tatra kṛtvā caiva tilodakam ||
upāharanti mantreṇa sāmavedād dvijātayaḥ |
mantraiḥ pūrvaṃ tadā toyaṃ sarvapāpapraṇāśanam ||
gṛhya caudumbaraṃ pātraṃ vipreṇa niyatātmanā |
mantreṇābhyukṣayet sarvaṃ svagṛhe yat tu tiṣṭhati ||
devāṃś cāgnimukhān sarvāṃs tarpayitvā vibhāgaśaḥ |
bhūtānāṃ ca baliṃ dadyād brāhmaṇebhyaś ca bhojanam ||
pretabhāgena dattena pūjayitvā dvijātayaḥ |
mṛtasya nāmatoddiśya hy akṣayaṃ copakalpayet ||
svasti vācya ca viprāya dadyāt pretavisarjanam |
prāśayet pañcagavyaṃ ca madhuparkeṇa saṃyutam ||
śrīrāmāyaṇe |
samatīte daśāhe tu kṛtaśauco vidhānataḥ |
cakre dvādaśikaṃ śrāddhaṃ trayodaśikam eva ca ||
yānāni dāsadāsīś ca veśmāni sumahānti ca |
bhūṣaṇāni ca mukhyāni rājñas tasyaurdhvadaihikam ||
rājadharmeṣu vasiṣṭhaḥ [19, 29-30]
rājani ca prete dadyāt prāsaṅgikam | etena mātāpitṛvṛttir vyākhyātā ||
[250] 'prāsaṅgikaṃ' prasaṅgo viṣayopabhogaḥ, tadarthaṃ śayyāsanādi | 'mātāpitṛvṛttiḥ ' mātāpitror api śrāddhe prāsaṅgikaśayyādidānena vartitavyam ity arthaḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ [ 20, 1-11] |
athāśaucāpagame susnātaḥ suprakṣālitapāṇipādaḥ svācāntas tv evaṃvidhān eva ca brāhmaṇān yathāśakty udaṅmukhān gandhamālyavastrālaṃkārādibhiḥ pūjitān bhojayet | ekavan mantrān ūhed ekoddiṣṭe | ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhāv ekam eva tannāmagotrābhyāṃ piṇḍaṃ nirvapet | bhuktavatsu brāhmaṇeṣu dakṣiṇayābhipūjiteṣu pretanāmagotrābhyāṃ dattākṣayyodakeṣu caturaṅgulapṛthvīs tāvad antarās tāvad adhaḥkhātā vitastyāyatās tisraḥ karṣūḥ kuryāt | karṣūtrayasamīpe cāgnitrayam upasamādhāya paristīrya tatraikaikasminn āhutitrayaṃ juhuyāt | somāya pitṛmate svadhā namaḥ | agnaye kavyavāhanāya svadhā namaḥ | yamāyāṅgirase svadhā namaḥ | sthānatraye ca prāgvat piṇḍanirvaṇaṃ kuryāt | tato ’nnadadhimadhughṛtamāṃsaiḥ karṣūtrayaṃ pūrayitvā etat ta iti japet | evaṃ mṛtāhe pratimāsaṃ kuryāt |
'ekavan mantrān ūhed' iti, yatra mantre bahuvacanāntaṃ padaṃ tatraikavacanāntam ūhanīyam ity arthaḥ |
yājñavalkya-vṛddhaśātātapau |
mṛte ’hani tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caiva māgham ekādaśe’ hani ||
ekādaśe’hani ' ity āśaucāpagamānantaradivasopalakṣaṇaparam | athāśaucavyapagama iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt ||
[251] chandogapariśiṣṭhe kātyāyanaḥ |
śrāddham agnimataḥ kāryaṃ dāhād ekādaśe ’hani |
dhruvāṇi tu prakurvīta pramītāhani sarvadā ||
dvādaśa pratimāsyāni ādyaṃ ṣāṇmāsikaṃ tathā |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ ceti pretaśrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa ||
ekāhena tu ṣaṇmāsā yadā syur api vā tribhiḥ |
nyūnāḥ saṃvatsarācc aiva syātāṃ ṣāṇmāsike tathā ||
'agnimato' mṛtasya | 'dhruvāṇi' āvaśyakāni vakṣyamāṇāni | 'saṃvatsarān nyūnā' iti sambandhaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
nṛṇāṃ tu tyaktadehānāṃ śrāddhāḥ ṣoḍaśa sarvadā |
caturthe pañcame vātha navamaikādaśe tathā ||
tato dvādaśabhir māsaiḥ śrāddhā dvādaśasaṃkhyayā |
kartavyā śucibhis teṣāṃ tatra viprāṃs tu tarpayet ||
caturthāhādiśrāddhāntarābhāvenaikādaśāhādiśrāddhaṣoḍaśakāc chrāddhaṣoḍaśakāntaram etat ||
daśadinakṛtyasamanantaram hārītaḥ |
śvobhūta ekānuddiṣṭaṃ pradāya svastyayanādidharmārthān pravartayanti ||
'ekānudiṣṭam' ekasyoddeśena |
[252] baijavāpaḥ |
ūrdhva daśamyām apo ’bhyupetya tasmai śrāddhakaraṇam | gavāsvinājena vā tasya caivāvṛdaṣṭakā |
'āvṛdaṣṭakā' yasyām aṣṭakāyāṃ āvṛtavapāhomādyāḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
mṛtasya māsi māsi śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | saṃvatsarānte visarjanam | navamāsyam ity eke ||
devalaḥ |
ekādaśādibhiḥ śrāddhe mṛtasyāpyāyanaṃ caret |
samyak saṃvatsare pūrṇe pitṝṇāṃ sthānam ṛcchati ||
hārītaḥ |
mṛte pitari vai putraḥ piṇḍam abde samācaret |
annaṃ kumbhaṃ ca viprāya pretanirdeśadharmataḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 255] |
arvāk sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ yasya saṃvatsarād bhavet |
tasyāpy annaṃ sodakumbhaṃ dadyād varṣe dvijanmane ||
pretādhikāre pāraskaraḥ [ 3, 10, 54-55] |
ahar ahar annam asmai brāhmaṇāyodakumbhaṃ ca adyāt | piṇḍam apy eke
nigṛhṇanti |
[253]
|| 28 ||
|| atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇam ||
tatra śātātapaḥ |
saṃvatsare tu vijñeyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tv iha |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇāntā ca jñeyā pretakriyā budhaiḥ ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ daivapūrvaṃ niyojayet |
pitṝn evāśayet tatra punaḥ pretaṃ ca nirdiśet ||
nirūpya caturaḥ piṇḍān piṇḍadaḥ pratināmataḥ |
ye samānām iti dvābhyām ādyaṃ tu vibhajet tridhā ||
eka eva vidhiḥ pūrvam arghapātracatuṣṭaye |
āsiśiñcet pitṛpātrāṇi pretapātreṇa mantravit ||
'pretakriyā' pretaśabdollekhanayā yā kriyā sā kriyate | tena sapiṇḍīkaraṇāpakarṣapakṣe ’py udakumbhadāne pretaśabdollekho na kārya iti | 'pitṝn eva' na tu mātāmahādīn | 'āryaṃ' pretapiṇḍam ||
āśvalāyanapariśiṣṭam |
atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | catvāry udapātrāṇi | ekaṃ mṛtasya | trīṇītareṣām | prathamaṃ pātraṃ triṣu pātreṣu niyojayet | samānīva ākūtir ity evam eva prathamaṃ piṇḍaṃ triṣu piṇḍeṣu niyojayet | madhumatībhiḥ saṃgacchadhvam iti dvābhyām | evaṃ caturtho ’nujñāpito bhavati | na cātra daivaṃ bhojayet | prāg eva daive ’rdham annādyaṃ ca dattvā gandhamālyaiḥ pātram arcayitvā hutaśeṣaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pāṇiṣu dadyāt | punas tad eva ca ||
[254]                annaṃ pāṇitale dattaṃ pūrvam aśnanty abuddhayaḥ |
pitaras tena tṛpyanti sarvaṃ na prāpnuvanti ca ||
yac ca pāṇitale dattaṃ yac cānnam upakalpitam |
ekībhāvena bhoktavyaṃ pṛthagbhāgo na vidyate ||
'anujñapito' visarjitaḥ | 'na cātra' iti, pitrādīnām āsanadānādipadārthakāṇḍe devāsanādipadārthasaṃkaraṃ na kuryād ity arthaḥ | 'hutaśeṣa' agnaukaraṇahomāvaśiṣṭam ||
viṣṇuḥ [21, 12-21] |
saṃvatsarānte pretāya tatpitre tatpitāmahāya ca tatprapitāmahāya brāhmaṇān bhojayet | agnaukaraṇam āvāhanaṃ pādyaṃ ca kuryāt | saṃsṛjatu [tvā] pṛthivī samānī va iti pretapādyapātre [pitṛpātra]traye yojayet | ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau piṇḍacatuṣṭayaṃ kuryāt | brāhmaṇāṃś ca svācāntān dattadakṣiṇān anuvrajya visarjayet | tataḥ piṇḍam arghapātrodakavat piṇḍatraye nidadhyāt | karṣūtrayasaṃnikarṣe ’py evam eva | evaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ māsikārthavad dvādaśāhaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā trayodaśe ’hni vā kuryāt | mantravarjaṃ hi śūdrāṇāṃ dvādaśe ’hni | saṃvatsarābhyantare yady adhikamāso bhavet tadā māsikārthe dinam ekaṃ vardhayet |
'karṣūtrayasaṃnikarṣe ’py evam' iti, pretaikoddiṣṭasaṃbandhikaṣūtrayasaṣnihitān api piṇḍān pitrādipiṇḍeṣu saṃsṛjet | 'māsikārthavat' māsi bhavāni śrāddhāni 'māsikāni’, tadarthaḥ pretāpyāyanādiḥ, tena yuktāni śrāddhāni māsikārthāni | āśaucāpagame 'dvādaśāhaṃ' dvādaśā- haḥsu kṛtvātra trayodaśe ’hni sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt | ādyaṃ [255] caikoddiṣṭam āśaucānta eva kṛtam | ūnaṣāṇmāsikonādiko ’pi ṣaṣṭhadvādaśadivasayor eva kartavye | evam etasmin pakṣe ṣoḍaśaśrāddhāni bhavanti | etac ca sāgner niragneś ca vṛddhyādāv apakarṣanimitte sati boddhavyam | vṛddheś ca sapiṇḍīkaraṇāpakarṣanimittatvam
arvāk saṃvatsarād vṛddhau pūrṇe saṃvatsare ’pi vā |
ye sapiṇḍīkṛtāḥ pretāḥ |
iti śātātapavākye ’nuvādadarśanāj jñātavyam ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
saṃvatsarānte visarjanaṃ navamāsyam ity eke |
ayaṃ ca sapiṇḍīkaraṇe navamāsavidhir daśamamāsādau vṛddhiśrāddhanimittasaṃbhave boddhavyaḥ | na tato ’rvāk sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam ||
baijavāpaḥ |
atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | saṃvatsaram ekaṃ piṇḍam anuddiśya samāpte saṃvatsare catvāry udapātrāṇi saṃyunakti | tathaikaṃ pretāya trīn pitṛbhyaḥ | tatpretapātraṃ pitṛpātreṣu siñcati | ye samānā iti dvābhyām evam eva piṇḍān yathā vimṛśati |
eṣa vo ’nugataḥ pretaḥ pitaras taṃ dadāmi vaḥ |
śivam astv iti śeṣāṇāṃ jāyantāṃ cirajīvinaḥ ||
samānīva ākūtiḥ samānā hṛdayāni vaḥ |
samānam astu vo mano yathā vaḥ susahāsati ||
saṃgacchadhvaṃ saṃvadadhvaṃ saṃ vo manāṃsi jānatām |
devā bhāgaṃ yathā pūrve saṃjānānā upāsate || iti |
abhiśastapatitabhrūṇaghnān strīṃ ca vyabhicāriṇīṃ nātidiśet ||
[256] hārītaḥ |
samāpte tu paśuśrāddhaṃ vidhivat pratipādayet |
caturo nirvapet piṇḍān prathamaṃ teṣu nirvapet ||
vapāṃ paśūṃś caruṃ caiva hy avadānāni yāni ca |
hutvā nītividhānena śeṣān piṇḍān samāvapet ||
tataḥ prabhṛti vai pretaḥ pitṛsāmānyam āpnuyāt |
vindate pitṛlokaṃ ca tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pravartate ||
vidhinā saṃniyuktasya yāvallomāni vai paśoḥ |
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi pitṝṇām akṣayaṃ bhavet ||
tāvadvarṣasahasrāṇi svargaloke mahīyate |
sarvakāmaiś ca badhnāti paśuyājī na saṃśayaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
caturbhyaś cārghapātrebhya ekaṃ vāmena pāṇinā |
gṛhītvā dakṣiṇenaiva pāṇinā ca tilodakam ||
saṃsṛjatu tvā pṛthivī ye samānā iti smaran |
pretaviprasya haste tu caturbhāgaṃ jalaṃ kṣipet ||
tataḥ pitāmahādibhyas tattanmantraiḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
ye samānā iti dvābhyāṃ tajjale tu samarpayet ||
arghas tenaiva vidhinā pātrāc ca vidhipūrvakam |
tebhyaś cārdhaṃ nivedyaiva paścāc ca svayam ācaret ||
atha tenaiva vidhinā darbhamūle ’vanejanam |
pitur datvā ca piṇḍaṃ tu dadyād bhaktyā tu pūrvavat ||
datvā piṇḍam athāṣṭāṅgaṃ dhyātvā taṃ ca subhāsvaram |
suvarṇarūpyadabhais tu tasmin piṇḍaṃ tatas tridhā ||
kṛtvā pitāmahādibhyaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ pretam arpayet |
saṃsṛjatu tvā pṛthivī vāyur agniḥ prajāpatiḥ ||
etan mantraṃ japed bhaktyā samānīvantam eva ca |
[257]        ye samānā iti dvābhyāṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pretam arcayet ||
vartulāṃs tu tatas tāṃs trīn piṇḍān kṛtvā prapūjayet |
arghapuṣpais tathā dhūpair vīpamālyānulepanaiḥ ||
mukhyaṃ tu pitaraṃ kṛtvā punas tv anyān yathākramam |
mṛte pitari yasyātha vidyate ca pitāmahaḥ ||
tena deyās trayaḥ piṇḍāḥ prapitāmahapūrvakāḥ |
tebhyaś ca paitṛkaḥ piṇḍo niyoktavyas tu pūrvavat ||
mātary atha mṛtāyāṃ tu vidyate ca pitāmahī |
prapitāmahapūrvas tu kāryas tatrāpy ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
jābālaḥ |
nāsapiṇḍāgnimān putraḥ pitṛyajñaṃ samācaret |
pāpī bhavaty akurvan hi pitṛhā copajāyate ||
ayam arthaḥ | aśaucānāntaram āgāmini darśe ’vaśyaṃkartavyapiṇḍapitṛyajñaśrāddhaṃ māsikaśrāddhapūrvakaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvaiva kuryāt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 253-254] |
gandhodakatilair yuktaṃ kuryāt pātracatuṣṭayam |
arghārthaṃ pitṛpātre tu pretapātraṃ prasecayet ||
ye samānā iti dvābhyāṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavad ācaret |
etat sapiṇḍīkaraṇam ekoddiṣṭaṃ striyā api ||
'striyā api' 'ekoddiṣṭam' ekādaśāhādi 'sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ' caivam eva kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
bhrātā vā bhrātṛputro vā sapiṇḍaḥ śiṣṭa eva ca |
sapiṇḍakriyāṃ kṛtvā kuryād abhyudayaṃ tataḥ ||
[258] viṣṇuḥ [21, 22] |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ strīṇāṃ kāryam eva yathā bhavet |
yāvajjīvaṃ tathā kuryāc chrāddhaṃ tu prativatsaram ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
mātuḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kathaṃ kāryaṃ sutair bhavet |
pitāmahyā sahaivāsyāḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ smṛtam ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [gobhila, 2, 102] |
mātuḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ pitāmahyā sahoditam |
yathoktenaiva kālena putrikāyā na cet sutaḥ ||
asyārthaḥ — putreṇa mātuḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ pitāmahyādibhiḥ saha kartavyam | putrikāputreṇa mātāmahādibhiḥ saha |
baudhāyana-yajñapārśvau |
ādiśet prathame piṇḍe mātaraṃ putrikāsutaḥ |
dvitīye pitaraṃ tasyās tṛtīye ca pitāmaham ||
śātātapo, vṛddhaśātātapaś ca dvitīye |
aprattāyāṃ pitā kuryāt prattāyāṃ tu patis tathā |
svena svenaiva gotreṇa saṃsthitāyāṃ tilodakam ||
saṃsthitāyāṃ tu bhāryāyāṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāntikam |
paitṛkaṃ bhajate gotram ūrdhvaṃ tu patipaitṛkam ||
ekamūrtitvam āyāti sapiṇḍīkaraṇe kṛte |
patnīpatipitṝṇāṃ tu tasmāt tadgotrabhāginī |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ yat pitṛbhyaḥ pradīyate |
sarveṣv aṃśaharā mātā iti dharmeṣu niścayaḥ ||
[259] 'saṃsthitāyāṃ' mṛtāyām | 'tilodakam' ity aurdhvadehikamātropalakṣaṇam | pitṛgotrabhājanaṃ cāsurādininditavivāhoḍhāviṣayam iti smṛtimañjarīkāraprabhṛtayaḥ | 'ūrdhvaṃ tu patipaitṛkam' ity atra hetuḥ 'ekamūrtitvam' ityādi | 'sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvam' ity anena pitrādyuddeśenaiva vyajyamāne dravye 'mātā' 'aṃśaharā' tṛptibhāginī bhavatīty uktam |
bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha., 85, 29, 26] |
svena bhartrā samaṃ śrāddhaṃ mātā bhuṅkte svadhāmayam |
pitāmahī ca svenaiva tathaiva prapitāmahī ||
bhartṛgotreṇa dātavyaṃ strīṇāṃ piṇḍodakaṃ sutaiḥ |
pitāmahapitṛbhyāṃ ca svena svena vidhānataḥ ||
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrāḥ pitṛgotrāpahārakāḥ |
bhartṛgotreṇa nārīṇāṃ deyaṃ piṇḍodakaṃ tataḥ |
caturthīhomamantreṇa tvaṅmāṃsahṛdayendriyaiḥ ||
bhartrā saṃyujyate patnī tadgotrā tena sā bhavet |
viṣṇuḥ [74, 1-8] |
aṣṭakāsu daivapūrvaṃ śākamāṃsāpūpakaiḥ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tv anvaṣṭakāsv aṣṭakāvad vahnau hutvā devapūrvam evaṃ mātre pitāmahyai prapitāmahyai ca pūrvavad brāhmaṇān bhojayitvā dakṣiṇābhiś cābhyarcyānuvrajya visarjayet | tataḥ ṣaṭkarṣūḥ kuryāt | tanmūle ca prāgudgagnyupasamādhānaṃ kṛtvā hutvā ca piṇḍatrayanirvapaṇaṃ pratyekasmin karṣūmūle kuryāt | karṣūtraye puruṣāṇāṃ karṣūtraye strīṇām | puruṣakarṣūtrayaṃ sānnenodakena [260] pūrayet | strīkarṣūtrayaṃ sānnena payasā | dadhnā māṃsena payasā pratyekaṃ karṣūtrayam | pūrayitvā japed etad bhavadbhayo bhavatībhyo ’stu cākṣayam ||
‘strīkarṣūtrayaṃ sānnena payasā' pūrayed ity arthaḥ | 'dadhnā māṃsena payasā pratyekaṃ karṣūtrayaṃ' pūrayed ity aparaḥ ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [ gobhila, 2,103] |
na yoṣidbhayaḥ pṛthag dadyād avasānadinād ṛte |
svabhartṛpiṇḍamātrābhyas tṛptir āsāṃ yataḥ smṛtā ||
tathā |
pitāmahaḥ pituḥ paścāt pañcatvaṃ yadi gacchati |
pautreṇaikādaśāhādi kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṣoḍaśam ||
naitat pautreṇa kartavyaṃ putravāṃś cet pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ sapiṇḍatāṃ kṛtvā kuryān māsānumāsikam ||
asaṃskṛtau na saṃskāryau pūrvau pautraprapautrakaiḥ |
pitaraṃ tatra saṃskuryād iti kātyāyano ’bravīt ||
pāpiṣṭham api śuddhena śuddhaṃ pāpakṛtāpi vā |
pitāmahena pitaraṃ saṃskuryād iti niścayaḥ ||
'pūrvau' pitāmahaprapitāmahau yatra kenāpi nimittena 'asaṃskṛtau' akṛtasapiṇḍīkaraṇau, tatrāpi tābhyāṃ saha 'pitaraṃ saṃskuryāt ' sapiṇḍayed ity arthaḥ ||
iti śrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau śrāddhakāṇḍe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaparva ||
[261]
|| 29 ||
|| atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇottara śrāddham ||
tatra śātātapaḥ |
pradānaṃ yatra yatraiṣāṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt param |
tatra pārvaṇavac chrāddhaṃ jñeyam abhyudayād ṛte ||
arvāk saṃvatsarād vṛddhau pūrṇe saṃvatsare ’pi vā |
ye sapiṇḍīkṛtāḥ pretā na teṣāṃ tu pṛthakriyā ||
pṛthak piṇḍapradānaṃ tu tasmād ūrdhvaṃ vidhīyate |
pretānām iha sarveṣāṃ ye mantrais tu niyojitāḥ ||
kṛtārthās te hi saṃvṛttāḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇe kṛte |
pretatvāc ceha nistīrṇāḥ prāptāḥ pitṛgaṇaṃ tu ye ||
yaḥ sapiṇḍīkṛtaṃ pretaṃ pṛthak piṇḍe niyojayet |
vidhighnas tena bhavati pitṛhā copajāyate ||
'pṛthak piṇḍe', pṛthak itarapiṇḍāsahabhāvena piṇḍo yatra dīyate ekoddiṣṭe, tasmin 'niyojayet' devatātveneti śeṣaḥ |
jābālaḥ |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya māsikaṃ śrāddham eva ca |
śrāddhaṃ prati rucau caiva mātāpitror mṛte ’hani ||
asapiṇḍīkṛtaṃ pretam ekoddiṣṭena tarpayet |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ tribhiḥ sāmānyam iṣyate ||
aurasakṣetrajau putrau vidhinā pārvaṇena tu |
pratyabdam itare kuryur ekoddiṣṭaṃ sutā daśa ||
[262] 'pitṛyajñaḥ' atra pitṛtarpaṇam |
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 256] |
mṛte ’hani tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ ca vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivam ādyam ekādaśe ’hani ||
matsyapurāṇe |
tataḥ prabhṛti saṃkrāntāv uparāgādiparvasu |
tripiṇḍam ācarec chrāddham ekoddiṣṭaṃ mṛtāni ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ parityajya pārvaṇaṃ yaḥ samācaret |
sadaivapitṛhā sa syān mātṛbhrātṛvināśakaḥ ||
mṛtāhe pārvaṇaṃ kurvann adho ’dho yāti mānavaḥ |
saṃpṛkteṣv ākulībhāvaḥ preteṣu ca tato bhavet ||
tathā |
yatra yatra pradātavyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt param |
pārvaṇena vidhānena deyam agnimatā sadā ||
atra ca pratyabdaṃ mṛtāhani pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭayos tulyavadvikalpe prāpte vyavasthitir yathā — aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś ca yaḥ sāgniḥ pratyabdaṃ pārvaṇam eva kuryāt,
aurasakṣetrajau putrau vidhinā pārvaṇena tu |
iti jābālavacanāt,
yatra yatra pradātavyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt param |
pārvaṇena vidhānena deyamagnimatā sadā ||
iti matsyapurāṇavacanāc ca | niragnikau tv aurasakṣetrajau, itare tu putrāḥ sāgnayo niragnayaś ca pratyabdaṃ mṛtāhany ekoddiṣṭam eva kuryur iti mahārṇavaprakāśakāraḥ ||
[263]
|| 30 ||
|| atha śrāddhādhikāriṇaḥ ||
tatra marīciḥ |
mṛte pitari putreṇa kiyā kāryā vidhānataḥ |
bahavaḥ syur yadā putrāḥ pitur ekatra vāsinaḥ ||
sarveṣāṃ tu mataṃ kṛtvā jyeṣṭhenaikena yat kṛtam |
dravyeṇa cāvibhaktena sarvair eva kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
yady ekajātā bahavaḥ pṛthakkṣetrāḥ pṛthagdhanāḥ |
ekapiṇḍāḥ pṛthakśaucāḥ piṇḍas tv āvartate triṣu ||
'pṛthakkṣetrāḥ' vijātīyamātṛjātāḥ | 'ekapiṇḍāḥ' ekam eva piṇḍaṃ dadyuḥ, na punaḥ pratipuruṣaṃ pṛthak piṇḍadānam | 'pṛthakśaucāḥ' vijātīyamātṛsaṃbandhena pṛthakśuddhayaḥ | 'piṇḍas tv āvartate triṣu' sapiṇḍatā tu tripuruṣeṣu bhavatīty arthaḥ ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
yāvat tripuruṣam avicchinnaṃ tāvat piṇḍaḥ pradīyate |
tripuruṣavināśe tu pṛthak piṇḍodakakriyā ||
'avicchinnaṃ' vijātīyapuruṣasaṃpraveśena jīvanena ca patitatvena ca vicchedarahitam | 'tripuruṣavināśe' vijātīyakṣatriyādipraveśena ||
[264] bṛhaspatiḥ [śrāddha; 150] |
pramītasya piṇḍaṃ putraiḥ śrāddhe deyaṃ prayatnataḥ |         jñātibandhusuhṛcchiṣyair ṛtvigbhṛtyapurohitaiḥ ||
atra ca putrādipūrvapūrvābhāva uttarottarādhikāro boddhavyaḥ |
gautamaḥ [15, 13-14] |
putrābhāve sapiṇḍā mātṛsapiṇḍā śiṣyāś ca dadyuḥ | tadabhāve ṛtvigācāryau ||
viṣṇupurāṇe (3, 13, 31-40] |
putraḥ pautraḥ prapautro vā tadvad vā bhrātṛsantatiḥ |
sapiṇḍasantatir vāpi kriyārhā nṛpa jāyate ||
teṣām abhāve sarveṣāṃ samānodakasantatiḥ |
mātṛpakṣasya piṇḍena saṃbandho yo jalena vā ||
kuladvaye ‘pi cocchinne strībhiḥ kāryāḥ kriyā nṛpa |
saṅghātāntargatair vāpi kāryāḥ pretasya yā kriyāḥ ||
ucchinnabandhurikthād vā kārayed avanīpatiḥ |
pūrvāḥ kriyā madhyamāś ca tathā caivottarāḥ kriyāḥ ||
triprakārāḥ kriyā hy etā saṃsthābhedān śṛṇuṣva me |
ādāhād vāryāyudhādisparśād antyāś ca yāḥ kriyāḥ ||
tāḥ pūrvā madhyamā māsi māsy ekoddiṣṭasaṃjñitāḥ |
prete pitṛtvam āpanne sapiṇḍīkaraṇād anu ||
[265]        kriyante yāḥ kriyāḥ pitryāḥ procyante tā nṛpottarāḥ |
pitṛmātṛsapiṇḍais tu samānasalilais tathā ||
saṅghātāntargataiś caiva rājñā vā dhanahāriṇā |
pūrvāḥ kriyās tu kartavyāḥ putrādyair eva cottarāḥ ||
dauhitrair vā naraśreṣṭha kāryās tattanayais tathā |
mṛtāhani tu kartavyāḥ strīṇām apy uttarāḥ kriyāḥ ||
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ rājann ekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ |
'mātṛpakṣasya piṇḍena saṃbandhaḥ' mātṛsapiṇḍataḥ, 'mātṛpakṣasya jalena saṃbandho' mātṛsamānodakataḥ | ' utsannabandhurikthāt ' utsannā bandhavo yasya sa tathā, tasya 'rikthāt' | 'ādāhādvāryāyudhādisparśād antyādi', 'antyāḥ' anto maraṇaṃ, tatra bhavāḥ, tāś ca dāhopakramā vāryāyudhādisparśaparyantāḥ pūrvā ity arthaḥ | 'māsi māsi' ity ekādaśāhādisapiṇḍīkaraṇapūrvapretakriyopalakṣaṇam | atra ca pitṛmātṛsapiṇḍādayaḥ pūrvakriyām eva kuryur nottarām | madhyamakriyāyāṃ tv eṣām aniyamaḥ | uttarakriyāyāṃ tu putrādyā bhāvisantatiparyantā dauhitrās tattanayāś ca niyatāḥ | 'dauhitraiḥ' 'tattanayaiḥ' iti ca putrikāputraviṣayam ||
brahmapurāṇe |
na kadācit sagotrāya śrāddhaṃ kāryam agotrajaiḥ |
asamāptavratasyāpi kartavyaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ ||
śrāddhādi mātāpitṛbhir na tu teṣāṃ karoti saḥ |
nityaṃ gurukulasthasya guruḥ sabrahmacāriṇaḥ ||
[266]        niranvaye sapiṇḍe tu mṛte sati dayānvitaḥ |
tadaśaucaṃ purā tīrtvā kuryāt tat pitṛvat kriyām ||
trayāṇām āśramāṇāṃ tu kuryād dāhādikāṃ kriyām |
yateḥ kiṃcin na kartavyaṃ na cānyeṣāṃ karoti saḥ ||
guruḥ karoti śiṣyāṇāṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ sadā |
kṛtvā tat paitṛkaṃ śaucaṃ svajātivihitaṃ ca yat ||
bhrātur bhrātā svayaṃ cakre tadbhāryā cen na vidyate |
tasya bhrātṛsutaś cakre yasya nāsti sahodaraḥ ||
dattānāṃ cāpy adattānāṃ kanyānāṃ kurute pitā |
caturthe ’hani tās teṣāṃ kurvīran susamāhitāḥ ||
mātāmahānāṃ dauhitrāḥ kurvanty ahani cāpare |
te ‘pi teṣāṃ prakurvanti dvitīye ’hani sarvadā ||
jāmātuḥ śvaśuraś cakre teṣāṃ te ’pi ca saṃyatāḥ |
mitrāṇāṃ tadapatyānāṃ śrotriyāṇāṃ gurus tathā ||
bhāgineyasutānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ tv apare ’hani |
śrāddhaṃ tu kṛtvā prathamaṃ snātvā kṛtvā jalakriyām ||
mṛte rājñi sapiṇḍe tu nirapatye purohitaḥ |
mantrī vā tadaśaucaṃ tu kṛtvā paścāt karoti saḥ ||
brāhmaṇas tv anyavarṇānāṃ na karoti kadācana |
kāmāl lobhād bhayād vāpi kṛtvā tajjātitāṃ vrajet ||
putrāḥ kurvanti viprāya kṣatraviṭchūrayonayaḥ |
sa tādṛśebhyaḥ putrebhyo na karoti kadācana ||
svamātā kurute teṣāṃ te ’pi tasyāś ca kurvate ||
svaṃ gotraṃ yasya saḥ 'svagotraḥ', vidyamānasagotra ityarthaḥ | yasmai śrāddhaṃ prakartavyaṃ tasya sagotre vidyamāne anyagotreṇa saṅghātāntargatena rājñā vā śrāddhaṃ na kartavyam | 'asamāptavratasyāpi' 'tat pitṛvat' yathā pitā putrasya yāvad yādṛśīṃ [267] kriyāṃ karoti tām asapiṇḍo 'dayānvitaḥ' kuryāt | 'yateḥ kiṃcin na kartavyam' iti dāhādisapiṇḍīkaraṇāntapretakriyā-viṣayam |
tathā ca mahābhārate |
na dagdhavyo na dagdhavyo viduro ’yaṃ kadācana |
jñānadagdhaśarīrasya punardāho na vidyate ||
'svajātivihitaṃ ca yat' āśaucāntavāryāyudhādisparśādi |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
sarvābhāve striyaḥ kuryuḥ svamātṛṇām amantrakam |
tadabhāve tu nṛpatiḥ kārayet svakuṭumbavat ||
tajjātīyair naraiḥ samyag dāhādyāḥ sakalāḥ kriyāḥ |
'sarvābhāve striyaḥ kuryuḥ svabhartṛṇām amantrakam' iti patnīvyatiriktāvaruddhādistrīviṣayam,
bhrātur bhrātā svayaṃ cakre tadbhāryā cen na vidyate |
ity arvāg eva patnyā adhikāradarśanāt |
tathā |
strīṇām apy evam evaitad ekoddiṣṭam udāhṛtam |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tāsāṃ putrābhāve na vidyate ||
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ kāryam ekoddiṣṭaṃ naraiḥ striyāḥ |
mṛtāhani yathānyāyaṃ nṛṇāṃ yadvad ihoditam ||
'strīṇām apy evam eva' iti, yādṛśena sambandhena pitṛvyatvādinā puruṣāṇām ekādaśāhādi tādṛśenaiva sambandhena strīṇām api tat kartavyam iti ||
[168]
|| 31 ||
|| athābhyudayikam ||
tatra viṣṇupurāṇe [3, 13, 5-6] |
kanyāputravivāheṣu praveśe navaveśmanaḥ |
nāmakarmaṇi bālānāṃ cūḍākarmādike tathā ||
sīmantonnayane caiva putrādimukhadarśane |
nāndīmukhaṃ pitṛgaṇaṃ pūjayet prayato gṛhī ||
tathā |
yugmāṃs tu prāṅmukhān viprān bhojayen manujeśvara |
matsyapurāṇe |
utsavānandasantānayajñodvāhādimaṅgale |
mātaraḥ prathamaṃ pūjyāḥ pitaras tadanantaram ||
tato mātāmahāś caiva viśvedevās tathaiva ca |
brahmapurāṇe |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
trayo hy aśrumukhā hy ete pitaraḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
tebhyaḥ pūrvatarā ye ca prajāvantaḥ sukhaidhitāḥ |
te tu nāndīmukhā nāndī samṛddhir iti kathyate ||
tathā |
karmaṇy athābhyudayike maṅgalyavati śobhane |
janmany athopanayane vivāhe putrakasya ca ||
[269]        pitṝn nāndīmukhān nāma tarpayed vidhipūrvakam |
paṭhec chakunisūktaṃ tu svastisūktaṃ śubhaṃ tathā |
nāndīmukhān pitṝn bhaktyā sāñjaliś ca samāhvayet |
tathā |
paṭhan pavitraṃ mantraṃ tu viśvedevāḥ sa āgata |
praśastān śādvalān darbhān āstīrya prāṅmukhaṃs tathā ||
rakṣoghnīr juhuyād agnau samidarthe mahauṣadhīḥ |
tilārthaṃ tatra vikiret praśastāṃś ca tathā yavān ||
sarvaṃ yajñopavītena na kuryād apasavyakam |
annaṃ dadyāc ca daivena tīrthena ca japan svadhām ||
kaccit saṃpannam etan me tān pṛcchec ca praharṣayan |
susaṃpannaṃ ca te brūyuḥ sarvasiddhiṃ tataḥ kṣipet ||
nāndīmukhebhyaś cākṣayyam idam astv iti saṃjapet |
prāṅmukhāṃs tv atha darbhās tu dadyāt kṣīrāvanejanam ||
datvā nāndīmukhebhyaś ca prācīṃ gacchan diśaṃ kramāt |
śālyannaṃ dadhimadhvaktaṃ badarāṇi yavāṃs tathā ||
miśrīkṛtya tu catvāri piṇḍān śrīphalasaṃnibhān |
dadyān nāndīmukhebhyaś ca pitṛbhyo vidhipūrvakam ||
arghaṃ puṣpaṃ ca dhūpaṃ ca praśastam anulepanam |
vāsaś cāpy ahataṃ yugmaṃ deyaṃ ca sadaśaṃ samam ||
drākṣāmalakamūlāni yavāṃś cātha nivedayet |
tathaiva dakṣiṇārthaṃ tu dadyād vipreṣu sarvadā ||
athākṣayyodakasthāne dattvā kṣīrayavodakam |
prapitāmahasaṃjñebhyo nāndīmukhyaś ca mātaraḥ ||
mātāmahipitāmahyaḥ prapitāmahya eva hi |
mātāmahebhyaś ca tathā nāndīvaktrebhya eva ca ||
pramātāmahasaṃjñebhyo bhavadbhiś ca svaghocyatām |
[270]        astu svadheti te taṃ ca jalpanti prahasāḥ śubham ||
viśvedevāś ca prīyantām iti dātā bravīti tān |
prītā bhavantu te taṃ ca vadanti madhurākṣaram ||
tamūṣu vājinam iti paṭhaṃs tāṃś ca visarjayet |
atha nāndīmukhībhyaś ca mātṛbhyaḥ śrāddham uttamam ||
anena vidhinā kāryam sīmantonnayanādiṣu |
'samaṃ' sādhāraṇam ekam eva, pratipiṇḍaṃ bhinnam ity arthaḥ | 'mūlam' ārdrakādi | 'prahasāḥ' prakṛṣṭahāsāḥ | atra ca 'nāndīmukhāḥ pitaraḥ pitāmahāḥ prapitāmahā' ityādikātyāyana-vākyadarśanāt pitrādaya eva nāndīmukhaviśeṣaṇavanto devatāḥ | yat tu brahmapurāṇe pitrāditrikasyāśrumukhatvam uktvā tataḥ pareṣāṃ nāndīmukhatvena vṛddhiśrāddha-devatātvābhidhānam, tajjīvatpitrāditrayasya śrāddhakartur vṛddhiśrāddhe devatābhāvārtham | tataś ca tasyāpy avaśyaṃ kartavyam idam iti | yat tu pārvaṇādiśrāddhaṃ tat tena na kartavyam, viṣṇusmṛtau 'pitari pitāmahe prapitāmahe ca jīvati naiva kuryāt' iti niṣedhāt |
kātyāyanaḥ |
ābhyudayike pradakṣiṇam upacāraḥ | pūrvāhṇe pitryamantravarjṃ [japaḥ |] ṛjavo darbhāḥ | yavais tilārthaḥ | saṃpannam iti tṛptipraśnaḥ | [susaṃpannam itītare brūyuḥ] dadhibadarākṣatamiśrāḥ piṇḍāḥ | nāndīmukhān pitṝn āvāhaviṣya iti pṛcchati | āvāhayety anujñāto nāndīmukhāḥ pitaraḥ prīyantām iti akṣayyasthāne nāndīmukhān pitṝn vācayiṣya iti pṛcchati | vācyatām ity anujñāto nāndīmukhāḥ pitaraḥ pitāmahāḥ [271] prapitāmahāś ca [mātāmahāḥ pramātāmahāḥ vṛddhapramātāmahāś ca] prīyantām iti na svadhāṃ prayuñjīta | yugmān āśayet [ atra] ||
yājñavalkyaḥ [1, 250] |
evaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya vṛddhau nāndīmukhān pitṛn |
yajeta dadhikarkandhumiśrān piṇḍān yavaiḥ kriyāḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
pūrvāhṇe daivikaṃ kāryaṃ śrāddham abhyudayārthinā |
savyena copavītena ṛjudarbhaiś ca dhīmatā ||
apasavyaṃ ca yo dadyād vṛddhiśraddham atikramāt |
na tasya devās tuṣyanti pitaraś ca yathāvidhi ||
pitṝṇāṃ rūpam āsthāya devatānnaṃ samaśnute |
tasmāt savyena dātavyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddheṣu nityaśaḥ ||
yugmāṃs tu bhojayed vṛddhau brāhmaṇān niyataḥ śuciḥ |
nāndīmukhās tu pitaras tṛpyantām iti vācayet ||
mātuḥ śrāddhaṃ tu pūrvaṃ syāt pitṝṇāṃ tadanantaram |
tato mātāmahānāṃ ca vṛddhau śrāddhatrayaṃ smṛtam ||
triṣv apy eteṣu yugmāṃs tu bhojayed brāhmaṇān śuciḥ |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu savyena pradadyād devapūrvakam ||
vasavaḥ pitaro jñeyā rudrāś caiva pitāmahāḥ |
prapitāmahās tathādityā ity eṣā naigamī śrutiḥ ||
apasavyaṃ na kurvīta na kuryād apradakṣiṇam |
yathaivopacared devāṃs tathā vṛddhau pitṝn api ||
pavitrapāṇir ācānta upaviṣṭaḥ samāhitaḥ |
pradadyāt prāṅmukhaḥ piṇḍān vṛddhau savyena vāgyataḥ ||
[272]        prāgagreṣu ca darbheṣu piṇḍān dadyāt tu vṛddhiṣu |
pārvaṇe dakṣiṇāgreṣu sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tathā ||
nāniṣṭvā tu pitṝn śrāddhe karma daivikam ācaret |
tebhyo ’pi mātaraḥ pūrvaṃ pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
akṛtvā mātṛyāgaṃ tu yaḥ śrāddhe pariveṣayet |
tasya krodhasamāviṣṭhā hiṃsām icchanti mātaraḥ ||
'daivikam' atrābhyudayikam, pitṝṇāṃ nāndīmukhānāṃ devavadupacārāt | 'tṛpyantām iti vācayet' akṣayyasthāne iti śeṣaḥ | 'mātṛyāgo' mātṛpūjā ||
ābhyudayikaprastāve mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
vaiśvadevavihīne tu kecid icchanti mānavāḥ |
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ [1,13 - 19] |
karmādiṣu tu sarveṣu mātaraḥ sagaṇādhipāḥ |
pūjanīyāḥ prayatnena pūjitāḥ pūjayanti tāḥ ||
pratimāsu ca śuddhāsu likhitāsu paṭādiṣu |
api cākṣatapuñjeṣu naivedyaiś ca pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
kuḍyalagnāṃ vasor dhārāṃ saptadhārāṃ ghṛtena tu |
kārayet pañcadhārāṃ vā nātinīcāṃ na cocchritām ||
āyuṣyāṇi ca śāntyarthaṃ japtvā tatra samāhitaḥ |
ṣaḍbhyaḥ pitṛbhyas tad anu śrāddhadānam upakramet ||
vasiṣṭhokto vidhiḥ kṛtsno draṣṭavyo ’tra nirāmiṣaḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi viśeṣa iha yo bhavet ||
prātarāmantritān viprān yugmān ubhayatas tathā |
upaveśya kuśān dadyād ṛajūn eva hi pāṇinā |
'āyuṣyāṇi' āyurvṛddhikarasūktāni | 'ṣaḍbhyaḥ' pitrādibhyaḥ mātāmahādibhyaś ca tribhyaḥ | ṣaḍbhya iti ca [273] mātṛpitāmahiprapitāmahīnām upalakṣaṇam | 'prātarāmantritān' iti ca vacanād vaikalpikam sāyamāmantraṇam api nivartate | 'ubhayataḥ' pitṛpakṣe vaiśvadevapakṣe ca |
tathā gobhilaḥ 1, 24-27] |
nipāto na hi savyasya jānuno vidyate kvacit |
sadā paricared bhaktyā pitṝn apy atra devavat ||
pitṛbhya iti datteṣu upaveśya kuśeṣu tān |
gotranāmabhir āmantrya pitṝn argha pradāpayet ||
nātrāpasavyakaraṇaṃ na pitryaṃ tīrtham iṣyate |
pātrāṇāṃ pūraṇādīni daivenaiva hi kārayet ||
jyeṣṭhottarakarān yugmān karāgrāgrapavitrakān |
kṛtvārghaṃ saṃpradātavyaṃ naikaikasyātra dīyate ||
'jyeṣṭhottarakarān' jyeṣṭhasya prathamopaveśitakarasyopari yathā karo bhavati tathetyarthaḥ | 'karāgrāgrapavitrān ' karāgre agrapavitrakaṃ pavitrāgraṃ yeṣāṃ te tān ||
tathā [gobhilaḥ 1, 39-55] |
madhu madhv iti yas tatra trirjapo ’śitum icchatām |
gāyatryanantaraṃ so ’tra madhumantravivarjitaḥ ||
na cāśnatsu japet tv atra kadācit pitṛsaṃhitām |
anya eva japaḥ kāryaḥ somasāmādikaḥ śubhaḥ ||
yas tatra prakaro ‘nnasya tilavad yavavat tathā |
ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau so ’tra tṛpteṣu viparītakaḥ ||
saṃpannam iti tṛptāḥ stha praśnasthāne vidhīyate |
susaṃpannam iti prokte śeṣam annaṃ nivedayet ||
prāgagreṣu ca darbheṣu ādyam āmantrya pūrvavat |
[274]        apaḥ kṣipen mūladeśe ’vanenikṣveti nistilāḥ ||
dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyaṃ ca madhyadeśāgradeśayoḥ |
mātāmahaprabhṛtīṃs tu teṣām eva ca vāmataḥ ||
sarvasmād annam uddhṛtya vyañjanair upasicya ca |
saṃyojyākṣatakarkandhudadhibhiḥ prāṅmukhas tataḥ ||
avanejanavat piṇḍān kṛtvā bilvapramāṇakān |
tatpātrakṣālanenātha punar apy avanejayet ||
uttarottaradāne ’pi piṇḍānām uttarottaraḥ |
bhaved adhaś cādharāṇām adharaśrāddhakarmasu ||
tasmāc chrāddheṣu sarveṣu vṛddhimatsv itareṣu ca |
mūlamadhyāgradeśeṣu īṣatsaktāṃś ca nirvapet ||
gandhādīn nikṣipet tūṣṇīṃ tata āvāhayed dvijān |
anyatrāpy eṣa eva syād yavādirahito vidhiḥ |
dakṣiṇāpravaṇe deśe dakṣiṇābhimukhasya ca |
dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu eṣo ’nyatra vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
athāgrabhūmim āsiñceta [su]saṃprokṣitam astv iti |
śivā āpaḥ santv iti ca yugmān evodakena ca ||
saumanasyam astv iti ca puṣpadānam ataḥ param |
akṣataṃ cāriṣṭaṃ cāstv iti cākṣatān pratipādayet ||
akṣayyodakadānaṃ ca arghadānavad iṣyate |
ṣaṣṭhyaiva nityaṃ tat kuryān na caturthyāṃ kadācana ||
prārthamāsu pratiprokte sarvāsv eva dvijottamaiḥ |
pavitrāntarhitān piṇḍān siñced uttānapātrakṛt ||
yugmān eva svasti vācya aṅguṣṭhagrahaṇaṃ sadā |
kṛtvā dhuryasya viprasya praṇamyānuvrajet tataḥ ||
'tilavad yavavat tathā' iti, yo ’nyatra tilayukto ’nnaprakaraḥ kriyate so ’tra yavayukto yathā bhavati tathā kartavyaḥ | [275] 'viparītakaḥ’ devatīrthādinā dānaprakāreṇa | 'ādyaṃ' puruṣam |
"īṣatsaktān’ īṣallagnān |
tathā [ gobhilaḥ 1, 68-68] |
asakṛd yāni karmāṇi kriyeran karmakāribhiḥ |
pratiprayogaṃ naiva syur mātaraṃ śrāddham eva ca ||
ādhāne homayoś caiva vaiśvadeve tathaiva ca |
balikarmaṇi darśe ca paurṇamāse tathaiva ca ||
navayajñe ca yajñe ca vadanty evaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ |
ekam eva bhavec chrāddham eteṣu na pṛthak pṛthak ||
nāṣṭakāsu bhave cchrāddhaṃ na śrāddhe śrāddham iṣyate |
na soṣyantījātakarmaproṣitāgatakarmasu ||
vivāhādiḥ karmagaṇo ya ukto
garbhādhānaṃ śuśruma yaḥ sa cānte |
vivāhādāv ekam evātra kuryāc
chrāddhaṃ nādau karmaṇaḥ karmaṇaḥ syāt ||
pradoṣe śrāddham ekaṃ syād goniṣkāsapraveśayoḥ |
na śrāddhaṃ yujyate kartuṃ prathame puṣṭikarmaṇi ||
halādiyogādiṣu ca ṣaṭsu kuryāt pṛthak pṛthak |
pratiprayogam anyeṣām ādāv ekatra kārayet ||
bṛhatpatrakṣudrapaśusvastyarthaṃ pariviṣyatoḥ |
sūryendoḥ karmaṇī ye tu tayoḥ śrāddhaṃ na vidyate ||
na daśāgranthikenaiva viṣavad daṣṭakarmaṇi |
kṛmidaṣṭacikitsāyāṃ naiva śeṣeṣu vidyate ||
gaṇaśaḥ kriyamāṇeṣu mātṛbhyaḥ pūjanaṃ sadā |
sakṛd eva bhavec chrāddham ādau na pṛthagādiṣu ||
yatra yatra bhavec chrāddhaṃ tatra tatra ca mātaraḥ |
[276] 'asakṛ yāni karmāṇi' ity asyaiva viṣayaviśeṣe niyamam āha — 'ādhāne homayoś caiva' ityādinā | tena somayāgādau pratiprayogaṃ śrāddhāvṛttiḥ | 'navayajñaṃ' āgrayaṇaḥ | 'proṣitāgatakarmasu' proṣya etya gṛhādyupatiṣṭhate 'putraṃ dṛṣṭvā japati' ityādivihitam | 'karmagaṇaḥ' gṛhapraveśacaturthīkarmādi: | 'mātaraḥ' janīyāḥ iti śeṣaḥ ||
tathā |
svapitṛbhyaḥ pitā dadyāt sutasaṃskārakarmasu |
piṇḍān odvāhanāt teṣāṃ tasyābhāve tu tat kramāt ||
'ā udvāhanāt' vivāhaparyantam |
yamaḥ |
pitṝṇām apy atithayaḥ śrāddhakāleṣu devatāḥ |
devatānāṃ ca pitaro bhavanty atithayaḥ sadā ||
iti śrībhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsandhivigrahikabhaṭṭaśrīakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau śrāddhakāṇḍe ābhyudayikaparva ||
[277]
|| 32 ||
|| atha jīvacchrāddhavidhiḥ ||
tatra brahmapurāṇe |
deśakāladhanaśraddhāvyavasāyasamutthite |
jīvite sati jīvāya dadyāc chrāddhaṃ svayaṃ naraḥ ||
kṛtopavāsaḥ susnātas trayodaśyāṃ samāhitaḥ |
kartāram atha bhoktāraṃ viṣṇuṃ sarveśvaraṃ yajet ||
jale sthale ’mbare mūrtau kalaśe puṣkare ravau |
candrāgnigurugovipramātāpitṛṣu sarvagam ||
sadakṣiṇāś ca satilās tisras tu jaladhenavaḥ |
nivedayet pitṛbhyaś ca tadagreṣu samāhitaḥ ||
somāya tvā pitṛmate svadhā nama iti smaran |
agnaye kavyavāhanāya svadhā nama iti smaran ||
dakṣiṇe tu nidadhyāc ca tṛtīyāṃ dakṣiṇāyutām |
yamāyāṅgirase vātha svadhā nama iti smaran ||
tayor madhye tu nikṣipya viprān pañcopaveśayet |
āvāhanadinā pūrvaṃ viśvān devān prapūjya ca ||
ahaṃ vasubhyas tvāṃ vipra rudrebhyas tvām ahaṃ tataḥ |
sūryebhyas tvām ahaṃ vipra bhojayāmīti tān vadet ||
āvahanādikaṃ śeṣaṃ kuryāt svapitṛkarmavat |
saumyāṃ dhenuṃ tato dadyād vāsavāyā dvijāya tu ||
[278]        viśvebhyaś cātha devebhyas tilapātraṃ nivedayet |
svasty udakam athākṣayyaṃ jalaṃ datvā ca tān dvijān ||
visarjayet smaran viṣṇuṃ devam aṣṭākṣaraṃ vibhum |
tataḥ kāmakuleśānaṃ niśi nārāyaṇaṃ smaran ||
caturdaśyāṃ tato gacched yathāprāptāṃ saridvarām |
pūrveṇa vipraḥ saumyena rājā vaiśyo pareṇa ca ||
dakṣiṇena tathā śūdro mārge ca vikired yavān |
vastrāṇi lohakhaṇḍāni jīvanta iti saṃsmaran ||
dakṣiṇābhimukho vahniṃ jvālayet tatra ca svayam |
pañcāśatā kuśair brāhmīṃ kṛtvā viprakṛtiṃ dahet ||
kṛtvā śmaśānikaṃ homaṃ pūrṇāhutyantam eva hi |
niragnir athavā bhūmiṃ yamaṃ rudraṃ ca saṃsmaran ||
hutvā prādhānikasthāne paścād dāhāpayec ca tām |
śrapayec cāpare vahnau mudgamiśraṃ caruṃ tataḥ ||
tilataṇḍulamiśraṃ ca dvitīyaṃ sapavitrakam |
pūrayet karṣukāṃ cātra madhukṣīraghṛtodakaiḥ ||
tadupānte tato mudgaiḥ pātrāṇi trīṇi pūrayet |
oṃ pṛthivyai namas tubhyam iti caikaṃ nivedayet ||
oṃ yamāya namaś ceti dvitīyaṃ tadanantaram |
oṃ namaś cātha rudrāya śmaśānapataye tathā ||
tato dīptasamiddhāgniṃ bhūmau prakṛtidāhakam |
oṃ kravyādam agnitaptāyai bhūmyai nama iti smaran ||
kṣīrāktaṃ jalakumbhaṃ ca vikiret tatpraśāntaye |
nābhimātraṃ tatas toyaṃ praviśya yamadiṅmukhaḥ ||
saptabhyo yamasaṃjñebhyo dadyāt sapta jalāñjalīn |
yamāya dharmarājāya mṛtyave cāntakāya ca ||
vaivasvatāya kālāya sarvaprāṇaharāya ca |
svadhākāranamaskārapraṇavaiḥ saha saptadhā ||
[279]        oṃ śivāya ca rudrāya śmaśānapataye smaran |
lājodakābhyāṃ pūrṇaṃ tu kumbhaṃ ca vikired bhuvi ||
yāmyonmukheṣu darbheṣu pradadyād avanejanam |
svadheti yuktavāhāya rudraṃ nama iti smaran ||
amukāmukagotraitat tubhyam astu tilodakam |
pradadyād daśa piṇḍāṃs tu arghapuṣpasamanvitān ||
dhūpo dīpo balir gandhaḥ sarveṣām astu cākṣayam |
daśa piṇḍāṃs tato datvā viṣṇoḥ saumyaṃ mukhaṃ smaret ||
niruṣmaṇaś ca tat toyaṃ nābhimātraṃ praviśya tu |
prakṣipet pūrṇakumbhena jalamadhye pṛthak pṛthak ||
pradadyāt pañcapañcāśat kumbhān atha jalāñjalīn |
dvāropānte gṛhe vātha kṣīraṃ toyaṃ ca niḥkṣipet ||
jīvātra snāhi dugdhaṃ ca pibedaṃ cāpy anusmaran |
yāmyonmukheṣu darbheṣu svapet paścād udaṅmukhaḥ |
athāmāvāsyāyāṃ kuryāc ca jīvacchrāddham ataḥ param |
ghṛtānnamāṃsadadhibhiḥ pūrayet karṣukātrayam ||
kuryāc ca māsikaṃ māsi sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tathā |
āśaucānte tataḥ sarvam ātmano vā parasya tu ||
kuryād asthiratāṃ jñātvā śaktyārogyadhanāyuṣām |
'dakṣiṇena' dakṣiṇadigbhāge adūra eva | ' vāsavāya ' vasubhya upaveśitāya brāhmaṇāya | evaṃ raudrāya ity api | 'prādhānikasthāne' pradhāna homasthāne ||
iti bhaṭṭahṛdayadharātmajamahāsandhivigrahikabhaṭṭaśrīlakṣmīdharaviracite kṛtyakalpatarau śrāddhakāṇḍaṃ samāptam ||
|| śubhaṃ bhūyāt ||
